### [1] [Color Coding for the Sherrington-Kirkpatrick Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10443)
*Alina Harbuzova,Saba Lepsveridze,Mahbod Majid,Ankur Moitra*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个多项式时间（polynomial-time）确定性算法，可在整个第二矩区间（second-moment regime）内以任意高精度逼近平均场混合 $p$-自旋模型（mean-field mixed $p$-spin models）的配分函数，改进了此前 Bencs 等人的拟多项式时间算法并回答了其开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前 Bencs、Huang、Lee、Liu 与 Regts（arXiv:2507.15616）仅给出拟多项式时间（quasipolynomial-time）算法，其遗留的开放问题是将复杂度改进为多项式时间并拓展覆盖范围。该工作旨在解决这一开放问题，并覆盖 Sherrington--Kirkpatrick 模型的整个复本对称区间（replica-symmetric regime）。

Method: 核心技术创新是将颜色编码（color coding）以新的算法方式应用于 Aizenman、Lebowitz 与 Ruelle 在研究 Sherrington--Kirkpatrick 配分函数涨落时提出的经典高温展开（high-temperature expansion）。该方法与 Bencs 等人建立的零自由点性质（zero-freeness）相结合，把加性近似（additive approximation）转化为乘性近似（multiplicative approximation）。

Result: 算法为确定性（deterministic），运行时间关于 $n$ 与 $1/\varepsilon$ 呈多项式，并对无序（disorder）的每一个典型实现均成功。其结果覆盖平均场混合 $p$-自旋模型的整个第二矩区间，从而完整包含 Sherrington--Kirkpatrick 模型的复本对称区间。

Conclusion: 该工作通过颜色编码与高温展开、零自由点性质的结合，将配分函数逼近的复杂度由拟多项式提升为多项式，回答了 Bencs 等人的开放问题，并扩展到更广的模型区间。

Abstract: We give a polynomial-time algorithm for approximating the partition function of mean-field mixed $p$-spin models to arbitrarily high accuracy throughout the second-moment regime. This improves the quasipolynomial-time algorithm of Bencs, Huang, Lee, Liu, and Regts (arXiv:2507.15616) and answers their open question. In particular, our result covers the entire replica-symmetric regime of the Sherrington--Kirkpatrick model. The algorithm is deterministic, runs in time polynomial in $n$ and $1/\varepsilon$, and succeeds for every typical realization of the disorder.
  Our main technical contribution is a new algorithmic application of color coding to the classical high-temperature expansion introduced by Aizenman, Lebowitz, and Ruelle in their study of fluctuations of the Sherrington--Kirkpatrick partition function. We combine this approach with the zero-freeness established by Bencs et al. (arXiv:2507.15616) to convert additive approximations into multiplicative ones.

</details>

### [2] [A gauge-invariant measure for lattice chiral fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09093)
*Nathaniel Craig*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 该文在四维欧氏格点上，针对包含标准模型的一类非阿贝尔手征规范理论，非微扰地构造了重叠费米子的规范不变测度，并借助Lüscher的测度重构方案给出标准模型的候选非微扰格点定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手征规范理论（chiral gauge theory）在格点上长期缺乏严格的非微扰定义，尤其是标准模型（Standard Model）这类非阿贝尔情形，需要满足规范不变性的费米子测度。该工作旨在填补这一空白，提供候选的格点定义。

Method: 采用重叠费米子（overlap fermions）于四维欧氏格点（four-dimensional Euclidean lattice），在容许规范场（admissible gauge fields）上比较相邻格点间距的手征投影算符（chiral projectors），由此构造指数局域的流（exponentially local current），并检验Lüscher测度重构（Lüscher's reconstruction）所需的整体性质。

Result: 在容许规范场范围内，相邻格距手征投影算符的比较给出了指数局域的流，并确立了重构光滑、规范不变的费米子测度所需的整体性质，从而完成对重叠费米子规范不变测度的非微扰构造。

Conclusion: 该构造为包含标准模型的非阿贝尔手征规范理论提供了候选的非微扰格点定义，推动标准模型在格点上的严格表述。

Abstract: We nonperturbatively construct a gauge-invariant measure for overlap fermions on four-dimensional Euclidean lattices in a class of nonabelian chiral gauge theories that includes the Standard Model. On admissible gauge fields, comparing chiral projectors at successive lattice spacings yields an exponentially local current and establishes the global properties needed for Lüscher's reconstruction of a smooth, gauge-invariant fermion measure. This provides a candidate nonperturbative lattice definition of the Standard Model.

</details>


### [3] [Sampling SU(N) gauge theory on a 2D lattice from independent plaquettes via holonomies and corner reweighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09147)
*Javad Komijani*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 在二维 SU(N) Wilson 格点规范理论中，将 plaquette 独立采样转化为群对易子（group commutator）的条件采样问题，并借助归一化流（normalizing flow）给出可用的生成采样方案，在测试格点上取得超过 99% 的单 plaquette 接受率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wilson 格点规范理论的基本自由度是群取值的连线变量（link variables），而作用量由 plaquette 的迹求和给出。对归一化流等生成采样方法而言，单个 plaquette 的分布易于建模，但由采样得到的 plaquette 反推连线变量存在障碍。该工作旨在寻找绕开这一障碍的统计途径。

Method: 在二维 SU(N) 规范理论中将作用量改写为 holonomy 变量，其仅在扩展格点四角满足一致性条件（consistency condition）时确定连线变量。通过选取只影响 holonomy 的边界条件，把该一致性条件转化为条件采样问题，即给定群对易子 Z = XYX†Y† 采样 X, Y ∈ SU(N)，其中 Z 由四个角决定。对 SU(2) 与 SU(3) 给出 Z 密度的闭式表达以确定权重；单 plaquette 分布由归一化流建模，群对易子问题对 SU(2) 用闭式采样器、对 SU(3) 用训练好的归一化流。

Result: 在 2×2 与 32×32 格点、各两个耦合常数下的测试中，单 plaquette 接受率超过 99%，最终权重的有效样本量（effective sample size）为中等水平，通常高于二分之一。

Conclusion: 通过将一致性条件转化为群对易子的条件采样，可在二维 SU(N) Wilson plaquette 作用量下实现 plaquette 独立采样并附带可计算的权重，SU(2) 与 SU(3) 的数值测试表明该方案具有高接受率与可接受的有效样本量。

Abstract: In the Wilson formulation of lattice gauge theory, the fundamental degrees of freedom are group-valued link variables, while the action is a sum over the trace of the plaquettes, the smallest Wilson loops. For generative sampling methods such as normalizing flows, this poses a challenge: the distribution of an individual plaquette is easy to model, but mapping sampled plaquettes to the links is the obstruction. We explore a statistical way around this in two dimensions for the $\mathrm{SU}(N)$ gauge theory with the Wilson plaquette action. The action can be written in terms of holonomy variables, which determine the links once a consistency condition at the four corners of an extended lattice is satisfied. Using a boundary condition that only affects the holonomies, we trade this consistency condition for a conditional sampling problem, which reduces to sampling $X,Y\in\mathrm{SU}(N)$ given the group commutator $Z=XYX^\dagger Y^\dagger$, where $Z\in\mathrm{SU}(N)$ depends on the four corners. Plaquettes are sampled independently, and the resulting configurations carry weights due to the additional conditional sampling. We obtain the density of $Z$, which determines these weights, in closed form for $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ and $\mathrm{SU}(3)$. A normalizing flow models the single-plaquette distribution for $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ and $\mathrm{SU}(3)$; for the group-commutator problem we use a closed-form sampler for $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ and a trained normalizing flow for $\mathrm{SU}(3)$. In our tests on $2\times2$ and $32\times32$ lattices at two couplings each, per-plaquette acceptance rates exceed $99\%$ and the effective sample size of the final weights is moderate, typically above one half.

</details>


### [4] [Finite-size scaling analysis of three dimensional Z(2) and O(2) spin models with non-vanishing symmetry breaking parameter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10350)
*Jishnu Goswami,Frithjof Karsch,Sabarnya Mitra*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文对三维 $Z(2)$ 与 $O(2)$ 自旋模型在外场 $H$ 下进行有限尺寸标度（finite-size scaling）分析，给出主导有限尺寸修正的参量形式，并据此改进 (2+1)-味 QCD 手征相变温度的分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非零外场下体能热力学量的有限尺寸修正此前缺乏良好控制，导致 (2+1)-味 QCD 手征相变温度分析中的无穷体积外推（infinite volume extrapolation）存在系统性误差。

Method: 基于高统计量蒙特卡罗（Monte Carlo）数据，对三维 $Z(2)$ 与 $O(2)$ 自旋模型在有限外场 $H$ 下开展有限尺寸标度分析，提取无穷体积标度函数的主导有限尺寸修正项。

Result: 该修正与 $\widetilde{z}_L^2=1/(H L^{3/(1+1/\delta)})^2$ 成正比，并为体能热力学可观测量及非零 $H$ 下确定的赝临界温度（pseudo-critical temperature）提供了有限尺寸修正的参量形式。

Conclusion: 该结果可消除 (2+1)-味 QCD 手征相变温度分析中的系统性误差；同时确立了主导修正的有效范围，并指出三维 $O(2)$ 与 $Z(2)$ 普适类（universality class）之间存在显著差异。

Abstract: We present results from a detailed finite-size scaling analysis of the $3$-$d$, $Z(2)$ and $O(2)$ spin models in an external field $H$. Using high statistics Monte Carlo data, we obtain the leading finite-size scaling correction to the infinite volume scaling functions. We show that these corrections are proportional to $\widetilde{z}_L^2=1/(H L^{3/(1+1/δ))})^{2}$. This provides the parametric form for finite-size corrections to bulk thermodynamic observables as well as the pseudo-critical temperatures determined at non-vanishing $H$. In particular, it allows to eliminate systematic errors in the analysis of the chiral phase transition temperature in (2+1)-flavor QCD, that arose from the previously not well-controlled ansatz for infinite volume extrapolations. We also establish the validity range of this leading order correction and point out that there are significant differences between the $3$-$d$, $O(2)$ and $Z(2)$ universality classes.

</details>

### [5] [The Differential Structure of Generators of KMS-Symmetric Quantum Markov Semigroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09678)
*Matthijs Vernooij,Melchior Wirth*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 该文完整刻画了有界量子狄利克雷形式（quantum Dirichlet forms），证明其可用导子（derivations）表示，并给出 I 型因子（type I factors）上的显式特征；同时证明任意无界量子狄利克雷形式亦可由导子表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KMS 对称量子马尔可夫半群（quantum Markov semigroup）由其在 GNS 希尔伯特空间（GNS Hilbert space）上诱导的二次型（quadratic form）完全决定，此类二次型即量子狄利克雷形式。理解其结构需要借助导子给出描述。

Method: 以导子（derivations）为工具对有界量子狄利克雷形式给出完整描述，并显式刻画 I 型因子（type I factors）上的情形；对无界情形亦给出导子表示。关键技术洞察为：模群（modular group）总限制为量子狄利克雷形式定义域上的强连续群，且解析生成元（analytic generator）的平方根相对形式范数（form norm）为增殖的（accretive）。

Result: 得到有界量子狄利克雷形式的导子完全描述，特别是 I 型因子上有界量子狄利克雷形式的显式特征；并证明任意无界量子狄利克雷形式均可用导子表示。

Conclusion: 量子狄利克雷形式（无论有界或无界）均可通过导子统一刻画；模群在形式定义域上的强连续限制及其解析生成元平方根的增殖性是实现该刻画的关键技术基础。

Abstract: A KMS-symmetric quantum Markov semigroup is completely determined by the quadratic form in induces on the GNS Hilbert space. The quadratic forms that arise in this way are called quantum Dirichlet forms. In this article we give a complete description of bounded quantum Dirichlet forms in terms of derivations. In particular, we exhibit an explicit characterization of the bounded quantum Dirichlet forms on type I factors. We also show that any unbounded quantum Dirichlet form can be represented in terms of derivations. The key technical insight is that the modular group always restricts to a strongly continuous group on the domain of a quantum Dirichlet form such that the square root of the analytic generator is accretive with respect to the form norm.

</details>

### [6] [High Bulk Modulus Pentamodes: the Three-Dimensional Metal Water](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.14502)
*Giacomo Brambilla,Sebastiano Cominelli,Marco Verbicaro,Gabriele Cazzulani,Francesco Braghin*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对三维声学超流体（acoustic metafluid）难以实现的问题，提出受绳索运动学（rope kinematics）启发的细纤维连接结构，设计出首个在低频段声学特性与水的不可区分的“三维金属水”（3D metal water）超材料（metamaterial）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管声子晶体（phononic crystals）领域取得显著进展，但能在三维中复现液体行为、尤其是达到水那类五模（pentamode, PM）材料性能的声学超流体仍难以实现。其核心矛盾在于：低剪切模量（shear modulus）的需求与高体积模量（bulk modulus）和高密度的追求内在冲突，现有PM几何结构因此受限。

Method: 作者首先阐明现有五模（PM）几何结构的局限性，继而提出一种构成网络的连接单元新形状。该连接受绳索运动学（rope kinematics）启发，由细纤维（thin fibres）构成，从而有望实现类液体性质的PM材料，并据此完成“三维金属水”（3D metal water）超材料的设计。

Result: 所设计材料在低频范围（low frequency regime）的声学特性与水的不可区分，成为首个名副其实的三维金属水超材料。此外，研究还揭示了晶格色散图（lattice dispersion diagram）中的剪切带隙（shear band gap），并说明了几何参数对高频段动态特性的影响。

Conclusion: 该新型超流体设计为需要各向异性材料（anisotropic materials）的应用提供了前景，如声学透镜（acoustic lenses）、波导（waveguides）与隐身斗篷（cloaks）。

Abstract: Despite significant advances in the field of phononic crystals, the development of acoustic metafluids that replicate the behaviour of liquids in three dimensions remains elusive. For instance, water - the quintessential pentamode (PM) material - has a bulk modulus two orders of magnitude higher than current state-of-the-art PMs. The need for a low shear modulus inherently conflicts with the desire of high bulk modulus and density. In this letter, we shed light on the limitations of existing PM geometries and propose an innovative shape for the links that constitute the network. Inspired by the kinematics of ropes, these links are constructed from thin fibres and demonstrate the potential to create PMs with properties akin to those of liquids. As a prime example, we present the design of the first metamaterial that fully deserves the name 3D metal water, since its acoustic properties in the low frequency regime are indistinguishable with water. Additionally, we highlight a shear band gap in the lattice dispersion diagram, and illustrate the influence of geometric parameters on the dynamic properties at higher frequencies. This novel design of metafluids holds promise for applications requiring anisotropic materials such as acoustic lenses, waveguides, and cloaks.

</details>

### [7] [Plunge bounds for Fourier concentration operators: geometry, smoothing, and wave packets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09406)
*Ahmadreza Azimifard*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该文证明了傅里叶集中算子（Fourier concentration operators）在 plunge region 中特征值的单对数上界，去掉了 Kulikov–Dam Larsen 界中的额外对数因子，并给出最优几何常数与相关估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目的是改进现有关于傅里叶集中算子 plunge region 特征值计数的结果，消除 Kulikov–Dam Larsen 界中的额外对数因子，并建立与几何分解常数之间的定量联系。

Method: 采用正带限平滑（positive band-limited smoothing）方法；不需要对称性、凸性或连通性假设。同时确定球与标准单纯形递归分解的最优坐标 Lipschitz 常数，并利用一致实阶 Bessel 估计（uniform real-order Bessel estimates）与高斯包相关估计。

Result: 得到上界 Λ_ε(cA,B) ≤ C(d,A,B,β)c^{d-1}D(1+log_+(c/D))，其中 D=log(1/(ε(1-ε)))；二维情形常数关于窗口参数一致。还得到最优坐标 Lipschitz 常数 d-1、三维 C^1 凸体递归坐标斜率最优下确界 2，以及显式几何常数的 box-frequency 估计与深层尾部项。

Conclusion: 该结果将全局谱界与尖锐几何构造以及分组边界相互作用所保留的相消联系起来，提供了更精确的单对数界及配套几何与相关估计。

Abstract: We prove a single-logarithm bound for the eigenvalues in the plunge region of Fourier concentration operators, removing the extra logarithmic factor in the Kulikov--Dam Larsen bound for two general admissible sets throughout each fixed exponential window. For bounded spatial and frequency sets $A,B\subset\mathbb{R}^d$ of positive measure with finite upper codimension-one Minkowski boundary content, the number $Λ_\varepsilon(cA,B)$ of eigenvalues in $(\varepsilon,1-\varepsilon)$ satisfies $Λ_\varepsilon(cA,B)\le C(d,A,B,β)c^{d-1}D(1+\log_+(c/D))$, where $D=\log(1/(\varepsilon(1-\varepsilon)))$, for every fixed $β>0$, $c\ge2$, $0<\varepsilon<1/2$, and $D\leβc$, with $\log_+t=\max(0,\log t)$. The argument uses positive band-limited smoothing and requires no symmetry, convexity, or connectedness. In dimension two, the corresponding exponential-window estimate has a constant uniform in the window parameter.
  We also determine the optimal coordinate-Lipschitz constant $d-1$ for recursive decompositions of balls and standard simplices in every dimension, and obtain box-frequency estimates with explicit geometric constants. For $C^1$ convex bodies in three dimensions, the optimal infimum of recursive coordinate slopes is $2$. Uniform real-order Bessel estimates give independent disk and ball bounds at every spectral threshold, including an explicit deep-tail term. For smooth strictly convex planar frequency bodies with positive curvature, we prove correlation estimates for aligned Gaussian packets at arbitrary relative scales and quantitative lower bounds on the cost of estimating spatial blocks separately. Together these results connect the global spectral bound with sharp geometric constructions and the cancellation retained by grouping boundary interactions.

</details>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [8] [The Dichotomy Between Pattern Recognition and Step-by-Step Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09186)
*Amrut Nadgir,Pratik Chaudhari,Vijay Balasubramanian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出模式识别（pattern recognition）与逐步推理（step-by-step reasoning）是同一谱系的两端，其区分取决于下一词元（next token）所依赖的前文长度 c；推理轨迹构成 De Bruijn 图的有向无环子图，边数远少于轨迹数，故逐步推理具有样本效率，并存在精度与鲁棒性的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文试图统一解释大语言模型（LLM）中模式识别与逐步推理两种能力的关系，并回答何种数据结构使模型学会逐步推理、其样本效率来源为何，以及精度与鲁棒性如何权衡。

Method: 将下一词元仅依赖最近 c 个词元的任务建模为 De Bruijn 图（其节点为长度 c 的上下文，边为上下文间的下一词元转移），推理轨迹集合构成该图的有向无环子图；通过维护使未来推理独立于过去的“状态”来诱导 De Bruijn 结构，并以状态频率决定 c；同时对 Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct 在解方程与故事问答任务上微调，并在 GSM8K、MATH-500、GPQA-Diamond 上对 Qwen3-14B/32B 施加滑动窗口注意力限制。

Result: 理论证明边的数量相对推理轨迹数量趋于可忽略；实证显示 transformer 所需训练样本数随边数呈幂律（power law）增长，即逐步推理样本高效。微调实验表明，频繁状态（小 c）带来更高准确率但对测试时扰动更脆弱，大 c 训练的模型仅相当于无推理的模式识别；中等状态密度可平衡准确率与鲁棒性。真实数据具备 De Bruijn 结构：当注意力限制在不足完整推理轨迹 15% 长度的滑动窗口时，Qwen3-14B 与 Qwen3-32B 在 GSM8K、MATH-500 与 GPQA-Diamond 上仍保留超过 75% 的准确率。

Conclusion: 模式识别与逐步推理构成连续谱系而非对立概念，其分界由有效上下文长度 c 刻画；逐步推理因可组合稀疏边而具备样本效率，且状态密度决定了准确率与鲁棒性之间的权衡，而真实任务数据呈现出可验证的 De Bruijn 结构。

Abstract: We argue that pattern recognition and step-by-step reasoning are two ends of a spectrum. A large language model (LLM) learns to reason step-by-step when data is structured such that the next token depends on a small amount of preceding context. Inference in LLMs resembles pattern recognition when the next token depends on a large amount of preceding context. If the next token depends on only the $c$ most recent tokens, reasoning traces are paths on a De Bruijn graph whose nodes are $c$-length contexts and edges are next-token transitions between contexts. The set of reasoning traces of a task forms a directed acyclic subgraph of the De Bruijn graph. An LLM that has learned all edges of this subgraph can compose them to solve longer, unseen tasks, i.e., it reasons step-by-step. We prove that the number of edges is vanishingly small compared to the number of reasoning traces. Empirically, the number of training samples a transformer needs is a power law in the number of edges, so learning to reason step-by-step is sample efficient. We can induce De Bruijn structure in any task by maintaining a ``state'' that makes future reasoning independent of the past. The frequency of states in the reasoning trace determines $c$. We show, by fine-tuning Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct to solve equations and answer questions about stories, that frequent states (small $c$) result in higher accuracy but greater fragility to perturbations at test time. LLMs trained with a large $c$ are only as good as models that perform pattern recognition without reasoning. A moderate density of states balances accuracy and robustness. We show that real-world data has De Bruijn structure: Qwen3-14B and Qwen3-32B retain over 75% of their accuracy on GSM8K, MATH-500 and GPQA-Diamond when attention is restricted to a sliding window less than 15% as long as the full reasoning trace.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经能量求解器（neural energy solvers）此前已被用于相场断裂（phase-field fracture），并通过网络输入表示尖锐裂纹，但含裂纹域的热传导与由此产生的热应力驱动的裂纹扩展尚未被联合处理，二者耦合是热-力断裂问题中缺失的环节。

Result: 在静止热边缘裂纹算例中，提取的应力强度因子与文献值误差为 0.11%；在功能梯度剪切试验中，起裂预测与独立尖锐裂纹有限元解相差不超过一个荷载步；在缺口十字形试样上，裂纹路径在力学、热及联合载荷下均与已发表解一致。

Conclusion: 该扩展深度能量方法可实现无正则化长度的热-力耦合尖锐裂纹扩展模拟，并在热边缘裂纹、功能梯度剪切及缺口十字形试样等算例中验证了其精度与路径预测的可靠性。

Abstract: Thermo-mechanical fracture couples transient heat conduction on a cracked domain with a crack that grows as the temperature and the displacement evolve. Neural energy solvers have been proposed for phase-field fracture and later extended to represent a sharp crack through the network input, but heat conduction on the cracked domain and crack propagation under the resulting thermal stresses have not yet been treated together in these solvers. We present an extended deep energy method for thermo-mechanical crack propagation in which the crack remains a sharp polyline. Two networks represent the temperature and the displacement and receive the crack through a scalar embedding function, discontinuous across the crack and smooth elsewhere, so that both fields can jump across it without a regularization length, and the displacement is enriched near the tip by the Williams expansion with trainable amplitudes. The two fields are obtained by minimizing an incremental conduction functional and the thermoelastic potential energy in a staggered sequence, with Monte Carlo integration on points stratified over background elements, densified near the tip and redrawn during training. The stress intensity factors are extracted by the interaction integral with the area term of Wilson and Yu and checked by a sweep of the contour radius, and the crack advances at the maximum hoop stress angle when the energy release rate of the kink reaches the critical value at the crack-tip temperature. On a stationary thermal edge crack the extracted stress intensity factor agrees with the published value to 0.11%, in a functionally graded shear test initiation agrees with an independent sharp-crack finite element solution to within one load step, and on a notched cruciform specimen the crack paths follow the published solutions under mechanical, thermal and combined loading.

</details>

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究以纳米多孔氧化铌（nanoporous niobium oxide）物理储备池为平台，揭示噪声相关时间、储备池记忆与预测时域之间的匹配关系决定相关噪声是被滤波还是被预测，并提出储备池记忆时域与预测区制指数两个量化指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理储备池计算（physical reservoir computing）虽比传统机器学习更节能，但其内在响应时间尺度固定，而真实信号却在多个时间尺度上同时包含确定性成分与随机成分，二者之间的尺度失配构成待解问题。

Method: 采用纳米多孔氧化铌储备池，处理合成含噪信号与加密货币价格波动（cryptocurrency-price volatility）数据，考察噪声相关时间（noise correlation time）、储备池记忆（reservoir memory）与预测时域（forecast horizon）三者关系对噪声处理方式的影响。

Result: 变化快于相关储备池记忆与预测时域的噪声被储备池平均化；变化较慢的噪声其时间结构足以用于算法预测。作者据此提出储备池记忆时域（reservoir memory horizon）与预测区制指数（forecasting regime index）以区分不同运行区制。

Conclusion: 时间尺度匹配（timescale matching）可为输入时间序列的编码方式以及物理储备池架构的设计提供指导，从而在不同时间尺度上实现随机信号成分的滤波、分析与预测。

Abstract: Physical reservoir computing exploits the nonlinear dynamics of physical systems to process time-dependent data with greater energy efficiency than conventional machine learning approaches. However, physical reservoirs have fixed intrinsic response timescales, whereas real-world signals combine deterministic and stochastic components across multiple timescales. Here we show, using a nanoporous niobium oxide reservoir, synthetic noisy signals and cryptocurrency-price volatility, that the relationship among noise correlation time, reservoir memory and forecast horizon determines whether correlated noise is filtered or predicted. Noise varying faster than the relevant reservoir memory and forecast horizon is averaged by the reservoir, whereas the temporal structure of slower-varying noise is sufficient for algorithmic forecasting. We introduce the reservoir memory horizon and forecasting regime index to distinguish these operating regimes. These contributions demonstrate that timescale matching can guide the encoding of input time series and development of physical reservoir architectures that filter, analyse and predict stochastic signal components across distinct temporal scales.

</details>

*Robert Hammann,Steffen Form,Humaira Nazneen,Gabriel Schöpfer,Milan Ončák,Teresa Marrodán Undagoitia*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 该工作测量了气态氙中α粒子相互作用在约950–1620 nm、共12个波段内的近红外（near-infrared, NIR）闪烁时间响应，将其分解为ns量级快成分与μs量级主导慢成分，并用两步动力学模型解释其压力依赖，推断出发射态本征寿命约3 μs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氙的近红外闪烁信号有望提升基于氙的探测器性能，但其性质尚缺乏可靠理解，这是实现应用的前提。

Method: 在气态氙中对α粒子相互作用测量12个波长波段（约950–1620 nm）的NIR时间响应，将响应分解为快、慢两个成分，并用含三体成态、二体碰撞解激发的两步动力学模型拟合连续谱的压力依赖；同时进行理论计算以甄别发射跃迁候选。

Result: 快成分谱与强原子发射线（主要为6p→6s）吻合，慢成分谱对应以1.3 μm为中心的宽连续谱；两步动力学模型可描述连续谱的压力依赖，速率中含与密度无关的项，将其中较慢者解释为辐射衰变给出约3 μs的本征寿命；理论计算指认中性激基复合物跃迁0_u^-→0_g^-与离子二聚体跃迁1/2_u→1/2_g为连续谱的可能来源。

Conclusion: 离子二聚体跃迁在总体符合度上优于中性激基复合物跃迁，因此是所观测连续谱发射更可能的候选机制。

Abstract: The near-infrared (NIR) scintillation signal of xenon has the potential to enhance the performance of xenon-based detectors, but a robust understanding of its properties is a prerequisite for its application. In this work, we measure the NIR time response for $α$-particle interactions in gaseous xenon in 12 wavelength bands spanning approximately $(950-1620)\,$nm. The time response is decomposed into a fast (ns-scale) component and a dominant slow ($μ$s-scale) component. The fast-component spectrum coincides with strong atomic emission lines, predominantly 6p$\,\to\,$6s, whereas the slow-component spectrum follows the broad continuum centred around $1.3\,μ$m. The pressure dependence of the continuum can be described by a two-step kinetic model in which the emitting state is formed by a three-body process and depopulated by a two-body collisional process, with density-independent terms in both rates. Interpreting the slower of these as radiative decay yields an intrinsic lifetime of $\sim3\,μ$s. Theoretical calculations identify a neutral-excimer transition $0_u^- \rightarrow 0_g^-$ and an ionic-dimer transition $1/2_{u} \rightarrow 1/2_g$ as plausible candidates for the continuum emission, with the ionic-dimer transition showing better overall agreement with the measurements.

</details>

<details>

Motivation: 传统混合像素探测器（hybrid pixel detector）的时间游走校正依赖启发式拟合，需按输入变量分箱独立进行，既需显式函数假设与分箱方案，又需大量标定数据，且随输入变量增加数据需求迅速增长。

Result: 仅使用 20% 标定数据时，神经网络已达到与拟合基线相同的渐近时间分辨率，两种厚度分别对应 180 ps 与 300 ps。

Conclusion: 基于神经网络的 timewalk 校正可在显著减少标定数据需求的同时，达到与传统拟合方法相当的时间分辨率，体现出无需显式函数形式与分箱的方案优势。

Abstract: Timewalk corrections of hybrid pixel detectors conventionally employ heuristic fits performed independently in bins of the relevant input variables. This requires both an explicit functional ansatz and binning scheme, as well as densely populated calibration data whose required size grows rapidly as further input variables are added. This paper presents a timewalk correction for the Timepix4 readout chip based on a compact feed-forward neural network (NN), which learns the dependence directly from the input variables without requiring an explicit functional ansatz or binning. The NN is compared against the fit-based baseline using data from the Timepix4 beam telescope at the CERN SPS in 2025, with $100\,\mathrm{μm}$ and $300\,\mathrm{μm}$ thick planar sensors. Both methods parameterise the timewalk correction as a function of the measured charge and reconstructed intrapixel impact position. With only 20 % of the calibration data, the NN already achieves the same asymptotic time resolutions as the fit-based baseline: $180\,\mathrm{ps}$ and $300\,\mathrm{ps}$ for the two thicknesses, respectively.

</details>

### [13] [Gravitational scattering amplitudes in collinear and high-energy limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08860)
*Lorenzo Pietrini*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本论文研究引力散射振幅（gravitational scattering amplitudes）的高能与共线（collinear）行为，及其与经典散射和辐射可观测量的关系；通过多雷吉运动学（multi-Regge kinematics）及其放松推广，提取领头对数与次领头对数精度的因子化构件，并构造弹性 H 核、辐射扩展及经典次多雷吉核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力散射振幅在高能极限下的因子化结构是连接量子振幅与经典散射、引力辐射观测量（如波形）的关键。多雷吉运动学提供了领头对数结构，但次领头对数精度还需引入相邻粒子快度可比的情形，相关构件此前尚不完整。

Method: 采用多雷吉运动学及放松快度排序的次多雷吉运动学（next-to-multi-Regge kinematics）作为因子化框架；从纯引力子（pure gravitons）树级 MHV 振幅提取次多雷吉碰撞因子并导出引力分裂函数（gravitational splitting functions）；利用 BCFW 递推关系重建双大质量标量与单引力子发射的五点树级振幅；通过 KMOC 形式获得高能共线波形表示；并以幺正切割（unitarity cuts）缝合相关顶点构造各类核。

Result: 得到纯引力子树级 MHV 振幅对应的次多雷吉碰撞因子及其引力分裂函数；重建了双大质量标量散射加单引力子发射的五点树级振幅，其高能极限给出大质量标量—引力子碰撞因子，并经 KMOC 形式得到领头高能共线波形的表示；通过幺正切割构造出弹性 H 核、其辐射扩展及经典次多雷吉核。

Conclusion: 所构造的经典次多雷吉核在多雷吉极限下退化为标准 H 核，在软极限下退化为普适的温伯格流（Weinberg current），表明该因子化框架与已知极限结果一致，为高能引力散射与经典辐射的统一描述提供了基础。

Abstract: In this thesis we study the high-energy and collinear behaviour of gravitational scattering amplitudes and their relation to classical scattering and radiation observables. Multi-Regge kinematics, characterised by a strong rapidity ordering, provides the factorised building blocks governing the leading logarithmic structure. Relaxing this ordering to next-to-multi-Regge kinematics allows two neighbouring particles to have comparable rapidities and provides the real emission building blocks entering at next-to-leading logarithmic accuracy. We extract the next-to-multi-Regge impact factor from tree-level MHV amplitudes for pure gravitons and derive its gravitational splitting functions. We then use BCFW recursion relations to reconstruct the five-point tree amplitude for two massive scalars scattering with the emission of one graviton. Its high-energy limit yields the massive scalar--graviton impact factor and, through the KMOC formalism, a representation of the leading high-energy collinear waveform. Finally, by sewing the relevant vertices across unitarity cuts, we construct the elastic \(H\) kernel, its radiative extension and a classical next-to-multi-Regge kernel. The latter reproduces the standard \(H\) kernel in the multi-Regge limit and the universal Weinberg current in the soft limit.

</details>


### [14] [The Cosmological $δ$-shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08904)
*Carolina Figueiredo,Jiajie Mei,Yuyu Mo,Francisco Vazão*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将连接 Tr(φ³) 振幅与π介子振幅的 δ-shift 推广至宇宙学可观测量的计算，通过频率表示将波函数（wavefunction）中的非线性形变线性化，并进一步构造到 de Sitter 空间（de Sitter space）的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: δ-shift 此前已在振幅层面联系 Tr(φ³) 理论与π介子理论，但能否将其扩展到宇宙学中的波函数与关联函数（correlators）尚不明确；且波函数依赖于动量模长而非动量平方，导致原有形变不再线性，构成推广的主要障碍。

Method: 先在平直空间（flat-space）中通过简单的运动学形变（kinematic deformation）从 Tr(φ³) 理论提取π介子波函数与关联函数；采用频率表示（frequency representation）将该形变“线性化”，从而把任意图（graph）的贡献改写为留数求和（sum over residues）；随后将该构造推广至 de Sitter 空间。

Result: 在频率表示下，π介子的 de Sitter 波函数与关联函数可写成与平直空间完全一致的形式；该统一表述还揭示出平直空间与 de Sitter 共有的普适软极限（soft-limit），在此极限下波函数转化为π介子与标量混合理论（mixed theory）中波函数的求和。

Conclusion: δ-shift 可从振幅扩展至宇宙学波函数与关联函数，频率表示是实现该扩展并统一平直空间与 de Sitter 情形的关键工具，并由此给出统一的软极限结构。

Abstract: We consider the extension of the $δ$-shift connecting Tr$(φ^3)$ and pion amplitudes to observables in cosmology. We start in flat-space and explain how one can extract the pion wavefunction and correlators from those of Tr$(φ^3)$ theory, via a simple kinematic deformation. As opposed to the amplitude, the $δ$-shift for the wavefunction is no longer a linear deformation on the kinematics since the wavefunction depends on magnitudes rather than the squares of momenta. We "linearize" this deformation by using the frequency representation - allowing us to recast the contribution from any graph as a sum over residues. We then show how this construction extends to de Sitter, and find that, remarkably, in the frequency representation, the pion de Sitter wavefunction and correlators can be formulated in precisely the same way as in flat-space. Amongst other things, this unified formulation reveals a simple universal soft-limit for flat-space and de Sitter, where, in the soft-limit, the wavefunction turns into a sum of wavefunctions in a mixed theory of pions and scalars.

</details>


### [15] [Worldsheets reconstructed: Duals to two-cut matrix models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08913)
*Sounak Pal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文把谱曲线的矩阵数据推广到高亏格情形，重建了LG理论中顶点算符的世界面关联函数，并给出高阶谱曲线数据、CohFT与矩阵模型可观测量之间的显式对应字典。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前相变仅在零角势、$Tr\\sim\\sqrt{N}$ 条件下被研究；本文引入角势（angular potential）作为额外调控参数，旨在考察相变条件如何被调制，以及能否在固定温度下触发相变。

The model undergoes a phase transition when the temperature $T$ and angular potential attain the relation $T^2r^2\sim N (1-ω^2)$, where $ω=rΩ$ and $r$ is the radius of $S^2$, with $Tr\gg1$, at both the fixed points, with different numerical coefficients. The angular potential introduces an additional parameter to such a transition, tuning it, the transition can occur even at a fixed temperature. For the interacting model the computation is performed at a limit $Tr\gg1$, thus we show the same at a large but fixed temperature which does not scale as $\sqrt{N}$. In the ungapped phase, the free energy exhibits a double pole near $ω^2=1$ for both the fixed points.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一部分旨在检验半经典爱因斯坦场方程（Einstein field equations, EFEs）这一假设的计算后果，并据此为引力量子化提供证据；第二部分则试图将准晶体这一凝聚态与几何概念拓展到时空语境，以探索其对基本物理问题的启示。

Method: 第一部分：假设引力场为经典、且通过半经典 EFEs 与量子场耦合，分析大质量非相对论量子比特的弱场动力学。第二部分：将 Penrose 平铺等自相似准晶体从欧氏空间推广到闵可夫斯基时空，构造洛伦兹准晶体（Lorentzian quasicrystals），并提出将 (3+1) 维宇宙嵌入 (9+1) 维环面 T^{9,1} 的设想、以有序高对称准晶因果集（causal sets）替换随机因果集，并检验其量子-体积对应与超均匀性（hyperuniformity）。

Result: 第一部分：该量子比特的弱场动力学原则上可在多项式时间内求解一个 NP 完全问题，其计算能力源于半经典 EFEs 带来的非线性量子比特动力学，因而上述两个假设蕴含对物理扩展丘奇-图灵论题（Physical Extended Church-Turing Thesis）的违背。第二部分：构造出首批洛伦兹准晶体并识别其关键新特征；该 (9+1) 维环面嵌入设想可能有助于理解普朗克、真空能与电弱能标之间的跷跷板关系（seesaw relation）M_Pl M_vac ≈ M_EW^2；准晶因果集在 (1+1) 维与 (3+1) 维的量子-体积对应上似乎优于泊松随机集；且 (1+1) 维准晶体成为首个被证明的时空超均匀结构。

Conclusion: 第一部分认为，半经典引力假设导致违背物理扩展丘奇-图灵论题，这可作为引力量子化的证据。第二部分表明，时空准晶体为引力量子化之外的诸多基础物理问题提供了富有启发的几何与因果结构视角，包括能标跷跷板关系、因果集理论与时空超均匀性。

Abstract: Part I: Semiclassical Gravity Efficiently Solves $\mathsf{NP}$-Complete Problems
  Assuming the gravitational field is classical and that it couples to quantum fields via the semiclassical Einstein field equations (EFEs), we show that the weak-field dynamics of a massive and non-relativistic qubit can in principle be used to solve an $\mathsf{NP}$-complete problem in polynomial time. We attribute this vast computational power to the non-linear qubit dynamics afforded by the semiclassical EFEs. Consequently, the above two assumptions entail a violation of the Physical Extended Church--Turing Thesis, which we regard as evidence for the quantization of gravity.
  Part II: Spacetime Quasicrystals
  We generalize self-similar quasicrystals, such as the Penrose tilings, from Euclidean space to Minkowski spacetime. We construct the first examples of Lorentzian quasicrystals and identify their key novel features. We then explore possible connections to fundamental physics. First, we propose a speculative scenario in which our $(3+1)$D universe is embedded in a particularly symmetric $(9+1)$D torus $\mathbb{T}^{9,1}$, previously identified as the most symmetric toroidal compactification of the superstring. We suggest that this construction may shed light on the seesaw relation $M_{\rm Pl}M_{\rm vac} \approx M_{\rm EW}^{2}$ between the Planck, vacuum-energy, and electroweak scales. Second, we replace the random causal sets of causal set theory with ordered, highly symmetric quasi-crystalline causal sets. We find suggestive evidence that our quasicrystals outperform Poisson random sets in the number-volume correspondence in both $(1+1)$D and $(3+1)$D, where such behaviour has been conjectured to be impossible. Finally, extending hyperuniformity from space to spacetime, we show that our $(1+1)$D quasicrystal is the first demonstrated example of a spacetime-hyperuniform structure.

</details>

Abstract: We study the pole mass for a heavy quark probe in the Gross--Neveu model, at the first non-trivial order in the $1/N$ expansion. Conclusion: 有限 $N$ 构造保留了原理论的对称性破缺特征，并借助弱耦合区的参量控制，将结论从超流推广至超导，为在有限场数下研究任意温度下的对称性破缺提供了可控框架。

Abstract: We construct unitary, local, 3+1-dimensional conformal field theories with finitely many fields whose thermal state breaks a continuous global symmetry at every temperature. The circle is lifted at order $1/N^2$, and an infinite family of fixed points on this arc reaches arbitrarily weak coupling, where the superfluid is under parametric control. Abstract: We show that the radial sector of a Carroll-Weyl gauged null string in Poincaré $\mathrm{AdS}_D$ is locally trivial in BRST cohomology. An explicit contracting homotopy extends this reduction to the full algebra of local spatial jets, leaving precisely the $(D-1)$-dimensional ILST BRST complex. The same quartet carries zero net Virasoro central charge, so that the anomaly condition of the parent theory, $D=27$, descends to the ILST condition $d=26$.

</details>

Motivation: 作者由不变有限差分格式（invariant finite-difference schemes）的经验出发，提出关于9点模板上非线性多项式格式无法同时保持能量与动量守恒律差分类比（difference analogues of conservation laws）的猜想，并希望明确该限制的适用范围及能否在更大模板上突破。

Method: 对9点模板，用多项式次数的任意性给出该不可能性的证明；对更大模板，采用直接法（direct method）的差分类比，在多项式次数有界的条件下进行构造性搜索与分析，并辅以精确解上的数值测试验证逼近阶与守恒律精度。

Result: 9点模板上该假设对任意次多项式均成立（已证明）；在更大模板上，有界次多项式格式仅在模板足够大时才存在，且这类格式所逼近的二阶方程为Monge–Ampère型。由此得到一个齐次Monge–Ampère方程的格式，具有五条守恒律、保持微分方程的大部分对称性，并在离散二面体群（discrete dihedral group）D4下不变。

Conclusion: 非线性多项式守恒格式的存在性受模板大小与守恒律数目之间关系的严格约束：9点模板下能量与动量守恒不可兼得，而在更大模板上需付出逼近方程类型改变（Monge–Ampère型）的代价才可能实现。所得Monge–Ampère格式在精确解上的数值测试确认了其二阶逼近精度，且守恒律在同阶精度下得到满足。

Abstract: Experience with invariant finite-difference schemes for one-dimensional hydro\-dynamic-type equations for a function $u(t,x)$ suggests that nonlinear polynomial schemes on orthogonal uniform meshes with the 9-point stencil cannot simultaneously preserve difference analogues of the energy and momentum conservation laws, with the multipliers given by the standard difference approximations of $u_t$ and $u_x$. For the 9-point stencil this hypothesis is proved for polynomials of arbitrary degree. On larger stencils it is investigated by means of the difference analogue of the direct method: for polynomials of bounded degree, such schemes are found to exist only on sufficiently large stencils, and the second-order equations they approximate are of Monge--Ampère type. As a by-product, a scheme for the homogeneous Monge--Ampère equation is obtained that has five conservation laws, admits most of the symmetries of the differential equation, and is invariant under the discrete dihedral group $D_4$. Numerical tests on exact solutions confirm its second order of approximation and show that the conservation laws are satisfied on these solutions with the same order of accuracy.

</details>

*Valentin D. Kunz,Saleh Tanveer,Darren G. Crowdy*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该工作将无粘流体力学（inviscid fluid mechanics）中两类二维自由边界问题（free boundary problems）之间的数学类比（mathematical analogy）从已有结果推广到新的问题对：含三阶无旋应变（third order irrotational strain）剪切流中的空心涡（hollow vortex）平衡与简单应变流中含表面张力（surface tension）的稳态气泡（steady bubble）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究发现了无粘流体力学中两类物理上不同的二维自由边界问题之间的数学类比：一类为内部具有常涡量（constant vorticity）但无边界表面张力（surface tension）的自由表面流，另一类为内部无涡量（irrotational）但具有非零边界表面张力（即毛细作用，capillarity）的自由表面流。本文旨在检验并拓展该类比是否适用于另一组物理上不同的问题。

Method: 在参数 $γ=0$ 的情形下为两类问题构造孤立的精确解（exact solution），并在 $γ\neq 0$ 时给出数值解（numerical solutions）；同时分析由毛细作用类问题导出的共形映射恒等式（conformal mapping identities），并在参数适当约束下将其应用于空心涡问题。

Result: 该类比成功扩展至新的问题对，即剪切流（shear flow）中的空心涡平衡与简单应变流（straining flow）中的稳态气泡；对两类问题均在 $γ=0$ 时获得孤立精确解，并在非零 $γ$ 下获得数值解；此外，毛细作用类问题中出现的非平凡共形映射恒等式在参数受到适当约束时同样适用于空心涡问题。

Conclusion: 研究表明，先前建立的两类无粘自由边界问题之间的数学类比具有更广的适用性，可涵盖带有三阶无旋应变的剪切流中的空心涡与含表面张力的稳态气泡这一新的物理问题对，并通过共形映射恒等式的跨类适用性进一步体现了两类问题在数学结构上的深层联系。

Abstract: In a recent paper, a mathematical analogy was found between two physically distinct classes of two-dimensional free boundary problems in inviscid fluid mechanics. One class involves free surface flows with constant vorticity in the bulk but without boundary surface tension, the other comprises free surface flows without vorticity in the bulk but with non-zero boundary surface tension (capillarity). Several examples were given of pairs of physically-distinct free boundary problems that amount to solving the same mathematical problem, albeit with the possibility of a single constraint arising for the capillary flows. The present work shows that this analogy extends to another pair of physically-distinct problems: a hollow vortex equilibrium in a shear flow with a third order irrotational strain and a steady bubble with surface tension in a simple straining flow. For both problems, we construct an isolated exact solution when a parameter $γ=0$ and provide numerical solutions for non-zero $γ$. An interesting feature is that non-trivial conformal mapping identities emerge from the class of problems with capillarity that apply equally for the hollow vortex problems when the parameters are appropriately constrained.

</details>

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在寻找一种简单、可编程的方式，对弹跳液滴（bouncing droplets）施加横向力，以实现对其横向运动的控制，服务于导波流体动力学（pilot-wave hydrodynamics）中液滴的操纵需求。

Method: 在常规垂直驱动力（vertical forcing）之上叠加小幅周期性摇摆运动（periodic rocking motion）；以实验观测横向速度随摇摆振幅 α 及相对垂直驱动的相位 φ 的变化；并建立谐振弹跳者（harmonic bouncer）的时间平均模型。

Result: 实验显示横向速度与摇摆振幅 α 呈线性关系，与相位 φ 呈正弦关系；响应强烈依赖液滴的垂直弹跳模式（bouncing mode）。时间平均模型给出时间平均横向力 \overline{F}_x=mgαsin(Φ_b+φ)，其中 Φ_b 为液滴撞击相位（impact phase）。

Conclusion: 周期性摇摆提供了一种简单且可编程的横向力施加手段，可用于被动与主动液滴（passive and active droplets）的可控操纵，拓展了导波流体动力学中的液滴操控能力。

Abstract: We introduce a method for controlling the lateral motion of droplets bouncing on a vibrated liquid bath by superimposing a small periodic rocking motion onto the conventional vertical forcing. We show experimentally that the lateral velocity varies linearly with the rocking amplitude $α$ and sinusoidally with its phase $φ$ relative to the vertical forcing, while the response depends strongly on the droplet's vertical bouncing mode. A time-averaged model of a harmonic bouncer yields a time-averaged lateral force $\overline{F}_x=mgα\sin(Φ_b+φ)$, where $Φ_b$ is the droplet impact phase. Periodic rocking thus provides a simple, programmable means of exerting lateral forces on bouncing droplets, enabling controlled manipulation of passive and active droplets in pilot-wave hydrodynamics.

</details>

### [35] [The Geometry of Lee-Yang Tensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09485)
*Jake Hofgard*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于 Bravyi、Gosset、Liu 与 Wong 的近期结果，给出 Lee-Yang 张量（Lee-Yang tensors）的几何刻画，并借助复 Perron-Frobenius 理论（complex Perron-Frobenius theory）将其中一个关键引理推广至 Lee-Yang 半径（Lee-Yang radius）大于 1 的非正规算子（non-normal operators），进而得到 Suzuki-Fisher 哈密顿量（Suzuki-Fisher Hamiltonians）谱间隙（spectral gap）的局部到全局（local-to-global）下界原理，并用微扰方法界定 k-局部哈密顿量 Gibbs 态（Gibbs states）的 Lee-Yang 半径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画 Lee-Yang 张量的几何结构，并在既有结果基础上拓展谱比率（spectral ratio）与谱间隙的分析工具，以理解局部算子的 Lee-Yang 半径如何控制整体哈密顿量的谱性质。同时，作者希望厘清哪些哈密顿量的 Gibbs 态在所有逆温度（inverse temperature）下均保持 Lee-Yang 性质。

Method: 方法上采用复 Perron-Frobenius 理论，将 Bravyi 等人的关键引理推广到 Lee-Yang 半径大于 1 的非正规算子以界定其谱比率；并发展一种简洁的微扰技术（perturbative technique）分析 k-局部哈密顿量的 Gibbs 态。基于此得到 Suzuki-Fisher 哈密顿量的局部到全局谱间隙下界原理。

Result: 结果包括两部分：其一，仅用每个局部算子的 Lee-Yang 半径即可从下方界定 Suzuki-Fisher 哈密顿量（一类 2-局部哈密顿量）的谱间隙；其二，对 k ≥ 3 的 k-局部哈密顿量，证明其 Gibbs 态不可能在所有逆温度 β ≥ 0 下具有至少为 1 的 Lee-Yang 半径。

Conclusion: 结论指出 Suzuki-Fisher 哈密顿量恰好是那些在所有逆温度 β ≥ 0 下 Gibbs 态均为 Lee-Yang 的哈密顿量，从而给出该族哈密顿量的刻画性特征，并建立了 Lee-Yang 半径与谱间隙之间的定量联系。

Abstract: We provide a geometric description of Lee-Yang tensors, building upon several recent results of Bravyi, Gosset, Liu, and Wong. By applying tools from complex Perron-Frobenius theory, we generalize one of their key lemmas to bound the spectral ratio of non-normal operators with Lee-Yang radius greater than one. As a direct consequence, we provide a local-to-global principle for bounding spectral gaps of Suzuki-Fisher Hamiltonians, a family of $2$-local Hamiltonians, using only the Lee-Yang radius of each local operator to bound the gap from below. On the other hand, we develop a simple perturbative technique to show that the Gibbs states of $k$-local Hamiltonians, $k \geq 3$, cannot have Lee-Yang radius at least one for all inverse temperatures $β\geq 0$. We apply this same method to conclude that Suzuki-Fisher Hamiltonians are precisely those Hamiltonians whose Gibbs states are Lee-Yang at all inverse temperatures $β\geq 0$.

</details>


### [36] [Redundant Records of the Past: Unifying Quantum Darwinism and Decoherent Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09845)
*Philipp Strasberg*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出“过去冗余（past redundancy）”框架，将量子达尔文主义（quantum Darwinism）与退相干历史（decoherent histories）统一起来，指出只有关于过去事件的冗余记录才重要，并借助巴塔查里亚系数（Bhattacharyya coefficient，即保真度）将冗余与历史退相干及概率求和规则相联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孤立量子系统中存在两种关于“记录（records）”的范式：退相干历史刻画哪些过去事件留下形式记录，量子达尔文主义刻画哪些当前事件被冗余记录；二者互不蕴含，其联系鲜有研究。作者旨在弥合这一理论裂隙。

Method: 以“只有关于过去事件的冗余记录才重要”为核心洞见，构建“过去冗余”框架：将量子达尔文主义视为该框架在当前时刻事件上的特例，并通过巴特查里亚系数（Bhattacharyya coefficient，或保真度 fidelity）建立冗余与历史退相干及概率求和规则的联系，辅以多个示例加以说明。

Result: 多个示例显示，过去冗余比量子达尔文主义所要求的精细调谐（fine-tuned）模型更为普遍；冗余可由不同于量子达尔文主义的机制（如宏观事件 macroevents、级联结构 cascade structures）产生。

Conclusion: 由此得到一个更丰富的图景：冗余对客观性（objectivity）是必要但不充分的，该框架使“冗余在自然界中究竟有多普遍”这一问题得以被现实地研究。

Abstract: There exist two different paradigms for records in isolated quantum systems: decoherent histories (which determines which past events leave formal records) and quantum Darwinism (which determines which current events get redundantly recorded). Neither one implies the other, and connections between the two are scarcely investigated. We unify quantum Darwinism and decoherent histories based on the insight that only redundant records about past events matter. The resulting framework of past redundancy encompasses quantum Darwinism when applied to events at the current time, and it connects redundancy to decoherence of histories and the probability sum rule via the Bhattacharyya coefficient (or fidelity). Importantly, various examples show that past redundancy is a more widespread phenomenon than the fine-tuned models necessary for quantum Darwinism suggest. We thus arrive at a richer picture in which redundancy can be caused by mechanisms different from quantum Darwinism (such as macroevents or cascade structures), which emphasizes that redundancy is necessary but not sufficient for objectivity, and which, overall, allows to realistically investigate the question: how generic is redundancy in nature?

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多为改善紫外行为（引入高阶导数项）或追求引力统一描述的量子场论（Quantum Field Theory）模型，不可避免引入负能量的"鬼态"（ghosts），引发理论稳定性的根本性疑问。本文旨在探究这类幽灵态在相互作用下的动力学命运。

Method: 采用简化的量子力学模型，由两个耦合的子系统构成，分别对应正常态与鬼态；虽完整谱下无界、不存在真正基态，但聚焦于解耦系统零能本征态这一"准基态"（would-be ground state），分析其在相互作用下的亚稳性（metastability），并讨论与相应经典及半经典动力学的联系。

Result: 与标准范式相反，在广泛的耦合范围内，该准基态并不衰变，并在长时间尺度上保持动力学稳定。

Conclusion: 结果为解决下无界哈密顿量（Hamiltonian unbounded from below）的稳定性问题提供了证据，并暗示鬼态相关模型的动力学行为可能优于传统预期。

Abstract: Many Quantum Field Theory models, either designed to improve the ultraviolet behavior via higher derivative terms or aimed at providing unified descriptions of Gravity, inevitably introduce negative-energy 'ghosts' states. These states raise fundamental questions regarding the stability of the theory. In this study, we investigate the time evolution of simplified quantum mechanical models consisting of two coupled normal and ghost subsystems. Although the full spectrum of these models is unbounded from below and, therefore, lacks a true ground state, we focus the time evolution of a 'would-be ground state': a zero-energy eigenstate of the decoupled system. By analyzing its metastability under interactions, we gain insights into the dynamics of ghost-like states. Contrary to the standard paradigm, our results demonstrate that, across a broad range of couplings, the would-be ground state does not decay and remains dynamically stable over long times. This provides evidence towards resolving the stability problems of Hamiltonians that are unbounded from below. We also discuss the connections with the corresponding classical and semiclassical dynamics.

</details>


### [38] [An efficient variational polaron master equation for non-Markovian spin-boson dynamics: Transformed initial states and general observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08953)
*Chien-Chang Chen,Hsi-Sheng Goan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用变分极化子主方程（variational polaron master equation）方法，研究初始态对量子自旋-玻色模型（spin-boson model）动力学的影响，并提出在扩展辅助刘维尔空间（auxiliary Liouville space）中高效求解的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因子化系统-环境初始态在变分极化子框架下的变换会引入非均匀项（inhomogeneous terms），这些项对准确描述弱耦合区之外的非马尔可夫动力学（non-Markovian dynamics）至关重要；而此前方法在计算与极化子变换不对易的可观测量时存在局限。

Method: 将动力学表述于扩展辅助刘维尔空间，把积分-微分方程转化为耦合常微分方程；并引入简化的变分极化子技术，用于计算一般（包括与极化子变换不对易的）可观测量的时间演化，从而统一处理相干性与布居动力学。

Result: 通过期望值（expectation values）与迹距离（trace-distance）分析系统刻画了由任意因子化初始态产生的变换后初始态效应；与弱耦合主方程比较发现与多玻色量子子过程相关的延迟现象（delay phenomenon），在中等耦合强度与隧穿幅度下尤为显著；相干叠加初始态比局域态引发更强的非平衡效应，非均匀项在短时动力学中起关键作用。

Abstract: We investigate the effects of initial states on the dynamics of a quantum spin-boson model using an efficient variational polaron master equation approach. The transformation of a factorized system-environment initial state into the variational polaron frame introduces inhomogeneous terms in the master equation, essential for accurately describing non-Markovian dynamics beyond the weak coupling regime. To efficiently account for these effects, we formulate the dynamics in an extended auxiliary Liouville space, converting the resulting integro-differential equations into coupled ordinary differential equations. We further introduce a simplified variational polaron technique for computing the time evolution of expectation values of general observables, including those that do not commute with the polaron transformation, overcoming a major limitation of previous approaches and enabling the unified treatment of coherence and population dynamics. The effects of transformed initial states generated from arbitrary factorized initial states are systematically characterized through expectation values and trace-distance analysis, providing insight into the associated bath-relaxation dynamics over a broad range of tunneling amplitudes, temperatures, and coupling strengths. Comparison with the weak-coupling master equation reveals a delay phenomenon associated with multi-bath-quanta processes, pronounced at intermediate coupling strengths and tunneling amplitudes. We further show that coherent superposition initial states induce stronger nonequilibrium effects than localized states, with inhomogeneous terms playing a crucial role in the short-time dynamics. The proposed formalism applies to both undriven and resonantly driven spin-boson models, providing a computationally efficient framework for open quantum-system dynamics with arbitrary factorized initial states and general physical observables.

</details>


### [39] [Geometric heat pumping on a quantum processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09062)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何热泵浦描述等温两热储之间、其绝热循环热交换仅由参数空间驱动轮廓决定的现象，此前缺乏基于量子处理器的实验实现与定量验证。该研究旨在通过可控量子平台检验该机制及驱动轮廓与周期对热交换的影响。

Method: 采用碰撞模型（collision model）：一个被驱动的量子比特（qubit）反复与两个充当热储的辅助量子比特（ancilla qubit）相互作用，辅助比特制备于热态。通过在每次相互作用前后进行能量测量，获得与各热储热交换的量热记录；并反转驱动轮廓以分离出与泵浦相关的热贡献。

Result: 提取出的泵浦热贡献依赖于驱动周期与驱动轮廓，其行为与碰撞模型的精确模拟（无自由拟合参数）一致，并在慢驱动（slow-driving）区域趋近对应Lindblad动力学（Lindblad dynamics）的几何极限。

Conclusion: 结果表明几何热泵浦可在超导量子处理器上实验实现并定量刻画，验证了碰撞模型与Lindblad描述在慢驱动极限下的一致性，为量子热力学中的几何泵浦研究提供了实验依据。

Abstract: Geometric heat pumping is the transfer of energy between reservoirs at equal temperature where the heat exchanged in an adiabatic cycle depends only on the driving contour in parameter space. We implement this mechanism on a superconducting quantum processor using a collision model, where a driven qubit interacts repeatedly with two ancilla qubits that are prepared in thermal states and emulate the reservoirs. Energy measurements before and after each interaction provide a calorimetric record of the heat exchanged with each reservoir. By reversing the driving contour, we extract a heat contribution associated with pumping. Its dependence on the period and on the contour follows exact simulations of the collision model, without adjustable parameters, and approaches in the slow-driving regime the geometric limit of the corresponding Lindblad dynamics.

</details>


### [40] [Non-Hermitian Cooperation Induced Chern Insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09413)
*Weiwei Zhu,Bingbing Wang,Mengxiang Xie,Hong-yu Zou,Yang Long,Hong-xiang Sun,Haoran Xue,Jie Ren*

TL;DR: 该文提出一种无需外场或规范场的陈绝缘体（Chern insulator）实现范式：静态晶格仅通过格点损耗（on-site loss）与非互易耦合（non-reciprocal coupling）两种非厄米（non-Hermitian）要素的协同作用即可产生拓扑带隙与非零陈数的稳健手性边缘态。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统陈绝缘体（Chern insulator）依赖磁场或合成规范场（synthetic gauge field）破坏时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry），本文旨在探索不借助任何规范场或外场即可诱导陈拓扑的新机制。

Conclusion: 研究确认非厄米协同（non-Hermitian cooperation）可作为诱导陈拓扑的通用资源，无需外部场或时间调制（temporal modulations），为开放经典与量子系统中的拓扑相（topological phases）开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Conventional Chern insulators rely on breaking time-reversal symmetry via magnetic fields or synthetic gauge fields. Neither effect alone suffices; only their synergy opens a topological gap and gives rise to robust chiral edge states with a nonzero Chern number. The underlying mechanism originates from a non-Hermitian chiral molecule formed by the lossy sites, whose dynamically dominant mode carries an orbital angular momentum that effectively generates a synthetic flux after the fast-decaying degrees of freedom are eliminated. In this Letter, we propose using the \textit{replica twist entropy}, defined through the expectation value of a replica rotation operator with a tunable angle $α$, as a natural probe of non-Gaussianity in pure fermionic states. After deriving its behavior for typical many-body states, we establish a general framework for analyzing the replica twist entropy in solvable Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev models in the large-$N$ limit. Applying this framework to the dynamics of the thermofield double state, we uncover a novel dynamical phase diagram in the parameter space of $α$ and evolution time $t$, exhibiting spontaneous $Z_2$ symmetry breaking at $α=π/4$ and universal cusps as a function of $α$ in the long-time regime. Result: 成功探测到该模型的 XY 相与 Ising 相，并在两个相区以及临界点（critical point）处复现了若干单体与两体可观测量（one- and two-body observables）的定性行为。

In prior work, we have developed a method based on domain wall encoding to simulate one-dimensional spin chains using only Ising Hamiltonians, which would otherwise require XX+YY-couplings as well. Here, we implement the method on a commercially accessible Rydberg atom quantum device, and simulate the time evolution of the Heisenberg XXZ model with various anisotropies. We successfully probe the XY and Ising phases of the model and reproduce the qualitative behavior of several one- and two-body observables in both phases, as well as at the critical point. Motivation: 半导体-超导体混合系统（semiconductor-superconductor hybrid systems）是多种量子现象的基础，深埋量子阱异质结构具备优异电子性质，但向其建立高质量超导接触仍具挑战。

Conclusion: 干法与湿法刻蚀的结合构成一种稳健策略，可将深埋量子阱集成到超导混合器件（superconducting hybrid devices）中。

However, establishing high-quality superconducting contacts to such quantum wells remains challenging. Here, we address this problem with an edge-contact approach that combines anisotropic dry etching to access the quantum well followed by wet etching for interface preparation. Josephson junctions fabricated using this approach consistently exhibit near-unity interface transparency, while quantum point contacts show strong Andreev enhancement, with the first conductance plateau approaching $4e^2/h$, the limit of perfect Andreev reflection. In superconducting quantum point contacts, the high contact quality is further manifested by quantized supercurrent. These results establish the combination of dry and wet etching as a robust strategy for integrating deeply buried quantum wells into superconducting hybrid devices.

</details>


### [44] [Valley transitions during spin shuttling in Si/SiGe heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08984)
*Jonas R F Lima,Guido Burkard*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文理论研究了 Si/SiGe 异质结构中自旋穿梭（spin shuttling）过程中的谷动力学（valley dynamics），揭示系统随穿梭速度呈现三种不同区域，并指出在数十 m/s 速度下可实现免受谷激发影响的相干自旋穿梭。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谷激发（valley excitations）被认为是 Si/SiGe 异质结构中实现相干自旋穿梭的主要挑战之一，因此需要厘清穿梭过程中谷态如何演化、以及何种条件下量子信息得以保持。

Method: 将移动量子点（mobile quantum dot）的两个低能谷态视为一个二能级系统（two-level system），设电子初始处于谷基态，分析其在穿梭过程中的演化，并提出基于部分 Landau-Zener 模型（Partial Landau-Zener model）的谷穿梭保真度提升策略。

Result: 依据穿梭速度的不同，系统存在三个明显区域；其中某一区域内量子信息即使在谷激发存在时仍被保留，可实现与穿梭距离无关的相干自旋穿梭。该区域对应数十 m/s 的穿梭速度，且被认为可抑制自旋-谷热点（spin-valley hotspots）与空间变化的自旋劈裂等退相干机制。基于部分 Landau-Zener 模型的策略在提升谷穿梭保真度上比基于标准 Landau-Zener 模型的既往策略更为高效。

Conclusion: 理论结果与近期在快速、长距离穿梭后获得高自旋穿梭保真度的实验工作相一致，表明数十 m/s 的穿梭速度是有利的操作区域，并提示部分 Landau-Zener 模型策略可用于改善谷穿梭保真度。

Abstract: Valley excitations are considered one of the biggest challenges for a coherent spin shuttling in Si/SiGe heterostructures. In this work we theoretically investigate the valley dynamics during spin shuttling in a Si/SiGe heterostructure by considering a two-level system given by the two low-lying valley states of the mobile quantum dot. We assume that the electron is initially in the valley ground state and analyze the evolution of this state during shuttling. We find that, depending on the shuttling velocity, the system can be in three distinct regimes. In one of these regimes, the quantum information is preserved even in the presence of the valley excitations, allowing for coherent spin shuttling independently of the shuttling distance. This regime is obtained for a shuttling velocity of tens of m/s, a desired regime where dephasing mechanisms, such as spin-valley hotspots and spatially varying spin splitting, can be suppressed. This result is in accordance with recent experimental work that obtained a high spin shuttling fidelity after a fast and long shuttling. We also propose strategies based on the Partial Landau-Zener model to enhance the valley shuttling fidelity, which was found to be much more efficient than previously proposed strategies based on the standard Landau-Zener model.

</details>

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: In molecular crystals, molecular excitations can hybridize into collective Frenkel excitons. Using a model inspired by aggregates of zinc phthalocyanine molecules deposited on insulating surfaces, we demonstrate that the existence of edge excitons depends sensitively on the crystallographic orientation of the boundary and is governed by the Zak phase of the underlying bulk bands. The resulting edge states exhibit momentum-locked elliptical polarization textures originating from the internal structure of the molecular excitations, potentially enabling directional optical coupling and excitation transport. Our results establish a route toward topological and polarization-textured excitonic states in two-dimensional molecular materials.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用扫描隧道显微镜诱导发光（scanning tunnelling microscopy-induced luminescence）对二维锌酞菁分子晶体（zinc phthalocyanine molecular crystals）中的Frenkel激子进行纳米分辨率成像；通过改变团簇尺寸（cluster size）观察从离散量子受限激发到集体激子能带的转变，并结合微观理论（microscopic theory）进行对比分析。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了分子晶体作为工程化拓扑激子态（topological excitonic states）的平台地位，并提供了通过激子能带工程（excitonic band engineering）调控能量传输的策略。

Abstract: Collective molecular excitations govern energy transport in light-harvesting and optoelectronic materials. Theory predicts that excitonic band topology can generate edge-confined states, offering a route to direct energy flow in molecular systems, yet experimental evidence is lacking. Here we use scanning tunnelling microscopy-induced luminescence to image Frenkel excitons in two-dimensional zinc phthalocyanine molecular crystals with nanometer resolution. By varying cluster size, we observe the transition from discrete quantum-confined excitations to collective excitonic bands and directly resolve their real-space wavefunctions. We identify an excitonic state localized at the crystal perimeter and show, through comparison with microscopic theory, that it originates from the momentum-resolved non-trivial topology of the bulk excitonic bands. Our results establish molecular crystals as a platform for engineering topological excitonic states and provide a strategy for controlling energy transport through excitonic band engineering.

</details>


### [47] [Influence of nonlocal excitonic optical response on in-plane exciton polaritons supported by monolayer transition-metal dichalcogenides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09232)
*Kuniyuki Miwa*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文建立非局域激子光学响应的微观理论，研究六方氮化硼（hBN）封装单层WS$_2$中面内激子极化激元（IPEPs）的近场特性，揭示空间非局域性使极化激元色散向低面内波矢移动并产生更平滑、扩展更远的近场分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现纳米尺度光局域与长程传播是纳米光子学的核心挑战，对分子光物理过程与能量转移调控具有重要意义。单层过渡金属二硫族化合物（TMDs）支持的IPEPs处于深亚波长可见光波段，其电磁场变化尺度与激子尺寸相当，因此激子光学响应中的空间非局域性（spatial nonlocality）不可忽略。

Method: 发展非局域激子光学响应的微观理论，针对hBN封装的单层WS$_2$研究IPEPs近场特性。将两带大质量狄拉克费米子（massive Dirac fermion）描述与广义Mott-Wannier处理相结合，推导出同时考虑激子质心运动与内部相对运动的非局域电极化率（nonlocal electric susceptibility）。

Result: 与局域响应近似相比，激子光学响应的空间非局域性使极化激元色散向更低的面内波矢移动，抑制高波矢分量，产生横向延伸更大、更平滑的近场分布，同时保持纳米尺度局域。在更高激发能量下，多个激子极化激元模式在不同波矢处贡献，其相对贡献可通过改变封顶hBN层厚度调控。

Conclusion: 研究阐明了空间非局域性在范德华异质结构（van der Waals heterostructures）极化激元性质中的基础性作用。

Abstract: Achieving nanoscale light confinement and long-range propagation is a central challenge in nanophotonics, with important implications for controlling molecular photophysical processes and energy transfer. Monolayer transition-metal dichalcogenides (TMDs) support in-plane exciton-polaritons (IPEPs), deeply subwavelength optical modes in the visible range. In this regime, electromagnetic fields vary on length scales comparable to the exciton size, making spatial nonlocality in the excitonic optical response essential. Here, we develop a microscopic theory of the nonlocal excitonic optical response and investigate the near-field properties of IPEPs in hexagonal boron nitride (hBN)-encapsulated monolayer WS$_2$. Combining a two-band massive Dirac fermion description with a generalized Mott-Wannier treatment, we derive a nonlocal electric susceptibility that accounts for both the center-of-mass and internal relative motion of excitons. Spatial nonlocality in the excitonic optical response shifts the polariton dispersion toward lower in-plane wavevectors compared with the results based on the local-response approximation. This reduces high-wavevector contributions and produces smoother near-field distributions that extend over larger lateral distances while retaining nanoscale confinement. Furthermore, at higher excitation energies, multiple exciton-polariton modes contribute at distinct wavevectors, and their relative contributions can be tuned by varying the thickness of the capping hBN layer. These insights elucidate the fundamental role of spatial nonlocality in the polaritonic properties of van der Waals heterostructures.

</details>


### [48] [Topological and kinetic origins of fractional thermal conductance at the topological insulator--superconductor interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10011)
*Laurence A. Jacobs,Guillermo R. Zemba*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文证明了一条定理：在完全热平衡（full thermal equilibration）下，理想浮置接触（ideal floating contacts）连接的手性边缘段网络，其两端热导仅由各段净手性中心荷（net chiral central charges）决定，因而当两种实现给出相同反常数据（anomaly data）时，热导测量无法区分它们。据此，半整数量子化热导平台并不足以判定非阿贝尔拓扑（non-Abelian topology），需借助电荷扇区（charge sector）的可观测量加以分辨。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Roy 等人在双层石墨烯（bilayer graphene）双极性 (2,-1) 结观测到的 κ_0 T/2 平台，其来源为共传播电子与空穴边缘模的完全平衡，而非非阿贝尔拓扑，这使热导平台作为拓扑判据的可靠性受到质疑。论文旨在给出判别这两类机制何时可区分、何时不可区分的严格判据。

Method: 以手性边缘段网络为对象，假设各段在理想浮置接触处完全热平衡，证明两端热导是各段净手性中心荷的有理函数；中心荷由引力反常（gravitational anomaly）锁定，且对任意局域边界动力学（local boundary kinetics）不变。由此推出不可能性推论，并发展拓扑绝缘体-超导体界面（topological insulator-superconductor interface）作为应用，考察涡旋分数电荷、手性马约拉纳模（chiral Majorana mode）与侧向相邻整数量子霍尔（integer quantum Hall, IQH）区域共同构成的复合器件。

Result: 定理表明：只要两种实现向同一网络呈现相同的反常数据，任何热导测量都无法区分二者；平衡后的 (2,-1) 数值等于手性马约拉纳模中心荷只是一个填充组合的算术事实，此类巧合在测温学（thermometry）范围内不可分辨。应用层面，涡旋因 θ=π 磁电耦合（magnetoelectric coupling）携带分数电荷 e/4，边界承载中心荷 1/2 的手性马约拉纳模，侧向 IQH 区域提供动力学实现；复合器件的洛伦兹比（Lorenz ratio）为 L_0/(1+4|ν_{IQH}|)，并伴随过量散粒噪声（excess shot noise），后者随 B 增大（e/4 涡旋隧穿）或锁定于 IQH 平台，可区分机制。

Conclusion: 热导测量无法区分具有相同反常数据的实现，因此非阿贝尔拓扑的判定必须依赖电荷扇区：在超导边界（superconducting boundary）处电荷扇区不受反常锁定。洛伦兹比与过量散粒噪声（需保证量子霍尔边缘不与超导体相接）共同提供了机制分辨手段。

Abstract: A recent experiment by Roy et al. (Nat. Commun. 17, 2853 (2026)) demonstrated a robust half-integer thermal conductance plateau, κ_0 T/2, at a bipolar (ν,ν')=(2,-1) junction in bilayer graphene, produced not by non-Abelian topology but by full equilibration of co-propagating electron and hole edge modes. We prove a theorem that fixes when the two origins can be told apart: under full thermal equilibration, the two-terminal thermal conductance of a network of chiral edge segments joined at ideal floating contacts is a rational function of the net chiral central charges of the segments alone, quantities pinned by the gravitational anomaly and invariant under arbitrary local boundary kinetics. The corollary is an impossibility statement: whenever two realizations present the same anomaly data to the same network, no thermal-conductance measurement can distinguish them. That the equilibrated (2,-1) value equals the central charge of a chiral Majorana mode is an arithmetic fact about one filling combination, but wherever such a coincidence occurs it is beyond the reach of thermometry, and the separation must come from the charge sector, which is not anomaly-pinned at a superconducting boundary. We develop the topological insulator-superconductor interface as the application: a vortex carries fractional charge e/4 from the θ= πmagnetoelectric coupling, the boundary hosts a chiral Majorana mode of central charge 1/2, and a laterally adjacent integer quantum Hall (IQH) region supplies the kinetic realization. The Lorenz ratio (anomalous for the isolated Majorana boundary, L_0 / (1 + 4|ν_{IQH}|) in the composite device) and the excess shot noise (growing with B from e/4 vortex tunneling versus locked to IQH plateaus) together resolve the mechanism, provided the quantum Hall edge does not abut the superconductor.

</details>

### [51] [Construction-Dependent Floquet Topology in Circularly Driven $N$-Stacked Su-Schrieffer-Heeger Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09925)
*Aayushi Agrawal,Jayendra N. Bandyopadhyay*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作研究由堆叠 SSH（Su-Schrieffer-Heeger）链构成的二维晶格中依赖构造方式（construction-dependent）的 Floquet 拓扑，发现静态下幺正等价（unitarily equivalent）的两种构造在圆偏振驱动下因 Peierls 相位（Peierls phase）差异而产生截然不同的 Floquet 拓扑相图与边缘态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探究：构成链的物理构造方式是否会影响驱动系统的 Floquet 拓扑结构，即便这些构造在静态极限下幺正等价、且与静态体谱无关。

Method: 在解耦链极限下提出两种构造（Construction-I 的构成 SSH 链拓扑平庸，绕数 w=0；Construction-II 的构成 SSH 链拓扑非平庸，w=1），采用 Floquet-Sambe 形式（Floquet-Sambe formalism）求解准能谱与相图，并以低能带陈数（lower-band Chern number）C_L 与方向性 Zak 相位（Z_x, Z_y）刻画，继而计算动力学绕数（dynamical winding numbers）W_0、W_π 及时间平均光学霍尔电导率（time-averaged optical Hall conductivity）。

Result: 两种构造呈现不同的 Floquet 相结构，包括低频相 C_L=-3 与 C_L=-4；陈数无法区分两个准能隙各自的拓扑，而动力学绕数可分辨。在相同驱动参数下，两种构造可具有相同的 C_L=-2，但其隙分辨绕数分别为 (W_0, W_π)=(0,+2) 与 (-2,0)，并伴有相应的手征边缘态（chiral edge states）。时间平均光学霍尔电导的直流值在理想全带占据下由 C_L e²/h 决定，有限频率响应则包含光子辅助（photon-assisted）贡献。

Conclusion: 构成链的物理构造方式虽与静态体谱无关，却可在 Floquet 拓扑结构中起决定性作用；文中还讨论了该驱动模型可能的实验实现途径。

Abstract: We investigate construction-dependent Floquet topology in a two-dimensional lattice formed by stacking Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) chains with co-located sublattice orbitals. We present two constructions in the decoupled-chain limit. Construction-I: the constituent SSH chains are topologically trivial with winding number $w=0$. Construction-II: the constituent SSH chains are topologically nontrivial with $w=1$. Although these constructions are unitarily equivalent in the static limit, circular driving distinguishes them because the hopping processes related by the transformation correspond to different physical bond vectors and therefore acquire different Peierls phases. Using the Floquet-Sambe formalism, we determine the quasienergy spectra and phase diagrams in terms of the lower-band Chern number $C_L$ and directional Zak phases $(Z_x,\, Z_y)$. We find distinct Floquet phase structures, including low-frequency phases with $C_L = -3$ and $C_L = -4$ in the two constructions. Since the Chern number does not resolve the topology of the two quasienergy gaps separately, we further calculate the dynamical winding numbers $W_0$ and $W_π$, and verify the corresponding chiral edge states. In particular, at identical driving parameters, both constructions can have the same Chern numbers $C_L=-2$, while their gap-resolved winding numbers are $(W_0,\, W_π)=(0,\,+2)$ and $(-2,\,0)$, respectively. We also calculate the time-averaged optical Hall conductivity and show that $ C_Le^2/h$ determines its dc value for ideal full-band occupation. At the same time, its finite-frequency response contains photon-assisted contributions. Our results demonstrate that the physical construction of the constituent chains, although irrelevant to the static bulk spectrum, can play a decisive role in the Floquet topological structure. We discuss possible experimental realizations of the driven model.

</details>

Abstract: Spin logic devices provide a promising route toward ultralow-power and nonvolatile information processing. Combined experiments and macrospin simulations reveal that the asymmetric switching originates from an in-plane effective field Hy , which breaks the Myz mirror symmetry of the system. By controlling the magnetization direction of the IMA layer, both the switching polarity and bias direction can be tuned. Building on four distinct types of asymmetric switching behaviors, we realize a reconfigurable spin logic operation within a single Hall-bar device. These results clarify the physical origin of asymmetric magnetization switching and demonstrate an approach for realizing reconfigurable spin logic devices.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合金属-半导体量子电路（hybrid metal-semiconductor quantum circuits）为研究强关联量子物质提供了可控平台。一个尚未解决的关键问题是：耦合的临界态能否组合成新的非费米液体（non-Fermi liquid, NFL）相。受近期双格点电荷近藤电路（two-site charge-Kondo circuit）实验实现的启发，本文探究每个格点上双通道近藤临界点（two-channel-Kondo critical point）所涌现的耦合近藤任意子（Kondo anyons）的归宿。

Abstract: Hybrid metal-semiconductor quantum circuits offer a controlled setting for realizing and probing strongly correlated quantum matter. Inspired by the recent realization of a two-site charge-Kondo circuit [W. Pouse \textit{et al.}, \href{https://www.nature.com/articles/s41567-022-01905-4}{Nat. Phys. \textbf{19}, 492 (2023)}], we uncover the fate of coupled Kondo anyons emerging from the two-channel-Kondo critical point at each site. Bosonization and quantum Brownian motion methods yield the full quasiballistic phase diagram and low-temperature transport properties, while numerical renormalization group calculations resolve the complementary weak-coupling limit. Together, these provide a unified description, and reveal robust NFL phases with continuously tunable transport and susceptibility exponents. Stronger inter-site coupling ultimately produces a screened Fermi liquid.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 近电中性处电阻近似按 T² 增长，与对称狄拉克等离子体预期的近温度无关电阻率形成鲜明对比。在弱无序极限下采用短程电子-空穴相互作用可复现数据，此时电导率按 T^{-2} 标度，过剩电阻率（excess resistivity）与重空穴密度成正比；相反，未屏蔽库仑相互作用（unscreened Coulomb interaction）会给出与温度无关的电导率。提取的相互作用幅度符合预期的库仑尺度。

Conclusion: 上述结果确立了近临界 HgTe 量子阱（near-critical HgTe quantum wells）作为多组分体系中相互作用驱动输运（interaction-driven transport）的平台，该体系结合了简并无质量载流子与非简并有质量载流子。

Abstract: Electron--hole plasma in two-dimensional systems has mainly been studied in two limiting cases: degenerate semimetals with parabolic bands and nondegenerate symmetric Dirac systems such as graphene. Here we investigate a different regime realized in a gapless HgTe quantum well: a multicomponent plasma where massless Dirac carriers coexist with thermally activated heavy holes from lateral valence-band valleys. Near charge neutrality, the resistance increases approximately as $T^2$, in sharp contrast to the nearly temperature-independent resistivity expected for a symmetric Dirac plasma. We show that once heavy holes are populated, charge neutrality pins the chemical potential above the Dirac point, leaving the Dirac electrons moderately degenerate while the heavy holes remain nondegenerate and obey Boltzmann statistics. The light electrons therefore scatter almost elastically from the heavy holes, allowing a relaxation-time treatment of interparticle transport. Using carrier densities from self-consistent band-structure calculations, we reproduce the data with a short-range electron--hole interaction in the weakly disordered limit, for which the conductivity scales as $T^{-2}$ and the excess resistivity is proportional to the heavy-hole density. In contrast, an unscreened Coulomb interaction would give a temperature-independent conductivity. The extracted interaction amplitude is of the expected Coulomb scale. These results establish near-critical HgTe quantum wells as a platform for interaction-driven transport in multicomponent systems combining degenerate massless and nondegenerate massive carriers.

</details>


### [55] [Gentle Floquet control of orbital Hall effect and orbital inverse Faraday effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10109)
*Pei-Han Lin,Roderich Moessner,Egor I. Kiselev*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Floquet 工程（Floquet engineering）通过时间周期电磁驱动调控材料响应，但实际应用中降低所需驱动强度是一项重要任务。该研究旨在利用系统自身的相空间约束来放大共振驱动的相对影响。

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Pseudospin-1 Dirac systems exhibit unique physics distinct from conventional Dirac cones, such as flat-band crossings and non-Abelian characteristics, yet their topological transport properties have remained largely untapped in passive, time-reversal-invariant settings. Here we uncover an in-plane polarity of the Berry-curvature texture in a pseudospin-1 Dirac Hamiltonian, a previously unexplored geometric degree of freedom encoded in the sign-resolved distribution of Berry curvature despite zero net Berry flux, and reveal a new mechanics where Berry curvature induce pseudospin Hall behaviors in a system. We show that the oriented coupling between this momentum-space polarity and a real-space mass gradient governs a geometric selection rule that dictates the emergence of gapless pseudospin Hall modes. By engineering the intracell couplings of a four-site planar lattice, we independently program the Berry-curvature polarity and the spatial mass gradient without altering the host lattice symmetry. Acoustic experiments directly confirm this directional selection rule: reversing the mass gradient closes or reopens the dispersive gap, while pseudospin-selective source excitation launches counterpropagating pseudospin branches along arbitrary prescribed axes. </details>

### [61] [Magnon Polarization Textures and Transport in Antiferromagnets and Altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09035)
*Konstantin S. Denisov,Igor Žutić*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该摘要从理论上分析共线反铁磁体（antiferromagnet, AFM）与交变磁体（altermagnet, AM）中动量空间的磁振子（magnon）极化纹理及输运，指出极化纹理对磁振子动量方向的敏感性决定两种极化动力学机制，并预测磁场方向、温度及各向异性相互作用对弛豫与自旋扩散长度的调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁体中的磁振子可呈现完全圆、椭圆与线性的亚晶格磁化动力学极化，但不同动量空间极化纹理及其对输运的影响尚待系统刻画。研究动机在于厘清极化纹理如何决定磁振子极化动力学，并为AFM与AM的自旋输运调控提供理论依据。

Method: 采用理论分析手段，考察共线反铁磁体与交变磁体中动量空间的磁振子极化纹理与磁振子输运。以极化纹理对磁振子动量方向的敏感性为依据，划分出Dyakonov-Perel弛豫与非同步拉莫尔进动（Larmor precession）导致的退相位两类极化动力学区间。

Result: 在易轴（easy-axis）反铁磁体中，磁场平行于奈尔矢量（Néel vector）时，磁偶极相互作用诱导的Dyakonov-Perel弛豫被抑制，磁振子自旋扩散长度增加，且随温度升高反常增长；而在垂直取向下，拉莫尔进动使磁振子圆极化的弛豫增强。对于具有Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用的AM或AFM，预测存在极端各向异性的磁振子极化弛豫。

Conclusion: 磁振子极化纹理的动量方向敏感性是区分Dyakonov-Perel弛豫与拉莫尔进动退相位的判据，并可通过磁场取向、温度及Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用实现对AFM与AM中磁振子极化弛豫和自旋扩散的显著调控。

Abstract: Antiferromagnets (AFMs) host magnons that can exhibit fully circular, elliptic and linear polarizations of sublattice magnetization dynamics. We analyze theoretically different momentum-space magnon polarization textures and magnon transport in collinear AFMs and altermagnets (AMs). The sensitivity of the polarization texture to a magnon momentum direction determines two regimes of magnon polarization kinetics: The Dyakonov-Perel relaxation or its dephasing due to asynchronous Larmor precession. For an easy-axis AFM, magnetic field parallel to the N{é}el vector leads to the suppression of the magnetic dipolar interaction-induced Dyakonov-Perel relaxation, accompanied by the increase in the magnon spin-diffusion length and its counter-intuitive growth with temperature. For a perpendicular orientation, instead, relaxation of circular polarization of magnons is enhanced due to Larmor precession. Finally, extremely anisotropic relaxation of magnon polarization is predicted for AMs or AFMs having Dzyaloshinskii-Morya interaction.

</details>


### [62] [Progress and Prospect of AI in ARPES Workflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10140)
*Sandy Adhitia Ekahana,Aalok Tiwari,Pratik Saud,Aaron Bostwick,Chris Jozwiak,Eli Rotenberg,Jyoti Katoch*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文综述了人工智能（artificial intelligence, AI）与机器学习（machine learning, ML）在角分辨光电子能谱（angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, ARPES）全流程中的应用现状、局限与未来方向，并提出以共享预训练模型和标准化开放数据集推动自主实验室发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARPES 常规产出大规模多维电子结构数据集，而 AI 与 ML 的进展为整个 ARPES 流程带来新机遇；然而目前尚缺乏对 ML 应用及其在不同流程阶段能力与可靠性的系统性综述。

Method: 本文为综述性工作：首先介绍与凝聚态物理（condensed matter physics）和材料科学（materials science）实验工作者最相关的 ML 方法，随后沿 ARPES 工作流程逐步梳理各环节的现有 ML 应用，并分析其优势、局限与发展潜力。

Result: 文章指出当前 ARPES 数据生态中虽有若干开放数据库，但相较于 ImageNet 等大型共享数据集仍规模偏小且碎片化；据此建议社区侧重于共享可进一步训练、适配特定任务并再分发的预训练模型（pretrained models），同时推进更大规模、标准化的开放 ARPES 数据集仓库建设。

Conclusion: 作者借助实验室自动化（laboratory automation）的六级框架，展望 AI 在 ARPES 流程中的未来，并强调迈向完全自主、自驱动（self-driving）ARPES 实验室所面临的机遇与挑战。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is becoming an increasingly useful tool across the experimental sciences, including angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES), which routinely produces large, multidimensional datasets of electronic structure. Recent advances in AI and machine learning (ML) have opened new opportunities across the entire ARPES workflow, from automated sample preparation and real-time data acquisition to post-experiment data analysis and comparison with theoretical calculations. Despite this progress, a comprehensive review of ML applications, their capabilities, and reliability across the different stages of ARPES workflow is still lacking. In this review, we first introduce ML methods that are most relevant to experimentalists working in condensed matter physics and materials science. We then follow the ARPES workflow, reviewing existing ML applications at each step and discussing their advantages, limitations and potential for future development. We also examine the current ARPES data landscape, where several open databases are available but remain relatively small and fragmented compared with large, shared datasets such as ImageNet. Given these limitations, we suggest that the community focus on sharing pretrained models that can be further trained, adapted to specific tasks, and redistributed, while working toward a larger and standardized open ARPES dataset repository. Finally, we discuss our perspectives on the future of AI within the ARPES workflow using a six-level framework of laboratory automation, highlighting the opportunities and challenges in moving toward a fully autonomous, self-driving ARPES laboratory.

</details>

### [63] [A repeating sequence of simple rules creates a universal computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09879)
*Riz Fernando Noronha*

Main category: nlin.CG

TL;DR: 该研究表明，基本元胞自动机（elementary cellular automata）中图灵完备（Turing complete）的规则 110 可由三条简单对称规则在空间上交替排列来模拟，说明仅靠规则的多样性即可产生涌现现象（emergent phenomena）。手性（chirality）源于排列方式而非局部规则本身，随机初始条件下超过 90% 会锁定到规则 110。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究的动机在于探讨复杂性（complexity）能否仅由简单、对称且彼此不同的单元组合产生，而非依赖规则本身的复杂结构。这一问题与生物学中重复且受扰动的单元以及量子场论（quantum field theory）元胞自动机模型中对称性破缺（symmetry breaking）的起源相关。

Method: 方法上，将图灵完备的规则 110 用空间中交替排列的三条简单对称规则进行模拟；三条规则自身无法区分左右。随后采用随机初始条件，并引入随机噪声（random noise）以产生错误，通过域壁（domain wall）运动与速率方程（rate equation）进行分析。

Result: 结果显示，随机初始条件有超过 90% 的概率锁定到规则 110，该现象由失同步区域之间域壁的运动解释。加入噪声后错误会播种域壁，其比例随噪声强度的平方根（square root）缩放，并可由简单速率方程解释。

Conclusion: 结论指出，重复且受扰动的单元提供了一条通向复杂性的路径，这在生物学中很常见。同时表明，在量子场论的元胞自动机模型中，诸如宇称（parity）等破缺的对称性（broken symmetries）无需出现在局部规则之中。

Abstract: We show that the Turing complete rule 110 in elementary cellular automata can be emulated by alternating three simple symmetric rules in space. As the constituent rules themselves are trivial, this shows that diversity alone can generate emergent phenomena. The rules cannot distinguish left from right, and the chirality of rule 110 is created by their arrangement. Random initial conditions lock on to rule 110 over 90% of the time, explained through the motion of domain walls between desynchronized regions. Adding random noise creates errors that seed domain walls, whose fraction scales with the square root of noise strength, explained through a simple rate equation. This points to a route to complexity through repeated, perturbed units, common in biology, and shows that in cellular automaton models of quantum field theory, broken symmetries such as parity need not be present in the local rules.

</details>
