### [64] [Zero Mode Operators and Charge-Conjugation Defects in Non-Abelian Fractional Quantum Hall-Superconductor Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08907)
*Junyi Cao,Eduardo Fradkin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文研究了分数量子霍尔-超导体（FQH-SC）异质结中束缚于电荷共轭缺陷的零模，将体-边构造推广至非阿贝尔母拓扑序情形，并给出适用于费米子与玻色子朗道态、广义 Moore-Read 态及 Read-Rezayi 态的零模代数结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此类缺陷虽可由 $G$-crossed braided tensor categories 描述，但其零模的局域算符描述一般尚不清楚，亟需建立体-边对应框架以刻画局域零模算符及其代数。

Method: 将局域零模算符的 bulk-to-boundary construction 推广至具非阿贝尔母拓扑序的 FQH-SC，将折叠理论中的体 Wilson 算符映射为一维 FQH-SC 理论中的局域零模算符，并区分零模任意子与作用在缺陷希尔伯特空间上的局域零模算符。

Result: 该构造给出 generalized zero mode algebra，并确定隧穿的融合道依赖性：零模算符隧穿探测体 monodromy，而直接零模任意子转移探测 $G$-crossed associativity data。应用该框架，复现了费米子 Laughlin FQH-SC 在填充 $ν=1/m$ 时的 $\mathbb{Z}_{2m}$ parafermion 零模结构，得到广义 Moore-Read 态的 $\mathbb{Z}_2^{(n)}\times\mathbb{Z}_N$ 结构（费米子态 $N=2m$，玻色子态 $N=m$），并发现 $k=3$ Read-Rezayi 态的 $\mathrm{Fib}\times\mathbb{Z}_N$ 结构。

Conclusion: 在 Read-Rezayi 情形中，零模算符隧穿与直接零模任意子转移给出中性融合道之间不同的普适振幅比，构成底层 Fibonacci 缺陷结构的特征信号。

Abstract: We study zero modes bound to charge-conjugation defects in fractional quantum Hall-superconductor (FQH-SC) heterostructures built from Abelian and non-Abelian FQH states. Although the associated defects are well described by $G$-crossed braided tensor categories, a local operator description of their zero modes is not generally known. We generalize the bulk-to-boundary construction of localized zero mode operators to FQH-SCs with non-Abelian parent topological orders, mapping bulk Wilson operators in the folded theory to localized zero mode operators in the one-dimensional FQH-SC theory. This construction distinguishes zero mode anyons from the local zero mode operators that act on the defect Hilbert space and yields a generalized zero mode algebra. It also determines the fusion-channel dependence of tunneling: tunneling of zero mode operators probes bulk monodromy, while direct zero mode anyon transfer probes $G$-crossed associativity data. Applying this framework, we recover the $\mathbb{Z}_{2m}$ parafermion zero mode structure of fermionic Laughlin FQH-SCs at filling $ν=1/m$, obtain a $\mathbb{Z}_2^{(n)}\times\mathbb{Z}_N$ structure for generalized Moore-Read states at filling $ν=1/m$, with $N=2m$ for fermionic states and $N=m$ for bosonic states, and find a $\mathrm{Fib}\times\mathbb{Z}_N$ structure for the $k=3$ Read-Rezayi state at filling $ν=3/(3M+2)$, with $N=2(3M+2)$ for fermionic states and $N=(3M+2)/2$ for bosonic states. In the Read-Rezayi case, zero mode operator tunneling and direct zero mode anyon transfer yield distinct universal amplitude ratios between the neutral fusion channels, providing a characteristic signature of the underlying Fibonacci defect structure.

</details>


### [65] [Exact solutions for kinematics across interaction-deformed Fermi surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08921)
*Alexander Kruchkov,F. Duncan M. Haldane*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 论文在 Luttinger 定理框架之外，推导相互作用开启并调参时费米面内外占据数亏损与盈余的精确变化率，并揭示其与相干权重 $Z_{\vec k}$ 及非相干权重流的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Luttinger 定理（Luttinger's theorem）仅固定费米液体（Fermi liquid）费米海（Fermi sea）的体积，却无法说明相互作用如何在费米面（Fermi surface）附近重新分配占据。该工作旨在刻画这一被定理忽略的占据重分布细节。

Method: 采用解析推导：在固定密度（fixed density）下将相互作用开启并调参，分别计算相互作用费米面内部占据亏损（occupation deficit）与外部占据盈余（surplus）的精确变化率。

Result: 每个变化率可分解为两部分：形变费米面扫过的权重，以及固定动量（momentum）下 Luttinger 被积函数（Luttinger's integrand）的变化；两者表面项之差为非相干权重（incoherent weight）$1 - Z_{\vec k}$ 的通量。

Conclusion: 所导出的恒等式在 Luttinger 定理成立或不成立的情形下均适用，说明其刻画的是比该定理更普遍的占据重分布规律。

Abstract: Luttinger's theorem fixes the volume of the Fermi sea of a Fermi liquid, but not how interactions redistribute occupation around its surface. For an interaction switched on and tuned at fixed density, we derive the exact rates of change of the occupation deficit inside the interacting Fermi surface and of the surplus outside it. Each is the weight swept by the deforming surface plus a change of Luttinger's integrand at fixed momentum; the difference of the surface terms is flux of incoherent weight $1 - Z_{\vec k}$. These identities hold with or without the Luttinger theorem.

</details>


### [66] [From continuum neural quantum states to effective lattice models: Fluctuating Wigner molecules in moiré superlattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08955)
*Ahmed Khalifa,Shubhayu Chatterjee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种波函数读出方法，将连续神经量子态（continuum neural quantum states, NQS）映射为格点分辨的电荷与自旋统计，从而使有效自由度从微观解中自然涌现。应用于填充 ν=3 的莫尔过渡金属二硫化物（moiré transition metal dichalcogenides）时，揭示出低自旋基态及其由双占-空穴对（doublon–hole pairs）主导的电荷涨落，并据此导出含自旋双轨道哈伯德模型（spinful two-orbital Hubbard model）的有效描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子材料（quantum materials）的有效描述通常依赖能量尺度分离以识别低能自由度，但当动能、晶格势与相互作用相互竞争时该层级结构失效，微观态及其有效描述均难以确定。连续 NQS 可绕过预设能带、轨道或自旋直接求解基态，但其微观波函数难以用有效变量解读，故需要建立从连续波函数到有效格点模型的桥梁。

Method: 提出一种波函数读出（wavefunction readout）方法，将连续 NQS 映射为格点分辨的电荷与自旋统计，使有效自由度自发地从微观解中浮现。以填充 ν=3 的莫尔过渡金属二硫化物为研究对象，通过该读出手段分析其基态电荷与自旋结构。

Result: 在 ν=3 的莫尔过渡金属二硫化物中，NQS 给出低于自旋极化维格纳分子（Wigner molecules）铁磁晶体的低自旋基态。电荷密度虽保留三角形分子图案，但读出结果显示存在显著的电荷涨落，表现为束缚的最近邻双占-空穴对。每个分子电荷态具有惰性的自旋单态核心（spin-singlet core）与承载自旋的边缘电子；电荷与自旋涨落相互交织，且格点间呈反铁磁自旋关联（antiferromagnetic spin correlations）。

Conclusion: 上述交织的电荷-自旋涨落与反铁磁关联提示可用含自旋双轨道哈伯德模型进行有效描述。更普遍地，当竞争性尺度阻碍受控约化时，该结果为从连续波函数通向有效格点模型提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Effective descriptions of quantum materials rely on a separation of energy scales that identifies their low-energy degrees of freedom. When kinetic energy, lattice potential, and interactions compete, this hierarchy breaks down, and both the microscopic state and its effective description are unknown. Continuum neural quantum states (NQS) can find the ground state directly without projecting onto prescribed bands, orbitals, or spins, but their microscopic wavefunctions are difficult to interpret in terms of effective variables. Here, we introduce a wavefunction readout that maps the continuum NQS onto site-resolved charge and spin statistics, allowing effective degrees of freedom to emerge from the microscopic solution. Focusing on moiré transition metal dichalcogenides at filling $ν= 3$, the NQS reveals a low-spin ground state below the ferromagnetic crystal of spin-polarized Wigner molecules. Although the charge density retains triangular molecular motifs, our readout reveals significant charge fluctuations through bound nearest-neighbor doublon--hole pairs. Each molecular charge state has an inert spin-singlet core and rim electrons that carry the spin. Charge and spin fluctuations are therefore intertwined, with antiferromagnetic spin-correlations between sites, motivating an effective description by a spinful two-orbital Hubbard model. More generally, our results demonstrate a route from continuum wavefunctions to effective lattice models when competing scales preclude a controlled reduction.

</details>


### [67] [Ambipolar metal-insulator transition in bilayer MoSe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09036)
*Ha-Leem Kim,Hyungbin Lim,Terry Wu,Amogh Y. Waghmare,Garima Gupta,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,Archana Raja,Eric Y. Ma,Feng Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文开发了无接触射频反射测量法（contact-free radio-frequency reflectometry），通过电容栅耦合测量薄层电阻，从而在同一双层 MoSe₂ 器件中同时解析电子与空穴的密度驱动金属-绝缘体转变，并论证其由相互作用驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当库仑相互作用（Coulomb interactions）主导动能时，稀薄二维载流子会形成维格纳固体（Wigner solid），其熔化与无序诱导局域化均可驱动密度调控的金属-绝缘体转变，但两者相对作用与相互影响长期存在争议。原子级薄半导体（atomically thin semiconductors）因可对同一材料同时进行输运与扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunneling microscopy）探测而成为新检验平台，然而在关联最强处接触质量差、阻碍输运测量。

Method: 引入无接触射频反射测量法（contact-free radio-frequency reflectometry），通过电容栅耦合（capacitive gate coupling）感知薄层电阻（sheet resistance），规避接触限制。

Result: 在同一双层 MoSe₂ 器件中解析出电子转变及此前难以探测的空穴转变；两类载流子共享无序但质量不同，其临界密度相差四倍，却对应可比的库仑能与动能比值，隧道显微镜在该处观测到固-液共存。磁阻（magnetoresistance）在每个转变处出现尖锐峰值，反映类似波梅兰丘克（Pomeranchuk-like）的、场驱动的维格纳固体相对关联液体的膨胀。

Conclusion: 结果证明高质量二维半导体中存在相互作用驱动的转变，并为受接触限制体系中的定量输运研究开辟途径。

Abstract: When Coulomb interactions dominate kinetic energy, dilute two-dimensional carriers form a Wigner solid. Both its melting and disorder-driven localization can drive density-tuned metal-insulator transitions, but their relative roles and interplay have been debated for decades. Atomically thin semiconductors offer a new test because transport and scanning tunneling microscopy can probe the same material, yet poor contacts impede transport where correlations are strongest. Here we introduce contact-free radio-frequency reflectometry that senses sheet resistance through capacitive gate coupling. It resolves the electron and previously inaccessible hole transitions in the same bilayer MoSe2 device, whose carriers share disorder but not mass. Their critical densities differ fourfold yet correspond to comparable Coulomb-to-kinetic-energy ratios, where tunneling microscopy finds solid-liquid coexistence. Magnetoresistance peaks sharply at each transition, reflecting a Pomeranchuk-like, field-driven expansion of the Wigner solid against the correlated liquid. Our results demonstrate interaction-driven transitions in high-quality two-dimensional semiconductors, opening quantitative transport in contact-limited systems.

</details>


### [68] [Multiorbital Anisotropy and Magnetic Excitations in Trilayer Nickelate Pr$_4$Ni$_3$O$_{10}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09210)
*Ziqiang Guan,Sophia F. R. TenHuisen,Xuefei Guo,Wei He,Aulden K. Jones,Marli dos Reis Cantarino,Xinglong Chen,Hong Zheng,J. F. Mitchell,Nicholas B. Brookes,Mark P. M. Dean,Matteo Mitrano*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 结合 X 射线吸收谱（XAS）与共振非弹性 X 射线散射（RIXS），解析了 Pr$_4$Ni$_3$O$_{10}$ 的多轨道电子结构与磁激发，指出多层结构而非稀土替代是调控磁相互作用的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀土替代（rare-earth substitution）会显著改变 Ruddlesden-Popper（RP）镍酸盐的结构、金属性与超导行为，但其对磁相互作用的影响尚不明确。

Method: 联合使用 X 射线吸收谱（XAS）与共振非弹性 X 射线散射（RIXS）；利用无重叠稀土共振直接测量 Ni $L_3$ 边，并通过 Ni $L$ 边与 O $K$ 边的偏振依赖测量分辨面内与面外的 Ni-O 杂化通道；采用动量分辨 RIXS 与有效自旋波（spin-wave）分析。

Result: 动量分辨 RIXS 显示具有色散特征的磁激发，并在非公度自旋密度波（spin-density-wave）波矢处软化；有效自旋波分析给出可观的三层内（intra-trilayer）交换耦合，其量级与主要面内相互作用相当，且对稀土替代稳健。

Conclusion: 多层架构（multilayer architecture）而非单纯的稀土替代，是此类材料中磁相互作用的关键调控参量。

Abstract: Rare-earth substitution strongly modifies the structure, metallicity, and superconducting behavior of Ruddlesden-Popper (RP) nickelates, but its impact on their magnetic interactions remains unclear. Here, we combine x-ray absorption spectroscopy (XAS) and resonant inelastic x-ray scattering (RIXS) to resolve the multiorbital electronic structure and magnetic excitations of Pr$_4$Ni$_3$O$_{10}$. The absence of an overlapping rare-earth resonance provides direct access to the Ni $L_3$ edge, while polarization-dependent measurements at the Ni $L$- and O $K$-edges resolve distinct planar and out-of-plane Ni-O hybridization channels. Momentum-resolved RIXS reveals dispersive magnetic excitations that soften toward the incommensurate spin-density-wave wave vector. An effective spin-wave analysis yields a sizable intra-trilayer exchange coupling, comparable to the leading in-plane interactions and robust against rare-earth substitution. Our results identify the multilayer architecture, rather than rare-earth substitution alone, as a key control knob of the magnetic interactions in these materials.

</details>


### [69] [Polarized neutron diffraction with ex-situ $^3$He neutron spin filter and two-dimensional detector for noncollinear incommensurate magnetic order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09345)
*Shingo Takahashi,Yuta Osawa,Ryuju Kobayashi,Takashi Ino,Hiraku Saito,Takuro Kawasaki,Takayuki Oku,Tatsuya Nakamura,Yoichi Ikeda,Masaki Fujita,Daisuke Koto,Yusuke Tokunaga,Taka-hisa Arima,Taro Nakajima*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文开发了将氦-3中子自旋过滤器（$^3$He neutron spin filter, $^3$He-NSF）与二维（2D）探测器结合的实验装置及数据处理流程，实现对偏离水平面的散射中子偏振分析，并在多铁材料 Mn$_3$WO$_6$ 上验证了其对复杂非公度磁序的探测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统极化中子散射（polarized neutron scattering）多采用配备铁磁单晶单色器与分析器的三轴谱仪（triple-axis spectrometer），仅适于水平散射面内的逐点测量。而多铁体（multiferroics）、磁性斯格明子（magnetic skyrmions）等具有交叉关联现象的复杂磁序，其非公度磁调制矢量（incommensurate magnetic modulation vectors）常沿倒空间多个方向分布，受限于水平散射面的测量难以捕获。

Method: 构建了结合 $^3$He-NSF 与二维探测器的实验装置，从而可分析偏离水平面散射中子的偏振。同时建立了数据处理流程，将二维强度图转换为倒空间三维强度分布，并校正氦-3极化不完美所带来的影响。该方法用于多铁材料 Mn$_3$WO$_6$ 单晶衍射测量。

Result: 在 Mn$_3$WO$_6$ 上成功观测到面外非公度磁反射（out-of-plane incommensurate magnetic reflections）的偏振依赖强度，验证了该方法对复杂磁结构分析的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的 $^3$He-NSF 与二维探测器联合方案及配套数据处理流程，可突破水平散射面限制，为研究具有复杂非公度磁序与拓扑非平庸磁结构的材料提供有效手段。

Abstract: Polarized neutron scattering has been widely used for studying magnetic orders in condensed matter. A typical setup for this technique is a triple-axis spectrometer with a ferromagnetic single-crystal monochromator and analyzer, which is suited for point-by-point measurements within the horizontal scattering plane. However, this setup cannot fully meet the demands of recent studies on complex magnetic orders with emergent cross-correlated phenomena. For instance, multiferroics, magnetic skyrmions, and other topologically nontrivial magnetic orders tend to have incommensurate magnetic modulation vectors running along various directions in reciprocal space, which cannot be captured by measurements restricted to the horizontal scattering plane. Abstract: Mott gap closing induced by an external potential gradient is studied by considering the Hubbard model on a square lattice. Our model consists of hole- and electron-doped domains forming the p-n junction where the on-site potential varies smoothly across the interface. The effect of the potential drop direction, either along square edge or diagonal, is studied by describing the correlation effects of inhomogeneous 2x2 plaquette impurity clusters within a dynamical mean-field theory framework. It is found that the metallic phase is favored over the Mott insulators when the potential drop becomes comparable to the energy eigenvalue difference of an open 2x2 plaquette between the ground state and the first excited state. Given the same associated electric-field strength, the excitation energy is almost independent of the drop direction, which amounts ~0.35t at U=5.8t with the hopping parameter t. Upon insulator-to-metal transition, the correlated metal exhibits a coherent peak at zero energy whose intensity is greater for the drop direction along the square diagonal than along the edge. The main origin is attributed to the preserved degeneracy of two molecular orbitals for the former case, while it undergoes splitting in the energy levels for the latter. Our result is discussed regarding the experimentally measured Mott gap closing thresholds.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强电子关联材料的从头算研究本身存在理论复杂性与计算成本问题，若晶格自由度（lattice degrees of freedom）亦参与其中则更为困难；因此需要一种能一致处理电子与振动激发的可扩展框架。

Conclusion: 低估结果表明需要考虑顶点修正（vertex corrections）；该工作显示简洁的动力学表述可为关联材料研究提供可扩展框架，将电子激发与振动激发置于同等地位处理。

Abstract: First-principles studies of materials with strong electronic correlations introduce additional theoretical complexities and computational costs, even more so if lattice degrees of freedom also play a role. To address these challenges, we show how direct and phonon-mediated electron-electron interactions can be treated consistently. The key observation is that the GW and Fan-Migdal self-energies are topologically the same diagram, so that they can be unified using a single effective interaction, which is the sum of the two interactions. The Dyson equation for the electron Green function then yields normal-state Eliashberg equations, which can be viewed as a subset of the Hedin-Baym equations for coupled electrons and phonons [Phys. Rev. X 13, 031026 (2023)]. We solve these dynamical equations in a local approximation to calculate from first principles the temperature-dependent quasiparticle spectrum, with close attention to the low-energy window determining transport. We apply this extended dynamical Hubbard approach to the correlated metal Sr$_2$RuO$_4$, which has long served as a benchmark system for advanced electronic-structure methods. The resulting renormalization of quasiparticle bands is in qualitative agreement with experiments and results from dynamical mean-field theory. However, here the low-energy linewidth and associated resistivity within the Green-Kubo formalism in the bubble approximation are underestimated, suggesting the need for vertex corrections. This work highlights how simple dynamical formulations can provide an extensible framework for studying correlated materials, which treats electronic and vibrational excitations on equal footing.

</details>


### [72] [Efficient Optimization of Tensor Rings with Low-Rank Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10027)
*Matthieu Jeannin,Alessandro Chessari,Jan Von Delft*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文将周期性系统的张量环（tensor-ring, TR）分解与双站点 Ring-DMRG 结合，通过规范变换、广义 Davidson 求解器以及环境感知的截断策略提升数值鲁棒性，并论证在临界系统中所需环境秩 p 不随系统尺寸增长，从而在更小键维度下保持与标准 DMRG 相同的立方标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量环分解天然适配周期系统，但其闭合几何导致缺乏全局规范形式，环境不良条件化且优化代价高昂。现有周期性 DMRG 通过低秩压缩长环境降低局部操作复杂度，本文旨在将该思路扩展至双站点算法并提升其数值稳定性。

Method: 提出高效的双站点 Ring-DMRG 算法，利用适当的规范变换（gauge transformations）与广义 Davidson 求解器改善数值鲁棒性。针对双站点形式中的截断步骤：当环境充分可分离时，通过合适的参考系变换将截断化归为普通 SVD；当环境不可分离时，改用保留完整环境的交替优化（alternating optimization）。

Result: 低秩环境构造将局部操作降至 O(pχ³)（p 为保留的环境秩）。对临界系统，发散关联长度使长程可观测量在热力学极限附近仍对周期几何敏感；此时达到固定观测精度所需的环境秩不随系统尺寸增长，Ring-DMRG 因而保持与标准 DMRG 相同的键维度立方标度，但所需键维度显著更小。

Conclusion: 低秩环境构造为信念传播（belief propagation, BP）提供了系统化推广，在秩一极限下可退化为标准 BP。整体表明 Ring-DMRG 在周期临界系统的高效模拟上兼具标度优势与数值稳健性。

Abstract: Tensor-ring (TR) decompositions provide a natural representation of periodic systems but are difficult to optimize because the closed geometry prevents a global canonical form and leads to costly, ill conditioned environments. Existing periodic DMRG methods alleviate this difficulty by compressing long environments to a low-rank representation, reducing local operations to $\mathcal{O}(pχ^3)$, where $p$ is the retained environment rank. Here, we extend this approach to an efficient two-site Ring-DMRG algorithm and improve its numerical robustness through appropriate gauge transformations and a generalized Davidson solver. A central challenge in the two-site formulation is the truncation step, which must account for the surrounding environment. When this environment is sufficiently separable, a suitable change of frame reduces the truncation to an ordinary SVD. When it is not separable, we instead use an alternating optimization that retains the full environment.
  The computational advantage of tensor rings over tensor trains depends on the scaling of the required environment rank $p$ with bond dimension and system size. For critical systems, the divergent correlation length makes long-range observables remain sensitive to the periodic geometry as the thermodynamic limit is approached. In this regime, the environment rank required to reach a fixed accuracy in the observable does not grow with the system size. Ring-DMRG therefore retains the same cubic scaling with bond dimension as standard DMRG, but at a substantially smaller bond dimension. The low-rank environment construction also provides a systematic generalization of belief propagation (BP), with standard BP recovered in the rank-one limit.

</details>


### [73] [Fourth-order perturbation theory for the Frohlich polaron: Analytic structure of the weak-coupling series and the crossover to strong coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10030)
*Sankar Das Sarma*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文基于三维 Frohlich 极化子（Frohlich polaron）基态能量与有效质量在耦合常数 alpha 上的四阶 Rayleigh-Schrodinger 微扰级数，揭示系数呈现由 alpha≈8.5、指数≈1.4 的幂律奇异性描述的规律，并论证该表观奇点对应向强耦合区渡越（crossover）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fröhlich 极化子的弱耦合级数长期受限于收敛性与"教科书"失效点 alpha=6 的解释，作者希望借助更高阶（三、四阶有效质量）系数与收敛级数分析方法，澄清级数的收敛半径及其物理意义。

Method: 计算基态能量与有效质量在耦合常数 alpha 上直至四阶的 Rayleigh-Schrodinger 微扰级数（其中有效质量三、四阶系数为新结果）；利用 Fröhlich 相互作用作为固定总动量下孤立非简并基态的形状有界微扰（form-bounded perturbation）保证级数具非零收敛半径，并采用适用于收敛级数的比值法（ratio method）与 Pade 逼近（Pade approximants）进行分析。

Result: 系数表现出高度规律性，可由 alpha≈8.5 附近、指数≈1.4 的简单幂律奇异性描述；逆质量逐阶截断的零点（首个即教科书值 alpha=6）是该半径的缓慢收敛估计；仅由弱耦合系数构造的 Pade 逼近在 alpha 直至 5 范围内与图解析蒙特卡洛（diagrammatic Monte Carlo）结果吻合优于 1%。

Conclusion: 关于基态解析性与强耦合渐近行为的精确结果意味着系数不可能保持同号（尽管所有已知系数同号），故表观奇点应为贴近正实轴的复共轭对；该奇点标记向强耦合的渡越，弱耦合级数能准确定位这一渡越但无法穿过它继续延拓。

Abstract: We calculate the Rayleigh-Schrodinger perturbation series for the ground-state energy and the effective mass of the three-dimensional Frohlich polaron through fourth order in the coupling constant alpha. The third- and fourth-order coefficients of the effective mass are new. Because the Frohlich interaction is a form-bounded perturbation of an isolated nondegenerate ground state at fixed total momentum, the series has a nonzero radius of convergence, and we analyze it with the ratio and Pade methods appropriate to convergent series. The coefficients are strikingly regular and are described by a simple power-law singularity at alpha near 8.5 with an exponent near 1.4. The couplings at which successive truncations of the inverse mass vanish, the first of which is the textbook breakdown value alpha = 6, are slowly converging estimates of this radius. Pade approximants built from the weak-coupling coefficients alone agree with diagrammatic Monte Carlo results for the mass to better than one percent for alpha up to 5. Exact results on the analyticity of the ground state and on the strong-coupling asymptotics imply that the coefficients cannot keep a constant sign, although all known coefficients do, so that the apparent singularity must be a complex-conjugate pair close to the positive real axis. It marks the crossover to strong coupling: the weak-coupling series locates this crossover accurately but cannot be continued through it.

</details>


### [74] [Mapping Strain and Spatial-Modulation Phase from Local Wave Vectors in Scanning Tunneling Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10255)
*Xinze Yang,Sandra Sajan,Maria N. Gastiasoro,Miguel M. Ugeda,Eduardo H. da Silva Neto*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出局域波矢映射（local wave vector mapping, LWM）算法，通过直接在实空间测量局域波矢来提取应变，规避了Lawler--Fujita（LF）算法中二维相位解缠（phase unwrapping）在拓扑缺陷附近的失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间变化的晶格畸变会引起局域电子性质改变，但这类变化常被大范围平均测量所掩盖；扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunneling microscopy, STM）具备原子级空间分辨能力，原则上可局域量化此类畸变。然而广泛使用的LF算法依赖二维相位解缠，在相位定义不良区域（尤其刃位错等拓扑缺陷附近）易失效。

Method: 提出LWM算法，直接测量实空间中的局域波矢（local wave vector），推导局域波矢、应变与相位梯度之间的关系，从而在相位本身定义不良时仍保持稳健，适于成像涡旋及其他拓扑缺陷相关的相位梯度纹理。使用含已知晶格畸变的模拟数据和人工生成的涡旋进行基准测试，并应用于实验STM数据。程序包及示例用法已公开于GitHub。

Result: 在模拟数据与人工涡旋基准测试中算法表现稳健；应用于实验STM数据后，在4Ha-NbSe2与2H-NbSe2中解析出电荷密度波（charge density wave, CDW）的非公度性（incommensurabilities）、晶格畸变以及CDW相位涡旋。

Conclusion: LWM通过实空间局域波矢直接测量，克服了LF算法对相位解缠的依赖及其在拓扑缺陷处的局限，特别适用于涡旋等拓扑缺陷相关相位梯度纹理的成像；相关观测结果的物理意义另文报道。

Abstract: Spatially varying lattice distortions can produce local changes in electronic properties that are obscured by measurements averaging over extended regions. With its atomic-scale spatial resolution, scanning tunneling microscopy (STM) provides, in principle, a means of quantifying such distortions locally. A widely used approach for extracting local strain from STM images is the Lawler--Fujita (LF) algorithm [Lawler et al., Nature 466, 347 (2010)], which determines the displacement field from the phase of the lattice modulation and subsequently obtains the strain by differentiation. However, this approach requires two-dimensional phase unwrapping, which can fail in regions where the phase is ill-defined, particularly near topological defects such as edge dislocations. Here, we introduce an alternative approach that circumvents these limitations by directly measuring the local wave vector in real space. We derive the relationship between the local wave vector, strain, and the phase gradient, and show that the local wave vector mapping (LWM) algorithm remains robust even when the phase itself is ill-defined. This makes the method particularly well suited for imaging the phase-gradient textures associated with vortices and other topological defects. We benchmark the algorithm using simulated data containing known lattice distortions and artificially generated vortices. Finally, we apply the method to experimental STM data and resolve the charge density wave (CDW) incommensurabilities, lattice distortions, and CDW phase vortices in 4Ha-NbSe2 and 2H-NbSe2. The physical implications of these observations were recently reported by S. Sajan, X. Yang, et al. [arXiv:2607.20335 (2026)]. The program package and the example usage is publicly available at the GitHub: https://github.com/jsntxyang/LocalWavevectorMeasuremnt.

</details>


### [75] [Exact Excitation Spectra and Spectral Gaps of the Frustrated Spin-1/2 $J_1$-$J_2$ Model via Dual Spin-Sector Variational Quantum Eigensolvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10454)
*Sk Saniur Rahaman*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出对偶自旋扇区变分量子本征求解器（Dual Spin-Sector VQE）框架，用于解决阻挫自旋系统中激发态与激发能隙计算所遭遇的态序反转（state-ordering inversion）问题，实现对激发单重态（Excited Singlet）的无歧义求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在阻挫自旋-1/2的 $J_1$-$J_2$ 海森堡模型（Heisenberg model）中，当 $J_2/J_1 < 0.25$ 时，标准单扇区算法（如 $S_z=0$ 中的变分量子消除法 VQD）会失效：该区域最低激发态为三重态（Triplet, $S=1$），其能量低于激发单重态（$S=0$），标准消除会错误收敛到三重态并误判为激发单重态。

Method: 先在 $S_z=1$ 扇区优化基态三重态（该扇区不存在单重态），再通过总自旋下降算符 $S^- = \sum_i S_i^-$ 将其映射至 $S_z=0$，并结合对偶扇区惩罚代价函数（dual-sector penalty cost function）分离出激发单重态 $E_S$，从而避免变分歧义。该方法适用于一维自旋链与二维矩形晶格。

Result: 数值态矢量模拟（state-vector simulation）确认算法以单位重叠保真度（unit overlap fidelity）收敛至机器精度。方法可捕获关键物理特征：无能隙相（gapless phase）、$J_2/J_1 \approx 0.2411$ 处的 BKT 临界点、Majumdar-Ghosh 二聚化点（$J_2/J_1 = 0.50$），以及二维非磁性阻挫区（$J_2/J_1 \approx 0.40 - 0.60$）。

Conclusion: 有限采样（finite-shot sampling）模拟与至 $N=100$ 量子比特的门规模分析（gate-scaling analytics）表明，该算法具备资源高效性与采样鲁棒性，适合近期量子硬件执行。

Abstract: Finding excited states and excitation gaps in frustrated spin systems using Variational Quantum Eigensolvers (VQE) is complicated by state-ordering inversions. In the frustrated spin-1/2 $J_1$-$J_2$ Heisenberg model, standard single-sector algorithms like Variational Quantum Deflation (VQD) in $S_z=0$ fail when $J_2/J_1 < 0.25$. In this regime, the lowest-lying excitation in $S_z=0$ is a Triplet ($S=1$), which lies below the Excited Singlet ($S=0$). Standard deflation projects out the ground singlet and converges onto the Triplet state, misidentifying it as the Excited Singlet. Here, we present a Dual Spin-Sector VQE framework that resolves these state-ordering inversions across 1D spin chains and 2D rectangular lattices. By first optimizing the ground Triplet state in the $S_z=1$ sector (where singlet states cannot exist), mapping it into $S_z=0$ via the total spin-lowering operator $S^- = \sum_i S_i^-$, and evaluating a dual-sector penalty cost function, we isolate the Excited Singlet ($E_S$) without variational ambiguity. Numerical state-vector simulations confirm exact convergence to machine precision with unit overlap fidelity. The method captures key physical features, including the gapless phase, the BKT critical point at $J_2/J_1 \approx 0.2411$, the Majumdar-Ghosh dimer point ($J_2/J_1 = 0.50$), and the 2D non-magnetic frustrated regime ($J_2/J_1 \approx 0.40 - 0.60$). Finite-shot sampling simulations and gate-scaling analytics up to $N=100$ qubits demonstrate the resource efficiency and shot resilience of the algorithm for near-term quantum execution.

</details>


### [76] [Landau Theory for Non-relativistic Magnetism Beyond Altermagnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10523)
*Hana Schiff,Judit Romhányi,Paul McClarty,Jeffrey G. Rau*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过朗道理论系统构建零波矢磁序的对称允许自由能，揭示了共面/非共面序的两种典型结局（共线坍缩或自旋空间中相互正交的多分量序），并建立了朗道方法与自旋空间群方法之间的直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有朗道理论研究多聚焦于共线（collinear）磁序，而对共面（coplanar）与非共面（non-coplanar）磁序的系统性推广尚不充分，本文旨在填补该空白并将结果与自旋空间群（spin space group）框架衔接。

Method: 聚焦由晶体点群单一不可约表示（irreducible representation）描述的零波矢（k=0）磁序，针对所有可能的多维不可约序参量通道，构造至四次项的对称允许自由能，并据此计算总磁化、自旋电导、多极矩与压磁效应等可观测量。

Result: 发现四方、三方/六方与立方对称性对应不同的朗道理论；得到两种自然结局：坍缩为低对称共线相，或形成自旋空间内序参量互相正交的真正共面/非共面序，类似³He的A相与B相；并证明该对称破缺相实现与序参量空间表示相关联的自旋空间群。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了朗道方法与自旋空间群方法之间的直接联系，讨论了低对称共线相与常规共线自旋群的关系，并关联到原子交错磁性（altermagnetism）、超流³He及电子向列相（electronic nematic phases）。

Abstract: In this work we study phenomenological Landau theories of non-relativistic magnetic systems, generalizing work on the collinear case to coplanar and non-coplanar orders. Focusing on zero-wavevector ($\boldsymbol{k}=0$) magnetic orders described by a single irreducible representation of the crystallographic point group, we construct the symmetry-allowed free energies to quartic order for all possible multi-dimensional irreducible ordering channels. We find distinct Landau theories for tetragonal, trigonal/hexagonal, and cubic symmetry. Similar to the case of spin-nematic fluids, we find two natural outcomes: a collapse to a lower-symmetry collinear phase or a genuinely coplanar or non-coplanar order where the order parameters form a mutually orthogonal set in spin space -- reminiscent of the $A$ and $B$ phases in ${}^3$He. We show in general that this symmetry-broken phase realizes the spin space group associated with the spatial representation of the order parameter sharing the same parent space group as the paramagnetic phase. This provides a direct link between the Landau and spin space group approaches. We calculate key observables using this Landau approach, including net magnetization, spin conductivity, multipolar moments, and piezomagnetism, connecting our results to constraints from the underlying spin space group. Examples of coplanar and non-coplanar magnetic orders for each case are worked out in detail. Finally, we discuss the relationship of the lower-symmetry collinear phases to the usual collinear spin groups, as well as connections to atomic altermagnetism, superfluid ${}^3$He and electronic nematic phases.

</details>

### [77] [From a Hierarchy of Stochastic Differential Equations to a Hierarchy of Generalized Beta Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10476)
*Siqi Shao,R. A. Serota*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文提出一类含三分量随机项的均值回归随机微分方程（mean-reverting stochastic differential equation），其稳态分布构成层级结构，顶层为修正Beta分布（modified-Beta distribution），并可退化为紧支撑、幂律尾（power-law-tailed）与指数型极限族。研究进一步构造两种广义扩展，发现非线性变换与稳态约化（stationary-state reduction）不可交换，故广义层级并不唯一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机在于以单一随机动力学框架统一描述多种稳态分布形态（紧支撑、幂律尾、指数型），并厘清随机微分方程层面的变换与稳态分布层面变换之间的关系，从而检验广义Beta框架（Generalized Beta framework）的构造是否唯一。

Method: 方法上引入带三分量随机项的均值回归随机微分方程，由其导出稳态分布层级，顶层为修正Beta分布，并通过单参数与双参数约化得到各类极限族。随后构造两条广义扩展路径：其一在随机微分方程层面直接施加幂变换（power transformation），其二在获得修正Beta稳态层级后再施加同一变换。

Result: 结果为：两条扩展路径在重要的低阶分支与极限情形上一致，但在顶层一般不同，表明非线性变换与稳态约化不可交换（do not commute），广义层级因此不唯一。对两条路径均给出概率密度函数（probability density function）与累积分布函数（cumulative distribution function），并将参数表示为底层随机动力学量的函数。

Conclusion: 结论是：所提随机框架可在单一家族内涵盖多种稳态分布及其极限情形，且其广义扩展存在路径依赖性；研究进一步阐明了各极限情形之间的关系，并与传统广义Beta框架进行了比较。

Abstract: We introduce a mean-reverting stochastic differential equation with a three-component stochastic term and show that it generates a hierarchy of steady-state (stationary) distributions. At the top level, the hierarchy is described by a modified-Beta distribution, while one- and two-parameter reductions produce compact-support, power-law-tailed, and exponential-type limiting families within a single stochastic framework.
  We then construct two generalized extensions of this hierarchy. In the first, the power transformation is applied directly at the level of the stochastic differential equation; in the second, the same transformation is applied only after the stationary modified-Beta hierarchy has been obtained. While these two procedures agree on important lower branches and limiting cases they generally differ at the top level. The generalized hierarchy is therefore not unique: nonlinear transformation and stationary-state reduction do not commute.
  For both routes, we derive the probability density and cumulative distribution functions, express their parameters in terms of the underlying stochastic dynamics, clarify the relations among their limiting cases, and compare the resulting families with the traditional Generalized Beta framework.

</details>

TL;DR: 该研究构建了一个半柔性磁性聚合物（semiflexible magnetic polymer）模型，每个单体带有二态自旋，耦合构象、刚性与磁性自由度，通过蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）模拟与变分自由能上界方法刻画零场相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该模型旨在为体内核蛋白复合物（nucleoprotein complexes）提供最小化表示，即需同时容纳DNA弯曲刚性（bending rigidity）与桥连蛋白（bridging proteins）两种要素，从而连接柔性磁性聚合物与半柔性均聚物两个极限情形。

Result: 低刚度下发现一级相变（first-order transition），同时包含线团-球状坍缩（coil-to-globule collapse）与铁磁-顺磁转变（ferromagnetic-to-paramagnetic transition）；更低温度下，另一一级相变将无序球状相与具有类向列取向序（nematic-like orientational order）的键切线有序晶体分开。高刚度下球状相消失，出现直接的一级线团-晶体转变。

Conclusion: 链刚度与磁性自由度的耦合会重构聚合物的零场相图，该模型统一了柔性磁性聚合物与半柔性均聚物两个极限情形，后者对应所有自旋取向一致（aligned）的强场极限。

Abstract: We study a semiflexible magnetic polymer in which each monomer carries a two-state spin, giving rise to coupled conformational, stiffness, and magnetic degrees of freedom. The model provides a minimal representation of nucleoprotein complexes in vivo, in which DNA bending rigidity and bridging proteins must be simultaneously accommodated. We employ Monte Carlo simulations on a face-centered-cubic (FCC) lattice, complemented by a variational free-energy upper-bound approach, to characterize the zero-field phase diagram as a function of bending stiffness and temperature. At low stiffness, we identify a first-order transition combining coil-to-globule collapse with a ferromagnetic-to-paramagnetic transition. At lower temperatures, an additional first-order transition separates the disordered globule from an ordered crystal characterized by nematic-like orientational order of the bond tangents. At high stiffness, the globule disappears, resulting in a direct first-order coil-to-crystal transition. These results demonstrate how chain stiffness and magnetic degrees of freedom couple to polymer conformation and reorganize the zero-field phase diagram. Our model connects the two limiting cases of flexible magnetic polymers and semiflexible homopolymers, the latter corresponding to the strong-field limit in which all spins are aligned.

</details>


### [79] [Heat Transport of the $β$-Fermi--Pasta--Ulam--Tsingou chain in the long-wave limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09405)
*Henrique Santos Lima,Matheus M. R. Poltronieri Martins,Lucas Reis e Silva*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 通过长波长连续介质描述与通量守恒离散化，在约10³网格节点规模下即可获得接近2/5的异常输运指数，为研究非线性弹性介质中的反常输运提供实用途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接用分子动力学（molecular dynamics）求解对称β-Fermi–Pasta–Ulam–Tsingou链的异常电导指数，需极大规模体系，原因在于长程有限尺寸跨越（finite-size crossover）与热接触电阻（thermal-contact resistance）。作者因此转向连续介质建模，考察动力学理论预测的κ∝L^{2/5}标度能否以中等数值自由度实现。

Method: 推导长波长连续介质描述，非线性弹性场保留微观相互作用的立方应力项，并与Langevin热库（Langevin reservoirs）交换热量；采用通量守恒（flux-conservative）的空间离散格式，由同一应力构造力与能量流，从而给出一致的内部输运估计量。

Result: 当L>8（对应参考分辨率下约10³网格节点及以上）时，拟合指数为0.399±0.004；网格加密表明该指数在两种更细分辨率间保持稳定，但电导幅值仍依赖分辨率。

Conclusion: 主要结论是存在一个接近2/5的可及有限尺寸区间，而非网格间距趋于零时绝对电导的收敛。该框架为研究和声系统中的反常输运及非线性弹性介质中的能量流提供了保守连续介质方案。

Abstract: Resolving the anomalous conductivity exponent of the symmetric $β$-Fermi--Pasta--Ulam--Tsingou chain by molecular dynamics can require very large systems because of long finite-size crossovers and thermal-contact resistance. Motivated by this computational challenge, we derive a long-wavelength continuum description and investigate whether the kinetic-theory scaling $κ\propto L^{2/5}$ becomes accessible with a moderate number of numerical degrees of freedom. The nonlinear elastic field retains the cubic stress of the microscopic interaction and exchanges heat with Langevin reservoirs. A flux-conservative spatial discretization constructs the force and energy current from the same stress, providing a consistent interior transport estimator. For $L>8$, corresponding to approximately $10^3$ mesh nodes and above at the reference resolution, the fitted exponent is $0.399\pm0.004$. Mesh refinement supports the stability of this exponent between the two finer resolutions, although the conductivity amplitude remains resolution-dependent. The main result is thus an accessible finite-size regime near $2/5$, rather than convergence of the absolute conductivity as the mesh spacing vanishes. This formulation provides a practical route to studying anomalous transport in anharmonic systems and a conservative continuum framework for investigating energy flow in nonlinear elastic media.

</details>


### [80] [Vortex screening and the fate of the BKT transition with long-range couplings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10171)
*Guido Giachetti*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究长程相互作用下二维 XY 模型中 BKT 相（Berezinskii–Kosterlitz–Thouless phase）的稳定性。通过发展实空间重整化群（real-space renormalization-group）方法并保留完整多体涡旋相互作用，作者导出局域稳定性判据：稳定的准长程序（QLRO）区间仅在 σ>7/4 时存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BKT 相在长程相互作用下的稳定性仍属未决问题（open problem）。本文针对耦合强度按 r^{-2-σ} 衰减的二维 XY 模型，试图厘清长程耦合是否破坏准长程序（quasi-long-range order）以及其临界条件。

Method: 在准长程序（QLRO）态的高斯流形（Gaussian manifold）附近构造实空间重整化群（real-space renormalization-group）处理。保留完整的多体涡旋相互作用（many-body vortex interaction），在逸度（fugacity）领头阶证明小涡旋–反涡旋对（vortex–antivortex pairs）的屏蔽效应保持其高斯平均表示，且无需对长程耦合作展开；随后微扰地引入自旋波（spin waves）。

Result: 结合涡旋屏蔽与自旋波微扰后得到局域稳定性判据：稳定的准长程序区间仅当 σ>7/4 时存在。对 σ<7/4 的情形，识别出一个有限耦合下的 Wilson–Fisher 型不动点（Wilson–Fisher-like fixed point），所保留的流方程预测其与高斯线合并时 ν^{-1}∼√(7/4−σ)。

Conclusion: 长程相互作用会限制 BKT/准长程序的稳定范围，其稳定性阈值由 σ=7/4 界定；σ<7/4 时体系流向 Wilson–Fisher 型不动点并伴随 ν^{-1} 的平方根标度行为。

Abstract: The stability of the Berezinskii--Kosterlitz--Thouless phase against long-range interactions remains an open problem. We address this question in the two-dimensional XY model with couplings decaying as $r^{-2-σ}$, by developing a real-space renormalization-group treatment near the Gaussian manifold of quasi-long-range ordered (QLRO) states. We retain the full many-body vortex interaction and show that, at leading order in the fugacity, screening by small vortex--antivortex pairs preserves its Gaussian-average representation without an expansion in the long-range coupling. Including spin waves perturbatively then yields a local stability criterion: a stable QLRO interval exists only for $σ>7/4$. We also investigate a Wilson--Fisher-like fixed point at finite coupling for $σ<7/4$, for which the retained flow predicts $ν^{-1}\sim\sqrt{7/4-σ}$ as it merges with the Gaussian line.

</details>


### [81] [Correspondence between Asymptotic Quantum Many-Body Scars in Closed Systems and Diffusive Nambu-Goldstone Modes in Open Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09662)
*Masaya Kunimi,Taiki Haga,Masaya Nakagawa*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作揭示了封闭量子系统中弱遍历破坏与开放量子系统中扩散流体力学之间的共同结构：封闭系统中彩虹量子多体疤痕（rainbow quantum many-body scar, RQMBS）态的母哈密顿量（parent Hamiltonian）与强耗散开放系统的有效林德布拉德算符（effective Lindbladian）之间存在谱对应关系，从而将慢弛豫的不同机制联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放量子多体系统的流体力学弛豫可由与强到弱自发对称破缺（strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking, SWSSB）相关的扩散南部—戈德斯通（Nambu--Goldstone, NG）模式描述。本文旨在寻找封闭量子系统中弱遍历破坏与开放量子系统中扩散流体力学背后的共同结构。

Method: 针对具有强 U(1) 对称性与局域电荷退相位林德布拉德算符（local charge-dephasing Lindblad operators）的强耗散开放系统，建立其有效林德布拉德算符与 RQMBS 态母哈密顿量之间的谱对应；对自旋、费米子与玻色子系统构造渐近 RQMBS（asymptotic RQMBS, ARQMBS）态，给出单模变分构造，并在矢量化（vectorization）下分析对应关系。

Result: 在扩大的疤痕子空间内，母哈密顿量具有局域 Rokhsar--Kivelson 型无阻挫分解（frustration-free decomposition）；单模变分构造给出最低激发能量的 O(k^2) 上界，所得态满足 ARQMBS 的定义性判据：正交性、能量方差消失与特征纠缠标度。矢量化下 RQMBS 态映射为固定电荷扇区中的最大混合无穷温度态，在有限密度条件下表现出长程 SWSSB 序。在跃迁率一致有界且为正的假设下，有效林德布拉德算符与母哈密顿量共享相同局域投影算符但权重依赖于跃迁率，给出双侧谱界及相同的能隙系统尺寸标度。

Conclusion: 对于均匀跃迁率，两个算符彼此成正比且本征模式重合，因此精确的二次母哈密顿量分支直接映射为扩散 NG 模式。结果将封闭与开放量子多体系统中慢弛豫的不同机制联系起来。

Abstract: Hydrodynamic relaxation in open quantum many-body systems can be understood in terms of diffusive Nambu--Goldstone (NG) modes associated with strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking (SWSSB). Here, we find a common structure underlying weak ergodicity breaking in closed quantum systems and diffusive hydrodynamics in open quantum systems. We establish a spectral correspondence between the parent Hamiltonian of rainbow quantum many-body scar (RQMBS) states in closed systems and the effective Lindbladian of strongly dissipative open systems with strong $U(1)$ symmetry and local charge-dephasing Lindblad operators. For broad classes of spin, fermionic, and bosonic systems, we construct asymptotic RQMBS (ARQMBS) states. Within the enlarged scar subspace, the parent Hamiltonian admits a local Rokhsar--Kivelson-type frustration-free decomposition. A single-mode variational construction gives an $O(k^2)$ upper bound on its lowest excitation energy and yields states satisfying the defining ARQMBS criteria: orthogonality, vanishing energy variance, and characteristic entanglement scaling. Under vectorization, RQMBS states are mapped to maximally mixed infinite-temperature states in fixed-charge sectors, which exhibit long-range SWSSB order under finite-density conditions. Assuming uniformly bounded positive transition rates, the effective Lindbladian has the same local projectors as the parent Hamiltonian but with positive transition-dependent weights, yielding two-sided spectral bounds and the same system-size scaling of their gaps. For uniform rates, the two operators are proportional to each other and their eigenmodes coincide. Therefore, an exact quadratic parent-Hamiltonian branch maps directly to a diffusive NG mode. Our results connect different mechanisms of slow relaxation in closed and open quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [82] [Heat transport in weakly anharmonic Fermi-Pasta-Ulam-Tsingou chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09936)
*Kiratholly Nandakumar Madhav Sharma,Stefano Mossa,Jean-Louis Barrat,Markus Holzmann*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 通过对一维β-FPU-Tsingou链弱非谐区的大规模平衡分子动力学模拟，将热流分解为声子模式贡献，揭示其长时自相关由对角模式关联主导，衰减由声子阻尼率决定，并给出Γ_k ∝ (βT)^{4/3}k^{5/3}（含k^2次领头修正）及由此产生的两段幂律衰减与κ(N) ∝ N^{2/5}+O(N^{1/5})的标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于厘清一维β-Fermi–Pasta–Ulam–Tsingou（FPU-Tsingou）链在弱非谐（weakly anharmonic）区间的反常热输运（anomalous heat transport）机制，特别是弱非谐是否引入与强非谐不同的渐近输运机制。

Method: 采用大规模平衡分子动力学（equilibrium molecular dynamics）模拟，将热流（heat current）按声子模式（phonon-mode）分解，分析其长时自相关函数（autocorrelation），并考察对角与非对角模式关联的贡献、声子阻尼率（phonon damping rates）以及向热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）的外推。

Result: 长时热流自相关由对角模式关联（diagonal mode correlations）主导，其衰减由声子阻尼率定量决定；热力学极限外推给出Γ_k ∝ (βT)^{4/3}k^{5/3}，并含次领头k^2修正。该修正导致较长的前渐近区（pre-asymptotic regime）C(t)∼t^{-4/5}，随后进入渐近衰减C(t)∼t^{-3/5}，对应κ(N)∼N^{2/5}+O(N^{1/5})。弱非谐下，非对角模式关联（off-diagonal mode correlations）持续到特征时间τ_od∼(βT)^{-2}，而分辨长波声子（long-wavelength phonon）贡献需要越来越大的系统尺寸。

Conclusion: 弱非谐并未引入独立的渐近输运机制，而是将声子主导区（phonon-dominated regime）的起始推至更长时间与更大长度尺度；因此弱非谐主要改变标度交叉的位置而非渐近标度本身。

Abstract: We investigate anomalous heat transport in the one-dimensional $β$-Fermi--Pasta--Ulam--Tsingou chain in the weakly anharmonic regime using large-scale equilibrium molecular dynamics. By resolving the heat current into phonon-mode contributions, we show that its long-time autocorrelation is controlled by diagonal mode correlations, whose decay is quantitatively determined by the corresponding phonon damping rates. Extrapolation to the thermodynamic limit gives $Γ_k \propto (βT)^{4/3} k^{5/3}$, with a subleading $k^2$ correction. The latter produces an extended pre-asymptotic regime, $C(t) \sim t^{-4/5}$, before the asymptotic decay $C(t) \sim t^{-3/5}$ is reached, leading to $κ(N) \sim N^{2/5} + O(N^{1/5})$. At weak anharmonicity, off-diagonal mode correlations persist up to a characteristic time $τ_{\mathrm{od}} \sim (βT)^{-2}$, while increasingly large systems are required to resolve the long-wavelength phonon contribution. Weak anharmonicity therefore does not introduce a distinct asymptotic transport mechanism, but shifts the onset of the phonon-dominated regime to progressively longer times and larger length scales.

</details>


### [83] [Percolation on interdependent one-dimensional long-range networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10097)
*Adar Sharabi,Amir Bashan,Sergey V. Buldyrev,Guy Amit*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究探讨两个一维长程网络（long-range networks）构成的多层网络（multiplex network）中互巨分量（mutual giant component）的涌现条件，给出临界阈值 $C_c^{\mathrm{mul}}$ 的严格下界与树近似（tree approximation），并揭示其相变（phase transition）阶数依赖于长程指数 $\sigma$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多层网络（multiplex network）中的相互依赖（interdependency）通常削弱系统稳定性，但其在长程连接（long-range connections）情境下的临界行为尚不明确。该研究旨在刻画两层一维长程网络相互耦合时互巨分量（mutual giant component）的涌现阈值及其相对单层网络的稳定性差异。

Method: 模型由两个一维晶格（lattice）上的长程网络构成，节点间距 $r$ 处连边概率按幂律 $Cr^{-(1+\sigma)}$ 衰减，参数范围 $0<\sigma<1$、$0<C\leq1$。研究推导 $C_c^{\mathrm{mul}}(\sigma)$ 的严格下界（rigorous lower bounds），并构建树近似（tree approximation）估计该阈值，与数值模拟（simulation）结果比对。此外比较了依赖连边（dependency links）连接随机节点对的情形。

Result: 树近似给出的 $C_c^{\mathrm{mul}}$ 估计在 $\sigma\leq0.5$ 范围内与模拟结果吻合误差在 0.6% 以内。多层网络的 $C_c^{\mathrm{mul}}$ 显著高于单层情形，最高可达单层临界值的 2.4 倍，表明稳定性明显降低。模拟显示相变阶数依赖于 $\sigma$：$\sigma\leq0.35$ 时互巨分量以不连续方式坍塌，属一阶相变（first-order phase transition）；$\sigma\geq0.4$ 时为连续相变（continuous transition）。当依赖连边连接随机节点对时，所有 $\sigma$ 下均为一级相变。

Conclusion: 长程连接的多层网络相较单层网络稳定性显著下降，其互巨分量阈值随 $\sigma$ 变化且被严格下界与树近似有效刻画。相变性质由 $\sigma$ 与依赖连边（dependency links）的连接方式共同决定，依赖连边连接随机节点对时会普遍导致一阶相变。

Abstract: We study the emergence of a mutual giant component in a multiplex network comprised of two one-dimensional long-range networks on a lattice. The probability that two nodes on one lattice, with a distance $r$ between them, are connected with an edge falls algebraically with distance as $Cr^{-(1+σ)}$, where $0 < σ< 1$ and $0 < C \leq 1$. A mutual giant component exists if $C > C_c^{\mathrm{mul}}$, where $C_c^{\mathrm{mul}}(σ)$ is a critical value of $C$ that is dependent on $σ$. We find rigorous lower bounds on $C_c^{\mathrm{mul}}$, and a tree approximation whose estimate of $C_c^{\mathrm{mul}}$ agrees with simulations within 0.6% for $σ\leq 0.5$. The multiplex network is significantly less stable than the single network case, as is evident by the larger value of $C_c^{\mathrm{mul}}$, which is up to 2.4 times the single-layer value. Our simulations indicate that the order of the transition depends on $σ$. For $σ\leq 0.35$ the mutual giant component collapses abruptly, in a first-order phase transition, while for $σ\geq 0.4$ the transition is continuous. When the dependency links connect random pairs of nodes, the transition is first order for all $σ$.

</details>


### [84] [Gaussian Scrooge Ensemble from Deep Thermalization in Free-Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10279)
*Angelo Russotto,Katja Klobas,Pasquale Calabrese,Bruno Bertini*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究自由费米子链（free-fermion chain）中的深度热化（deep thermalization）现象，论证在保持高斯性（Gaussianity）但破坏粒子数守恒的局域测量下，子系统的投影系综趋于由广义吉布斯系综（generalized Gibbs ensemble）决定的费米子高斯 Scrooge 系综。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度热化揭示子系统在环境被测量后出现普适纯态系综，但非相互作用可积系统（non-interacting integrable systems）中该现象的统计特征尚待刻画，需将其描述从约化密度矩阵（reduced density matrix）推广到条件纯态的普适统计。

Method: 在自由费米子链中采用保持高斯性并破坏粒子数守恒的局域测量；分析与测量结果相关的局域守恒荷信息，并在子系统层面直接构造普适系综，辅以数值验证。

Result: 测量结果虽提供有限子系统内局域守恒荷的部分信息，但在热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）下该信息可忽略；由此出现的有效非揭示性导致费米子高斯 Scrooge 系综，数值证据表明该系综描述了有限温度下晚时刻的投影系综（projected ensemble）。

Conclusion: 结果将非相互作用可积系统中的热化描述从约化密度矩阵扩展至条件纯态的普适统计，表明深度热化的普适系综可由子系统的广义吉布斯系综决定。

Abstract: Deep thermalization indicates the emergence of universal ensembles of pure states in a subsystem after measuring its environment. We investigate this phenomenon in a free-fermion chain using local measurements that preserve Gaussianity and break particle-number conservation. Although the measurement outcomes provide partial information about local conserved charges within a finite subsystem, we argue that this information becomes negligible in the thermodynamic limit. This effective non-revelation underlies the emergence of a fermionic Gaussian Scrooge ensemble, determined by the subsystem's generalized Gibbs ensemble. We construct this universal ensemble directly at the subsystem level and provide numerical evidence that it describes the late-time projected ensemble at finite temperature. Our results extend the description of thermalization in non-interacting integrable systems beyond the reduced density matrix to universal statistics of conditional pure states.

</details>


### [85] [Effective-Geometry Rescaling and Universal Critical Behavior in the Anisotropic Three-State Potts Model on the Square Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10467)
*Fan Yang,Jian Gao,Lu Liu,Yuhai Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作用 Wolff 单团簇蒙特卡洛（single-cluster Monte Carlo）与有限尺寸标度（finite-size scaling）研究方形格点上各向异性三维态 Potts 铁磁体，发现空间各向异性仅改变有效临界几何，而不改变三维态 Potts 普适类（universality class）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各向异性耦合会引入方向不等价性，需要判断其是否影响临界普适性以及是否可用有效几何描述，因此作者考察不同耦合比 λ=J_y/J_x 下的临界行为与方向相关量。

Method: 采用 Wolff 单团簇蒙特卡洛模拟与有限尺寸标度，对 λ=0.5、0.75、1 计算关联比（correlation ratio）标度、领先 Fisher 零点（Fisher zeros）标度及其累积密度，并引入方向关联比 R_x、R_y 与方向 FK 环绕概率（FK wrapping probabilities）刻画各向异性。

Result: 关联比标度与二维三维态 Potts 普适类一致；Fisher 零点给出关联长度指数 ν=5/6，累积密度给出比热指数 α=1/3。λ=0.5 时 R_x 与 R_y 趋于不同的临界值但共享同一 ν；由环绕概率独立定出有效长宽比 ρ_e^□=0.6413(5)，与等径向表示（isoradial representation）理论值 ρ_e^□,th≈0.64150030 接近。

Conclusion: 用理论长宽比设定物理长宽比可恢复方向等价性（R_x≈R_y），并使整体关联比趋近各向同性方格的参考值；结果表明空间各向异性改变有效临界几何，但不改变体相三维态 Potts 普适类。

Abstract: We study the two-dimensional anisotropic three-state Potts ferromagnet on the square lattice using Wolff single-cluster Monte Carlo simulations and finite-size scaling. For coupling ratios $λ=J_y/J_x=0.5$, $0.75$, and $1$, finite-size scaling of the correlation ratio yields critical behavior consistent with the two-dimensional three-state Potts universality class. The scaling of the leading Fisher zeros gives a correlation-length exponent consistent with $ν=5/6$, while their cumulative density is consistent with the expected specific-heat exponent $α=1/3$. We further characterize the anisotropy at criticality using directional correlation ratios $R_x$ and $R_y$ together with directional FK wrapping probabilities. For $λ=0.5$ on a physically square lattice, $R_x$ and $R_y$ approach distinct critical values while yielding a common correlation-length exponent. From the wrapping probabilities, we independently determine an effective aspect ratio $ρ_e^\square=0.6413(5)$, in close agreement with the theoretical value $ρ_e^{\square,\mathrm{th}}\simeq0.64150030$ obtained from the isoradial representation. Using the theoretical value to set the physical aspect ratio restores directional equivalence, $R_x\simeq R_y$, and brings the overall correlation ratio toward the isotropic-square reference. The results show that spatial anisotropy changes the effective critical geometry without altering the bulk three-state Potts universality class.

</details>


### [86] [Trend formation with sparse global sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10521)
*Sarath Sankar,Abhijit Sinha,Shankar Ghosh,Vikram Tripathi,Amitava Bhattacharya*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文提出并分析了一个极简模型：有限域内自驱动智能体周期性地朝随机抽取的少数同伴质心重新定向，无需感知个体邻居的位置或朝向。稀疏非局域采样可驱动无序群体进入全局排列的向列态（nematic state），且采样子集越小（最少两个）排序越快。研究给出与模拟定量吻合的解析判据，并在最多十五台差分驱动机器人群体中实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无中央控制器与稠密全局通信条件下实现全局协调，是生物集群与工程集群共同面临的核心难题。研究旨在探索一种信息高效的协调途径，即稀疏随机采样是否足以驱动全局有序，并解释其机制。

Method: 构建极简模型：有界区域内的自驱动智能体周期性朝随机选取的少量同伴质心重新定向，不直接感知任何单个邻居的位置或朝向。通过解析推导给出排序成功与否的判据（作为采样规模与采样频率的函数），并以数值模拟对照，最后用最多十五台差分驱动机器人（differential-drive robots）进行实验验证。

Result: 稀疏非局域采样规则可稳定地将初始无序群体驱动为全局排列的向列态（nematic state）。缩小采样子集（直至最少两个智能体）不仅未阻碍排序，反而加速排序；由此产生的估计噪声经几何转向规则过滤后，本身即为对称破缺（symmetry breaking）的驱动力。所推导的解析判据与模拟结果定量吻合。

Conclusion: 稀疏随机采样可作为一种信息高效的集群协调途径，对理解动物集群行为与设计通信受限的机器人集群（robotic swarms）具有启示意义。

Abstract: Achieving global coordination without a central controller or dense global communication is a defining challenge for both biological collectives and engineered swarms. We introduce and analyze a minimal model in which self-propelled agents in a bounded domain periodically reorient their motions toward the centroid of a small, randomly chosen subset of their peers, with no direct sensing of any individual neighbor's position or heading. We show that this sparse, non-local sampling rule reliably drives an initially disordered population to a globally aligned, nematic state, and that shrinking the sampled subset -- down to the minimum of two agents -- accelerates ordering rather than impeding it: the resulting estimation noise, filtered through a geometric turning rule, is itself the engine of symmetry breaking. We derive an analytical criterion, in quantitative agreement with simulation, that predicts when this ordering succeeds as a function of the sampling size and sampling frequency. We confirm the mechanism experimentally in a swarm of up to fifteen differential-drive robots. These results identify sparse random sampling as an information-efficient route to collective coordination, with implications for understanding animal collectives and for designing communication-limited robotic swarms.

</details>

### [87] [Normalized nonlinear Schrödinger ground states on defected grids with unbounded defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09908)
*William Borrelli,Nicolò Cangiotti,Simone Dovetta,Lorenzo Tentarelli*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究二维方格网络（square grid）上带无界缺陷（unbounded defects）的非线性薛定谔能量（nonlinear Schrödinger energy）的归一化基态（normalized ground states），揭示缺陷对维度交叉（dimensional crossover）与紧性缺失（loss of compactness）的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察无界缺陷是否会破坏二维方格图上非线性薛定谔能量的维度交叉现象，并探讨含宏观二维区域与无界一维通道共存时基态存在性的变化。

Method: 方法上结合小质量能量渐近分析（small-mass energy asymptotics）与大质量爆破论证（large-mass blow-up argument），在half-grid、quarter-grid及原型缺陷网络（prototypical defected grid）上进行分析。

Result: 结果表明：无界缺陷不必然破坏维度交叉，其在half-grid与quarter-grid上持续存在；在二维区域与一维通道共存的网络上，对小质量与大质量均不存在归一化基态，说明一维组分可主导变分问题并导致紧性缺失；该机制对图的局部几何敏感，适当附加缺陷可保持小质量不存在性、同时恢复大质量基态存在性。

Conclusion: 结论指出无界缺陷与维度交叉的关系并非破坏性，而一维通道主导与紧性缺失机制依赖于网络局部几何结构，可通过附加缺陷加以调控。

Abstract: We study normalized ground states for the nonlinear Schrödinger energy on two-dimensional square grids with unbounded defects. We first show that unbounded defects do not necessarily destroy the dimensional crossover, proving its persistence on the half-grid and the quarter-grid. We then consider a prototypical defected grid where a macroscopically two-dimensional region coexists with an unbounded one-dimensional channel. On this graph, normalized ground states do not exist for sufficiently small or sufficiently large masses, showing that the one-dimensional component can dominate the variational problem and induce loss of compactness. Finally, we show that this mechanism is sensitive to the local geometry of the graph: suitable additional defects preserve small-mass nonexistence but restore the existence of ground states for large masses. Our analysis combines small-mass energy asymptotics with a large-mass blow-up argument.

</details>


### [88] [Rigidity of stationary black holes with small ADM angular momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09729)
*Alexandru F. Radu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文证明：在整体正则性、分叉与极大超曲面假设下，具有足够小 ADM 角动量的光滑渐近平坦稳态真空黑洞必为 Kerr 黑洞（Kerr black hole），从而回答了 Alexakis、Ionescu 与 Klainerman 提出的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Alexakis、Ionescu 与 Klainerman 此前的结果需假设分叉球面处稳态场足够小；本文旨在将该小性假设替换为无穷远处角动量（ADM angular momentum）的小性，即回答其提出的公开问题。

Method: 将真空度规变换为 Einstein--Maxwell 族，将其极限端的零聚焦（null focusing）与紧致极大超曲面上的面积--电荷不等式（area--charge inequality）进行比较；该极大超曲面通过沿族连续延拓构造。等号成立同时确定变换后的度规及其稳态场，再经逆变换恢复 Kerr。

Result: 建立了任意角动量下无穷远附近的 Kerr 刚性（Kerr rigidity）；等号情形同时确定变换度规与稳态场，并由两个 Kerr 恒等式给出整体结论所需的视界估计。

Conclusion: 在给定几何界所确定的阈值内，小 ADM 角动量的光滑渐近平坦稳态真空黑洞具有整体刚性，即必为 Kerr 解，完成对 Alexakis--Ionescu--Klainerman 问题的回答。

Abstract: We prove global rigidity of smooth asymptotically flat stationary vacuum black holes with sufficiently small ADM angular momentum, under global regularity, bifurcation, and maximal-hypersurface assumptions, with a threshold depending on fixed geometric bounds. This answers the question of Alexakis, Ionescu, and Klainerman concerning replacement of their smallness assumption on the stationary field at the bifurcation sphere by smallness of the angular momentum at infinity. The main step establishes Kerr rigidity near infinity for arbitrary angular momentum. We transform the vacuum metric into an Einstein--Maxwell family and compare null focusing at its limiting end with the area--charge inequality on compact maximal hypersurfaces, constructed by continuation through the family. Equality determines both the transformed metric and its stationary field, from which the inverse transformation recovers Kerr. Two Kerr identities then give the horizon estimate required for the global conclusion.

</details>

### [89] [A long time Gutzwiller trace formula for perturbations of Schrödinger operators near Anosov energy levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09046)
*Julien Moy*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文在半经典框架下研究扰动算子 $P_h+h^\alpha Q$，在未扰动经典动力学于规则能面上呈双曲性的假设下，证明了有效至 $|\log h|$ 时间的 Gutzwiller 迹公式，并指出扰动仅改变迹公式中出现的相位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子能级分布与经典周期轨道之间的联系由 Gutzwiller 迹公式刻画，但实际系统常受扰动影响，其半经典谱对应关系能否在扰动下保持尚需明确。本文旨在考察含振荡符号扰动的半经典算子在双曲经典动力学条件下的迹公式有效性。

Method: 设 $(M,g)$ 为闭 Riemann 流形上的半经典拟微分算子（semiclassical pseudodifferential operator）$P_h$，主符号为 $p$；考虑扰动 $P_h+h^\alpha Q$（$0<\alpha\le 1$），其中 $Q$ 的符号在尺度 $h^\beta$ 上振荡且满足 $2\beta<\alpha$。在未扰动系统于规则能面 $p^{-1}(E)$ 上经典动力学为双曲的假设下，采用半经典分析推导扰动算子的迹公式。

Result: 在所设条件下，扰动算子的 Gutzwiller 迹公式成立，且有效时间可达 $|\log h|$ 量级；该公式将能量 $E$ 附近量子系统的能级分布与其经典对应系统的周期轨道相联系。扰动项 $h^\alpha Q$ 仅影响迹公式中出现的相位。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在双曲经典动力学与小尺度振荡扰动的组合条件下，半经典迹公式在直至 $|\log h|$ 的时间范围内保持稳定，扰动的效应局限于相位修正，从而维持了量子能级与经典周期轨道之间的对应结构。

Abstract: Let $P_h$ be a semiclassical pseudodifferential operator on a closed Riemannian manifold $(M,g)$, with principal symbol $p$. We consider perturbations $P_h+h^αQ$, with $0<α\le 1$, where $Q$ is a semiclassical pseudodifferential operator whose symbol oscillates at scale $h^β$, with $2β<α$. Assuming that the classical dynamics of the unperturbed system on a regular energy surface $p^{-1}(E)$ is hyperbolic, we prove a Gutzwiller trace formula for the perturbed operator that holds up to times of order $|\log h|$. This formula relates the distribution of energy levels of the quantum system near the energy $E$ to the periodic orbits of its classical counterpart. The perturbation $h^αQ$ affects only the phases appearing in the trace formula.

</details>


### [90] [Graph transformations and eigenvectors of the Laplacian, signless Laplacian and Adjacency matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09599)
*Jean-Guy Caputo,Leonardo de Lima,Arnaud Knippel*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 该研究将 Merris 关于拉普拉斯矩阵（Laplacian matrix）特征向量的边与匹配原理推广至无符号拉普拉斯矩阵（signless Laplacian matrix）与邻接矩阵（adjacency matrix），并研究双值图（bivalent graph）在 A 与 Q 下的性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有关于拉普拉斯矩阵特征向量与图变换的结果（如 Merris 的边与匹配原理）尚未系统推广到邻接矩阵与无符号拉普拉斯矩阵，且对双值图在 A、Q 下的刻画仍有空白。

Method: 将 Merris 的拉普拉斯特征向量边与匹配原理推广到 Q 与 A；将部分已知的 L 图变换推广到 Q 与 A；分析 A、L、Q 共享特征向量 x 的图结构；沿用作者此前对 L 的双值图研究，考察 A 与 Q 的双值图。

Result: 证明同时具有 A、L、Q 公共特征向量 x 的图，其对应 x 非零分量的顶点具有相同度数；对树刻画了 Q-双值性，并证明 A-双值性迫使所有顶点度数为奇数。

Conclusion: 特征向量原理与图变换可从 L 推广至 A 与 Q，且双值性在树结构下对 A 与 Q 施加了明确的度数约束。

Abstract: We study graph transformations and their effects on the eigenvectors of three fundamental graph matrices: the adjacency matrix $A$, the signless Laplacian $Q$, and the Laplacian $L$. We extend the Laplacian eigenvector edge and matching principles introduced by Merris to $Q$ and $A$. We also extend some of the known $L$ graph transformations to $Q$ and $A$ and prove that graphs admitting a common eigenvector $\mathbf{x}$ for $A, L$ and $Q$ are such that vertices corresponding to nonzero components of $\mathbf{x}$ have same degree. Following our previous work for $L$, we investigate bivalent graphs for $A$ and $Q$, that is graphs admitting an eigenvector with all components in $\{-1, 1\}$. In particular, for trees, we characterize $Q$-bivalence and establish that $A$-bivalence forces all vertex degrees to be odd.

</details>


### [91] [Smooth spectral statistics of random perturbations of Schrödinger operators near Anosov energy levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09869)
*Julien Moy*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 在半经典薛定谔算子（semiclassical Schrödinger operators）的随机扰动中，于经典混沌动力学对应的能级附近，平滑计数函数的谱涨落（spectral fluctuations）在特定介观尺度（mesoscopic scale）上呈现与随机矩阵理论（random matrix theory）预测一致的普适行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探讨经典混沌对应的半经典量子系统中，随机扰动如何影响能级谱统计，并检验其是否与随机矩阵理论的普适预测相符。

Method: 以紧流形（compact manifolds）上的算子 h^2Δ+V(x) 为原型，加入光滑随机势 h^αV_ω（α∈(0,1)，V_ω 在距离 h^β 上退相关，0<β<2α），分析平滑计数函数（smoothed counting function）的谱涨落。

Result: 对一般性扰动（generic perturbation），本征值平滑计数函数的谱涨落在某一介观尺度上服从普适行为，该行为与随机矩阵理论的预测相吻合。

Conclusion: 随机扰动的半经典薛定谔算子在经典混沌能区附近的谱涨落具有普适性，支持随机矩阵理论在该介观尺度下的有效性。

Abstract: We investigate the spectral statistics of random perturbations of semiclassical Schrödinger operators on compact manifolds, near energy levels corresponding to chaotic classical dynamics. The prototypical example is that of an operator $h^2Δ+V(x)$ that is perturbed by a smooth potential $h^αV_ω$ with $α\in (0,1)$, and $V_ω$ is a random potential that decorrelates on distances $h^β$, with $0<β<2α$. We show that for a generic perturbation, the spectral fluctuations of the smoothed counting function of eigenvalues obey a universal behavior at a certain mesoscopic scale, which is coherent with the predictions of random matrix theory.

</details>

### [92] [Scalar Perturbations and Induced Gravitational Waves from First-Order Phase Transitions in Lattice Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09325)
*Yuan-Jie Li,Jing Liu,Zuan Ning,Xiang-Xi Zeng,Zong-Kuan Guo*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文首次在包含标量度规扰动与辐射扰动的三维格点模拟中，研究宇宙学一阶相变（first-order phase transition）通过非均匀量子隧穿（inhomogeneous quantum tunneling）产生的曲率扰动与引力波，覆盖气泡壁尺度至超视界尺度，给出精确的标量扰动功率谱及能量密度扰动概率密度函数，并发现超视界尺度上非标量场源对引力波的贡献可超过标量场贡献的 1/3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于宇宙学一阶相变产生曲率扰动的研究仅在超视界尺度（superhorizon scales）上进行，缺乏涵盖气泡壁尺度（bubble wall scales）至超视界尺度的完整数值模拟。

Method: 采用三维格点模拟（three-dimensional lattice simulations），首次同时纳入标量度规扰动（scalar metric perturbations）与辐射扰动（radiation perturbations），模拟标量场本身、标量度规扰动以及辐射能量密度与速度扰动等各源产生的引力波能谱，并比较不同相变强度（transition strength）与速率（rate）取值的影响。

Result: 获得精确的标量扰动功率谱（scalar perturbation power spectrum）与能量密度扰动概率密度函数（probability density function）；对引力波而言，超视界尺度上其他源的贡献可超过标量场贡献的 1/3；并给出了不同相变强度与速率下的结果对比。

Conclusion: 该研究为通过引力波与曲率扰动探测或限制一阶相变提供了更精确的数值结果。

Abstract: Cosmological first-order phase transitions can generate curvature perturbations through inhomogeneous quantum tunneling, as studied previously on superhorizon scales. In this work, we for the first time conduct three-dimensional lattice simulations that incorporate scalar metric perturbations and radiation perturbations, covering a range from bubble wall scales to super-horizon scales. We obtain the precise scalar perturbation power spectrum and the probability density function of energy density perturbations. Furthermore, we simulate the gravitational-wave energy spectra generated by each source during the first-order phase transition, including the scalar field itself, scalar metric perturbations, and the energy density and velocity perturbations of radiation. For gravitational waves, the contribution from other sources can exceed $1/3$ of that from the scalar field at superhorizon scales. Additionally, we compare the effects of different values of the transition strength and rate on the results. This paper provides more accurate numerical results for research aimed at detecting or constraining first-order phase transitions via gravitational waves and curvature perturbations.

</details>


### [93] [LazyTB: Extended LTB-solutions with Multiple Interacting Fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09625)
*Kimmo Kainulainen,Enrico Schiappacasse,Linda Tenhu,Olli Väisänen*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: LazyTB 是一个低门槛的数值代码，用于求解 ADM 形式下一般球对称、任意流体数目的爱因斯坦方程（Einstein equations），并配备光线追踪等诊断工具以提取可观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为一般球对称问题提供一个易扩展、低使用门槛的数值求解工具，支持用户自定义状态方程与流体间由拖曳矢量（drag-vectors）中介的相互作用。

Method: 在 ADM 形式（ADM formulation）下以精心选取的无量纲变量（dimensionless variables）表述方程，通过解析消去潜在发散项来避免部分数值不稳定性；并实现包括光线追踪算法（ray-tracing algorithms）在内的一套诊断工具。

Result: 通过多个示例验证代码，包括标准 LTB 模型、辐射与暗物质（dark matter）模型、具有非零声速（sound speed）的多方暗能量（polytropic dark energy）模型，以及耦合相互作用的暗物质与暗能量模型；可提取任意位置观测者的光度距离（luminosity distances）等物理可观测量。

Conclusion: 代码设计便于扩展，不限于本文示例中的晚期宇宙（late-universe）应用；LazyTB 已在 https://github.com/ollijvaisanen/LazyTB 公开提供。

Abstract: We present a low entry barrier numerical code LazyTB that solves the Einstein equations in the ADM-formulation for a general spherically symmetric problem with an arbitrary number of fluids with user defined equations of state and optional interactions between the fluids mediated by drag-vectors. We present the ADM equations in terms of carefully chosen dimensionless variables that avoid some numerical instabilities by analytic cancellation of potentially diverging terms. We demonstrate the code by applying it to a number of examples including the standard LTB model, a model with radiation and dark matter, a polytropic dark energy model with a non-vanishing sound speed and a model with coupled interacting dark matter and dark energy. We also implement a suite of diagnostic tools, including ray-tracing algorithms, that allow extracting physical observables such as luminosity distances for observers at arbitrary locations from the simulations. The code is built to be easy to extend also beyond the late-universe applications considered in the examples of this paper. LazyTB is available at https://github.com/ollijvaisanen/LazyTB.

</details>


### [94] [Statistics of large-scale structure from the transition probability of Lagrangian trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30251)
*Lawrence Dam*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文提出一种在位形空间（configuration space）中处理引力成团（gravitational clustering）的概率方法，将两点函数（two-point function）表示为跃迁概率（transition probability），并由此给出红外重求和（infrared resummation）中两点函数被有效压低的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上引力成团的分析多依赖其他表述，而本文旨在探索一种纯位形空间下的概率描述视角，以刻画粒子从初始位置到最终位置的统计行为。其动机在于用一个物理直观的跃迁概率图像来理解两点函数的演化与压低现象。

Method: 方法完全在位形空间（configuration space）中进行，将两点函数以跃迁概率（transition probability）的形式表述；该跃迁概率描述给定初始位置时粒子的可能最终位置。摘要未给出具体解析或数值实现细节。

Result: 跃迁概率初始时呈尖峰分布（sharply peaked），随后随时间以类扩散（diffusion-like）方式展宽。该效应在相距较远的粒子对上更为显著，例如在重子声学尺度（baryon acoustic scale）上，从而对两点函数产生有效压低。

Conclusion: 该跃迁概率的展宽及其对远距粒子对的更强作用，为通常所称的红外重求和（infrared resummation）提供了一种概率性诠释，即在红外尺度上两点函数被有效压低。

Abstract: We describe a probabilistic approach to gravitational clustering. Working purely in configuration space, we show that the two-point function can be formulated in terms of a transition probability describing the likely final position of a particle given its initial position. The transition probability is initially sharply peaked and then over time broadens out in a diffusion-like manner. The effect is more pronounced for widely-separated particles (e.g.\ at the baryon acoustic scale), leading to an effective suppression of the two-point function in what is normally referred to as infrared resummation.

</details>

### [95] [Phase-space structure of cosmological thermogravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08853)
*Charlotte Brereton,João Magueijo,Mar Alí Caballo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 对一类涌现引力（emergent gravity）理论的齐次动力学（homogeneous dynamics）进行系统研究，发现单流体情形可由二维自主体系统（autonomous system）完全刻画并分三类解，而仅冷暗物质（cold dark matter, CDM）破坏能量守恒的三维相空间（phase space）具有排斥子线与真空不动点线，且CDM丰度趋零的极限是非解析的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该涌现引力模型蕴含洛伦兹不变性（Lorentz invariance）的涌现破缺与应力-能量守恒（stress-energy conservation）的破缺，其最简单的平坦尘埃解在晚期无真空能加速，已作为观测检验的解析基准。为理解该理论更一般的宇宙学行为，需研究其更广泛的齐次动力学与解空间。

Method: 采用动力学系统（dynamical systems）方法：对单一不守恒尘埃流体，以 $Ω_m=ρ/(3H^2)$ 与 $u=(HL_2)^{-1}$ 构造二维自主体系统并求精确首次积分（first integral）；进而分析仅CDM破坏能量守恒、重子与光子保持常规行为的三维相空间模型。

Result: 单流体情形：展开解空间分为三类——终止于渐近de Sitter不动点线（asymptotically de Sitter fixed line）的轨迹、对应Isichei与Magueijo解析解的临界轨迹、以及掉头塌缩（turn around and recollapse）的轨迹。三维情形：出现物质排斥子线（matter repellers）与真空不动点线（vacuum fixed points）；每个 $0<u_0<3$ 的常吸引真空点拥有二维稳定叶面（stable sheet）。CDM丰度趋零的极限为奇异：有限 $u_0$ 叶面在含类宇宙学常数积分常数的轨迹上与纯重子平面相交，而零积分常数的重子解仍停留在 $Ω_b=1$ 且 $u\to\infty$。

Conclusion: 临界端点 $u_0=3$ 将上述晚期行为分隔开，并推广了单流体情形下的特殊解。该理论在更一般的齐次设定下展现出丰富的相空间结构，其CDM极限的奇异性为连接纯重子与含CDM情形的关键特征。

Abstract: An emergent gravity model was shown to contain an associated emergent breaking of Lorentz invariance and violations of stress-energy conservation. Its simplest flat dust solution accelerates at late times without vacuum energy and has already been used as an analytic benchmark for observational tests. We study the wider homogeneous dynamics and solutions of this theory. For a single non-conserved dust fluid, the variables $Ω_m=ρ/(3H^2)$ and $u=(HL_2)^{-1}$ form a two-dimensional autonomous system with an exact first integral. The complete expanding solution space separates into three classes: trajectories ending on an asymptotically de Sitter fixed line, a critical trajectory corresponding to the analytic solution of Isichei and Magueijo, and trajectories that turn around and recollapse. We then consider the phenomenologically cleaner model in which only cold dark matter violates energy conservation (while the baryons and photons behave as normal). The phase space becomes three-dimensional, with a line of matter repellers and a line of vacuum fixed points. Each normally attracting vacuum point with $0<u_0<3$ possesses a two-dimensional stable sheet. The limit in which the CDM abundance tends to zero is singular: finite-$u_0$ sheets meet the exactly baryonic plane on trajectories containing a cosmological-constant-like integration constant, whereas the zero-integration-constant baryonic solution remains at $Ω_b=1$ and runs to $u\to\infty$. The critical endpoint $u_0=3$ separates these late-time behaviours and generalizes the special single-fluid solution.

</details>


### [96] [Lifetime reversals of neutral and charged scalar clouds around nonminimal Einstein-Yang-Mills black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08855)
*S. V. Bolokhov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算了渐近平直非最小爱因斯坦–杨–米尔斯（nonminimal Einstein–Yang–Mills）黑洞周围的中性规范单态标量（neutral gauge-singlet scalars）以及带电标量二重态与三重态（charged scalar doublets and triplets）的准束缚态（quasi-bound states）。核心结论是：规范荷（gauge charge）与非最小耦合（nonminimal coupling）均不必然延长标量云的存活时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察非最小耦合与规范荷如何影响黑洞周围标量云的准束缚态寿命，并检验其与对应 Reissner–Nordström 几何中行为的差异。

Method: 采用双侧积分（two-sided integration）与独立的任意精度连分数（arbitrary-precision continued fraction）方法，同时检验实频与远小的虚部；并通过精确视界域（horizon-domain）分析确定黑洞参数范围，以正势（positive-potential）论证排除正则测试标量的指数增长模式。

Result: 对中性单极子，非最小耦合使寿命响应发生逆转：弱束缚云在非最小爱因斯坦–杨–米尔斯几何上衰减更慢，而增大场质量将吸收由抑制转为增强；弱束缚行为趋近视界面积律（horizon-area law），但该律本身不能决定有限质量排序。偶极与四极宽度显著减小而能级变化极小。带电多重态经磁 Wu–Yang 联络（magnetic Wu–Yang connection）分离为量子化单极子扇区，无电频移；其最低通道在采样捕获区内出现十二次寿命交叉及非整数弱束缚衰减幂。在三重态中固定总角动量时，带电态可比重中性态快数百倍衰减。

Conclusion: 结果复现了 Schwarzschild 与 Reissner–Nordström 极限，验证了类氢束缚（hydrogenic binding）与低频吸收标度；并区分了势阱消失与准束缚极点消失。总体表明规范荷与非最小耦合均不普遍延长标量云存活时间。

Abstract: We calculate quasi-bound states of neutral gauge-singlet scalars and charged scalar doublets and triplets around the asymptotically flat, nonminimal Einstein--Yang--Mills black hole. For neutral monopoles, the principal result is a reversal in the lifetime response to the nonminimal coupling: weakly bound clouds decay more slowly than on the corresponding Reissner--Nordström geometry, whereas increasing the field mass changes suppression into enhancement of absorption. The weak-binding behavior approaches a horizon-area law, but that law alone does not determine the finite-mass ordering. At representative couplings, dipole and quadrupole widths instead decrease substantially while their level energies change very little. Two-sided integration and an independent arbitrary-precision continued fraction check both the real frequencies and the much smaller imaginary parts. We recover the Schwarzschild and Reissner--Nordström limits, test hydrogenic binding and the low-frequency absorption scaling, and distinguish the loss of a potential well from the disappearance of a quasi-bound pole. An exact horizon-domain analysis fixes the black-hole parameter range, and a positive-potential argument excludes exponentially growing modes of the canonical test scalars. The magnetic Wu--Yang connection separates the charged multiplets into quantized monopole sectors, without an electric frequency shift. Their lowest channels exhibit twelve located lifetime crossings within the sampled trapping region and noninteger weak-binding decay powers. At fixed total angular momentum within the triplet, charged states can instead decay hundreds of times faster than neutral states. These comparisons show that neither gauge charge nor nonminimal coupling universally prolongs cloud survival.

</details>


### [97] [Phase transitions, shadows, and microstructure of Reissner-Nordström-Anti-de-Sitter black holes from a geometrothermodynamic perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.10062)
*Jose M. Ladino,Carlos E. Romero-Figueroa,Hernando Quevedo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 论文利用阴影热力学（shadow thermodynamics）与几何热力学（geometrothermodynamics）研究含宇宙学常数、以曲率半径（curvature radius）表示的 Reissner-Nordström 黑洞热力学性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为刻画含宇宙学常数的 Reissner-Nordström 黑洞的热力学与相变行为，并检验不同热力学几何方法在该背景下的适用性与一致性。

Method: 采用阴影热力学方法与几何热力学形式体系，将阴影半径（shadow radius）以视界（horizon）、光子球（photon sphere）与观测者半径显式表达；同时引入黑洞为拟齐次热力学系统（quasi-homogeneous thermodynamic system）的条件以保障几何热力学方法的一致性。
