Conclusion: 曲率半径显著影响该黑洞的相变结构与微观结构描述，两种几何方法的不一致凸显了拟齐次条件的重要性。

Abstract: We study the thermodynamic properties of the Reissner-Nordström black hole with cosmological constant, expressed in terms of the curvature radius, by using the approach of shadow thermodynamics and the formalism of geometrothermodynamics. We derive explicit expressions for the shadow radius in terms of the horizon, photon sphere, and observer radii. The phase transition structure turns out to strongly depend on the value of the curvature radius, including configurations with zero, one, or two phase transitions. We also analyze the black hole microscopic structure and find differences between the approaches of thermodynamic geometry and geometrothermodynamics, which are due to the presence of the curvature radius. We impose the important condition that the black hole is a quasi-homogeneous thermodynamic system to guarantee the consistency of the geometrothermodynamic approach.

</details>


### [98] [The price of regularity: core gradient instability in nonsingular dyonic black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08909)
*Lodovico Capuano,Alessandro Longo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在爱因斯坦-非线性电动力学（Einstein-nonlinear-electrodynamics）框架下，对含两个不变量的一般二子（dyonic）静态球对称解推导线性微扰的二次作用量，证明中心正则性、无鬼（ghost）与角向梯度（angular gradient）稳定性不可兼得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典广义相对论（General Relativity）黑洞解存在时空奇点（spacetime singularity），通常期望由高能效应修正；同时已知非线性电动力学耦合引力可给出非奇异黑洞解，但近期显示纯电/纯磁情形存在梯度不稳定性（gradient instability）。本文旨在将该稳定性分析推广至更一般的二子解与一般不变量依赖情形。

Method: 在爱因斯坦-非线性电动力学理论中，取静态、球对称的二子（dyonic）解，并允许拉格朗日量对两个电磁不变量（invariants）具有一般依赖；据此推导线性微扰的二次作用量（quadratic action），并分析正则性、鬼模与角向传播速度符号。

Result: 中心正则性、无鬼与角向梯度稳定性无法同时满足：在任意接近正则中心处，至少有一种光子极化（photon polarization）具有负的角向传播速度平方。此外，正则性会驱使一个电磁动能本征值在中心趋于零，可能限制中心附近的微扰可控性（perturbative control）。

Conclusion: 该阻碍（obstruction）表明，在现框架内难以同时实现非奇异黑洞与非微扰不稳定性；需通过扩展理论（例如引入额外自由度）来规避。

Abstract: Spacetime singularities displayed by classical black hole solutions are expected to be cured by high-energy effects, beyond the regime of validity of General Relativity. On the other hand, there are well-known examples of classical theories allowing for nonsingular black hole solutions. The most prominent is obtained by complementing the gravitational sector of General Relativity with a minimally coupled nonlinear-electrodynamics sector. It has recently been shown that purely electric/purely magnetic nonsingular black holes in an Einstein-nonlinear-electrodynamics setup are always plagued by a gradient instability. In this work, we derive the quadratic action for linear perturbations of static, spherically symmetric dyonic solutions of Einstein-nonlinear-electrodynamics theories with a general dependence on both invariants. We show that regularity of the center, absence of ghosts and angular gradient stability cannot hold simultaneously: arbitrarily close to a regular center, at least one photon polarization has a negative squared angular propagation speed. We further show that regularity drives one electromagnetic kinetic eigenvalue to zero at the center, which may limit perturbative control near the core. Evading the obstruction requires extending the theory, for example by introducing additional degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [99] [Anisotropic Cosmology with Inhomogeneous $SU(2) $ and $SL(2,\mathbb{R})$ Nonlinear $σ$-Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09049)
*N. Dimakis,A. Giacomini,G. Leon,F. Moncada,A. Paliathanasis*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究非线性 σ-模型（nonlinear σ-model）在局部旋转对称 Bianchi V 背景几何中的宇宙学动力学，采用 SU(2) 与 SL(2,ℝ) 内部对称性，对指数势（exponential potential）进行相空间（phase space）分析，确定不动点及其稳定性。结果显示标量场非均匀性排除慢滚（slow-roll）与双曲暴胀（hyperbolic inflation），宇宙加速需依赖宇宙学常数（cosmological constant）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察非线性 σ-模型在非均匀、各向异性的 Bianchi V 几何中的宇宙学行为，以检验内部对称性结构下多标量场动力学能否产生暴胀与加速膨胀。

Method: 方法上采用 SU(2) 与 SL(2,ℝ) 内部对称性，使多标量场动能度规（kinetic metric）对应常曲率空间；对指数势下的标量场引入非均匀假设（inhomogeneous ansatz），进行相空间分析，求解不动点（stationary points）并研究其稳定性。

Result: 结果显示标量场非均匀性（scalar field inhomogeneities）同时排除了慢滚暴胀与双曲暴胀阶段；相空间分析给出了不动点位置及稳定性特征。

Conclusion: 结论为在 Bianchi V 背景下，非线性 σ-模型的非均匀标量场配置无法支持慢滚或双曲暴胀；要实现宇宙加速膨胀，必须引入宇宙学常数。

Abstract: We investigate the cosmological dynamics of the nonlinear $σ$-model in a locally rotational symmetric Bianchi V background geometry. We employ the $SU(2)$ and the $SL(2,\mathbb{R})$ internal symmetries, for which the kinetic metric of the multi-scalar fields is a space of constant curvature. We assume an inhomogeneous ansatz for the scalar fields, such that the $σ$-model, which allows the existence of nontrivial solutions in the Bianchi V geometry. For an exponential potential, we perform a detailed analysis of the phase space, we determine the stationary points, and we study their stability properties. The scalar field inhomogeneities prevent both the slow-roll and hyperbolic inflationary epochs. The existence of a cosmological constant is required to describe cosmic acceleration.

</details>


### [100] [Intrinsic spatial-strain shortcuts in general relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09143)
*Francisco S. N. Lobo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在固定参考体积下，通过空间度规的纵向收缩与横向膨胀构造时空捷径（spacetime shortcuts），证明这类光滑有界超静态通道全局双曲且测地完备，并可产生精确的旅行时间提前。研究给出Olum端点曲面判据、精确Einstein源恒等式、局域零能量条件违反证明与定量界，以及有限剖面Ricci谱与轴向乘客潮汐张量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察一类预制备几何（prepared geometry）自身的因果与源约束，而非其物理形成机制或物质动力学。作者关注在固定参考体积下由空间度规变形产生的通道，能否在保持良好因果结构的同时实现时间提前，并厘清其能量条件与物质源性质。

Method: 方法上采用精确解析与几何恒等式分析：构造光滑有界超静态（ultrastatic）通道并检验全局双曲性与测地完备性；利用轴向极大值条件分析光线全局最小化行为；通过Einstein源恒等式区分数值积分Eulerian物质能量与渐近质量；借助横向Ricci恒等式证明局域零能量条件违反；并计算有限剖面的Ricci谱与轴向乘客潮汐张量。

Result: 结果显示：外部时钟之间存在精确的旅行时间提前（travel-time advance）；轴向极大值使轴向光线全局最小化，内部区间的严格极大值满足Olum端点曲面判据。无限管状通道具有负的积分能量，而有限通道存在相互竞争的梯度贡献。每个非平凡局域有限通道均存在局域零能量条件违反；对局域轴向极大剖面，轴向平均零能量严格为负，且其大小与参考时间提前及峰值应变存在定量界。

Conclusion: 结论是这类通道在因果结构与源性质上受到明确约束：其时间提前伴随零能量条件违反与负的轴向平均零能量，且能量积分与渐近质量之间存在差异。作者强调结果仅针对预制备几何给出显式约束，未涉及其物理形成过程或物质动力学。

Abstract: We analyze spacetime shortcuts generated by longitudinal contraction and transverse expansion of the intrinsic spatial metric at fixed reference volume. Smooth bounded ultrastatic corridors are globally hyperbolic and geodesically complete and produce an exact travel-time advance between exterior clocks. An axial maximum makes the axial light ray globally minimizing; a strict maximum on an interior interval establishes Olum's endpoint-surface criterion for exactly matched corridors. Exact Einstein-source identities distinguish integrated Eulerian matter energy from asymptotic mass: infinite tubes have negative integrated energy, whereas finite corridors admit competing gradient contributions. A transverse Ricci identity proves local null-energy violation in every nontrivial localized finite corridor. The axial averaged null energy is strictly negative for localized axial-maximum profiles, and quantitative bounds relate its magnitude to the reference time advance and peak strain. We also obtain the complete finite-profile Ricci spectrum and the axial passenger tidal tensor. These results provide explicit causal and source constraints on a prepared geometry without specifying its physical formation or matter dynamics.

</details>


### [101] [Toward a complete understanding of the properties of GW190521](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09199)
*Aasim Jan,Sophia Nicolella,Aaron Zimmerman,Deirdre Shoemaker,Katerina Chatziioannou,Richard O'Shaughnessy,Muhammed Saleem*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文对 GW190521 进行综合重分析，采用多族波形模型（含同时涵盖偏心率与自旋进动的模型），发现源具有高偏心率（5 Hz 处约 0.81），非偏心解释源于未探测高偏心率高似然区，且表观自旋进动可能部分来自偏心率—自旋进动简并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GW190521 因总质量极高、可观测引力波周期数极少，导致不同物理解释之间存在简并，围绕该事件的悬而未决问题促使作者开展系统性重分析。

Method: 使用来自多个族（families）的波形模型（waveform models）进行重分析，其中包括同时包含偏心率（eccentricity）与自旋进动（spin-precession）的模型；并通过 GW190521 类注入（injections）的推断以及高偏心率数值相对论（numerical-relativity）波形的恢复来检验稳健性。

Result: 发现源高度偏心，5 Hz 处推断偏心率为 0.81^{+0.05}_{-0.01}；纳入偏心率后无自旋进动迹象，提示准圆（quasicircular）分析中的表观进动至少部分由偏心率—自旋进动简并驱动。所有模型偏好等质量比双星，但一个模型允许在更不对称质量下存在次级模式；主黑洞位于对不稳定超新星质量间隙（pair-instability supernova mass gap，∼60–130 M⊙）的概率稳健（≥97%），次黑洞位于该间隙的概率则依赖波形模型。

Conclusion: GW190521 分析中观察到的波形系统误差（waveform systematics）可能源于模型对未建模偏心率的响应，而非波形模型之间的内在差异；对类似 GW190521 的准圆自旋进动注入未发现显著模型依赖；由于推断偏心率超出当前模型的校准范围（calibration regime），作者通过恢复高偏心率数值相对论波形检验其稳健性。

Abstract: Interpreting GW190521 remains challenging due to its exceptionally high total mass and correspondingly small number of observable gravitational-wave cycles, which gives rise to degeneracies between different physical interpretations. Motivated by the outstanding questions surrounding the event, we present a comprehensive reanalysis of GW190521 using waveform models drawn from multiple families, including a model that includes both eccentricity and spin-precession. We find evidence that the source was highly eccentric, with an inferred eccentricity of $0.81^{+0.05}_{-0.01}$ at 5 Hz, and show that non-eccentric interpretations arise from failing to probe the high-likelihood region at large eccentricities. We further demonstrate that when eccentricity is included in the analysis, there is no indication of spin precession, suggesting that the apparent precession in quasicircular analyses is at least partially driven by an eccentricity--spin-precession degeneracy. While all waveform models favor a comparable-mass binary, one model admits a secondary mode at more asymmetric masses. We find a robust probability $(\geq97\%)$ that the primary black hole lies within the pair-instability supernova mass gap ($\sim60-130\,M_\odot$), whereas the probability that the secondary lies in the gap depends on the waveform model. Through inference of GW190521-like injections, we show that the waveform systematics observed in the analysis of GW190521 could arise from the response of models to unmodeled eccentricity, rather than from intrinsic differences between waveform models. When analyzing a quasicircular spin-precessing injection similar to GW190521, we find no significant model dependence in the inferred parameters. Finally, because the inferred eccentricity lies beyond the calibration regime of current models, we test its robustness by recovering highly eccentric numerical-relativity waveforms.

</details>


### [102] [Logarithmic corrections to massive particle trajectories for the classical soft log theorems at leading order in $G$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09216)
*T. H. Nadai*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究大质量粒子渐近轨迹的对数修正（logarithmic corrections），该修正进入四维引力散射（gravitational scattering）中零无穷（null infinity）处辐射场软对数扇区（soft-log sector）的计算。作者分离了度规中影响粒子运动方程的部分，并将物质能动张量（energy-momentum tensor）沿粒子世界线反演，用勒让德多项式（Legendre polynomials）得到领头阶 G 下各对数阶的轨迹系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究引力散射中辐射场软对数扇区对数修正对粒子渐近轨迹的影响，并厘清软塔（soft tower）与轨迹系数之间的关系。

Method: 先分离度规中进入粒子运动方程的部分；分析非线性源对该扇区的影响仅通过双曲面（hyperboloid）上的齐次模（homogeneous modes）实现；利用与所提软塔的匹配论证这些模除第一模外消失；并沿粒子世界线反演物质能动张量，借助勒让德多项式求解轨迹系数。

Result: 非线性源仅能通过双曲面上的齐次模影响该扇区；与软塔匹配后这些模除第一模外为零，而第一模在领头阶 G 不能影响轨迹系数；由此得到领头阶 G 下每一对数阶的轨迹系数，前两个系数复现了 G 阶与 G² 阶的已知结果。

Conclusion: 轨迹系数可在领头阶 G 下解析求得并覆盖所有对数阶，且与低阶已知结果一致，验证了软塔匹配与齐次模处理的自洽性。

Abstract: We study the logarithmic corrections to the asymptotic trajectories of massive particles. These corrections enter the calculation of the soft-log sector of the radiative field at null infinity in four-dimensional gravitational scattering. We first isolate the part of the metric which enters the particle equations of motion. We show that the nonlinear source can only affect this sector through homogeneous modes on the hyperboloid, and that matching to the proposed soft tower implies that these modes vanish, except for the first mode. We show that this mode cannot affect the trajectory coefficients at leading order in G. We then invert the matter energy-momentum tensor on the particle worldlines to obtain the trajectory coefficients at leading order in $G$ and at every logarithmic order using Legendre polynomials. The first two coefficients reproduce the known results at orders $G$ and $G^2$. .

</details>


### [103] [Collisions of Multiple Black Holes: A Numerical Relativity Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09336)
*Chen-Kai Qiao,Shu-Yuan Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在数值相对论（numerical relativity）框架下，采用BSSN表述与AMSS-NCKU代码系统研究了多个黑洞系统的正面碰撞（head-on collisions），揭示对称构型对引力波辐射的强约束及对称系统中不存在引力反冲（gravitational kick）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值相对论对引力理论与引力波天文学具有深远影响，本文旨在系统研究多黑洞系统正面碰撞的动力学演化及其引力波辐射特征，尤其是在不同对称与非对称构型下的差异。

Method: 使用Baumgarte-Shapiro-Shibata-Nakamura（BSSN）表述与AMSS-NCKU代码进行数值模拟；黑洞初始静止，由Bowen-York型初始数据描述；考察正多边形、正多面体等对称构型及非对称构型。

Result: 完全对称构型对引力波辐射施加了强约束，且不产生引力反冲；正多边形构型中辐射以l=2、m=0模的加号偏振（plus polarization）为主；正多面体构型中所有l=2四极模几乎消失，引力波振幅被强烈抑制至10^-4量级或更低，与四极近似分析一致；m≠0模的辐射仅在不满足对称性时才不可忽略。

Conclusion: 多黑洞系统的对称性显著决定了其引力波辐射模式与反冲特征；对称构型下的引力波振幅大幅抑制，非对称构型才会激发m≠0模的显著辐射，结果与四极近似相符。

Abstract: Numerical relativity has a profound impact on gravitational theory and gravitational wave astronomy. In this work, we present a systematic study of the head-on collisions of multipule black hole systems within the framework of numerical relativity using the Baumgarte-Shapiro-Shibata-Nakamura (BSSN) formulation. The numerical simulations of the dynamical evolution of gravitational fields are computed using the AMSS-NCKU code. We report numerical results for black hole collisions in several symmetric and non-symmetric configurations, in which the black holes are initially at rest and are described by Bowen-York type initial data. Our results suggest that black hole systems with perfectly symmetric configurations (e.g., black holes located at the vertices of a regular polygon or a regular polyhedron) impose strong constraints on the emitted gravitational waves, and no gravitational kick arises in these symmetric systems. For regular polygonal configurations, the radiation is dominated by the plus polarization of the $l=2$, $m=0$ mode. For regular polyhedral configurations, all the quadrupole modes with $l=2$ nearly vanish, and gravitational wave amplitudes are strongly suppressed to the order of $10^{-4}$ or below, which is in accordance with an analysis based on the quadrupole approximation. Gravitational radiations from $m \ne 0$ modes become non-negligible only when non-symmetric configurations are encountered.
  Keywords: Numerical Relativity, Black Holes, Gravitational Waves, N-Body Systems

</details>


### [104] [Anisotropic Fluid Model for Massive Boson Stars: Spherically Symmetric Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09432)
*Ethan McKeever,Victor Guedes,Shu Yan Lau,Kent Yagi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文放宽强耦合假设，提出一种球对称大质量玻色星（boson star）的唯象各向异性流体（anisotropic-fluid）模型，并给出可直接使用的拟合公式与Python实现。该方法在保持与完整玻色星计算高度一致的同时，将计算成本降低约10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色星是研究最充分的奇异致密天体（exotic compact objects）之一，也是少数可从第一性原理研究其形成机制与动力学行为的黑洞替代物（black-hole mimickers）。但其解构造通常比中子星（neutron star）解更困难，往往需要极精确的振荡频率初值或计算代价高昂的弛豫方法（relaxation methods）。既有强耦合极限下的完美流体（perfect-fluid）描述仅在强耦合条件下适用，限制了适用范围。

Method: 放宽强耦合假设，推导球对称大质量玻色星的唯象各向异性流体模型，使其在广泛模型参数范围内均适用。提供各向异性剖面（anisotropy profile）与有效状态方程（effective equation of state）的现成拟合，拟合自变量为中心标量场振幅与自相互作用耦合常数同标量场质量平方之比。验证时采用标准中子星构造方法计算玻色星质量与半径，并与完整玻色星计算对比。

Result: 两种方法给出的质量与半径结果高度一致（excellent agreement），而新方法的计算成本降低约一个数量级（约10倍）。模型适用于广泛的参数范围，并附带可直接使用的Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff求解器（Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff solver）的Python实现。

Conclusion: 该唯象流体框架为构造球对称大质量玻色星及计算其可观测量提供了高效且准确的方法。所公开的Python实现使用户可在该框架内直接构建玻色星构型，便于实际应用与推广。

Abstract: Boson stars are among the best-studied exotic compact objects and constitute one of the few black-hole mimickers for which both the formation mechanism and dynamical behavior can be investigated from first principles. Constructing boson star solutions is generally more challenging than constructing neutron star solutions, often requiring either extremely accurate initial guesses for the oscillation frequency or the use of computationally intensive relaxation methods to solve the field equations. In the strong-coupling limit, massive boson stars can be described effectively as perfect- fluid configurations, enabling their construction using methods analogous to those developed for neutron stars. In this paper, we relax the strong-coupling assumption and derive a phenomenological anisotropic-fluid model for spherically symmetric massive boson stars that remains applicable over a broad range of model parameters. We provide ready-to-use fits for both the anisotropy profile and the effective equation of state in terms of the central scalar-field amplitude and the ratio of the self-interaction coupling constant to the squared scalar-field mass. To validate the model, we compute the masses and radii of boson stars using a standard neutron-star construction approach and compare the results against full boson-star calculations. We find excellent agreement between the two methods, while the new approach reduces the computational cost by a factor of about 10. Our phenomenological fluid framework offers an efficient and accurate method for constructing spherically symmetric massive boson stars and computing their observable properties. We provide a ready-to-use Python implementation of the Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff solver, allowing users to construct boson star configurations within the proposed framework.

</details>


### [105] [Semiclassical Cosmological Constant in Anti-de Sitter and de Sitter Spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09436)
*Milton C. Mamani-Leqque*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本论文从两个互补视角研究四维反德西特（anti-de Sitter, AdS）与德西特（de Sitter, dS）时空中量子化标量场的半经典反作用（semiclassical backreaction）。结果显示量子贡献不能仅由局部曲率决定，并揭示了AdS与dS之间解析延拓（analytic continuation）映射的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探究半经典反作用如何修正有效宇宙学常数（effective cosmological constant），并检验经典层面AdS与dS之间的解析延拓关系能否延伸至量子层面。摘要指出该问题涉及量子态、边界条件与整体结构信息是否会影响反作用结果。

Method: 方法包含两条路径：其一，确定使给定几何仍为精确解、且裸宇宙学常数（bare cosmological constant）固定为经典几何值的自洽场参数；其二，在固定背景上保留重整化真空应力-能量张量（renormalized vacuum stress–energy tensor），考察其对有效宇宙学常数的贡献。AdS情形依赖可容许边界条件（admissible boundary conditions），dS情形则在Bunch–Davies态下求解；对无质量构型给出有效宇宙学常数的通用形式，并通过形式延拓ℓ→iℓ检验经典关系的量子对应。

Result: AdS自洽解依赖边界条件，dS解则依赖Bunch–Davies态，显示对整体与态相关信息的敏感性。无质量构型下有效宇宙学常数呈通用形式Λ_eff=A/ℓ²+B/ℓ⁴，可比较经典与量子贡献并识别相对重要性变化的特征曲率尺度（characteristic curvature scales）。在形式延拓ℓ→iℓ下，经典曲率项被正确映射，但量子贡献不能：最小耦合（minimal coupling）时AdS与dS系数比为29/61，共形耦合（conformal coupling）时二者大小相等、符号相反。

Conclusion: 这些差异表明半经典反作用并非仅由局部曲率决定，而保留量子态、边界条件与整体结构的信息。研究结果澄清了将解析延拓作为AdS与dS半经典量子理论之间映射的局限性。

Abstract: We investigate semiclassical backreaction of a quantized scalar field in four-dimensional anti-de Sitter (AdS) and de Sitter (dS) spacetimes from two complementary perspectives. First, we determine self-consistent field parameters for which the prescribed geometry remains an exact solution with the bare cosmological constant fixed to its classical geometric value. Second, we retain the renormalized vacuum stress--energy tensor on the prescribed background and study its contribution to the effective cosmological constant. In AdS, the self-consistent solutions depend on the admissible boundary conditions, while the corresponding dS solutions are obtained within the Bunch--Davies state, revealing sensitivity to global and state-dependent information. For massless configurations, the effective cosmological constant takes the universal form $Λ_{\rm eff}=A/\ell^{2}+B/\ell^{4}$, allowing comparison of classical and quantum contributions and identification of the characteristic curvature scales at which their relative importance changes. We then test whether the classical relation between AdS and dS extends to the quantum level through the formal continuation $\ell\rightarrow i\ell$. Although the classical curvature term is mapped correctly, the quantum contribution is not: for minimal coupling the AdS and dS coefficients are in the ratio $29/61$, while for conformal coupling they have equal magnitude and opposite sign. These discrepancies show that semiclassical backreaction is not determined by local curvature alone, but retains information about the quantum state, boundary conditions, and global structure. The results clarify the limitations of analytic continuation as a map between the semiclassical quantum theories of AdS and dS.

</details>


### [106] [Ringdown analysis of GW250114 with RingCWB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09503)
*Achal Kumar,Poulami Dutta Roy,Sergey Klimenko*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 利用 RingCWB 算法对迄今最响的引力波事件 GW250114 进行铃宕（ringdown）分析，测量主导四极模及其一阶泛音，所得频率与阻尼时间相对广义相对论（general relativity, GR）预言的偏差均与零一致，给出了对 GR 偏离的严格限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GW250114 是迄今探测到的最响双黑洞并合（binary black hole merger）引力波信号，其极高信噪比（signal-to-noise ratio）为精确检验爱因斯坦广义相对论、测量准正规模（quasi-normal modes, QNMs）与残余黑洞性质提供了难得机会。

Method: 采用 RingCWB 算法对 GW250114 开展铃宕分析，测量主导的 (2,2,0) 准正规模及其一阶泛音（first overtone），并约束其频率与阻尼时间相对 GR 预言值的分数偏差（fractional deviations）。

Result: 测得分数偏差为 δf₂₂₀ = 0.004₋₀.₀₂₃⁺⁰.⁰²² 与 δτ₂₂₀ = 0.010₋₀.₀₈₇⁺⁰.⁰⁹⁶，两者在给定不确定度内均与零一致；分析同时对残余自旋（remnant spin）给出紧致约束，结果与经由恒星演化（stellar evolution）形成的双黑洞相符。

Conclusion: 该类测量构成对广义相对论偏离的严格限制，表明现有数据与 GR 预言一致，并支持该事件源自恒星演化形成的双黑洞。

Abstract: The binary black hole merger GW250114, the loudest gravitational-wave signal detected to date, offers an opportunity to perform a precise test of Einstein's general relativity (GR). Its exceptionally high signal-to-noise ratio enables accurate measurements of the quasi-normal modes (QNMs) and properties of the remnant black hole. In this work, we present a ringdown analysis of GW250114 using the RingCWB algorithm. We measure the dominant $(2,2,0)$ QNM along with its first overtone and constrain the fractional deviations in frequency and damping time from their GR-predicted values, finding $δf_{220} = 0.004_{-0.023}^{+0.022}$ and $δτ_{220} = 0.010_{-0.087}^{+0.096}$ , both consistent with zero within the quoted uncertainties. These results represent stringent constraints on deviations from GR. The analysis also places tight constraints on the remnant spin, consistent with a binary black hole formed through stellar evolution.

</details>


### [107] [Black holes with conical infinity in gravitational caloric theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09606)
*S. X. Tian,Zong-Hong Zhu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究引力热量理论（gravitational caloric theory）在施瓦西（Schwarzschild）解之外是否存在球对称黑洞解。作者将纯径向热量场的真空方程化为三维自治动力系统，发现正则可渐近几何可为闵可夫斯基（Minkowski）型或锥形（conical）型，且在特定参数切面上存在具有锥形无穷远的非施瓦西黑洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力热量理论是否容许超出施瓦西解的球对称黑洞解，此前尚属未决问题；而一个良定义的黑洞解需同时具备正则视界（regular horizon）与延伸至无穷远的非奇异外部区域，因此需要对该问题作系统的全局分类研究。

Method: 将一般静态球对称度规中纯径向热量场（purely radial caloric field）的真空方程改写为三维自治系统（three-dimensional autonomous system），以此实现对解的全局分类；在锥形无穷远所要求的特定参数切面上求解黑洞解，并推导局部度规修正（local metric corrections），进而利用近拱点移动（periapsis shift）构造轨道检验。

Result: 正则可渐近几何为闵可夫斯基型或锥形型，后者要求模型参数满足特定关系。在该参数切面上，施瓦西解是唯一渐近平坦（asymptotically flat）的黑洞，同时存在具有锥形无穷远的非施瓦西黑洞；此类黑洞从近视界区到常规弱场区（如太阳系尺度）可任意接近施瓦西解，但在无穷远处保留量级为 1 的立体角亏损或盈余（solid-angle deficit or excess）。渐近立体角仅依赖于单一模型参数，与热量场振幅无关；在固定局域推断质量下，热量振幅设定锥形转变尺度（conical transition scale），场越弱转变越向外移。

Conclusion: 引力热量理论确实容许一类新的球对称黑洞解，其特征为锥形无穷远与量级为 1 的渐近立体角缺陷，且这些解在太阳系尺度内难以与施瓦西解区分。所导出的近拱点移动可用于构建针对锥形转变尺度的轨道检验。

Abstract: Whether gravitational caloric theory admits spherical black-hole solutions beyond Schwarzschild remains open. A well-defined black-hole solution requires both a regular horizon and a nonsingular exterior extending to infinity. To investigate this question, we cast the vacuum equations for a purely radial caloric field in a general static spherically symmetric metric into a three-dimensional autonomous system, which facilitates the global classification of solutions. We show that the regular asymptotic geometry can be Minkowski or conical, with the latter requiring a specific relation between the model parameters. Within this parameter slice, Schwarzschild is the only asymptotically flat black hole, whereas non-Schwarzschild black holes with conical infinity exist. These black holes can remain arbitrarily close to Schwarzschild from the near-horizon region to the conventional weak-field region (e.g., Solar System scales), yet retain an order-unity solid-angle deficit or excess at infinity. The asymptotic solid angle depends on a single model parameter and is independent of the caloric field amplitude. At fixed locally inferred mass, the caloric amplitude sets the conical transition scale, with weaker fields shifting the transition outward. We derive the local metric corrections and use the resulting periapsis shift to formulate an orbital test of the transition scale.

</details>


### [108] [Axial-metric/polar-gauge perturbations of the Maldacena-Milekhin-Popov wormhole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09847)
*Abhishake Sadhukhan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在宇称扇区中研究 Maldacena-Milekhin-Popov（MMP）虫洞的线性微扰，该扇区将轴式度规微扰与极式规范微扰耦合，并包含带电费米子最低朗道能级（lowest Landau level, LLL）的贡献。系统约化为三个封闭形式的耦合主方程，结果表明有质量模频移为实且负、中性通道无束缚态、在 r≫1/α 满足 a/l≳1/α 时该扇区在 O(α) 阶无增长模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 MMP 虫洞在含费米子反作用（backreaction）情形下的稳定性，需要考察轴式与极式混合的宇称扇区中规范场与引力模的耦合行为。先前无费米子时两宇称扇区等谱，本文旨在检验费米子引入后该性质及稳定性如何改变。

Method: 将规范分量 a_τ、a_ρ 视为每条磁力线世界面上的二维规范场，令费米子以精确 Schwinger 电流（Schwinger current）响应；提升到球面上后电模获得 MMP 屏蔽质量 m_S^2=αq^2。通过要求力平衡与电荷守恒确定伴随的极化电流（polarisation current，极式扇区 Hall 电流的对偶），从而在一阶费米子反作用 α 下闭合 Bianchi 恒等式，并将系统约化为三个耦合主方程，与无费米子的喉部（mouth）匹配。费米子本构关系由局域性、协变性与指标定理（index theorem）选定。

Result: 在 α=0 时喉部退化为质量 l(l-1) 与 (l+1)(l+2) 的 Pöschl-Teller 问题（m_S^2=0）；有限 m_S^2 时一个模被屏蔽、一个趋于质量 l^2+l+2、一个中性电荷密度波保持严格无质量。有质量正则频率的 O(α) 频移为实且负；两宇称扇区仅在无费米子时等谱。中性通道在每个喉部存在吸引区，与引力通道耦合的数值解在精确喉部势中未找到束缚态。

Conclusion: 在所考察的阶数与 l≥2 条件下，该宇称扇区不存在增长模（growing mode），表明费米子反作用未在此扇区引入不稳定性。

Abstract: We study linear perturbations of the Maldacena-Milekhin-Popov (MMP) wormhole in the parity sector that pairs axial metric perturbations with polar gauge perturbations. It contains the axial gravitational mode and the electric field along the magnetic field lines. The lowest Landau level (LLL) of the charged fermions responds through its charge. The gauge components $a_τ$, $a_ρ$ form a two-dimensional gauge field on the worldsheet of each field line, and the fermions respond with the exact Schwinger current. Lifted over the sphere, it gives the electric field the MMP screening mass $m_S^2=αq^2$, which is not small and survives in the mouth. In the throat the perturbation tilts the worldsheets, and the Casimir stress tensor acquires mixed components. Force balance then requires a current across the field lines, and charge conservation fixes an accompanying polarisation current, the counterpart of the Hall current of the polar sector. These currents close the Bianchi identities at first order in the fermionic backreaction $α$. The system reduces to three coupled master equations, given in closed form and matched to the fermion-free mouth. Consistency alone does not select the fermionic constitutive relation, but locality, covariance and the index theorem do. At $α=0$ the throat reduces to Poschl-Teller problems with masses $l(l-1)$ and $(l+1)(l+2)$ at $m_S^2=0$. At finite $m_S^2$ one mode is screened, one tends to the mass $l^2+l+2$, and one neutral charge-density wave stays exactly massless. The $O(α)$ shifts of the massive normal frequencies are real and negative. The two parity sectors are isospectral only without fermions. The neutral channel sees an attractive region in each mouth, and a numerical solution coupled to the gravitational channel in the exact mouth potential finds no bound state. The sector has no growing mode for $l\ge2$ at this order.

</details>


### [109] [Kerr geodesics on conformal projection diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09878)
*Przemysław Podleśny,Patryk Mach*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用 Chruściel、Ölz 与 Szybka 提出的投影图（projection diagrams），对最大延拓 Kerr 时空（maximally extended Kerr spacetime）中的类时测地线（timelike geodesics）给出了严格表示，并借助两套穿透视界的坐标系处理事件视界与柯西视界的穿越问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准共形图（conformal diagrams）要求因果曲线局限于与图关联的二维曲面内，难以刻画一般因果曲线。为在保留因果特征的前提下严格表示最大延拓 Kerr 时空中的类时测地线，需要引入可绘制一般因果曲线的投影图工具。

Method: 采用 Chruściel、Ölz、Szybka 引入的投影图，并使用两套穿透视界的坐标系（入射与出射 horizon-penetrating coordinates），以保证穿越事件视界（event horizon）与柯西视界（Cauchy horizon）的测地线获得正则描述；同时分析基于 Kruskal-Szekeres 型构造的因果块拼接方式。

Result: 在 Kruskal-Szekeres 型构造下，至多四个因果块（causal blocks）可被光滑拼接；由于共形因子（conformal factor）在块边界处或为零或发散，描述跨越多个因果块的轨迹可能需要多于一张图。

Conclusion: 投影图为最大延拓 Kerr 时空中的类时测地线提供了保留因果特征的严格表示手段，但受块边界处共形因子行为的限制，单一图难以覆盖跨越多个因果块的整体轨迹。

Abstract: We provide a rigorous representation of timelike geodesics in the maximally extended Kerr spacetime, using projection diagrams introduced by Chruściel, Ölz, and Szybka. In contrast to standard conformal diagrams, projection diagrams allow for plotting general causal curves (not necessarily confined to the two-dimensional surface associated with the diagram), preserving their causal characteristics. Our analysis utilizes two systems of horizon-penetrating coordinates (both ingoing and outgoing) to ensure a regular description of geodesics crossing the event and Cauchy horizons. On the other hand, at most four causal blocks can be joined smoothly by a Kruskal-Szekeres-type construction proposed for projection diagrams. Consequently, depicting a trajectory spanning several causal blocks may require more than one diagram, as the conformal factor either vanishes or diverges at the block boundaries.

</details>


### [110] [Acceleration radiation, quasinormal modes and quasi-bound states in generic rotating regular black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09955)
*Uktamjon Uktamov,Ali Övgün,Reggie C. Pantig,Bobomurat Ahmedov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文研究可分离类Kerr旋转度规（separable Kerr-like rotating metrics）中含非线性电动力学质量剖面时中性标量场的视界增亮加速辐射（horizon-brightened acceleration radiation），给出首个有限能隙修正与多种探测器协议下的热性、熵产生及准正规模验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在旋转且具非线性电动力学（nonlinear electrodynamics）质量剖面的度规中，视界附近的加速辐射与霍金型热性是否仍成立、其修正结构如何，尚需明确的解析框架。该文旨在用可分离度规与解析极值参数化区分视界热性、有限探测器协议与外部标量动力学。

Method: 对主支（M=0、μ=3）采用解析极值电荷—自旋参数化（extremal charge–spin parametrization）确定黑洞区域；在简单外视界将标量方程化为局域共形量子力学（local conformal quantum mechanics），共转能量为ε=ω−mΩ_H。以固有时间推导出射通道的首个有限能隙修正（含径向Frobenius系数、角向变化、测地线项与开关斜率），并用协议定义的细致平衡亲和度（detailed-balance affinity）替代坐标依赖的轨迹相位因子。同时分析受迫赤道圆轨道原子、随机原子注入的主方程，以及Chebyshev标量准正规模（quasinormal frequencies）计算与第一定律可积性检验。

Result: 出射标量方程的直接积分与赤道下落轨迹验证了在明确有限门（finite gate）下的展开；可解对数模极限（logarithmic-mode limit）将开关效应与几何贡献分离。共转与反转的超相对论运动给模式依赖的轨道响应，而非下落霍金亲和度。随机注入得到选定模式主方程与非负粒子数熵产生恒等式。第一定律可积性检验表明该离壳度规的视界面积记账仍是有条件的；独立收敛的Chebyshev准正规模频率验证了WKB结果并探测了超出主导视界数据的外部几何。

Conclusion: 计算区分了分支分辨的视界热性（branch-resolved horizon thermality）、有限探测器协议与外部标量动力学，识别出近极值径向尺度收缩并量化未选定第二径向支的影响，但未断言统一的极值极限。

Abstract: We study horizon-brightened acceleration radiation for a neutral scalar field in separable Kerr-like rotating metrics with nonlinear-electrodynamic mass profiles. The principal branch has $M=0$ and $μ=3$, with finite but direction-dependent central curvature limits. An analytic extremal charge--spin parametrization fixes the black hole domain. At a simple outer horizon, the scalar equation reduces to local conformal quantum mechanics with corotating energy $\epsh=ω-m\OmH$. For an isolated outgoing channel we derive the first finite-gap correction in proper time, including the radial Frobenius coefficient, angular variation, geodesic terms, and switching slope. This replaces a coordinate-dependent trajectory-phase multiplier by a protocol-defined detailed-balance affinity. Direct integration of the outgoing scalar equation and an equatorial infalling trajectory validates the expansion for an explicitly specified finite gate; a solvable logarithmic-mode limit separates switching from geometric contributions. We also analyze forced equatorial circular atoms and show that co-rotating and counter-rotating ultra-relativistic motion yields a mode-dependent orbital response rather than the infalling Hawking affinity. We identify the shrinking near-extremal radial scale and quantify the effect of an unselected second radial branch, without asserting a uniform extremal limit. Random atomic injection gives a selected-mode master equation and a nonnegative population entropy-production identity. A direct first-law integrability test shows why horizon-area bookkeeping remains conditional for this off-shell metric. Independently converged Chebyshev scalar quasinormal frequencies validate the WKB results and probe the exterior geometry beyond the leading horizon data. The calculation distinguishes branch-resolved horizon thermality, finite detector protocol, and exterior scalar dynamics.

</details>


### [111] [Geometric interpretation of electromagnetic memory and large gauge transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09960)
*Pujian Mao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文以带电粒子汇（charged-particle congruence）的光学数据（optical data）为工具，对电磁记忆效应（electromagnetic memory）作几何刻画。通过推导零无穷远处带电粒子的世界线偏离（worldline deviation），并构造相应光学数据，作者表明带电粒子汇的渐近剪切（asymptotic shear）可等价编码电磁记忆效应及其大规范变换（large gauge transformation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电磁记忆效应与大规范变换的既有描述多依赖场量本身，缺乏直接的几何图像。该研究旨在以带电粒子汇的渐近演化提供一种几何解释，从而统一理解记忆效应与相关大规范变换。

Method: 方法上，针对无质量（massless）与有质量（massive）粒子，分别推导其在零无穷（null infinity）附近的世界线偏离，并据此构造带电粒子汇的光学数据。

Result: 结果表明，带电粒子汇的渐近剪切（asymptotic shear）构成对电磁记忆效应及其伴随大规范变换的等价编码。

Conclusion: 由此建立电磁记忆效应与大规范变换在带电粒子汇渐近演化框架下的几何解释。

Abstract: We develop a geometric characterization of electromagnetic memory in terms of the optical data of charged-particle congruences. We derive the worldline deviation of charged particles near null infinity for both massless and massive particles and construct the corresponding optical data. We show that the asymptotic shear of the charged-particle congruence provides an equivalent encoding of electromagnetic memory and its associated large gauge transformation. This establishes a geometric interpretation of electromagnetic memory and large gauge transformations in terms of the asymptotic evolution of charged-particle congruences.

</details>


### [112] [Analytic parity-violating greybody factors of a dynamical Chern-Simons black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10280)
*Abhishek Rout*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在动力学陈-西蒙斯（dynamical Chern-Simons, dCS）引力框架下，解析研究了非旋转黑洞对引力波与赝标量波的低频散射，给出了引力子-赝标量转换概率与引力子灰体不对称性的精确闭式结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dCS引力中轴性（奇宇称）引力扇区与动力学赝标量不可约耦合，而极性扇区仍为广义相对论，这种宇称破缺（parity violation）可能通过吸收与模式转换留下可观测印记。研究旨在为将dCS宇称破缺与轴/极准正规模分裂相联系提供解析基准。

Method: 在无量纲耦合 zeta = (ell_CS/M)^4 的一阶展开下，采用匹配渐近展开（matched asymptotic expansions）求解低频散射问题，并与直接双通道积分进行交叉检验；确定 kappa_2 的一维积分在解析可控的超越基（transcendental basis）内高精度求值。

Result: 得到引力子到赝标量转换概率的精确闭式 d_2 = 3(pi^2-9)^2/8，以及四极（ell=2）通道引力子灰体（吸收）不对称性的高精度重构 kappa_2 = (21/80)pi^4 + (27/8)pi^2 - 89833/1536；两者均为 pi^4、pi^2 与有理数的组合，重构基中不含 ln2 或奇 zeta 项。透射相位扇区包含解析导出的 zeta(3)-9/8 静态积分，但其整体匹配前因子为数值确定。领头反射相位不对称性为零，故在低频领头阶反射波不印记偏振旋转，而透射相位仍保留次领头 O(omega zeta) 不对称性。归一化通量不对称性在几何光学极限下与 1/(M omega)^2 衰减数值一致。

Conclusion: 结果为 dCS 引力中吸收与模式转换的宇称破缺同轴性/极性准正规模（quasinormal-mode）分裂之间的联系提供了可用的解析目标。

Abstract: We study the low-frequency scattering of gravitational and pseudoscalar waves off a non-rotating black hole in dynamical Chern-Simons (dCS) gravity, where the axial (odd-parity) gravitational sector is irreducibly coupled to the dynamical pseudoscalar while the polar sector remains general relativistic. Working to first order in the dimensionless coupling zeta = (ell_CS/M)^4 and using matched asymptotic expansions, we obtain an exact closed form for the graviton-to-pseudoscalar conversion probability, d_2 = 3(pi^2-9)^2/8, and a high-precision reconstruction, in a proven transcendental basis, of the graviton greybody (absorption) asymmetry, kappa_2 = (21/80) pi^4 + (27/8) pi^2 - 89833/1536, for the quadrupole (ell=2) channel. Both reduce to combinations of pi^4, pi^2, and rational numbers, with no ln2 or odd-zeta terms in the reconstructed basis; the transmission-phase sector, by contrast, contains an analytically derived zeta(3)-9/8 static integral, while its overall matching prefactor is identified numerically. The leading reflection-phase asymmetry vanishes, so that no polarization rotation is imprinted on the reflected wave at leading low-frequency order, while a subleading O(omegazeta) asymmetry remains in the transmitted phase. The normalized flux asymmetries are numerically consistent with a 1/(Momega)^2 falloff in the geometric-optics limit, where the leading remaining flux effect is conversion loss. The matched-asymptotic results are checked against direct two-channel integration, while the one-dimensional integral determining kappa_2 is evaluated to high precision within an analytically controlled transcendental basis. The results provide analytic targets linking dCS parity violation in absorption and mode conversion to the axial/polar quasinormal-mode splitting.

</details>


### [113] [Charge-regular formulation and radiative amplitudes of Kerr-Newman perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10529)
*Hayato Motohashi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文建立了分离变量形式下 Kerr–Newman 微扰的电荷正则表述，并给出从主方程解到物理辐射场的显式映射，得到带电情形下引力与电磁辐射振幅及能流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有 Kerr–Newman 微扰研究多依赖零电荷或非正则化约定，难以一致地处理非零电荷与无穷远处的零电荷极限问题。

Method: 推导正则的角向与径向一阶系统及其二阶约化，构造自旋 ±1 与 ±2 Teukolsky 方程的有限电荷形变，并设定场与边界归一化以兼容标准 Kerr 约定。

Result: 对具有通常 peeling 行为的物理 Einstein–Maxwell 微扰，给出非零实频率下显式的外行引力与电磁振幅及能流；有限电荷辐射组成比仅依赖正则角向函数。

Conclusion: 有限电荷辐射组成比与径向激发无关，表明角向结构在此表述中决定辐射成分比例。

Abstract: We establish a charge-regular formulation of separated Kerr--Newman perturbations and an explicit map from master solutions to physical radiative fields. We derive regular angular and radial first-order systems and their second-order reductions, with finite-charge deformations of the spin $\pm1$ and $\pm2$ Teukolsky equations. The field and boundary normalizations recover the standard Kerr conventions while accounting for the nonuniform zero-charge limit at infinity. For physical Einstein--Maxwell perturbations with the usual peeling behavior, we obtain explicit outgoing gravitational and electromagnetic amplitudes and energy fluxes at nonzero real frequency. The resulting finite-charge radiation-composition ratios depend only on the regular angular functions, independently of the radial excitation.

</details>


### [114] [A weakly modelled view of the joint compact-binary mass plane: population structure and spectral-siren cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10535)
*Viola De Renzis,Alessandro Agapito,Michele Mancarella*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该工作提出一个用于致密双星联合源系-参考系成分质量分布（joint source-frame component-mass distribution）的灵活混合模型（mixture model），与宇宙学参数同步推断，并配套提出识别重构密度中结构、量化其后验稳定性、局部显著性与宇宙学相关性的框架；应用于 GWTC-5.0 后厘清了若干已知与新生结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有质量分布重构多聚焦于单维（尤其是主质量）投影，可能掩盖二维参数空间中的真实结构，也无法完整刻画谱-汽笛（spectral-siren）信息对宇宙学参数的约束能力。因此需要一个能够同时拟合二维成分质量分布与宇宙学、并可系统识别结构中哪些几何特征与宇宙学推断相关的通用工作流。

Method: 构建联合源系-参考系双星成分质量的灵活混合模型，与宇宙学参数同时推断；并开发结构识别框架，对重构密度中的特征给出后验稳定性、局部显著性（local prominence）与宇宙学相关性评估。将该流程应用于 GWTC-5.0，并与仅重建主质量的模型、以及强参数化的一维谱结构分析进行对比。

Result: 约 35 M⊙ 特征被判定为明确的近等质量峰（near-equal-mass peak），尽管其在主质量投影中解释含糊；约 (21,12) M⊙ 的较弱非等质量聚集被频繁恢复但显著性较低；约 60 M⊙ 处存在试探性联合跃迁（joint transition），出现在轻微非等质量处，并在主质量投影中表现更稳健。宇宙学方面，二维质量模型对 H0 的约束比仅灵活重建主质量的模型窄 33%，仅比强参数化一维谱结构分析宽约 10%。约 10 M⊙ 与约 35 M⊙ 聚集对 H0 的依赖主要通过一个共同尺度（对应两质量的整个 ~(1+z) 宇宙学重标定）实现，而中间的非等质量特征则保留沿正交质量比方向的残余依赖。

Conclusion: 总体而言，群体的显著性、能否被正式纳入天体物理特征目录、以及与宇宙学的相关性三者并不必然一致。结果提示，需采用真正的二维视角才能充分利用谱-汽笛信息；该流程可复现地用于探测群体结构并识别其几何中与谱-汽笛约束相关的部分，特征查找器亦直接适用于其他群体重构。

Abstract: We introduce a flexible mixture model for the joint source-frame component-mass distribution of compact binaries, inferred simultaneously with cosmology, together with a framework that identifies structures in the reconstructed density, quantifying their posterior stability, local prominence, and cosmological relevance. Applied to GWTC-5.0, the workflow clarifies several familiar and emergent structures. The $\sim35~M_\odot$ feature is classified as a clear near-equal-mass peak despite an ambiguous interpretation in the primary-mass projection. A weaker unequal-mass concentration near $(21,12)~M_\odot$ is recovered frequently but with lower prominence. A tentative joint transition near $60~M_\odot$ appears at mildly unequal masses, with a more robust manifestation in the primary-mass projection. For cosmology, the two-dimensional mass model constraint on $H_0$ is $33\%$ narrower than a model that flexibly reconstructs only the primary mass, and only $\sim~10\%$ wider than analyses based on strongly parameterized one-dimensional spectral structure. At the feature level, we show that the $\sim10~M_\odot$ and $\sim35~M_\odot$ concentrations depend on $H_0$ mainly through a common scale which captures the entire $\sim~(1+z)$ cosmological rescaling of both masses; the intermediate unequal-mass feature instead retains residual dependence in the orthogonal mass-ratio direction. More generally, we argue that population prominence, formal inclusion in an astrophysical feature catalogue, and cosmological relevance need not coincide. These results suggest that a genuinely two-dimensional view may be required to fully exploit spectral-siren information. Our workflow provides a reproducible way to detect population structure and identify which aspects of its geometry are associated with spectral-siren constraints. The feature finder is directly applicable to other population reconstructions.

</details>

### [115] [Compact toric Einstein four-manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10425)
*Mingyang Li,Song Sun*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文是环面引力瞬子（toric gravitational instanton）研究的续作，系统发展紧致单连通环对称爱因斯坦四维流形（compact simply-connected Einstein four-manifold with toric symmetry）理论，涵盖构造、拓扑刻画、唯一性与微分同胚分类，并改进 Hitchin-Thorpe 不等式 $\chi\geq3|\tau|$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 承接前作对环面引力瞬子的研究，旨在建立紧致单连通环对称爱因斯坦四维流形的系统理论，并解决其构造、分类与唯一性等基础问题。

Method: 采用局部粘合（local gluing）结合全局虚拟计数论证（global virtual counting argument），通过杆结构（rod structure）设计限制退化；利用 $W^+$ 的曲率恒等式进行拓扑刻画；借助 Einstein–Hilbert 泛函的变分研究证明唯一性。

Result: 构造出 $b_2$ 任意大的单连通正爱因斯坦四维流形；给出代数特殊环面爱因斯坦度量的拓扑刻画；证明在环面四维流形上代数特殊爱因斯坦度量在缩放与等距意义下唯一；以改进的 $\chi\geq3|\tau|$ 实现微分同胚分类，并恢复 Page 与 Chen–LeBrun–Weber 等经典度量。

Conclusion: 该理论统一了环对称爱因斯坦四维流形的构造、刻画与分类，并从圆球面出发经锥形爱因斯坦度量模空间（moduli space of conical Einstein metrics）恢复全部经典环面爱因斯坦度量。

Abstract: This is a continuation of our previous paper on toric gravitational instantons. We develop a systematic theory of compact simply-connected Einstein four-manifolds with toric symmetry. Applications include
  (1) The construction of simply connected positive Einstein four-manifolds with arbitrarily large $b_2$. Our method is based on local gluing combined with a global virtual counting argument. The key idea involves a particular design of rod structures to enable a gluing construction and restrict possible degenerations.
  (2) A topological characterization of algebraically special toric Einstein metrics. This uses special curvature identities for $W^+$ in the toric setting.
  (3) Uniqueness, up to scaling and isometry, on toric four-manifolds admitting algebraically special Einstein metrics. The proofs uses a variational study of the Einstein--Hilbert functional.
  (4) Diffeomorphism classification of compact toric Einstein four-manifolds in terms of an improved Hitchin-Thorpe inequality $χ\geq3|τ|$.
  In particular, our construction recovers all the classical toric Einstein metrics, including the Page and Chen--LeBrun--Weber metrics, starting from the round sphere through moduli spaces of conical Einstein metrics.

</details>


### [116] [Almost-splitting of spacetimes under timelike sectional curvature bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10051)
*Nicola Gigli,Melanie Graf,Robert J. McCann,Argam Ohanyan,Eric Woolgar,Matteo Zanardini*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在类时截面曲率（timelike sectional curvature）下界条件下，仿照 Cheeger–Colding 的黎曼几何结果，建立了洛伦兹时空的几乎分裂定理（almost-splitting theorem）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究受 Cheeger–Colding 著名黎曼几乎分裂定理的启发，旨在将这一思想推广至洛伦兹几何框架，并为 Beem–Ehrlich–Markvorsen–Galloway 的洛伦兹分裂定理提供定量版本。

Method: 考虑类时截面曲率下界为非正且接近零的常数的全局双曲时空（globally hyperbolic spacetime），假设其中存在一条长的极大类时线段（maximizing timelike segment），并采用 Minguzzi–Suhr 的洛伦兹 Gromov–Hausdorff 收敛（Lorentzian Gromov–Hausdorff sense）来比较该线段上的因果钻石（causal diamonds）与乘积度规时空中的对应结构。

Result: 若全局双曲时空满足上述曲率下界条件并包含长的极大类时线段，则该线段沿线的因果钻石在 Minguzzi–Suhr 意义下接近乘积度规时空中的因果钻石。

Conclusion: 该结果可视为具有非负类时截面曲率的时空之洛伦兹分裂定理的定量类比（quantitative analogue）。

Abstract: We establish an almost-splitting theorem for spacetimes under timelike sectional curvature bounds in the spirit of the celebrated Riemannian result due to Cheeger and Colding. More concretely, we show that if a globally hyperbolic spacetime with timelike sectional curvature bounded below by a nonpositive constant close to zero contains a long maximizing timelike segment, then causal diamonds along that segment are close in the Lorentzian Gromov--Hausdorff sense of Minguzzi--Suhr to those in a product metric spacetime. Our result can be interpreted as a quantitative analogue of the Lorentzian splitting theorem for spacetimes with nonnegative timelike sectional curvature by Beem--Ehrlich--Markvorsen--Galloway.

</details>

### [117] [Can LISA See Inside the Little Red Dots?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08964)
*Samuel Gómez Gómez,Xisco Jimenez Forteza,Carlos Palenzuela,Rosalba Perna*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文将极端质量比旋近（extreme-mass-ratio inspiral, EMRI）作为探测"小红点"（Little Red Dots, LRDs）内部气体环境的引力波探针，计算了不同LRD内部模型下10–30倍太阳质量黑洞的旋近波形，并用失配判据检验其与真空及相互之间的可区分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LRDs的中心环境存在多种理论模型（准星、辐射性黑洞包层、超爱丁顿吸积盘），它们对EMRI所探测半径处的气体密度预言相差数个量级，需要一个独立探针来判别这些模型。

Method: 对一系列LRD内部模型计算10–30倍太阳质量黑洞的旋近过程，纳入气体环境效应，并采用失配判据（mismatch criterion）检验所得波形能否与真空情形及彼此区分；同时结合JWST观测的LRD样本与LISA探测率估计。

Result: 稠密内部（Bondi型自由落体空腔与辐射包层）在几乎所有可探测事件中产生相对真空的可测偏差；对流准星内部在黑洞质量小于约10^5倍太阳质量且z小于约0.6时可测，共转盘则无可见印记。有利情形下各模型也可相互区分。z>3.9的14个JWST LRD均不可探测，但z<0.9的31个中有30个在中心黑洞质量比维里估计低约100倍时可探测。

Conclusion: 面向z=6以内的LRD族群，若LRD核以普通星系核的速率发生旋近，4年LISA任务对10^5倍太阳质量宿主与10倍太阳质量伴星可探测0.08–0.8个事件（30倍太阳质量伴星为1.5–15个）；在致密星团中因俘获为直接吞噬，该率降至约10^-4以下。

Abstract: The Little Red Dots (LRDs) are compact, broad-line sources that appear to contain accreting black holes (BHs) embedded in dense gas. Proposed models for their central environments (including quasi-stars, radiative BH envelopes and super-Eddington accretion discs) predict gas densities at the radii probed by an extreme-mass-ratio inspiral that differ by many orders of magnitude. We compute the inspiral of a $10$--$30\Msun$ BH for a range of LRD interior models, including environmental effects from the gas, and test with a mismatch criterion whether the resulting waveforms can be distinguished from vacuum and from one another. Dense interiors (Bondi-like free-fall cavities and radiative envelopes) produce measurable deviations from vacuum in essentially every detectable event. Convective quasi-star interiors are measurable for $\MBH\lesssim10^5\Msun$ and $z\lesssim0.6$, while a co-rotating disc leaves no measurable imprint. In favorable cases the models are also mutually distinguishable. None of the 14 JWST LRDs at $z>3.9$ would be detectable with a $10\Msun$ companion, but 30 of 31 LRDs at $z<0.9$ would be if their black holes are $\sim100$ times less massive than their virial estimates, and in many of them the interior's imprint would be measurable. Across the LRD population to $z=6$, a 4-yr LISA mission would yield $0.08$--$0.8$ detectable events for $10^5\Msun$ hosts and $10\Msun$ companions ($1.5$--$15$ for $30\Msun$) if LRD nuclei host inspirals at the rate of ordinary galactic nuclei. In dense clusters, where captures are plunges, this falls to $\lesssim10^{-4}$.

</details>


### [118] [Composition $g$-modes and $f$-modes of neutron stars with gravitationally coupled dark matter: degeneracy with the symmetry energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10007)
*Probit J Kalita,Bharat Kumar,H C Das*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该工作在全广义相对论（general relativity）框架下计算了仅通过引力耦合的费米子暗物质（fermionic dark matter）中子星的 g 模与 f 模，并结合 NICER 与 GW170817 数据给出暗物质质量分数上限，指出引力耦合图像下 g 模的响应远弱于单流体模型且主要反映核态方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组分引力模（composition gravity modes，g-modes）此前被提出作为探测中子星内暗物质的探针，但相关结论基于暗物质与中子处于化学平衡的单流体模型（one-fluid models）。本文旨在检验在暗物质仅通过引力与核子耦合的更一般情形下，该探针是否仍然有效。

Method: 在全广义相对论下计算含暗核恒星的本征 g 模与 f 模，其中费米子暗物质仅通过引力与核子耦合；采用包含 63 个核子态方程（equations of state，EOSs）的先验，涵盖 Skyrme 与相对论平均场泛函，包括 Dutra 等人的模型及 Sun、Bhattiprolu 与 Lattimer 的汇编；并据此从 NICER 与 GW170817 数据推断暗物质质量分数。

Result: 数据并不偏好暗物质，对粒子质量 0.5–2 GeV 情形给出质量分数上限 0.12–0.26（95% 置信度）。仅通过引力耦合时，暗物质只能借助引力场获得恢复 g 模的浮力；在固定 EOS 下，占总质量 10% 的暗核对基频 g 模的提升为 10–15%，比单流体图像小若干倍。更大的效应是间接的：含暗核恒星需要更硬的核子 EOS，而 g 模由对称能（symmetry energy）的密度依赖性决定，其频率反映的是恒星所需的 EOS 而非其所含暗物质；在固定半径与对称能下，占质量 10% 的暗核等价于斜率 L 改变 9–12 MeV。f 模行为不同，含暗核恒星会以约 1% 的幅度破坏 f 模与潮汐形变（tidal deformability）之间的关系，远超出核子星后验分布范围。

Conclusion: 在暗物质仅通过引力耦合的情形下，g 模对暗物质的直接敏感性显著弱于单流体模型，其频率主要受暗核所要求的核子 EOS 支配；相比之下，f 模-潮汐形变关系的破坏（约 1%）是更为明确的暗核信号，且现有数据不支持中子星中存在暗物质。

Abstract: Composition gravity ($g$) modes have been proposed as a probe of dark matter in neutron stars, on the basis of one-fluid models in which the dark matter is in chemical equilibrium with the neutrons. We computed in full general relativity the $g$- and $f$-modes of stars in which fermionic dark matter couples to the nucleons only through gravity, and inferred the dark mass fraction from NICER and GW170817 data with a prior that comprises $63$ nucleonic equations of state (EOSs) from Skyrme and relativistic mean-field functionals, among them the models of Dutra \textit{et al.} and of the compilation of Sun, Bhattiprolu and Lattimer. The data do not prefer dark matter and bound its mass fraction at $0.12 - 0.26$ ($95\%$) for particle masses of $0.5$--$2$~GeV. Coupled only through gravity, the dark matter reaches the buoyancy that restores a $g$-mode only through the gravitational field: a dark core holding $10\%$ of the mass raises the fundamental $g$-mode by $10-15\%$ at fixed EOS, several times less than in the one-fluid picture. The larger effect is indirect: a star with a dark core needs a stiffer nucleonic EOS, and since the $g$-mode is set by the density dependence of the symmetry energy, its frequency reflects the EOS that the star requires rather than the dark matter it contains; at fixed radius and symmetry energy a core holding $10\%$ of the mass is equivalent to a change of the slope $L$ of $9-12$~MeV. The $f$-mode, which follows the mean density, behaves differently: a dark core breaks the relation between the $f$-mode and the tidal deformability by about one percent, far outside the posterior spread of nucleonic stars.

</details>


### [119] [Quasi-periodic eruption spectral-timing: EMRIs crossing warped accretion disks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10110)
*Joheen Chakraborty,Andrew Mummery,Eliot Quataert,Riccardo Arcodia,Itai Linial,Erin Kara*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个联合光谱-时变（spectral-timing）拟合框架，将基于克尔测地频率（Kerr geodesic frequencies）与翘曲盘（warped disk）几何的解析QPE时变模型，与共享同一黑洞质量、自旋及盘参数的自辐照盘SED模型耦合，对GSN 069和eRO-QPE2进行拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准周期爆发（quasi-periodic eruptions, QPE）可能是极端质量比旋进（extreme mass-ratio inspiral, EMRI）中恒星质量伴星反复撞击超大质量黑洞（supermassive black hole, SMBH）吸积盘所致。爆发到达时间编码轨道及其相对论性进动，而宁静态光谱能量分布（spectral energy distribution, SED）探测同一盘-黑洞系统，二者可对同一轨道-盘系统提供两类独立约束。

Method: 构建联合光谱-时变框架，同时拟合解析QPE时变模型（基于克尔测地频率与翘曲盘几何）与自辐照盘SED模型，两者共享相同的SMBH质量、自旋和盘参数；并将其应用于QPE源GSN 069与eRO-QPE2。

Result: 两类源的QPE时变数据与宁静态光谱可被同时再现，为该系统中的EMRI模型与翘曲盘提供间接支持。但两个源的时变数据均较稀疏，均无法给出唯一解；在eRO-QPE2中识别出六种物理模式，它们对时变与SED的拟合效果相当。

Conclusion: 结果表明QPE光谱-时变分析有望成为精确探测SMBH、吸积盘及轨道伴星的工具，同时揭示稀疏采样QPE时变数据所导致的混叠（aliasing）复杂性，并因此呼吁更密集的观测活动以消除模式简并。

Abstract: Quasi-periodic eruptions (QPEs) are repeating X-ray transients from galactic nuclei potentially caused by a stellar-mass orbiter repeatedly colliding with the accretion disk of a supermassive black hole (SMBH) in an extreme mass-ratio inspiral (EMRI). In this picture, the eruption arrival times encode the orbit and its relativistic precessions, while the quiescent spectral energy distribution (SED) probes the same disk and SMBH, providing two independent constraints on the same orbit-disk system. We present a joint spectral-timing framework that takes advantage of this by simultaneously fitting an analytic QPE timing model built on Kerr geodesic frequencies and a warped disk geometry, coupled to a self-irradiated disk SED model sharing the same SMBH mass, spin, and disk parameters. We apply it to the QPE sources GSN 069 and eRO-QPE2, showing that the QPE timings and quiescent spectra can be reproduced simultaneously in each, providing circumstantial support for the EMRI model and warped disks in these systems. However, given the sparse timing data in both sources, neither admits a unique solution. In eRO-QPE2, we identify six physical modes which reproduce the timings and SED comparably well, motivating denser observing campaigns to identify one unambiguously. Our results demonstrate both the promise of QPE spectral-timing as a precision probe of SMBHs, their accretion disks, and orbiting companions, and the aliasing subtleties of sparsely sampled QPE timings.

</details>

*BESIII Collaboration,M. Ablikim,M. N. Achasov,P. Adlarson,X. C. Ai,C. S. Akondi,R. Aliberti,A. Amoroso,Q. An,Y. H. An,M. S. Anderson,Y. Bai,O. Bakina,H. R. Bao,X. L. Bao,M. Barbagiovanni,V. Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于北京谱仪III（BESIII）采集的约101亿$J/\psi$事件，首次观测到电磁达利茨衰变（electromagnetic Dalitz decay）$J/\psi\to e^+e^-\eta_c$，并测量其相对分支比及形状因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究针对$J/\psi$的电磁达利茨衰变过程$J/\psi\to e^+e^-\eta_c$，此前尚无实验观测，旨在首次确证该衰变道并检验相关理论预言。

Method: 利用在$e^+e^-$对撞机BEPCII上以BESIII探测器采集的$(10.087\pm0.044)\times10^9$个$J/\psi$事件进行分波/事例分析，测定相对分支比$R\equiv\Gamma(J/\psi\to e^+e^-\eta_c)/\Gamma(J/\psi\to\gamma\eta_c)$，并提取依赖动量转移平方$q^2$的形状因子（form factors）。

Result: 首次观测到$J/\psi\to e^+e^-\eta_c$；测得相对分支比$R=(0.65\pm0.02_{\rm stat.}\pm0.07_{\rm sys.})\%$（第一项为统计误差，第二项为系统误差）；提取了$q^2$相关形状因子，未发现与理论预言有显著偏离。

Conclusion: $J/\psi\to e^+e^-\eta_c$电磁达利茨衰变被首次实验确认，其相对分支比与形状因子测量结果与理论预期一致，为进一步研究该过程提供了实验依据。

Abstract: Using $(10.087\pm0.044)\times10^9$ $J/ψ$ events collected with the BESIII detector at the $e^+e^-$ BEPCII collider, we present the first observation of the electromagnetic Dalitz decay $J/ψ\to e^+ e^- η_c$. The relative branching fraction $R \equiv \frac{Γ(J/ψ\to e^+e^-η_c)}{Γ(J/ψ\to γη_c)}$ is determined to be $(0.65\pm0.02_{\rm stat.}\pm0.07_{\rm sys.})\%$, where the first uncertainty is statistical and the second systematic. The $q^2$-dependent form factors are also extracted, and no significant deviation from the theoretical prediction is seen.

</details>


### [121] [Green's functions and weighting fields for dielectric multilayer geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09961)
*Werner Riegler*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该论文推导了在由两个接地平面（grounded planes）限定的、含任意数量不同介电常数（permittivity）平行层的几何结构中，点电荷（point charge）所产生的静电势（electrostatic potential），并讨论了横向边界条件，进而用于推导分段电极（segmented electrodes）的加权场（weighting fields）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多平行介电层结构（如条带与焊盘组成的分段平面）中的静电势解析解在电极加权场计算中具有需求，而一般性多层解尚需给出。论文旨在为此类几何构型提供统一的解析处理。

Method: 在两侧接地平面约束下，对含任意数量不同介电常数平行层的几何结构求解点电荷静电势，并讨论横向方向（transverse direction）的不同边界条件；对于接地平面被分割为条带（strips）与焊盘（pads）的情形，利用该解推导相应电极的加权场。

Result: 给出了任意层数、不同介电常数平行层结构中的点电荷静电势解，并据此得到分段电极（条带与焊盘）的加权场表达式。

Conclusion: 所提出的解析解可推广至任意多层介电结构，并为分段平面电极的加权场计算提供通用方法。

Abstract: The electrostatic potential of a point charge in a geometry with an arbitrary number of parallel layers of different permittivity, bounded by two grounded planes, is derived. Different boundary conditions in the transverse direction are discussed. For the case where a grounded plane is segmented into strips and pads, the solution is used to derive the weighting fields for these electrodes.

</details>
