### [122] [Chiral Extensions of Cotangent Yangian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10202)
*Keyou Zeng*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文利用顶点代数的广群（vertex algebroid）构造余切杨氏代数（cotangent Yangian）的手征扩张，在零能级与非零能级分别给出顶点代数及其相容余积，并计算球面单点共形块（conformal block）上的结合代数结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确陈述研究动机，仅说明构造目标为余切杨氏代数的手征扩张（chiral extension），并将其与 Abedin–Niu 的余切杨氏代数相衔接。

Method: 采用顶点广群（vertex algebroids）作为构造工具，建立余切杨氏代数的顶点代数扩张；零能级构造给出带相容余积的顶点代数，其 Zhu 约化（Zhu reduction）还原 Abedin–Niu 的余切杨氏代数；同时在非零能级构造扩张并研究能级可加余积（level-additive coproduct）。

Result: 零能级顶点代数具有两种自然拟共形结构（quasi-conformal structure）；计算了相应的球面单点共形块，并在各块空间上赋予由余积诱导的结合乘积。第一种拟共形结构下，块空间可等同于某李广群同调（Lie algebroid homology）的对偶；第二种结构下，用迷你扭量几何（mini-twistor geometry）描述块代数及其经典极限。

Conclusion: 顶点广群方法可系统实现余切杨氏代数的手征扩张，并在零能级与非零能级均获得带余积的顶点代数结构；所得共形块代数可通过李广群同调对偶或迷你扭量几何加以刻画。

Abstract: We construct chiral extensions of the cotangent Yangian using vertex algebroids. At zero level, the construction yields a vertex algebra with a compatible coproduct, whose Zhu reduction recovers the cotangent Yangian of Abedin and Niu. We also construct vertex algebra extensions at non-zero levels and study level-additive coproducts. The zero-level vertex algebra admits two natural quasi-conformal structures. We compute the corresponding sphere one-point conformal blocks and equip each block space with an associative product induced by the coproduct. For the first quasi-conformal structure, the blocks space can be identified with the dual of a certain Lie algebroid homology; for the second, we describe the block algebra and its classical limit in terms of mini-twistor geometry.

</details>


<div id='physics.optics'></div>

# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [Bogoliubov-de Gennes spectrum, Cherenkov radiation and recoil of solitons with third-order dispersion underlying optical analogue horizons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09814)
*Hasan Oguz*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文在不假设热辐射定律（thermal emission law）的前提下，计算三阶色散（third-order dispersion）所致弱辐射孤立波的三个性质：线性化谱结构、稳态切伦科夫辐射（Cherenkov emission）速率，以及渐近通道的普查界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三阶色散使非线性薛定谔方程（nonlinear Schrödinger equation）的孤子——光纤中事件视界（event horizon）光学类比的基础——变为弱辐射孤立波，故需在不预设热发射律的情况下刻画其辐射性质。

Method: 围绕色散方程的定常态（stationary state）而非可积剖面进行线性化（Bogoliubov-de Gennes）分析，采用周期网格；由孤立波后方辐射通量测量绝热制备孤子的稳态切伦科夫发射，跨八个数量级；并对渐近通道作普查，配合流动的运动学模型与缩放的色散系数。

Result: 绕可积剖面线性化产生收敛于网格与箱、随色散强度平方根增长且为伪模的复四元组；绕定常态零模扇区为两个尺寸为二的若尔当块（Jordan block），工作点谱为实，含共振支的负能箱模。测得辐射与漂移孤子同帧相位匹配，一阶速率比实测低 29 至 45 倍；以缩放色散系数表达的实测速率可描述三种振幅的发射孤子，结合范数与动量平衡、无额外拟合参数，重现其后坐与损耗率的缓慢衰减；弱色散下发射孤子的损耗即其发射失配的脱落。长波长上游发射要求孤子化学势（被漂移孤子的随动帧多普勒位移降低）位于分析带内，从而给出孤子振幅上界；流动的运动学模型中表面重力有闭式解，热关系及其上的纠缠为该模型的外加输入。

Conclusion: 三阶色散下孤子的辐射性质可在不诉诸热发射律的条件下由定常态线性化谱、稳态辐射通量测量与渐近通道普查确定；该构造适用于无拉曼的克尔介质（Raman-free Kerr media）。

Abstract: Third-order dispersion turns the soliton of the nonlinear Schrödinger equation, which underlies optical analogues of event horizons in fibers, into a weakly radiating solitary wave. We compute three of its properties without assuming a thermal emission law. First, the linearized (Bogoliubov-de Gennes) problem must be posed about the stationary state of the dispersive equation: linearizing about the integrable profile produces a complex quartet that is converged in grid and box, grows as the square root of the dispersion strength and is spurious. About the stationary state, on a periodic grid, the zero-mode sector consists of two Jordan blocks of size two and the spectrum is real at the operating point, including negative-energy box modes of the resonant branch. Second, we measure the steady Cherenkov emission of adiabatically prepared solitons from the flux of the radiation behind them, over eight decades. The radiation is phase-matched in the frame of the drifting soliton, and the first-order rate falls short of the measured one by factors of 29 to 45. Expressed through a scaled dispersion coefficient of the local soliton, the measured rate describes launched solitons of three amplitudes; with norm and momentum balance, and no further fitted parameter, it reproduces their recoil and the slow decay of their loss rate; at weak dispersion the loss of a launched soliton is the shedding of its launch mismatch. Third, a census of the asymptotic channels shows that long-wavelength upstream emission requires the chemical potential of the soliton, lowered by the comoving-frame Doppler shift of a drifting soliton, to lie inside the analysis band, which bounds the soliton amplitude. In a kinematic model of the flow the surface gravity has a closed form; the thermal relation and the entanglement built on it are imposed inputs of that model. The construction applies to Raman-free Kerr media.

</details>


### [124] [Compact Fully Integrated Alignment-Free and Linearly Polarized Ho$^{3+}$ Single-Frequency Waveguide Laser](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09435)
*Jeswin Jacob,T Toney Fernandez,Dale Otten,Ori Henderson-Sapir,3 Karen Privat,David Lancaster*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 首次报道了全集成Ho³⁺掺杂氟化物玻璃波导激光器，集成飞秒激光刻写波导与波导布拉格光栅，实现2062.21 nm单频激光输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向中红外集成单频激光源的需求，寻求紧凑、稳健且免对准（alignment-free）的集成化平台。

Method: 在Ho³⁺掺杂氟化物玻璃（fluoride glass）中，采用飞秒激光刻写波导（femtosecond laser-inscribed waveguide）并集成波导布拉格光栅（waveguide Bragg grating），构成12.4 mm紧凑腔体。

Result: 实现2062.21 nm处稳定单频激射，线宽优于5 MHz；输出为线偏振光，并通过SM1950单模光纤高效传输。

Conclusion: 该免对准架构为中红外集成单频激光源提供了紧凑且稳健的平台。

Abstract: We report, for the first time, a fully integrated Ho$^{3+}$-doped fluoride glass waveguide laser incorporating a femtosecond laser-inscribed waveguide and waveguide Bragg grating. The compact 12.4 mm cavity achieves stable single-frequency lasing at 2062.21 nm with a line width of better than 5 MHz. The laser emits linearly polarized light, which is efficiently delivered through an SM1950 single-mode fiber. This alignment-free architecture provides a compact and robust platform for integrated mid-infrared single-frequency laser sources.

</details>


### [125] [Vector beams for real-time monitoring of crystalline symmetry on μm length scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09481)
*Yongbo Lv,Zidu Yu,Haotian Zhang,Liejia Qian,Hao Chu*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作利用矢量光束（vector beam）实现非旋转斜入射装置，在直径约 2 μm 的样品区域上以 > 10 fps 实时可视化晶体对称性，反馈延迟与焦斑尺寸均较传统高斯光束加机械旋转方案改善一个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体对称性决定其物理性质，但常规确定手段依赖大型衍射装置与复杂分析软件，无法实时反馈；基于光学各向异性（optical anisotropy）的台式方案（如二次谐波产生，second harmonic generation）虽灵敏，却需逐角度旋转散射面并配合偏振态，导致装置笨重、反馈延迟大、焦斑 > 20 μm，难以用于少层二维材料等微区样品。

Method: 采用矢量光束实现无需机械旋转的斜入射（oblique-incidence）测量装置，替代传统高斯光束（Gaussian beam）配合机械旋转台逐角度扫描散射面与偏振态的方式，从而获取晶体的非线性极化率（nonlinear susceptibility）。

Result: 在约 2 μm 直径样品区域实现 > 10 fps 的晶体对称性实时可视化；反馈延迟与焦斑尺寸相较此前基于高斯光束与机械旋转台的设计均提升一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 矢量光束大幅简化并降低了非线性光学各向异性测量的成本，有望在应变监测（strain monitoring）与晶体取向对准（crystal alignment）等科研与工业场景中广泛应用，并推动结构光场（structured optical field）与固体物理及凝聚态物理研究的更广融合。

Abstract: Symmetry constrains and determines the physical properties of crystalline solids. Determining crystalline symmetry often requires large-scale diffraction facility and advanced analysis software and therefore cannot be realized with real-time feedback. Alternatively, optical anisotropy has also been exploited for elucidating the rotation, mirror and inversion symmetries of solids on smaller tabletop setups. Second harmonic generation, in particular, has demonstrated a remarkable sensitivity to subtle changes in these symmetries during phase transitions. To access the full nonlinear susceptibility of a crystal in a cryogenic or vacuum environment, angle-by-angle rotation of the scattering plane of an obliquely incident and outgoing beam in co-ordination with their polarization states is required. This typically results in a bulky and wobbly setup, significant feedback latency, and a focal spot size > 20 μm unsuitable for microscopic samples such as few-layer 2D materials. In this work, we make use of vector beams to realize a non-rotating oblique-incidence setup, demonstrating a real-time (> 10 fps) visualization of crystalline symmetry on a sample area of ~ 2 μm in diameter. Both the feedback latency and focal spot size represent more than an order of magnitude improvement over previous designs based on a Gaussian beam and mechanically-rotating stages. Here, the use of a vector beam greatly simplifies and reduces the cost of nonlinear optical anisotropy measurements, heralding widespread applications of this approach in both research and industrial settings, for example, for strain monitoring and crystal alignment. Our work may also promote broader integration of structured optical field into solid state and condensed matter research in the future.

</details>


### [126] [Phonon-Enabled Collective Strong Coupling of Color Centers in a Plasmonic Cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09716)
*Bharti Garg,Prabhdeep Singh,Masoud Taleb,Nahid Talebi*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 六方氮化硼（hBN）量子发射体在室温下与等离激元金空腔（plasmonic Au void cavity）耦合，通过阴极发光（CL）与光致发光（PL）光谱观测到空腔介导的发射体相互作用，其中443 nm发射体的振动电子跃迁与腔模杂化产生约36.2 meV（CL）与约24.1 meV（PL）的分裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: hBN量子发射体具有室温明亮发光特性，是固态量子光子学（solid-state quantum photonics）的潜在构件，但其与晶格振动（lattice vibrations）的强烈相互作用机制及多发射体间耦合调控尚待厘清。研究旨在探索振动电子跃迁（vibronic transition）能否成为控制混合等离激元体系中多量子发射体相互作用的有效途径。

Method: 采用互补的阴极发光（cathodoluminescence, CL）与光致发光（photoluminescence, PL）光谱技术，在室温下研究耦合至同一等离激元金空腔（plasmonic Au void cavity）的hBN量子发射体；并结合Maxwell-Bloch数值模拟复现实验光谱，以及激发功率依赖的PL测量。

Result: CL测量显示443 nm发射体的一个振动电子跃迁与腔模杂化，产生可光谱分辨的分裂，而邻近的537 nm发射体尽管存在光谱重叠仍处于弱耦合（weak-coupling）区。CL峰分裂约36.2 meV，PL分辨峰约24.1 meV，差异归因于每次电子激发下多发射体的集体激发（collective excitations）及相互相互作用。Maxwell-Bloch模拟复现了实验光谱并确认分裂源于腔诱导杂化（cavity-induced hybridization）。

Conclusion: 激发依赖的PL表明杂化态可通过零声子跃迁（zero-phonon transition）以下的多声子辅助激发（phonon-assisted excitations）进入。结果表明振动电子跃迁为在混合等离激元纳米光子系统中控制多个量子发射体之间的相互作用提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Quantum emitters in hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) are promising building blocks for solid-state quantum photonics because of their bright room-temperature emission. These emitters strongly interact with lattice vibrations, which partially underpins their excitation efficiency. Here, using complementary cathodoluminescence (CL) and photoluminescence (PL) spectroscopy, we demonstrate cavity-mediated interactions between hBN quantum emitters coupled to the same plasmonic Au void cavity at the room temperature. Cathodoluminescence measurements reveal that a vibronic transition of the 443 nm emitter hybridizes with the cavity mode, giving rise to a spectrally resolved splitting, while the neighboring 537 nm emitter remains in the weak-coupling regime despite spectral overlap. The energy split of the CL peak is at the order of 36.2 meV, whereas the PL-resolved peak is approximately 24.1 meV, which is due to the collective excitations of multiple emitters per electron excitation and their mutual interactions. Maxwell-Bloch simulations reproduce the experimental spectra and identify the observed splitting as the result of cavity-induced hybridization. Excitation-dependent PL further shows that the hybridized state can be accessed through phonon-assisted excitations below the zero-phonon transition. These results demonstrate that vibronic transitions provide an effective pathway for controlling interactions between multiple quantum emitters in hybrid plasmonic nanophotonic systems.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mtrl-sci'></div>

# cond-mat.mtrl-sci [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Thermal Diffusivity in Metallic Nanowire Aerogels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08874)
*Madeline A. Maben,WLNC Namila Liyanage,Cameron S. Jorgensen,Erin Marlowe,Kai Liu,Gerd Duscher,Dustin A. Gilbert*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过冷冻铸造（freeze casting）制备密度为 20-500 mg cm⁻³ 的 Ag 与 Cu 纳米线气凝胶，并利用锁相热成像（lock-in thermography）在连续机械压实过程监测热输运。结果表明热扩散率（thermal diffusivity）强烈依赖纳米线直径，且随密度增加反常降低近 300 倍，主要源于热扩散率公式中的厚度项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属纳米线气凝胶（metallic nanowire aerogels）兼具金属功能特性与气凝胶高孔隙率，但其在机械压实下的热输运行为尚不明确。研究旨在探究纳米线直径与密度如何影响此类材料的热输运，尤其是验证"更致密材料导热更高效"这一常规预期是否适用。

Method: 采用冷冻铸造（freeze casting）合成 Ag 和 Cu 纳米线气凝胶，密度范围为 20-500 mg cm⁻³。在连续机械压实（mechanical compaction）过程中使用锁相热成像（lock-in thermography）进行原位表征，并据此计算热扩散率与拟合输运系数。

Result: 热输运强烈依赖纳米线直径，30 nm Ag 纳米线的热扩散率显著低于 60 nm 纳米线，可能源于线径接近 Ag 中电子平均自由程（electron mean free path）时表面散射（surface scattering）增强。机械压实提高密度后，计算得到的热扩散率反而下降近 300 倍，与常规预期相反；该下降主要由热扩散率公式中的厚度项主导，拟合的输运系数变化较小。

Conclusion: 压实过程中特征热扩散时间尺度（thermal diffusion timescale）仅小幅下降，表明致密化主要压缩孔隙体积而未从根本上重构导电纳米线网络，行为类似于拓扑保持型致密化（topology-preserving densification）。因此纳米线气凝胶不能总被视为均质块体介质（homogeneous bulk media），其宏观几何与导电网络在压实中演化方式不同。

Abstract: Metallic nanowire aerogels combine the functional properties of metals with the high porosity of aerogels. Here, Ag and Cu nanowire aerogels with densities of 20-500 mg cm-3 were synthesized by freeze casting and investigated using lock-in thermography during sequential mechanical compaction. Thermal transport depended strongly on nanowire diameter, with 30 nm Ag nanowires exhibiting substantially lower thermal diffusivity than 60 nm wires, likely due to enhanced surface scattering as the wire diameter approaches the electron mean free path in Ag. Surprisingly, increasing the aerogel density through mechanical compaction reduced the calculated thermal diffusivity by nearly 300x, opposite the conventional expectation that denser materials transport heat more efficiently. Further analysis showed that this decrease is dominated by the thickness term in the thermal diffusivity formalism, while the fitted transport coefficient changes comparatively little. The characteristic thermal diffusion timescale decreased only modestly during compaction, indicating that densification primarily collapses pore volume without fundamentally restructuring the conductive nanowire network. This behavior resembles topology-preserving densification, where large geometric compression produces comparatively small changes in the effective percolative transport pathway. These results show that nanowire aerogels cannot always be treated as homogeneous bulk media, because their macroscopic geometry and conductive network evolve differently during compaction.

</details>


### [128] [Probing magnetic interactions in real space via magnetic pair distribution function analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08957)
*Edison P. Carlisle,Benjamin A. Frandsen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种基于平均场理论（mean-field theory）并引入昂萨格反应场（Onsager reaction field）的方法，用于从磁对分布函数（magnetic pair distribution function, mPDF）分析结果中提取磁交换相互作用（magnetic exchange interactions）参数，并在多种材料上验证其可得到与文献相当的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定磁交换相互作用对理解材料磁行为至关重要，但传统方法依赖单晶非弹性中子散射（inelastic neutron scattering）测量长程有序基态的自旋波色散。交换作用的影响并不局限于有序态自旋波，也支配包括顺磁区（paramagnetic regime）短程自旋关联在内的所有磁态；已有研究表明顺磁体能量积分漫散射（diffuse neutron scattering）数据可恢复交换参数，说明短程关联蕴含丰富信息。

Method: 将磁对分布函数（mPDF）分析（直接在实空间探测短程磁关联）与引入昂萨格反应场（Onsager reaction field）的稳健平均场理论（mean-field theory）方法相结合，用后者从 mPDF 分析结果中确定交换相互作用参数。

Result: 该方法成功应用于若干相关材料，所恢复的交换相互作用数值与文献报道值相当（comparable to literature values）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，结合昂萨格反应场的平均场方法可从 mPDF 分析中稳健地提取交换参数，为利用关联顺磁体（correlated paramagnets）的短程磁关联信息确定交换相互作用提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Determining magnetic exchange interactions is vital for understanding the magnetic behavior of materials. The standard method for exchange interaction determination involves performing inelastic neutron scattering experiments on single crystals to measure spin wave dispersions from a long-range-ordered magnetic ground state. However, the influence of exchange interactions is not limited exclusively to spin waves in ordered states, but also governs magnetic behavior in all magnetic states, including the short-range spin correlations that persist into the paramagnetic regime. Exchange parameters have been successfully recovered in the past from energy-integrated diffuse neutron scattering data from paramagnets, indicating that the diffuse scattering and underlying short-range magnetic correlations are rich in information. Consequently, magnetic pair distribution function (mPDF) analysis, which directly probes short-range magnetic correlations in real space, is expected to be well suited for extracting exchange parameters from correlated paramagnets. Here, we demonstrate a robust mean-field theory approach incorporating the Onsager reaction field for determination of exchange parameters from the results of mPDF analysis. We successfully apply this approach to several relevant materials to recover exchange interaction values comparable to literature values.

</details>


### [129] [Analytical Prediction of Buckling Limits in Isotropic Pentamode Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09158)
*M. Verbicaro,F. Braghin,R. V. Craster*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文建立了通用解析框架，用于确定预应力二维与三维各向同性五模式晶格（pentamode lattices）的屈曲极限，区分整体与局部两种失稳机制，并给出临界压力的闭式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 五模式晶格以单一变形模式主导弹性响应，但其余变形模式刚度较低，在压预应力（compressive prestress）下稳定性受限，从而制约其力学性能；因此需要明确其屈曲极限的判定方法。

Method: 针对具有一般连接几何（generic link geometries）的预应力二维与三维各向同性五模式晶格，构建通用解析框架；考虑两种失稳机制——与有效介质（effective medium）失稳相关的整体失稳，以及晶格单元尺度有限波长模式的局部失稳；推导相应临界压力的闭式表达式，并以两者中较低者作为屈曲极限；通过多种五模式晶格的数值模拟进行验证。

Result: 解析预测与所考虑构型的数值模拟结果吻合良好；结果表明，逼近五模式极限（pentamode limit）与保持足够抗屈曲能力之间存在权衡（trade-off）。

Conclusion: 合理的连接设计可在保持所需五模式行为的同时显著提高屈曲极限；该框架为预应力五模式晶格的稳定性评估与设计提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Pentamode lattices are architected materials characterized by an elastic response dominated by a single deformation mode. The low stiffness of the other deformation modes, however, may result in limited stability under compressive prestress, thereby limiting their mechanical performance. In this work, we develop a general analytical framework to determine the buckling limits of prestressed 2D and 3D isotropic pentamode lattices with generic link geometries. Two distinct instability mechanisms are considered: a global instability associated with the loss of stability of the effective medium, and a local instability associated with finite-wavelength modes at the scale of the lattice cell. Closed-form expressions for the corresponding critical pressures are derived, and the buckling limit is obtained as the lower of the two. The analytical predictions are validated through numerical simulations of different PM lattices, showing good agreement over the configurations considered. The results reveal a trade-off between approaching the pentamode limit and maintaining sufficient buckling resistance, but also show that appropriate link designs can substantially increase the buckling limit while retaining the desired pentamode behavior.

</details>


### [130] [Quasicrystalline oxide interfaces by twisting SrTiO3 membranes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09222)
*Shivasheesh Varshney,Sooho Choo,Huan Liu,In Hyeok Choi,Nicolas Gauquelin,John Mangeri,Seung Gyo Jeong,Edwin Dollekamp,William Sandholt,Andrea Roberto Insinga,Nikolas Vitaliti,Bridget R. Denzer,Bonnie Y. X. Lin,Andrew T. Danbury,Majid Mohseni,Uditha Mihiranga Jayathilake,Sharup Sheikh,Lidia A. Lapinski,Jonas M. Hansen,Seungjun Lee,Dong Kyu Lee,Jitin Sathish Kumar,Shivam Sharma,Rishi Raj,Jay Shah,Alevtina Smekhova,Florian Kronast,Juan Maria García Lastra,K. Andre Mkhoyan,Tony Low,James M. LeBeau,Alexander X. Gray,Jo Verbeek,Keith A. Nelson,Nini Pryds,Richard D. James,Bharat Jalan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究报告了一种构筑强键合准晶界面（quasicrystalline interface）的确定性路径：通过生长、释放、扭转并键合 SrTiO3 外延膜（epitaxial membranes），分别实现八重对称的八角准晶界面与十二重对称的十二角准晶界面，并观测到电子重构与非线性光学响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准晶（quasicrystal）证明长程有序可无需平移周期性而存在，但如何确定性地设计强键合准晶界面仍未被探索。该工作旨在提供可扩展的界面工程途径，并将准周期性（quasiperiodic order）与界面键合、轨道重构及非线性光学响应耦合。

Method: 方法上先在牺牲层（sacrificial layer）上生长外延 SrTiO3 膜，释放后按预设角度扭转并键合到选定衬底；双层扭 45° 形成八角界面，三层依次旋转 30° 形成十二角界面。表征手段包括选区与纳米束电子衍射、多层电子叠层成像（multislice electron ptychography）、原子分辨暗场成像、傅里叶分析、实空间铺砌（tiling）及电子能量损失谱（electron energy-loss spectroscopy）；并辅以基于机器学习原子间势（machine-learned interatomic potentials）的原子模拟、极化相关 X 射线吸收谱与太赫兹场诱导二次谐波圆二色性。

Result: 多种衍射、成像与傅里叶/铺砌分析共同确认了长程准晶有序；退火后电子能量损失谱显示电子重构。原子模拟表明八角有序在双层扭转角趋近 45° 时出现。1.5 nm 扭转双层中极化相关 X 射线吸收谱显示与扭转角相关的电子响应；太赫兹诱导二次谐波圆二色性在 45° 准晶条件下识别出对称性依赖的非线性光学响应。

Conclusion: 扭转氧化物膜（twisted oxide membranes）提供了可扩展的途径来工程化准周期有序，并将其与复杂氧化物中的界面键合、轨道重构和光学非线性响应相耦合。

Abstract: Quasicrystals reveal that long-range order can exist without translational periodicity, but deterministic routes for designing strongly bonded quasicrystalline interfaces remain unexplored. Here, we show that such interfaces can be engineered by growing epitaxial SrTiO3 membranes on a sacrificial layer, releasing them, twisting them by prescribed angles, and bonding them onto a chosen substrate. SrTiO3 bilayers twisted by 45° form an octagonal quasicrystalline interface with eight-fold rotational symmetry, whereas trilayers assembled with successive 30° rotations form a dodecagonal interface with twelve-fold symmetry. Selected-area and nano-beam electron diffraction, multislice electron ptychography, atomic-resolution dark field imaging, Fourier analysis, and real-space tiling establish long-range quasicrystalline order with electronic reconstruction evident from electron energy-loss spectroscopy after annealing. Atomistic simulations based on machine-learned interatomic potentials show that octagonal order emerges as the bilayer twist angle approaches 45°. Polarization-dependent X-ray absorption spectroscopy in 1.5 nm twisted bilayers reveals a twist-dependent electronic response, and terahertz-field-induced second-harmonic generation circular dichroism identifies a symmetry-dependent nonlinear optical response at the 45° quasicrystalline condition. Twisted oxide membranes therefore provide an extendable route to engineer quasiperiodic order and couple it to interfacial bonding, orbital reconstruction, and nonlinear optical response in complex oxides.

</details>


### [131] [Bottom-Up Prediction of Amorphous Poly(1,3-dioxolane) using Ab Initio Reactive Machine-Learning Force Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09388)
*Yizhi Song,Bharatha K. Gunawardana,Kriti Alam,Teresa Shah,Deepa Ranabhat,Hsin-Yu Ko*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究采用 AI 加速的从头算自下而上聚合物结构预测（AI$^{2}$-BPSP）框架，模拟实验条件下聚 1,3-二氧戊环（pDXL）的活性阳离子开环聚合，预测其非晶凝聚相微观结构，并将预测的 X 射线与中子衍射信号在增长链与周围单体之间进行分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: pDXL 可化学回收，并可通过活性阳离子开环聚合精确控制链长至超高分子量（UHMW）区间而获得增强的力学性能；但其凝聚相为非晶态，仅凭衍射测量无法解析其背后的原子尺度微观结构，因此需要理论预测手段。

Method: 应用 AI$^{2}$-BPSP 框架，使用在范德华修正的杂化密度泛函理论（van der Waals-corrected hybrid density functional theory）内训练的机器学习力场（machine-learning force fields），模拟实验合成条件下 pDXL 的活性聚合过程，从而预测其微观结构系综。

Result: 将预测的 X 射线与中子衍射信号在增长链与周围单体之间进行划分后，其主特征被解析为两个相互抵消的模式：位于 $q \approx 1.5$ Å$^{-1}$ 附近、随单体消耗而衰减的单体贡献，以及位于 $q \approx 1.6$ Å$^{-1}$ 附近、在低聚物中不存在且随链长增长而增强的聚合物贡献。

Conclusion: 该工作为将此类具有统计显著性、基于第一性原理、且按链长分辨的衍射预测与实验进行直接精度验证奠定了基础。

Abstract: Poly(1,3-dioxolane) (pDXL) is a chemically recyclable polyether that can be synthesized by living cationic ring-opening polymerization with precise control of chain length into the ultra-high-molecular-weight (UHMW) regime, where it acquires enhanced mechanical properties. Like most polymers, however, it has amorphous condensed-phase structure, and diffraction measurements alone cannot resolve the atomistic microstructure that underlies them. In this work, we predict the ensemble of the pDXL microstructures by applying a recently developed AI-accelerated ab initio bottom-up polymer structure prediction (AI$^{2}$-BPSP) framework that simulates the living polymerization of pDXL under experimental synthetic conditions with machine-learning force fields trained within van der Waals-corrected hybrid density functional theory. Partitioning the predicted X-ray and neutron diffraction signal between the growing chain and the surrounding monomer resolves its dominant feature into two counteracting modes: a monomer contribution near $q \approx 1.5$ Å$^{-1}$ that decays as monomer is consumed, and a polymer contribution near $q \approx 1.6$ Å$^{-1}$ that is absent in oligomers and grows with chain length. This work paves the way to direct accuracy validation of these statistically significant, first-principles, and chain-length resolved diffraction predictions against experiments.

</details>


### [132] [Deep learning driven framework for optimization of polycrystalline microstructures under competing strength requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09461)
*Ashwini Gupta,Indrashish Saha,Lori Graham-Brady,Tamer A. Zaki*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出深度学习驱动的多晶微结构优化框架，用 3D U-Net 代理模型（surrogate model）直接预测板撞击（plate-impact）速度场历史，并结合随机微结构生成与无导数优化（derivative-free optimization），在保证精度前提下将评估时间降低约四个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向力学性能提升的多晶微结构计算设计需在随机形貌上反复进行高保真模拟，传统晶体塑性有限元（crystal plasticity finite element, CPFE）方法成本过高，难以支撑优化迭代。

Method: 构建 3D U-Net 代理模型，将多晶铜微结构直接映射到板撞击模拟的全场速度历史，保留评估动态性能所需的空间与时间分辨率；代理与随机微结构生成及无导数优化耦合，并引入基于最大均值差异（maximum mean discrepancy, MMD）的分布一致性可靠域（distribution-consistent reliability region, DCRR）监测优化过程中的分布偏移。

Result: 在包括层裂强度（spall strength）与屈服强度（yield strength）的约束优化及逆向设计（inverse design）等多个设计问题上，最优设计经 CPFE 验证预测误差均小于 3%，代理驱动优化相较直接基于 CPFE 的优化将累积评估时间减少约四个数量级。

Conclusion: 该框架面向多晶微结构的层裂与屈服强度设计得到验证，可推广至高保真全场模拟代价过高、但空间与时间分辨响应信息对评估与优化候选设计必不可少的一类优化问题。

Abstract: Computational design of polycrystalline microstructures for enhanced mechanical performance requires repeated high-fidelity simulations over stochastic morphologies, rendering conventional crystal plasticity finite element (CPFE) approaches prohibitively expensive for optimization. Here, we develop a deep learning-driven framework for optimizing polycrystalline microstructures under competing quasi-static and dynamic performance requirements. A 3D U-Net surrogate maps polycrystalline copper microstructures directly to full-field velocity histories from plate-impact simulations, preserving the spatial and temporal resolution needed to evaluate dynamic performance and interrogate underlying wave interactions and failure mechanisms. The surrogate is coupled with stochastic microstructure generation and derivative-free optimization to efficiently navigate the design space. To monitor surrogate reliability during optimization, we introduce a distribution-consistent reliability region (DCRR) based on maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) to quantify distributional shift from the training data. The framework is demonstrated for multiple design problems, including constrained optimization of spall and yield strengths and inverse design. The optimal designs are validated using CPFE simulations with less than 3% prediction error across all optimization cases, while the surrogate-driven optimization reduces cumulative evaluation time by approximately four orders of magnitude compared with direct CPFE-based optimization. Although demonstrated here for the design of polycrystalline microstructures targeting spall and yield strengths, the framework is broadly applicable to optimization problems where high-fidelity full-field simulations are prohibitively expensive but spatially and temporally resolved response information is essential for evaluating, interpreting, and optimizing candidate designs.

</details>


### [133] [Optimization-Based Thickness Estimation and Depth-Resolved FIB-Induced Damage Characterization via Multislice Electron Ptychography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09465)
*Keun-Yeol Park,Chuqiao Shi,Murat Tuna Pamuk,Sooyoung Cheong,Seonyu Lee,Juhui Oh,Yu-Tsun Shao,Celesta S. Chang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究评估联合贝叶斯优化（Bayesian optimization）能否从单套四维扫描透射电镜（4D-STEM）数据中获得物理有意义的局部厚度估计。在楔形硅样品上，优化结果重现了与电子能量损失谱（EELS）一致的厚度变化趋势，并偏移10-17%。利用优化参数，深度分辨多层电子叠层成像（MEP）进一步区分了晶态硅与FIB诱导的非晶表层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量电子显微术需准确知晓样品厚度，因为动力学散射会强烈影响图像衬度和衍射强度。在多层电子叠层成像中，厚度是前向模型的必需输入参数，但透射电镜薄片的局部厚度往往未知；且厚度与探针离焦和切片数相互耦合，难以从重构本身直接确定。

Method: 采用贝叶斯优化对厚度、探针离焦（probe defocus）和切片数（number of slices）进行联合优化，以从4D-STEM数据中获得局部厚度的物理估计。方法与电子能量损失谱测量结果进行比较，并用于楔形硅薄片的深度分辨重构。

Result: 优化重现了与EELS相似的局部厚度变化，相对EELS估计存在10-17%的系统偏移。使用优化后的重构参数，深度分辨MEP在同一重构体积内将晶态硅内部与FIB诱导的非晶表层分离。最终铣削电压为2、5、8、30 kV的区域分别得到3.2、5.0、7.0、26.4 nm的非晶层厚度，随铣削电压单调增加，且与前人截面测量较为一致。

Conclusion: 在单套4D-STEM数据集上结合MEP与贝叶斯优化，可同时提供局部厚度估计与FIB诱导损伤的深度分辨表征，为更自洽、厚度感知的定量4D-STEM分析提供了途径。

Abstract: Quantitative electron microscopy requires accurate knowledge of specimen thickness because dynamical scattering strongly affects image contrast and diffraction intensities. In multislice electron ptychography (MEP), specimen thickness is a required input parameter to its forward model, yet the local thickness of a transmission electron microscope (TEM) lamella is often precisely what is unknown. Moreover, thickness is coupled to probe defocus and the number of slices, making its determination from the reconstruction itself nontrivial. Here, we assess whether joint Bayesian optimization of these parameters can provide a physically meaningful estimate of local thickness from four-dimensional scanning transmission electron microscopy (4D-STEM) data. Applied to a wedge-shaped silicon lamella, the optimization reproduced similar local thickness variation measured by electron energy loss spectroscopy (EELS), with a systematic offset of 10-17% relative to the EELS estimates. Using the optimized reconstruction parameters, depth-resolved MEP further separated the crystalline silicon interior from focused ion beam (FIB)-induced amorphous surface layers within the same reconstructed volume. Regions milled at final voltages of 2, 5, 8, and 30 kV yielded amorphous-layer thicknesses of 3.2, 5.0, 7.0, and 26.4 nm, respectively, increasing monotonically with milling voltage and agreeing reasonably with previous cross-sectional measurements. These results show that applying MEP coupled with Bayesian optimization on a single 4D-STEM dataset can provide both local thickness estimates and depth-resolved characterization of FIB-induced damage, offering a route toward more self-consistent, thickness-aware quantitative 4D-STEM analysis.

</details>


### [134] [Vanishing Altermagnetism and Emergent Ferromagnetism at the Two-Dimensional Limit of CrSb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09664)
*Yunlong Liu,Zixuan Wu,Qinxi Liu,Yupeng Zhi,Mingqiang Ren,Xue Jiang,Canli Song,Ding Zhang,Xucun Ma,Huimin Zhang,Jijun Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究在 SrTiO3(001) 衬底上以分子束外延（molecular beam epitaxy, MBE）生长双原子层厚度 CrSb(0001) 薄膜，发现单双层膜呈室温铁磁（ferromagnetism）且具强垂直磁各向异性，而双双层膜铁磁性消失，与体相 CrSb 的交变磁性（altermagnetism）基态形成鲜明对比，揭示维度与表面终止对磁序的调控作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁体（altermagnets）兼具自旋劈裂电子态与零净磁化，被视为自旋电子学器件（spintronic devices）的潜在平台，但维度降低如何重塑其磁序尚不清楚。论文旨在探究超薄极限下 CrSb 磁序的演化机制。

Method: 采用分子束外延（molecular beam epitaxy）在 SrTiO3(001) 衬底上直接生长双原子层厚度（bilayer-thickness）的 CrSb(0001) 薄膜，并结合密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）计算分析表面与层间交换作用的来源。

Result: 单双层（one-bilayer）薄膜表现出室温铁磁性与强垂直磁各向异性（perpendicular magnetic anisotropy）；双双层（two-bilayer）薄膜中铁磁性消失，明显偏离体相 CrSb 的交变磁基态。DFT 计算表明，暴露的 Cr 表面引发电荷轨道重排（orbital rearrangement），增强层内铁磁交换并抑制层间反铁磁耦合，从而驱动从交变磁性到铁磁性的维度交叉（dimensional crossover）。

Conclusion: 研究确立维度（dimensionality）与表面终止（surface termination）为磁序的有效调控手段，并为自旋电子学应用中工程化超薄磁性态（ultrathin magnetic states）提供平台。

Abstract: Altermagnets offer a promising route to spintronic devices by combining spin-split electronic states with zero net magnetization, but how reduced dimensionality reshapes their magnetic order remains poorly understood. Here, we grow bilayer-thickness CrSb (0001) films directly on SrTiO3(001) substrates by molecular beam epitaxy and uncover an unexpected thickness-dependent magnetic transition. The one-bilayer films exhibit room-temperature ferromagnetism with strong perpendicular magnetic anisotropy, whereas ferromagnetism vanishes in two-bilayer films, contrasting sharply with the altermagnetic ground state of bulk CrSb. Density functional theory calculations reveal that the exposed Cr surface induces orbital rearrangement that strengthens intralayer ferromagnetic exchange while suppressing competing interlayer antiferromagnetic coupling, driving the dimensional crossover from altermagnetism to ferromagnetism. These findings establish dimensionality and surface termination as powerful controls of magnetic order and provide a platform for engineering ultrathin magnetic states for spintronic applications.

</details>


### [135] [Ab-initio Investigation on h-Be3N2 Monolayer for Photocatalytic Hydrogen Evolution Reaction and Oxygen Evolution Reaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09719)
*Karan Patel,Hetvi Jadav,Himanshu Pandey*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文采用第一性原理密度泛函理论系统研究二维h-Be3N2单层的结构稳定性、电子性质与光催化水分解性能，揭示其带隙、能带边位置及溶液pH与氢覆盖度对HER/OER效率的关键影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于评估h-Be3N2单层作为可见光驱动光催化水分解材料的可行性，并服务于太阳燃料绿色制氢与国家绿色氢能使命的目标。

Method: 方法为基于第一性原理密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）的计算，采用PBE泛函进行电子结构计算，并考察能带边对齐、溶液pH条件以及表面氢覆盖度（H coverage）等对反应的影响。

Result: 结果显示，h-Be3N2为带隙1.74 eV的直接带隙半导体，适合可见光光催化；在中性与碱性环境中具备可实现整体水分解的氧化还原电位，而在酸性条件下其价带位置足以驱动OER并表现为光电阴极行为。中性pH下HER需施加0.77 V外电位；OER仅在强碱性条件下热力学有利，中性pH需0.43 V外电位。此外，表面H覆盖量显著影响HER效率。

Conclusion: 结论指出，溶液pH与光生载流子对h-Be3N2单层的光催化效果具有显著影响，其优良的光催化能力使其有望成为未来太阳能绿色制氢的可行候选材料，并有助于推动高效可持续的氢能生产。

Abstract: In this work, we have systematically investigated the structural stability, electronic properties, and photocatalytic performance of the 2D h-Be3N2 monolayer for water splitting application, utilizing a first-principles density functional theory. Electronic structure calculations based on the PBE functional reveal that h-Be3N2 is a direct band-gap semiconductor with a band gap value of 1.74 eV, making it well suited for visible-light-driven photocatalysis. Furthermore, the alignment of band-edges illustrates that h-Be3N2 has suitable redox potential that facilitate overall photocatalytic water splitting in neutral and alkaline environment. Conversely, in acidic condition, the position of valence band is an adequate to enable the OER, thereby leading to photocathodic behavior. At neutral pH, the HER process is still possible with the application of an external potential of 0.77 V. For the OER process, it is thermodynamically favourable only under strongly alkaline conditions, while an external potential of 0.43 V is needed to promote the reaction at neutral pH. These results clearly underscore the significant impact of solution pH and photogenerated charge carriers on the photocatalytic effectiveness of the h-Be3N2 monolayer. Furthermore, the study of H coverage indicates that the amount of H that adheres to the surface has a considerable impact on the efficiency of the HER in h-Be3N2. The favourable photocatalytic capabilities of the h-Be3N2 monolayer imply that it might be a viable option for upcoming solar-powered green hydrogen generation and may assist in realizing the goals of the National Green Hydrogen Mission by facilitating effective and sustainable hydrogen production.

</details>


### [136] [Anomalous Hall enhancement in fully compensated non-collinear antiferromagnetic Cr-doped Mn3Sn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09727)
*Ying Zhang,Xin Chen,Hang Xie,Ziyan Luo,Yihong Wu,Lei Shen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: Cr掺杂可在保持Mn3Sn共面反三角反铁磁序的前提下增强其反常霍尔输运，并在宽温区内稳定该反铁磁相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非共线反铁磁体（non-collinear antiferromagnet, AFM）Mn3Sn具有超快自旋动力学与可忽略的杂散场，适用于快速、节能的自旋电子学器件，但其霍尔响应较铁磁体偏小，限制了实际应用。

Method: 结合第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）与实验，考察Cr掺杂对Mn3Sn反常霍尔输运的影响；并通过贝里曲率（Berry curvature）与Wannier哈密顿量分析阐明其微观机制。

Result: 计算揭示存在有效Cr掺杂窗口，可增强反常霍尔电导并基本保持母体的共面反三角反铁磁序；贝里曲率热点（hotspots）经能带能量对齐、局域带间带隙及Mn来源轨道权重的协同变化而被放大。实验证实反常霍尔电导增强的预测，同时Cr掺杂抑制了低温下向螺旋相或自旋玻璃相的转变。

Conclusion: Cr掺杂是一种既能稳定反铁磁序、又能在宽温度范围内改善Mn3Sn反常霍尔输运性能的有效策略。

Abstract: The non-collinear antiferromagnet (AFM), Mn3Sn, exhibits ultrafast spin dynamics and negligible stray fields, making it a promising candidate for fast and energy-efficient spintronic devices. However, its practical applications remain limited by the relatively small Hall response compared with ferromagnets. Here, we demonstrate through both first-principles calculations and experiments that Cr-doping is a promising strategy to enhance the anomalous Hall transport in Mn3Sn without breaking the antiferromagnetic order. Our first-principles calculations reveal an effective Cr-doping window that enhances the anomalous Hall conductivity while largely preserving the host coplanar inverse triangular AFM order. Berry curvature and Wannier Hamiltonian analyses further reveal that Cr substitution amplifies the pre-existing Berry curvature hotspots through coordinated changes in the energy alignment, local interband gap, and Mn-derived orbital weight of the relevant bands. Experimentally, we have found that Cr doping not only supports our computational predictions (enhancement of the anomalous Hall conductivity), but also suppresses the transition to helical or spin-glass phases at low temperatures. These findings demonstrate that Cr-doping is an effective strategy for stabilizing the AFM order while simultaneously improving the anomalous Hall transport properties over a wide temperature range in Mn3Sn.

</details>


### [137] [Electronic Properties of Ladder-Type Phenylenes Studied by Thermally-Assisted-Occupation Density Functional Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09783)
*Tsu-Hsuan Wang,Jeng-Da Chai*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究采用热辅助占据密度泛函理论（TAO-DFT）系统考察梯型苯撑（ladder-type phenylenes, n-LPs）的电子性质，发现随尺寸增大其基态由非自由基（nonradical）向多自由基（polyradical）转变，并呈现阻尼周期振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有自由基（radical）特征的扩展π共轭体系（π-conjugated systems）的电子性质精确预测仍是重大理论挑战，需借助能处理强电子相关的计算方法加以突破。

Method: 采用近年来发展的热辅助占据密度泛函理论（thermally-assisted-occupation density functional theory, TAO-DFT），对含n个稠合四元碳环的梯型苯撑（n-LPs，n = 1 ~ 40）进行系统计算，并结合活性轨道（active orbitals）的实空间表征分析其电子行为。

Result: 所有研究的n-LPs均具有单重态（singlet）基态；随尺寸增大，体系由小尺寸的非自由基特征过渡为大尺寸的多自由基特征；自由基特征呈现明显的阻尼周期振荡（damped periodic oscillation），在n = 3k − 1（k为正整数）处出现高度相关的热点（hotspots）。

Conclusion: 活性轨道的实空间表征表明，该全局电子行为由活性轨道沿中心梯型骨架的结构局域化所主导；研究为n-LPs尺寸依赖的电子性质提供基础认识，并为其在未来纳米材料应用中的理性设计提供指导。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of the electronic properties of extended $π$-conjugated systems with radical character remains a major theoretical challenge. To overcome this challenge, thermally-assisted-occupation density functional theory (TAO-DFT) has been developed in recent years. In this study, we employ TAO-DFT to systematically explore the electronic properties of ladder-type phenylenes ($n$-LPs), containing $n$ fused four-membered carbon rings. Our calculations reveal that all investigated $n$-LPs (with $n$ = 1 ~ 40) possess singlet ground states. With increasing $n$-LP size, we observe a transition from the nonradical character of smaller $n$-LPs to the polyradical character of larger $n$-LPs. Notably, we identify a distinct damped periodic oscillation in the radical character of $n$-LPs, featuring highly correlated hotspots at specific intervals ($n = 3k - 1$, where $k$ are positive integers). The real-space representation of active orbitals confirms this global electronic behavior is governed by the structural localization of active orbitals along the central ladder framework. These findings provide fundamental insights into the size-dependent electronic properties of $n$-LPs, guiding their rational design for future nanomaterial applications.

</details>


### [138] [Origins of Universal Machine Learning Force-Field Errors in Multicomponent Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09837)
*Hongwei Du,Dingyang Lv,Baole Wei,Yu Ren,Feng Yu,Xin He,Bonan Zhu,Yongda Huang,Yongheng Li,Jianjun Liu,Siqi Shi,Hong Wang,Ziheng Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究构建了7,599个受高熵设计、元素替换与阴离子混合启发的多组分构型基准，评估了11个预训练机器学习力场（machine learning force field, MLFF）模型相对于密度泛函理论（DFT）的精度，并将评估扩展至弹性、振动与吸附相关性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器学习力场向多组分环境的泛化能力尚缺乏充分评估，而这类环境正是成分设计（compositional design）的核心场景，因此需要系统基准来检验其可靠性与适用范围。

Method: 构建7,599个多组分构型基准，用DFT对11个预训练模型的能量、力与应力进行评估，并延伸至弹性、振动、吸附性质；从训练参考覆盖度、局部几何异质性、距离方向性与元素响应四个角度分析力误差，并进行参数匹配（parameter-matched）下的表示比较。

Result: 到训练参考环境的距离与误差增大呈定性关联，但相同距离下误差仍有显著离散；高误差组局部几何异质性更强，OMat24对这些环境覆盖较广；相对训练参考对中位数，压缩侧误差陡升、拉伸侧上升较弱，匹配元素对与距离偏差后压缩侧力误差为拉伸侧的1.81-1.95倍；压缩下模型预测的成对相互作用曲线曲率更大；独立元素体系的拟合难度与电子能带能量对原子位移及费米能级移动的响应相关；最大阶数为2和4的球谐表示可分别在43个元素中的38和40个上降低测试力误差。

Conclusion: 研究结果为实验成分设计中的力场选择提供依据，并指出训练数据采样与模型表示方面的改进目标。

Abstract: Universal machine learning force-field generalization to multicomponent environments generated by compositional design remains insufficiently assessed. We construct a benchmark of 7,599 multicomponent configurations inspired by high-entropy design, elemental substitution and anion mixing. Eleven pretrained models are evaluated against density functional theory for energies, forces and stresses, with assessment extended to elastic, vibrational and adsorption-related properties. Force errors are analysed through training-reference coverage, local geometric heterogeneity, distance directionality and elemental response. Distances to training-reference environments reveal a qualitative association between coverage differences and increasing errors, while substantial variation remains at similar distances. Higher-error groups show greater local geometric heterogeneity, although OMat24 provides broad coverage of these environments. Relative to training-reference pair medians, errors remain low near the median, rise steeply on the compression side and increase more weakly on the extension side. After matching element pairs and absolute distance deviations, compression-side force errors are 1.81-1.95 times extension-side errors. Model-predicted pairwise interaction curves show greater curvature under compression. Fitting difficulty in independent elemental systems correlates with electronic band-energy responses to atomic displacements and Fermi-level shifts, and a similar pattern is observed in multicomponent systems. In parameter-matched comparisons, spherical-harmonic representations with maximum degrees of 2 and 4 lower test force errors for 38 and 40 of 43 elements, respectively, while differences in elemental difficulty remain. These findings inform force-field selection for experimental compositional design and identify targets for training-data sampling and model representations.

</details>


### [139] [Screening of tensor properties by magnetic point group symmetries: The PythMPG code package](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10024)
*Andrea Urru,Turan Birol,Trey Cole,David Vanderbilt*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文介绍了 PythMPG 代码包，用于基于（磁性）点群对称性对张量性质进行筛选，判断各磁性点群允许哪些由 Jahn 符号表征的张量及其独立分量数目。其设计受 Bilbao 晶体学服务器 MTENSOR 工具启发，但实现方式为自洽（self-contained），并可给出示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在凝聚态物理（condensed matter physics）中，理解允许特定现象或物理效应存在的对称性要求，对识别可呈现这些效应的候选材料至关重要；因此，基于（磁性）点群对称性对张量性质进行筛选具有特别的价值。

Method: 提出 PythMPG 代码包，用于执行对称性分析（symmetry analysis），以自洽方式判断每个磁性点群（magnetic point group）允许哪些张量，以及每个张量具有多少对称性允许的独立分量；张量以 Jahn 符号（Jahn symbols）表征。文中还描述了代码结构，并给出若干示例。

Result: 摘要未提供定量结果或性能评估，仅说明代码可确定每个磁性点群允许的张量及其对称性允许的独立分量数目，并给出了若干用于说明的示例。

Conclusion: PythMPG 是一款面向凝聚态物理张量性质筛选的自洽代码包，受 Bilbao 晶体学服务器 MTENSOR 工具启发，可用于确定磁性点群允许的张量（以 Jahn 符号表征）及其独立分量数目。

Abstract: Understanding the symmetry requirements that permit specific phenomena or physical effects is crucial in condensed matter physics for identifying candidate materials that exhibit one or more of these effects. To this end, screening tensor properties based on (magnetic) point-group symmetries is of particular interest. Here, we present the PythMPG code package, a tool designed for this purpose. Inspired by the MTENSOR utility of the Bilbao Crystallographic Server, PythMPG performs symmetry analysis in a self-contained way to determine which tensors, characterized by their Jahn symbols, are allowed for each magnetic point group and how many symmetry-allowed independent components each tensor possesses. We describe the structure of the code and present some illustrative examples.

</details>


### [140] [Solphin: Photovoltaic efficiency analysis for bulk materials using Python](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10028)
*Philippa U Cox,Peter P Russell,Andrea Crovetto,Alexander G Squires,Louie Slocombe,David O Scanlon*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出 Solphin，一个基于 Python 的软件包，用于周期性固态密度泛函理论（Density Functional Theory, DFT）光伏计算的生成、后处理与分析，旨在提升光伏材料评估的效率、可复现性与易用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在光伏材料（photovoltaic materials）研发中，计算模拟可缩短新材料探索的时间与成本，并通过深化基础理解推动效率提升。因此需要一个便于评估材料光伏潜力的工具。

Method: 开发 Solphin 软件包，面向周期性固态密度泛函理论（Density Functional Theory, DFT）计算，提供光伏计算的生成、后处理与分析功能，并内置光伏品质因数（photovoltaic Figure of Merit）、细致平衡（detailed balance）与光谱极限最大效率（spectroscopic limited maximum efficiency）等计算方法。

Result: Solphin 集成了光伏品质因数、细致平衡与光谱极限最大效率等分析能力，并支持其他分析方法，能够高效且用户友好地评估材料的光伏潜力。

Conclusion: Solphin 通过改善可及性、可复现性与易用性，为基于 DFT 的光伏材料评估提供了高效工具。

Abstract: In the development of photovoltaic materials, computational simulation has become a important tool for reducing the time and cost of exploring novel materials, as well as providing insights that help drive improvements in efficiency through increasing fundamental understanding. We present Solphin, a Python package for the generation, post-processing and analysis of photovoltaic calculations from periodic solid state Density Functional Theory. We include the ability to calculate the photovoltaic Figure of Merit, detailed balance and spectroscopic limited maximum efficiency, alongside other analysis methods. Solphin has been built to improve the access, reproducibility and ease of evaluating the photovoltaic potential of a material in an efficient and user-friendly manner.

</details>


### [141] [Universal orbital-coupling rules for hydrogen-defect interactions in bcc metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10063)
*Qianxi Zhu,Hao-Xuan Huang,Zichen Zhang,Hong-Bo Zhou,Wang Gao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出基于配位数（coordination number）与原子间距（interatomic distance）的轨道耦合模型（orbital-coupling model），统一描述体心立方（body-centered-cubic, bcc）金属中氢与多种缺陷的相互作用，并揭示非常规的s-d耦合规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氢-缺陷相互作用决定bcc金属性能，但缺陷类型多样，现有经验模型（empirical models）缺乏跨缺陷的可迁移性（transferability）。

Method: 构建以配位数与原子间距为参量的轨道耦合模型，量化氢在纳米空腔（nanovoids）、空位环（vacancy loops）和晶界（grain boundaries）中的溶解能（solution energetics），并预测氢在纳米空腔处的势能面（potential-energy surfaces）。

Result: 模型揭示受限环境中非常规的s-d耦合（s-d coupling）规律：氢-金属相互作用呈配位数依赖规律；氢-氢相互作用偏离通常推测的s-s耦合，而呈现氢-金属耦合的距离衰减规律。该规律可重现实验观测的氢脱附双峰分布（bimodal profile of H desorption）。

Conclusion: 该基于电子结构（electronic-structure-origin）的统一模型有助于理解受限条件下化学键的本质，并指导耐氢（H-tolerant）材料的设计。

Abstract: Hydrogen-defect interactions control the performance of body-centered-cubic (bcc) metals. However, the complex variations among defects lead to various empirical models that lack transferability across defects. Here, we propose an orbital-coupling model, built on coordination number and interatomic distance, that quantifies the H solution energetics across nanovoids, vacancy loops and grain boundaries in bcc metals, and even predicts the potential-energy surfaces of H at nanovoids. Our model reveals an unconventional s-d coupling rule in the confined environment of defects: H-metal interactions exhibit a unique coordination-dependent law, whereas H-H interactions, deviating from the usually speculated s-s coupling, acquire the distance-decay law of H-metal coupling. This unusual rule proves essential to reproduce the experimentally observed bimodal profile of H desorption. Our electronic-structure-origin, unified model is thus crucial to understanding the nature of chemical bonds under constraint and engineering the H-tolerant materials.

</details>


### [142] [Surface Physics of Low-Dimensional Pnictogen Chalcohalides and Its Impact on Photovoltaic Technologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10136)
*Cibrán López,David Rovira,Álex Jiménez-Arguijo,Zacharie Jehl,Edgardo Saucedo,Claudio Cazorla*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过第一性原理计算与器件级建模，揭示低维硫卤化合物（pnictogen chalcohalides, MChX）表面取向对能带对齐（band alignment）的刚性调控可达0.8 eV，并在BiSBr中导致开路电压（open-circuit voltage）最多下降0.6 V，提出表面取向是电荷提取的隐性设计参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MChX（M = Bi, Sb；Ch = S, Se；X = I, Br）是一类有前景的无毒太阳能转换半导体，但其太阳能电池效率仍低于10%，远低于约30%的Shockley-Queisser极限。高效电荷提取关键取决于吸收层与电荷选择接触之间的带边对齐（band-edge alignment），而该对齐属于表面性质，此前认识不足。

Method: 结合第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）与器件级建模（device-level modelling），系统研究整个MChX家族的表面能量学与能带对齐；对晶体取向进行穷举采样，包括低对称晶面（low-symmetry facets）；并对BiSBr开展漂移-扩散模拟（drift-diffusion simulations）。

Result: 在全部八种化合物中，(011)与(010)表面为最稳定的终止面，其形成能（formation energy）仅相差约0.01–0.03 J m⁻²，但两者带边相对彼此发生刚性位移，幅度最高达0.8 eV。对BiSBr的漂移-扩散模拟显示，即使吸收层理想且无缺陷，这类晶面共存也会使开路电压最多降低0.6 V。

Conclusion: 表面取向是电荷提取的隐性设计参数，可解释MChX太阳能电池理论与实验效率之间的差距，并为MChX基光伏（photovoltaic）与光催化（photocatalytic）技术提供新的设计原则。

Abstract: Low-dimensional pnictogen chalcohalides (MChX; M = Bi, Sb; Ch = S, Se; X = I, Br) are promising non-toxic semiconductors for solar energy conversion, yet the power conversion efficiencies of MChX solar cells remain below 10%, far from their Shockley-Queisser limit of $\sim$30%. Efficient charge extraction depends critically on the band-edge alignment between the absorber and the charge-selective contacts, which is a surface property. Here, we combine first-principles calculations with device-level modelling to investigate the surface energetics and band alignments across the MChX family. An exhaustive sampling of crystal orientations, including low-symmetry facets, identifies the (011) and (010) surfaces as the most stable terminations in all eight compounds. Their formation energies differ by only $\approx 0.01$-$0.03\mathrm{J\,m^{-2}}$, yet their band edges are shifted rigidly with respect to each other by up to $0.8$eV. Drift-diffusion simulations of BiSBr show that the coexistence of such facets can reduce the open-circuit voltage by up to $0.6$V, even in an otherwise ideal, defect-free absorber. Surface orientation thus emerges as a hidden design parameter for charge extraction. This study may help to explain the gap between the theoretical and experimental efficiencies of MChX solar cells, and it provides new design principles for MChX-based photovoltaic and photocatalytic technologies.

</details>


### [143] [Lattice dynamics of disordered phases of silicon and spatially resolved computations of thermal conductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10165)
*A. Gautam,C. Ugwumadu,L. A. M. Rosset,R. M. Tutchton,S. Nakhmanson,D. A. Drabold*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究以非晶硅（amorphous silicon）为对象，通过高质量原子间势揭示其存在原子位移均方根涨差异常偏大的谐波模式（harmonic modes），并表明此类动力学决定热输运过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序体系（disordered systems）的晶格动力学常与晶态对应物不同，已有例证包括二能级系统（two-level systems）与玻色峰（Boson peak）；本文旨在考察经典材料非晶硅是否仍能提供新的动力学现象。

Method: 采用具密度泛函理论质量（DFT-quality）的原子间势构建真实网络，进行晶格动力学分析，并结合位点投影热导率（site-projected thermal conductivity）将原子动力学与热输运相关联。

Result: 发现两类结果：(1) 存在谐波模式，其原子位置的均方根变化（root-mean-square variation）异常之大；(2) 这些动力学模式决定了热输运过程。

Conclusion: 非晶硅展现出有别于既有认知的新动力学特征，且其动力学直接支配热输运；作者推测该现象对其他无序体系亦具相关性。

Abstract: The lattice dynamics of disordered systems (amorphous solids, polymers and glasses) are often distinct from crystalline analogues. Examples include the "two-level systems" and the Boson peak. Here, we show that even the venerable material amorphous silicon offers new wrinkles: (1) realistic networks studied with DFT-quality interatomic potentials show the existence of harmonic modes exhibiting anomalously large root-mean-square variation in atomic positions, and (2) these dynamics determine the processes of heat transport. We correlate the atomic dynamics with transport using the site-projected thermal conductivity [C. Ugwumadu et. al Phys. Status Solidi B 263, e202500316 (2026)], and conjecture that these observations are relevant to other disordered systems.

</details>


### [144] [Prediction of the thermal neutron response of grain-boundary impedance in Gd-doped ceria based on the radiation-ionic effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10191)
*Zhongming Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 基于已发表的伽马辐照数据与 Mott-Schottky/Seager 框架，本文预测 3 mol% Gd 掺杂 CeO$_2$（3GDC）在热中子辐照下晶界离子电阻的响应，并给出温度、通量与自屏蔽等约束条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辐射-离子效应（radiation-ionic effect）此前仅在光子（伽马）辐照下得到证实，缺乏对中子等其他辐照源的验证。由于 $^{157}$Gd 具有极大热中子俘获截面并偏析于晶界，3GDC 有望将中子能量直接沉积于阻碍离子输运的界面。

Method: 将中子俘获源项表达为晶界有效产生率（GB-effective generation rate），代入以已发表伽马数据标定的 Mott-Schottky/Seager 框架；利用伽马温度序列确定单陷阱参数（约 1.1 eV）的温度依赖，并用独立的薄膜紫外数据在 400 $^\circ$C 下校验该标定。

Result: 热中子通量 $6\times10^{9}$ cm$^{-2}$s$^{-1}$ 产生与 35 Gy/min 标定相同的晶界有效产生率。室温下预测晶界电阻比在 $10^{9}$ 通量时约为 150（90% 区间 100-290），在 $10^{10}$ 时约为 1300（900-2500）；响应在 $10^{9}$ 时 60 $^\circ$C、$10^{10}$ 时 80 $^\circ$C 以上消失。连续源描述在 $10^{10}$ 成立、在 $10^{9}$ 仅为临界成立。自屏蔽被俘获光子的康普顿吸收部分抵消；Gd 超过 5 mol% 时响应受弱暗势垒限制。

Conclusion: 本文给出可检验的热中子辐照预测，指出恢复时间常数（recovery time constant）是中子实验应首要测量的量，并提供源参数、样品几何与控制条件以供实验验证。

Abstract: The radiation-ionic effect was reported in 3 mol% Gd-doped CeO$_2$ (3GDC): under $^{60}$Co gamma irradiation at 35 Gy/min, the grain-boundary (GB) ionic resistance of a bulk pellet decreased 680-fold at room temperature because radiation-generated electrons trapped at the GB core lower the space-charge potential. The effect has only been demonstrated with photons. Here the response of 3GDC to thermal neutrons is predicted. Neutrons are absorbed by $^{157}$Gd (254 kb); about 50 keV per capture is deposited within 1 $μ$m by conversion and Auger electrons, and Gd segregates to the GBs, so the energy is delivered to the interfaces that block ionic transport. This source term is expressed as a GB-effective generation rate and used in the Mott-Schottky/Seager framework calibrated on the published gamma data. The gamma temperature series fixes the temperature dependence of the single trap parameter (about 1.1 eV), and this calibration reproduces the trap parameter obtained from independent thin-film UV data at 400 $^\circ$C. A thermal flux of $6\times10^{9}$ cm$^{-2}$s$^{-1}$ gives the same GB-effective generation rate as the 35 Gy/min calibration. At room temperature the predicted GB resistance ratio is about 150 (90% interval 100-290) at $10^{9}$ and about 1300 (900-2500) at $10^{10}$ cm$^{-2}$s$^{-1}$; the response vanishes above 60 $^\circ$C at $10^{9}$ and 80 $^\circ$C at $10^{10}$. Because capture events are discrete, the continuous-source description holds only if the trapped charge of one event outlasts the next; this is met at $10^{10}$ and marginal at $10^{9}$, so the recovery time constant is the first quantity a neutron experiment should measure. Self-shielding is partly offset by Compton absorption of the capture photons; above 5 mol% Gd the response is limited by the weak dark barrier. Source parameters, sample geometry and controls needed to test the prediction are given.

</details>


### [145] [Electronic Structure Descriptors for CO$_2$ Conversion Activity in Perovskite Oxides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10268)
*Hyo-sun Jin,Jiyeon Kim,Sooran Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作系统考察六种电子结构描述符与钙钛矿氧化物 CO₂ 制 CH₄ 活性的关系，发现未占据过渡金属 3d 带中心、电荷转移能及 W₃d/W₂p 与活性负相关，并通过参数化暴力搜索获得仅依赖 W₃d/W₂p 的显式拟合方程，用于筛选双钙钛矿候选材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子结构描述符（electronic structure descriptors）已广泛用于解释催化活性，但其在钙钛矿氧化物（perovskite oxides）CO₂ 制 CH₄ 转化中的应用仍相对有限，因此需要建立描述符与活性之间的定量关系。

Method: 研究考察六种与能带中心（band centers）和带宽（bandwidths）相关的电子结构描述符，对 CH₄ 活性进行相关性分析，并采用参数化暴力搜索（parametric brute-force searching, BFS）方法量化定性趋势，从而获得显式拟合方程。

Result: 未占据过渡金属（transition-metal, TM）3d 带中心、电荷转移能（charge-transfer energy）以及未占据 TM 3d 与占据 O 2p 带宽之比 W₃d/W₂p 均与 CH₄ 活性呈负相关；所选拟合方程仅依赖 W₃d/W₂p，为双钙钛矿（double perovskites）活性增强提供电子结构视角。

Conclusion: 将所选方程应用于 24 种镧基双钙钛矿（La-based double perovskites）筛选，表明 La₂CoGaO₆、La₂CoAlO₆ 及含 Ti 组分为有前景的候选材料；该方法有助于识别描述符—活性关系并指导钙钛矿氧化物中的材料筛选。

Abstract: Electronic structure descriptors have been widely used to rationalize catalytic activity, but their application to CO$_2$-to-CH$_4$ conversion in perovskite oxides remains relatively limited. Here, we investigate six electronic structure descriptors related to band centers and bandwidths. We find that the unoccupied transition-metal (TM) $3d$-band center, charge-transfer energy, and the ratio of the unoccupied TM $3d$ to occupied O $2p$ bandwidths, $W_{3d}/W_{2p}$, exhibit negative correlations with CH$_4$ activity. These qualitative trends are further quantified using a parametric brute-force searching (BFS) approach to obtain explicit fitting equations. Notably, the selected equations depend only on $W_{3d}/W_{2p}$, providing an electronic structure perspective on the enhanced activity in double perovskites. We apply the selected equation to screen 24 La-based double perovskites. This screening suggests that La$_2$CoGaO$_6$, La$_2$CoAlO$_6$, and Ti-containing compositions are promising candidates. The present approach can help identify descriptor-activity relationships and guide materials screening in perovskite oxides.

</details>


### [146] [Interface Doping Mechanism of p-Type Conductivity in Mist-CVD-Grown LiGa5O8 Thin Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10401)
*Dong Su Yu,Shashu Tomar,Zixiu Huang,Kaitian Zhang,Sarker Md. Sadman,Vijay Gopal Thirupakuzi Vangipuram,Ye Lin,Walter R. L. Lambrecht,Roberto C. Myers,Hongping Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过对比雾化学气相沉积（mist-CVD）生长的p型导电与绝缘LiGa5O8薄膜，揭示p型导电源于Li缺陷组分及β-Ga2O3第二相的形成，并结合II型能带排列与锂空位受主能级解释空穴积累机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiGa5O8作为超宽禁带氧化物半导体已被实验证实可呈现p型导电，但其p型输运的物理起源尚不明确，亟需厘清。

Method: 系统比较不同生长条件下mist-CVD制备的LiGa5O8薄膜的电学、组分与结构性质，采用XPS分析元素组分，结合Raman光谱与XRD探测物相。

Result: p型导电薄膜室温空穴浓度达约10^18 cm^-3；其呈现Li/Ga比降低（Li缺陷）及绝缘薄膜中不存在的β-Ga2O3第二相特征。

Conclusion: 结合已报道的LiGa5O8/β-Ga2O3 II型能带排列与理论预测的锂空位（V_Li）受主能级，提出混相结构促进电子由β-Ga2O3价带转移至LiGa5O8受主态，从而增强空穴积累与p型导电。

Abstract: LiGa5O8 is an ultrawide-bandgap oxide semiconductor that has recently been experimentally demonstrated to exhibit p-type conductivity; however, the origin of the observed p-type transport remains poorly understood. In this work, we investigate the origin of p-type conductivity by systematically comparing the electrical, compositional, and structural properties of mist chemical vapor deposition (mist-CVD) grown LiGa5O8 thin films exhibiting either p-type conductivity or insulating behavior under different growth conditions. The p-conductive films exhibit room-temperature hole concentrations on the order of 10^18 cm^-3. The emergence of p-type conductivity is found to be strongly correlated with the elemental composition of the films, with X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy (XPS) revealing a reduced Li/Ga ratio in the p-conductive films, indicative of a Li-deficient composition. Raman spectroscopy and X-ray diffraction (XRD) further reveal signatures of \b{eta}-Ga2O3 in the p-conductive films that are absent in the insulating films, demonstrating the formation of a secondary \b{eta}-Ga2O3 phase. Considering the reported type-II band alignment between LiGa5O8 and \b{eta}-Ga2O3, together with theoretically predicted acceptor levels associated Li vacancies (V_Li) in LiGa5O8, the formation of mixed LiGa5O8/\b{eta}-Ga2O3 phases is proposed to facilitate electron transfer from the \b{eta}-Ga2O3 valence band to acceptor states in LiGa5O8, thereby promoting hole accumulation and p-type conductivity.

</details>


### [147] [Magnetic Order from First-Principles Linear Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10412)
*Yaroslav Zhumagulov,Emiliano Cruz-Aranda,Denis Kochan,Oleg V. Yazyev*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种从顺磁态（paramagnetic state）自洽磁化响应（self-consistent magnetization response）的主导本征模直接识别磁序的方法，无需预设候选自旋构型，即能同时确定有序波矢与磁矩图案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统磁序预测依赖比较若干试算自旋构型（trial spin configurations）的能量，该方法无法覆盖候选集之外的序，且随磁性位点数目增加迅速变得不可行。

Method: 在顺磁态中计算自洽磁化响应，计入自旋—轨道耦合（spin--orbit coupling），并在原胞内任意波矢处求解该响应的主导本征模，由此直接读出有序波矢与磁矩排布，无需任何候选构型或对磁序的先验假设。

Result: 在无先验假设下，该方法正确重现了交错磁体（altermagnet）候选材料 KV$_2$Se$_2$O 的 G 型堆叠与面外磁矩、Cu$_2$OSeO$_3$ 的 3:1 亚铁磁（ferrimagnetic）四面体，以及单层 NiI$_2$ 的非公度螺旋（incommensurate spiral）不稳定性。

Conclusion: 顺磁态的响应可作为预测磁序的无偏途径，为复杂磁体的系统性搜寻提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The magnetic order of a material is usually predicted by comparing the energies of trial spin configurations, a strategy that cannot reach orders outside the chosen set and rapidly becomes intractable as the number of magnetic sites grows. We show that the order can instead be identified directly from the paramagnetic state: the leading eigenmodes of its self-consistent magnetization response, obtained with spin--orbit coupling at arbitrary wavevector within the primitive cell, determine both the ordering wavevector and the pattern of moments, with no candidate configurations. With no prior assumption about the order, the method correctly reproduces the G-type stacking with out-of-plane moments in the altermagnet candidate KV$_2$Se$_2$O, the $3{:}1$ ferrimagnetic tetrahedra of Cu$_2$OSeO$_3$, and the incommensurate spiral instability of monolayer NiI$_2$. Our results establish the response of the paramagnetic state as a predictive and unbiased route to assessing magnetic order, enabling systematic searches for complex magnets.

</details>
