# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [148] [Matrix Product State Representatives of Symmetry-Protected Phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09501)
*Thomas Jackson*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文为整数自旋（integer spin）的 SO(3)×Z₂^T×Z₂^R 不变矩阵乘积态（matrix product states, MPS）提供一个构造性 ansatz，覆盖 Tasaki 与 Ogata 所研究的每一组 Z₂ 拓扑指标（topological indices）三元组合，并给出自旋-1 的精确实例及可搜索任意整数自旋代表态的 Fortran 程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tasaki 与 Ogata 此前基于时间反演（time-reversal）与反射（reflection）对称性提出了 Z₂ 拓扑指标分类，但这些指标组合是否都能由显式的、具有相应对称性的矩阵乘积态实现尚缺构造性验证。本文旨在为每一组平凡与非平凡指标提供具体的 MPS 代表态。

Method: 提出一个简单的 ansatz，用于寻找满足自旋-1 的 SU(2)、时间反演与格点反射（site-reflection）对称性的 MPS；对每组指标给出注入长度（injectivity length）l ≤ 8 的代表态。此外提供自旋-1 的若干精确实例、一个可在任意整数自旋下搜索任意指标三元组代表态的 Fortran 程序，以及小自旋下的随机抽样数据。

Result: 每一种平凡与非平凡指标的组合都存在自旋-1 的 SU(2)、时间反演与格点反射对称的 MPS 代表态，且注入长度不超过 8；自旋-1 情形有精确示例，并给出了小自旋的随机抽样数据。

Conclusion: 此类态的存在性与 Chen、Gu、Wen 的分类结果一致，也与 Tasaki 近期将隐序（hidden-order）与 Ogata 指标之一相等同的结果一致，其中该 Ogata 指标对应于二面体 D₂ 对称性。

Abstract: We provide a simple ansatz for finding integer spin $SO(3)\times\Z_2^T\times\Z_2^R$-invariant matrix product states representing a phase for each triple of $\Z_2$ topological indices studied in the literature by Tasaki \cite{tasakiheistop}\cite{tasakitop} and Ogata (corresponding to time-reversal and reflection symmetries) \cite{ogatatime}\cite{ogatareflection}. Every combination of trivial and non-trivial indices has a spin-$1$ $SU(2)$, time-reversal, and site-reflection symmetric MPS representative with injectivity length $l\leq 8$. We provide some exact examples for spin-$1$ and a Fortran program which will find representatives of any triple for any integer spin, as well as some data from random sampling for small spin. The existence of such states agrees with the classification of Chen, Gu, and Wen \cite{chen}, and with the recent results of Tasaki \cite{tasakinew} equating the hidden-order and one of Ogata's indices, wherein the Ogata index is that associated to the dihedral $D_2$ symmetry.

</details>


### [149] [Absence of Absolutely Continuous Spectrum for One-Dimensional Capacitance Operators with Critical Long-Range Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09582)
*Habib Ammari,Jiayu Qiu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明具有乘性无序（multiplicative disorder）与临界长程跳跃（critical long-range hoppings）的一维标量 Laurent 算子不存在绝对连续谱（absolutely continuous spectrum），并将其应用于亚波长物理中的电容算子（capacitance operator）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有理论要求非对角衰减至少为四次方速率，此限制过强，无法覆盖临界长程相互作用情形；本文旨在绕过该要求，推进长程相互作用波系统中 Anderson 局域化（Anderson localization）的证明。

Method: 利用卷积核的有限差分界（finite-difference bound of the convolution kernel），在相互作用仅以 |n|^{-1}log^{-β}|n|（β>1）衰减时仍可适用；随后将该抽象主结果应用于亚波长物理中的具体物理模型——电容算子。

Result: 对每周期含一个谐振子的三维谐振子链（three-dimensional resonators），验证了所需的衰减与有限差分估计，并得出绝对连续谱不存在；纯点谱（pure point spectrum）与奇异连续谱（singular continuous spectrum）之间的区分仍悬而未决。

Conclusion: 结果虽未区分纯点谱与奇异连续谱，但构成证明具临界长程相互作用波系统中 Anderson 局域化的关键一步。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish the absence of absolutely continuous spectrum for scalar one-dimensional Laurent operators with multiplicative disorder and critical long-range hoppings. In particular, the requirement of at least quartic off-diagonal decay rate in the existing theory is bypassed by exploiting the finite-difference bound of the convolution kernel, which is available even when the interaction decays only as $|n|^{-1}\log^{-β}|n|$ with $β>1$. We then apply the abstract main result to a concrete physical model, the capacitance operator in subwavelength physics. For a chain of three-dimensional resonators with one resonator per period, we verify the required decay and finite-difference estimates and conclude the absence of absolutely continuous spectrum. These results leave open the distinction between pure point and singular continuous spectrum, but constitute a key step in proving Anderson localization in wave systems with critical long-range interactions.

</details>


### [150] [Operator Schmidt Rank and the Geometry of Schmidt Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09794)
*Mohsen Kian,Mohammad Sal Moslehian*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文刻画了在有限算子 Schmidt 秩（operator Schmidt rank, OSR）约束下的酉算子对态 Schmidt 秩（Schmidt rank, SR）及整个累积 Schmidt 谱的控制关系，给出最小 OSR 的精确公式，并导出累积谱的保序不等式、优序关系及 Schatten 范数与 Rényi 熵的扰动界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于超越单纯的“秩可达性（rank reachability）”：不仅关心酉算子能否把某态映射到指定 Schmidt 秩，更希望控制态的完整累积 Schmidt 谱（cumulative Schmidt profile），并在算子 Schmidt 秩受限时量化范数与熵的失真。

Method: 在复张量积空间 $\mathbb C^m\otimes\mathbb C^n$ 上，令 $d=\min\{m,n\}$，以 $SR(v)$ 与 $OSR(\mathbf U)$ 分别表示态的 Schmidt 秩与酉算子的算子 Schmidt 秩，对给定初态 $v$ 与目标秩 $q$ 求解满足 $SR(\mathbf Uv)=q$ 的酉算子最小 OSR，并建立相应的优序（majorization）关系。

Result: 主要结论为：当 $0\neq v$、$p=SR(v)$ 且 $1\le q\le d$ 时，$\min\{OSR(\mathbf U): \mathbf U \text{ 酉且 } SR(\mathbf Uv)=q\}=\lceil\max\{p/q,\,q/p\}\rceil$。此外，若酉算子 $\mathbf U$ 满足 $OSR(\mathbf U)\le k$，则对任意单位向量 $x$ 与 $1\le r\le d$，有 $F_{\min\{kr,d\}}(\mathbf Ux)\ge F_r(x)$ 与 $F_{\min\{kr,d\}}(x)\ge F_r(\mathbf Ux)$，其中 $F_r$ 为最大的 $r$ 个 Schmidt 系数平方和。

Conclusion: 结果强于秩可达性，因为它控制了整个累积 Schmidt 谱；所建立的优序关系进一步给出了在算子 Schmidt 秩有界条件下 Schatten 范数失真（Schatten-norm distortion）与 Rényi 熵（Rényi entropies）变化的精确界。

Abstract: The main result of the paper states that if $0\neq v\in\mathbb C^m\otimes\mathbb C^n$, $d=\min\{m,n\}$, and $p=SR(v)$, then for every $1\le q\le d$, \begin{equation*}
  \min\left\{OSR(\mathbf U):
  \begin{array}{l}
  \mathbf U\in\mathbb M_m\otimes\mathbb M_n\text{ is unitary},
  SR(\mathbf Uv)=q
  \end{array}\right\}
  =
  \left\lceil\max\left\{\frac pq,\frac qp\right\}\right\rceil . \end{equation*} Here $OSR(\mathbf U)$ denotes the operator Schmidt rank of $\mathbf U$. Furthermore, if $\mathbf U\in\mathbb M_m\otimes\mathbb M_n$ is unitary and $OSR(\mathbf U)\le k$, then, for every unit vector $x\in\mathbb C^m\otimes\mathbb C^n$ and every $1\le r\le d$, \begin{equation*}
  F_{\min\{kr,d\}}(\mathbf Ux)\ge F_r(x)
  \quad\text{and}\quad
  F_{\min\{kr,d\}}(x)\ge F_r(\mathbf Ux), \end{equation*} where $F_r(x)$ denotes the sum of the $r$ largest squared Schmidt coefficients of $x$. This result is stronger than rank reachability as it controls the entire cumulative Schmidt profile. We also establish a majorization relation and use it to obtain sharp bounds on Schatten-norm distortion and on the change of Rényi entropies under unitaries with bounded operator Schmidt rank.

</details>


### [151] [Asymptotic Neutrality for the Ionization Conjecture in the Two-Center Thomas-Fermi-Dirac-von Weizsäcker Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09997)
*Lu Chen,Yong Wu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究 Thomas-Fermi-Dirac-von Weizsäcker（TFDW）模型的双中心电离猜想（ionization conjecture），证明总电荷为 Z 的两个不同原子核所能束缚的电子数 N 满足 N ≤ Z + C Z^{153/154}(1+log Z)^{3/8}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TFDW 模型的电离猜想旨在确定给定核构型可束缚的电子数，此前仅由 Frank、Nam 与 Van Den Bosch 解决了单中心情形，而原始的多中心分子情形仍为开放问题。本文旨在推进该猜想至双中心（two-center）情形。

Method: 证明依赖于与中性 Thomas-Fermi 密度（neutral Thomas-Fermi density）的比较，并结合局域化（localization）与外部 L^1 估计（exterior L^1-estimates），二者通过屏蔽势（screened potential）耦合。该方法可推广至含任意固定核数 K 的分子。

Result: 对总电荷为 Z 的两个不同原子核，束缚电子数 N 满足 N ≤ Z + C Z^{153/154}(1+log Z)^{3/8}，其中常数不依赖于核位置与核电荷。

Conclusion: 研究结果在双中心情形下推进了 TFDW 模型的电离猜想，且其方法可扩展至任意固定核数 K 的分子体系。

Abstract: The ionization conjecture for the Thomas-Fermi-Dirac-von Weizsäcker model asks how many electrons a given nuclear configuration can bind. So far, only the single-center case has been settled by Frank, Nam, and Van Den Bosch, while the original molecular case has still remained open. We advance this conjecture in the two-center case. We prove that for two distinct nuclei of total charge $Z$, the number $N$ of bound electrons satisfies $N\le Z+C Z^{153/154}(1+\log Z)^{3/8}$, with constants independent of the nuclear positions and the nuclear charges. The proof relies on a comparison with the neutral Thomas-Fermi density and on localization and exterior $L^1$-estimates coupled through the screened potential. Our results could be extended to molecules with any fixed number $K$ of nuclei.

</details>


### [152] [Iterated Graph Systems (II): Bernoulli percolation and critical-exponent universality classes on hierarchical lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10469)
*Ziyu Neroli*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在由边迭代图系统（edge iterated graph systems, EIGS）生成的分层格子上研究伯努利渗流（Bernoulli percolation）及其普适类（universality classes），通过考察八个临界指数（critical exponents），证明有限个替换可乘维度（substitution-multiplicative dimensions）无法完全决定普适性，并进一步否证了临界指数普适性需要可通约离散重整化标度（commensurate discrete renormalisation scales）的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于检验维度是否能够决定普适性这一长期问题，并回应 Hauser 与 Saxena [Phys. Rev. B 34 (1986), 8193-8195] 所讨论的基于维度的普适性失效现象。同时，针对临界指数普适性是否需要可通约离散重整化标度这一猜想展开验证。

Method: 方法上，在由边迭代图系统（EIGS）生成的分层格子上研究伯努利渗流（Bernoulli percolation）及其普适类，并考察八个临界指数（critical exponents）。分析中借助六指数定理（six exponentials theorem）所提供的数论判据来讨论标度可通约性（scale commensurability），但该判据并非决定性依据。

Result: 结果表明，不存在任何有限的替换可乘维度（substitution-multiplicative dimensions）族能够完全决定普适性，从而对基于维度的普适性失效给出严格证明。此外，研究进一步否证了临界指数普适性要求可通约离散重整化标度（commensurate discrete renormalisation scales）的猜想。

Conclusion: 结论是维度无法完全决定普适类，且临界指数普适性并不以可通约的重整化标度为必要条件。六指数定理仅提供标度可通约性的数论判据，但不构成普适性成立的充要条件。

Abstract: We study Bernoulli percolation and its universality classes on hierarchical lattices generated by edge iterated graph systems (EIGS). By examining eight critical exponents, we prove that no finite family of substitution-multiplicative dimensions completely determines universality, providing a rigorous proof of the failure of dimension-based universality discussed by Hauser and Saxena [Phys. Rev. B 34 (1986), 8193-8195]. Although the six exponentials theorem provides number-theoretic criteria for scale commensurability, we further disprove the conjecture that critical-exponent universality requires commensurate discrete renormalisation scales.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.PE'></div>

# q-bio.PE [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [Mathematical statistics of wild mammal biomass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09744)
*Klaus M. Frahm,Dima L. Shepelyansky,José Lages*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 该研究将经济学中刻画财富不平等（wealth inequality）的工具应用于全球野生陆生哺乳动物生物量（biomass）分布，发现其基尼系数（Gini coefficient）高达 G=0.944，并可用 Rayleigh-Jeans（RJ）扩展谱模型在四个数量级范围内重现洛伦兹曲线（Lorenz curve）与帕累托曲线（Pareto curve）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望检验新近发布的全球野生陆生哺乳动物生物量普查数据的统计结构，并借助经济学中刻画财富分配不均的分析框架，为生物量极端集中现象提供定量描述。此举旨在将此前仅在经济分布上验证的财富热化假说（Wealth Thermalization Hypothesis, WTH）拓展至受生态与进化约束的生物分布。

Method: 针对 N_s=4795 个物种的生物量数据，计算其洛伦兹曲线与基尼系数，并与世界各国财富分配的不平等程度对比。随后将物种生物量视为能级，按 Rayleigh-Jeans（RJ）稳态分布填充，构建 RJ 扩展谱模型并与实际洛伦兹曲线、帕累托曲线进行拟合比较。

Result: 哺乳动物生物量分布的基尼系数为 G=0.944，超过世界各国内部财富分配所报告的不平等水平。RJ 扩展谱模型在四个数量级的生物量变化范围内重现了真实的洛伦兹曲线与帕累托曲线，刻画了生物量在稀有大型物种中的强烈凝聚（condensation）以及绝大多数小型物种中的近乎缺失。

Conclusion: 结果将 WTH 框架从人类经济来源的分布扩展至受生态与进化约束的生物分布，并把大型哺乳动物物种的极端稀有性与人类社会财富的寡头式集中纳入同一统计数学描述。作者进一步讨论了可能导致该分布的生态机制，包括种间相互作用（interspecific interactions）、食物可获得性与竞争。

Abstract: Using the recently published global census of the biomass of wild terrestrial mammals, we perform a detailed mathematical statistical analysis of its distribution over $N_s=4795$ species, drawing on tools developed in economics to characterize wealth inequality. We show that the Lorenz curve of the mammal biomass distribution is characterized by a large Gini coefficient $G=0.944$ exceeding the inequality reported for wealth distribution among world countries. This distribution is compared to the predictions of the Wealth Thermalization Hypothesis (WTH), in which species biomass values are treated as energy levels populated according to a Rayleigh-Jeans (RJ) steady-state distribution. We show that an RJ extended spectral model reproduces the real Lorenz and Pareto curves over four orders of magnitude of biomass variation, capturing the strong condensation of biomass among the rare, heavy-bodied species and its near-absence among the vast majority of light-bodied ones. These results extend the WTH framework, previously validated on distributions of human economic origin, to a biological distribution shaped by ecological and evolutionary constraints, and place the extreme rarity of large-bodied mammal species within the same statistical mathematical description as the oligarchic concentration of wealth in human societies. We finally discuss possible ecological mechanisms that may contribute to the observed distribution, including interspecific interactions, food availability, and competition.

</details>


<div id='physics.gen-ph'></div>

# physics.gen-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Shadow signatures and near-horizon energy accumulation in coherent quantum sine-integral black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08841)
*A. Errehymy,Y. Khedif,M. Daoud,B. Turimov,S. Usanov,Z. Yasakov,F. Turaev*

Main category: physics.gen-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究相干量子黑洞模型（coherent quantum black hole model）中正弦积分修正对光子运动与黑洞阴影的影响。结果表明，光子球（photon sphere）内移、临界碰撞参数（critical impact parameter）减小，阴影半径系统性变小且强度分布在阴影边界显著增强，可作为量子修正的潜在观测特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞时空的量子修正可能在事件视界附近的强引力区产生可观测效应，偏离经典广义相对论（general relativity）预言。黑洞成像与强场观测精度的提升，促使作者探究相干量子黑洞模型的量子诱导效应如何影响光子运动与阴影结构。

Method: 基于相干量子黑洞模型，该模型在引力势中引入由相干尺度（coherence scale）\(R_s\) 刻画的正弦积分修正（sine-integral correction），软化中心奇点并改变近视界几何。作者在 \(R_s \in [0.5M, 1.2M]\) 区间内取代表值，分析光子运动、阴影结构及相应强度分布。

Result: 光子球向内移动，临界碰撞参数减小，如 \(R_s=0.5M\) 时 \(b_{\rm crit}\approx 4.764M\)。阴影半径系统性变小，结构较史瓦西情形（Schwarzschild case，\(b_{\rm crit}=3\sqrt{3}M \approx 5.196M\)）更复杂。强度分布显示阴影边界附近辐射明显增强，尤其在中间相干尺度下，归因于光子在修正的近视界区停留更久。

Conclusion: 阴影边界的亮度增强可能成为视界尺度量子修正的潜在观测特征。此外，尽管该几何允许辐射与能量在有效视界面（effective horizon surface）周围积累，其对扰动的响应不同于具有稳定光球（stable light rings）的致密天体：在反作用效应（backreaction effects）下，积累面并非收缩，而是向外移动。

Abstract: Quantum modifications of black hole spacetimes can lead to observable departures from the predictions of classical general relativity, particularly in the strong-gravity regime near the event horizon. In this context, the coherent quantum black hole model introduces a sine-integral correction to the gravitational potential, characterized by a coherence scale \(R_s\), which softens the central singularity and alters the near-horizon geometry. Motivated by the growing precision of black hole imaging and strong-field observations, we explore how these quantum-induced effects influence photon motion and the resulting shadow structure. Considering representative values within the interval \(R_s \in [0.5M, 1.2M]\), we show that the photon sphere shifts inward while the critical impact parameter decreases, reaching values such as \(b_{\rm crit}\approx 4.764M\) for \(R_s=0.5M\). Consequently, the shadow radius becomes systematically smaller and develops a more intricate structure than in the Schwarzschild case (\(b_{\rm crit}=3\sqrt{3}M \approx 5.196M\)). The analysis of the corresponding intensity distributions also reveals a pronounced enhancement of radiation near the shadow boundary, especially for intermediate coherence scales. This feature can be attributed to photons remaining longer within the modified near-horizon region before escaping toward distant observers. Such an increase in brightness may therefore provide a possible observational signature of quantum corrections at horizon scales. Furthermore, although the geometry allows radiation and energy to accumulate around an effective horizon surface, its response to perturbations differs from that expected for compact objects with stable light rings. Instead of contracting under backreaction effects, the accumulation surface shifts outward.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.supr-con'></div>

# cond-mat.supr-con [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [Remote magnon sensing detects the universal phase stiffness jump in Bi$_2$Sr$_2$CaCu$_2$O$_{8+δ}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08934)
*Yue Sun,Mai Nguyen,Naiyuan James Zhang,Dingbin Huang,Shuai Yuan,Aljoscha Söll,Arun Ramanathan,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,Xavier Roy,Zdeněk Sofer,Shigeyuki Ishida,Hiroshi Eisaki,Makoto Hashimoto,Zhi-Xun Shen,Joseph Orenstein,Xiaodong Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究利用CrSBr中传播型反铁磁磁振子（antiferromagnetic magnons）作为远程传感器，实现对超导相位刚度（phase stiffness）的非接触、局域、全光学测量，并借此在块体铜氧化物中直接观测到平行场诱导的BKT转变及普适相位刚度跃变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相位刚度决定超流与超导的宏观量子相干性，是探测凝聚体损耗、涨落与相变（phase transitions）的敏感探针，但定量且空间分辨的测量手段仍具挑战。

Method: 以CrSBr中强激子-磁振子耦合（exciton-magnon coupling）实现磁振子波包的光学激发与探测并精确测定其群速度（group velocity）；将CrSBr与欠掺杂Bi$_2$Sr$_2$CaCu$_2$O$_{8+\delta}$界面接触，通过Meissner屏蔽电流（Meissner screening currents）读出相位刚度。

Result: 在超导转变温度以下，磁振子群速度显著增强，并定量编码相位刚度；据此通过直接观测普适相位刚度跃变，识别出平行场诱导的Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless转变。

Conclusion: 该工作确立传播型磁振子作为超导电动力学（superconducting electrodynamics）与低维量子材料中相位相干现象的局域探针，并提供场控途径以揭示块体铜氧化物中本征二维相位有序物理。

Abstract: Phase stiffness governs macroscopic quantum coherence in superfluids and superconductors, providing a sensitive probe of condensate depletion, fluctuations, and phase transitions. However quantitative and spatially resolved measurements remain challenging. Here we report a contactless, local, all-optical measurement of superconducting phase stiffness using propagating antiferromagnetic magnons as remote sensors. In CrSBr, strong exciton-magnon coupling enables optical excitation and detection of magnon wavepackets and precise determination of their group velocity. When CrSBr is interfaced with underdoped Bi$_2$Sr$_2$CaCu$_2$O$_{8+δ}$, we find that the magnon group velocity is substantially enhanced below the superconducting transition and quantitatively encodes the phase stiffness through Meissner screening currents. Using this capability, we identify a parallel-field-induced Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless transition through direct observation of the universal phase-stiffness jump, revealing a field-controlled route to expose intrinsic two-dimensional phase-ordering physics in a bulk cuprate. Our work establishes propagating magnons as local probes of superconducting electrodynamics and phase-coherent phenomena in low-dimensional quantum materials.

</details>


### [156] [Superconducting Diode Effect due to the Inverse Spin Hall Effect in Josephson Junctions with Extrinsic Spin-Orbit Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09248)
*Yositake Takane,Aurélien Manchon,Gen Tatara*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作通过数值方法研究含本征自旋轨道耦合的SNS结，揭示了平衡态下约瑟夫森电流引发的自旋霍尔效应与梯度塞曼场引发的逆自旋霍尔效应及二极管效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探究结构反演对称的SNS结中，自旋轨道耦合与约瑟夫森电流、塞曼场之间的耦合机制，并验证既有图解微扰理论预言。

Method: 采用数值方法，在N区含本征自旋轨道相互作用的超导体-正常金属-超导体（SNS）结中，计算平衡态自旋密度与约瑟夫森电流。

Result: 约瑟夫森电流产生自旋霍尔效应，使N区两侧边缘出现相反自旋密度；施加梯度塞曼磁场时，逆自旋霍尔效应导致反常相移，并在存在高次谐波时产生二极管效应，且N-S耦合增强时谐波增强使该效应更显著。

Conclusion: 数值结果与既有图解微扰理论一致，表明结构反演对称系统中自旋轨道耦合可驱动电流-自旋及磁场-电流的相互转换。

Abstract: The spin density and Josephson current at equilibrium are studied numerically in a superconductor-normal metal-superconductor (SNS) junction with extrinsic spin-orbit interaction in the N segment. This system preserves structural inversion symmetry. We find that the Josephson current generates the spin Hall effect, resulting in an opposite spin density near the two edges of the N segment. We also find that a Zeeman magnetic field with gradient applied to the N segment generates an anomalous phase shift due to the inverse spin Hall effect, resulting in a diode effect when higher harmonics of the Josephson current are present. These results are consistent with those of a previous theoretical study based on diagrammatic perturbation theory. We find that the diode effect becomes more pronounced when stronger coupling between the N and S segments enhances the higher harmonics.

</details>


### [157] [Delamination in Large REBCO Coils](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09308)
*Jun Lu,Yan Xin,Iain Dixon,Brent Jarvis,Kwangmin Kim,Yu Suetomi,Jeremy Levitan,Jan Jaroszynski,Hongyu Bai*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: REBCO 涂层导体（coated conductors）因本征层状结构而易发生分层（delamination），该问题成为 REBCO 磁体线圈的主要设计挑战之一。本文报道了在美国国家强磁场实验室 40 T 全超导磁体研发中，对双并绕（two-in-hand）带匝间绝缘的大型线圈在 4.2 K、11.4 T 背景场下的测试结果，发现绕组内与交叉接头处发生 REBCO 退化，经事后检查确认其由分层引起。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: REBCO 涂层导体已用于凝聚态物理研究、核聚变、粒子加速器和 NMR 等领域的超高场磁体，但其本征层状结构使其抗分层机械强度不足。分层问题因此成为 REBCO 磁体线圈的主要设计挑战之一，本文即在这一背景下开展相关线圈的研制与测试。

Method: 作为美国国家强磁场实验室 40 T 全超导磁体研发的一部分，设计并测试了采用双并绕 REBCO 导体、带匝间绝缘的大型线圈，测试条件为 4.2 K 液氦温度与 11.4 T 背景磁场；随后进行事后检查（postmortem inspection）与显微分析（microscopy），并对短样品开展磁矩测量（torque magnetometry）。

Result: 测试中发现绕组内部与交叉接头（crossover joints）处出现 REBCO 退化。事后检查表明退化由分层造成，分层在导体表面表现为气泡（bubbles），并经显微分析加以研究。此外，在短样品上通过磁矩测量也观察到类似气泡，支持线圈中分层气泡源于电磁应力（electromagnetic stress）的推断。

Conclusion: 分层是导致大型 REBCO 线圈退化的重要原因，且其气泡形貌可由电磁应力解释。文中将讨论这些发现对未来磁体设计的启示以及缓解方法。

Abstract: REBCO coated conductors have been used in ultrahigh field magnets for condensed matter physics research, nuclear fusion, particle accelerators, and NMR applications. It is well known, however, that they are not mechanically strong against delamination due to their intrinsic layered structures. As a result, the delamination issue has become one of the major design challenges of REBCO magnet coils. As a part of the development of the 40 T all-superconducting magnet at the National High Magnetic Field Laboratory, USA, large-scale coils wound by two-in-hand REBCO conductor with turn-to-turn insulation was designed and tested at 4.2 K in an 11.4 T background magnetic field. We found REBCO degradations within the windings and at the crossover joints during the tests. The postmortem inspection found that the degradation was caused by delamination. The delamination appears on the surface of the conductor as bubbles which were subsequently studied by microscopy. Additionally, a similar bubble was found on a short sample by torque magnetometry. This supports the inference that the delamination bubbles found in coils are due to electromagnetic stress. In this paper, we present these findings. The implication of these findings for future magnet designs and the method of mitigation will be discussed.

</details>


### [158] [Effect of uniform bond-charge order on longitudinal spin-susceptibility jump at a superconducting instability inside a magnetic phase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09799)
*Sher Baz,Hiroyuki Yamase*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作在前人最小模型基础上引入均匀键电荷序（uniform bond-charge order），发现纵向自旋磁化率（longitudinal spin susceptibility）在超导转变处的跳跃依然稳健，且该序会定量增强磁化率及其跳跃幅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究（Yamase 与 Zafur, 2021）用仅含磁性相互作用与超导相互作用的最小模型，揭示了超导在磁相内部连续发展时纵向自旋磁化率出现跳跃。但在关联电子系统（correlated-electron systems）中，磁性与键电荷相互作用同源于自旋-自旋交换作用，故需引入均匀键电荷序以更真实描述体系。

Method: 在原有最小模型基础上扩展，额外纳入均匀键电荷序（uniform bond-charge order），从而对系统进行更接近实际的建模，并分析其对纵向自旋磁化率行为的影响。

Result: 即使存在均匀键电荷序，纵向自旋磁化率的跳跃依然稳健存在；同时，该键电荷序在定量上增强了纵向自旋磁化率，并增大了超导临界温度（$T_c$）处的跳跃幅度。

Conclusion: 纵向自旋磁化率跳跃现象不依赖于是否包含均匀键电荷序，具有鲁棒性；但均匀键电荷序会定量改变该磁化率及其在 $T_c$ 处的跳跃大小，表明源自同一自旋-自旋交换的键电荷相互作用对相关物理量的定量描述不可忽略。

Abstract: A previous work [H. Yamase and M. Zafur, Phys. Rev. B \textbf{103}, 224527 (2021)] showed that the longitudinal spin susceptibility exhibits a jump when the superconductivity develops continuously inside a magnetic phase. That analysis employed a minimal model containing magnetic and superconducting interactions. However, in correlated-electron systems, both magnetic and bond-charge interactions originate from the same underlying spin-spin exchange interaction. We therefore extend the model by including the uniform bond-charge order to provide a more realistic description of the system. We find that the jump of the longitudinal spin susceptibility remains robust even in the presence of uniform bond-charge order, but the uniform bond-charge order quantitatively enhances the longitudinal spin susceptibility and the size of the jump at $T_c$.

</details>


### [159] [Correlating DC SQUID Performance with the Location of Trapped Magnetic Flux Using Scanning SQUID Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10442)
*Bochao Xu,Ian W. Haygood,Kyle Jackman,Coenrad J. Fourie,Pete F. Hopkins,Michael L. Schneider*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究结合扫描SQUID显微镜（SSM）成像与同一降温过程中的电路性能测量，直接关联超导接地平面中磁通涡旋位置与电路性能，揭示磁通子存在优先钉扎位点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导电路接地平面中的孔洞（moats）被用于缓解残余磁通的有害影响，但此前研究未将磁通位置与电路性能测量直接关联。

Method: 利用扫描SQUID显微镜（SSM）对DC SQUID附近多种moat构型的磁通捕获效果及其失效时接地平面中涡旋位置进行成像，并在同一降温过程中不干扰电路的前提下测量电路性能。

Result: moat构型有效时可捕获不想要的磁通；当moat失效时，观测到涡旋在接地平面中的位置；且无论moat构型如何，磁通子（fluxons）都存在优先钉扎位点。

Conclusion: 将SSM成像与电路性能直接关联可有效指导电路布局、建模与失效分析。

Abstract: Holes in the ground planes of superconducting circuits, commonly referred to as moats, are known to be an effective means of mitigating the deleterious effects of residual magnetic flux in superconducting circuits. Previous studies have utilized scanning SQUID microscopy (SSM) to image the effectiveness of various moat geometries and the maximum magnetic fields where they no longer prevent vortices in the ground plane but have not directly correlated the location of flux with circuit performance measurements. In this study we employ SSM to image the effectiveness of various moat configurations in trapping unwanted flux near a DC SQUID, and when the moats are not effective, the location of vortices in the ground plane. During the same cooldown, and without disturbing the circuit, we measure the circuit performance to directly correlate this with the vortex location. We show that there are preferential pinning sites for fluxons regardless of moat configuration, demonstrating the utility of the SSM in provide feedback for circuit layout, modeling, and failure analysis.

</details>

### [160] [Matter wave bistability with a momentum chirped Bose-Einstein condensate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08942)
*Mikhail Mamaev,Nicholas Mantella,Noah P. Baker,Joseph McGowan,Aephraim M. Steinberg*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文理论上提出，通过在玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（Bose-Einstein condensate, BEC）波包中引入动量啁啾（momentum chirp），可克服原子间碰撞导致的共振峰展宽问题，从而在物质波法布里-珀罗（Fabry-Perot, FP）干涉仪中实现增强的共振谱与双稳（bistability）特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物质波版FP干涉仪中，原子间碰撞（interatomic collisions）会引起腔共振的非线性移动，导致透射峰展宽，使相互作用效应迄今未能被实验观测。研究动机在于寻找克服该限制的方法，以观测相互作用辅助的隧穿（interaction-assisted tunneling）效应。

Method: 理论上在BEC波包中工程化一个动量啁啾，使其与透射过程中腔的动力学平均场能量移动（dynamical mean-field energy shift）相平衡。以准一维（quasi-one-dimensional）冷原子BEC与一对光诱导高斯势垒（light-induced Gaussian potential barriers）构成的腔碰撞为具体方案，并标定所需最优啁啾、表征相互作用共振处近不连续透射谱的对比度。

Result: 预测在啁啾与平均场能量移动相平衡时，可获得增强的共振谱与双稳特征，以及原本因相互作用而无法观测的相互作用辅助隧穿效应；该物理现象可用上述准一维BEC方案直接观测，并给出了所需最优啁啾的标定结果与近不连续透射谱对比度的表征。

Conclusion: 研究结果使得物质波开关协议（matter-wave switching protocols）与高精度速度测量（high-precision velocity measurements）等技术成为可能，并给出了后者可实现灵敏度（sensitivity）的估计。

Abstract: While a Fabry-Perot (FP) cavity interferometer is a standard tool in modern optics, analogous physics can also be observed with matter waves such as Bose-Einstein condensates (BECs). Interatomic collisions in BECs can induce nonlinear features such as multi-valued transmission spectra for interferometry experiments. Observation of interaction effects in the matter-wave analogue of FP interferometry has not yet been experimentally achieved, mostly due to broadening of transmission peaks by interaction-induced shifts of cavity resonances. In this work, we theoretically show that this limitation can be overcome by engineering a momentum chirp in a BEC wavepacket. By balancing this chirp against the dynamical mean-field energy shift of the cavity during transmission, we predict enhanced resonant spectra and bistability features due to interaction-assisted tunneling that would be otherwise inaccessible. We show that this physics is readily observable with quasi-one-dimensional BECs of cold atoms colliding with a pair of light-induced Gaussian potential barriers forming the cavity. We also benchmark the requisite optimum chirp needed and characterize the contrast of near-discontinuous FP transmission spectra at an interacting resonance. Our findings enable the realization of technologies like matter-wave switching protocols, and high-precision velocity measurements; estimates of achievable sensitivity for the latter are provided.

</details>


### [161] [Quench dynamics and quantum flutter properties of one-dimensional attractive single-spin flipped Fermi gases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09431)
*Xiangguo Yin,Shulong Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究一维无相互作用费米气体（ideal Fermi gas）中带初始动量的吸引型自旋向下杂质（spin-down impurity）的非平衡动力学（nonequilibrium dynamics），借由贝特拟设（Bethe ansatz）精确解简化关联矩阵元，揭示了弱吸引区的束缚态与混合振荡行为，以及强吸引区的局域束缚态主导动力学与 Friedel 型振荡，并刻画了量子颤振（quantum flutter）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杂质非平衡动力学是超冷原子物理（ultracold-atom physics）的前沿课题，有助于揭示极化子（polaron）与集体激发（collective excitation）的微观机制。现有研究多集中于排斥相互作用体系，而吸引相互作用动力学（attractive-interaction dynamics）因不同态之间耦合复杂而缺乏系统研究。

Method: 研究对象为一维含吸引型自旋向下杂质（具初始动量）的理想费米气体。采用贝特拟设精确解（exact Bethe ansatz solutions），将关联矩阵元化简为有限求和，从而高效计算本征态占据数与长时间动力学演化；并使用四种独立的贝特拟设途径提取杂质临界化学势（critical chemical potential）。

Result: 弱吸引区（weakly attractive regime）：总动量小于或等于费米动量（Fermi momentum）时出现束缚态特征，总动量较大时呈现混合振荡行为。强吸引区（strongly attractive regime）：动力学由局域束缚态主导，表现为局域化关联峰与散射诱导的 Friedel 型振荡（Friedel-like oscillations）。此外刻画了量子颤振（quantum flutter），即杂质动量的周期性振荡，四种独立方法给出自洽的临界化学势值。

Conclusion: 该工作阐明了吸引相互作用下的淬火动力学（quench dynamics）与量子颤振，深化了对非平衡量子多体（quantum many-body）性质的理解，并为相关超冷原子杂质实验提供理论支持。

Abstract: Impurity nonequilibrium dynamics in quantum many-body systems is a frontier subject in ultracold-atom physics, which helps uncover microscopic mechanisms of polaron and collective excitation phenomena. Although repulsive interacting systems have been extensively investigated, attractive-interaction dynamics lacks systematic studies owing to intricate couplings among different states. We study a one-dimensional ideal Fermi gas containing an attractive spin-down impurity with initial momentum and analyze time evolution of interspin two-body correlations and impurity momentum. Using exact Bethe ansatz solutions, we simplify correlation matrix elements to finite sums for efficient calculations of eigenstate occupations and long-time dynamical evolution. In the weakly attractive regime, bound-state features appear when the total momentum is below or equal to the Fermi momentum, while mixed oscillatory behaviors arise at larger total momentum. The strongly attractive regime shows locally bound-state dominated dynamics with localized correlation peaks and scattering-induced Friedel-like oscillations. We characterize quantum flutter, the periodic oscillation of impurity momentum, and obtain consistent critical chemical potential values of the impurity via four independent Bethe ansatz approaches. This work clarifies quench dynamics and quantum flutter under attractive interactions, improves the understanding of nonequilibrium quantum many-body properties, and provides theoretical support for relevant ultracold-atom impurity experiments.

</details>


### [162] [Multi-scale spectral statistics of intermediate quantum chaos in ultracold erbium collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09775)
*Ivan R. R. Gonzalez,Barnali Chakrabarti,Antonio M. S. Macedo*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 对超冷 166Er 与 168Er 原子共振谱开展多尺度统计分析，结果表明其谱统计更符合半泊松（semi-Poisson）而非标准 Wigner-Dyson 系综，呈现介于可积与混沌之间的中间量子混沌（intermediate quantum chaos）特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有混合相空间（mixed phase spaces）的量子系统，其谱关联需在多个尺度上刻画，单一尺度诊断不足以判别中间量子混沌。该工作旨在通过多尺度统计量，检验此类系统谱统计究竟接近 Wigner-Dyson 还是半泊松分布。

Method: 采用高阶间距比（higher-order spacing ratios）、间距增量（spacing increments）以及抽稀能级序列（decimated level sequences）的累积功率谱密度（accumulated power spectral density）等多种统计工具，对超冷 166Er 和 168Er 原子的共振谱（resonance spectra）进行分析。

Result: 谱虽表现出明显的能级排斥（level repulsion），但整体更符合半泊松分布而非标准 Wigner-Dyson 系综，且该中间特征在高阶关联中持续存在。累积功率谱显示中间态的 1/f^alpha 区间：全谱分辨率下 alpha 约为 1.5，在中等抽稀尺度下趋向可积随机行走极限 alpha = 2，表明谱刚性（spectral rigidity）随尺度衰减。此外，间距增量分布独立地显示介于拉普拉斯（Laplace）与高斯（Gaussian）极限之间的统计特征。

Conclusion: 多种互补诊断一致表明，半泊松特征反映了超越最近邻尺度的持续非 Wigner-Dyson 关联，为中间量子混沌提供了自洽图像，并可能与底层碰撞动力学中的层级俘获（hierarchical trapping）存在联系。

Abstract: Characterizing intermediate quantum chaos in systems with mixed phase spaces requires probing spectral correlations across multiple scales. Here, we present a multi-scale statistical analysis of the resonance spectra of ultracold 166Er and 168Er atoms using higher-order spacing ratios, spacing increments, and the accumulated power spectral density of decimated level sequences. Our analysis reveals that while the spectra exhibit clear level repulsion, they are consistently better described by semi-Poisson than by standard Wigner-Dyson ensembles, with this intermediate character persisting across higher-order correlations. The accumulated power spectrum reveals an intermediate 1/f^alpha regime, with alpha approximately 1.5 at full spectral resolution and a tendency toward the integrable random-walk limit alpha = 2 at intermediate decimation scales, indicating a scale-dependent loss of spectral rigidity. Furthermore, the spacing-increment distributions independently reveal statistics intermediate between Laplace and Gaussian limits. Together, these complementary diagnostics show that the semi-Poisson character reflects persistent non-Wigner-Dyson correlations beyond the nearest-neighbor scale, providing a consistent picture of intermediate quantum chaos and a possible connection to hierarchical trapping in the underlying collision dynamics.

</details>


### [163] [Interaction-enhanced photon blockade and Bell-state protection in microwave-shielded polar molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09959)
*Yun Chen,Geng Zhao,Huanhuan Wei,Jingjun You,Haoran Jia,Jing Tang,Su Yi,Yuangang Deng*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该工作研究微波屏蔽（microwave shielding）极性分子与光学腔模耦合的腔量子电动力学（cavity QED）平台，证明屏蔽诱导的相互作用可统一实现光子阻塞（photon blockade）与贝尔态（Bell state）保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微波屏蔽已成为调控超冷极性分子相互作用的强有力工具，但其在腔量子电动力学控制方面的潜力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建两个微波屏蔽极性分子与单一光学腔模耦合的分子腔QED平台，分析各向异性相互作用对少激发谱非谐性（anharmonicity）的重塑，并考察光子统计对分子相对构型的敏感性。

Result: 各向异性相互作用增强谱非谐性，抑制多光子跃迁，使单光子纯度提升三个数量级以上；增强的阻塞伴随负纵向自旋关联（negative longitudinal spin correlations）出现；光子统计对分子相对构型高度敏感，相对零点涨落（zero-point fluctuation）量级的位置变化即可显著改变阻塞性能；同一相互作用通过色散解耦（dispersive decoupling）抑制腔介导耗散，减缓贝尔态保真度衰减。

Conclusion: 微波屏蔽相互作用可作为统一相互作用资源，用于工程化少光子非线性（few-photon nonlinearities）并保护分子腔QED体系中的量子态。

Abstract: Microwave shielding has recently emerged as a powerful tool for engineering interactions in ultracold polar molecules, yet its potential for controlling cavity quantum electrodynamics remains largely unexplored. Here, we investigate a molecular cavity quantum electrodynamics platform in which two microwave-shielded polar molecules are coupled to a single optical cavity mode and demonstrate that shielding-induced interactions provide a unified mechanism for both photon blockade and Bell-state protection. The anisotropic interaction reshapes the few-excitation spectrum by enhancing its anharmonicity, thereby suppressing multiphoton transitions and improving the single-photon purity by more than three orders of magnitude. The enhanced blockade is accompanied by the emergence of negative longitudinal spin correlations, revealing the interaction-induced suppression of simultaneous molecular excitations. We further show that the photon statistics are highly sensitive to the relative molecular configuration, with positional variations on the scale of the relative zero-point fluctuation substantially modifying the blockade performance. Beyond few-photon nonlinear optics, the same interaction protects an initially prepared molecular Bell state by dispersively decoupling molecular excitations from the lossy cavity mode, thereby suppressing cavity-mediated dissipation and slowing the fidelity decay. Our results establish microwave-shielded interactions as a unified interaction resource for engineering few-photon nonlinearities and protecting quantum states in molecular cavity-QED systems.

</details>

### [164] [On Bonart's interpretation of the Square-Root Impact Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10053)
*J. -P. Bouchaud,I. Mastromatteo,B. Toth*

Main category: q-fin.TR

TL;DR: 本文重新审视并简化了 Bonart 关于平方根冲击律（square-root impact law, SRIL）的推导，强调其核心前提是市场将每位参与者的元订单流（metaorder stream）而非孤立元订单白化（whitening）。作者指出 Lillo–Mike–Farmer 模型满足该前提却给出超平方根冲击，并构造多主体传播子（propagator）说明其仅在交易可归属时复现 SRIL；由此提出流假设的可证伪预测与经验数据不符，并以体积守恒论证揭示扩散性路径必须隐含的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SRIL 同时捆绑了两个需由单一机制解释的事实：形状（冲击正比于交易量 Q 的平方根）与幅度（Y=O(1) 且与参与率 φ 无关）。Bonart 提出的扩散性解释虽优雅，但其关键前提与经验含义尚待澄清。本文旨在重新表述该论证、检验其前提的自洽性，并揭示替代推导路径中隐含的假设。

Method: 作者重新表述并简化 Bonart 的论证，突出"市场白化的是每位参与者元订单的平稳流（stationary stream），而非任何孤立元订单"这一要素。随后检验 Lillo–Mike–Farmer 模型是否满足该前提，并构造一个显式的多主体传播子（multi-agent propagator）以实现 Bonart 的白化条件。此外，采用体积守恒（volume-conservation）论证独立推导平方根律。

Result: Lillo–Mike–Farmer 模型虽满足 Bonart 的前提，却对孤立元订单给出超平方根冲击 φ^{(1-γ)/2}Q^{(1+γ)/2}（γ∈(0,1)），原因是单个元订单在统计上不可见而被机械定价。所构造的多主体传播子仅在市场能将交易归属到其发起者时精确复现干净的 SRIL，而匿名交易的正确再归属（re-attribution）要求市场表现为不切实际的高效信号处理机器。流假设给出一个尖锐的可证伪预测——时间上充分分离的孤立元订单不应服从 SRIL——但该预测似乎与已知经验数据不符。体积守恒论证则表明：扩散性只固定冲击的方差（一种可加、平稳、与年龄无关的量），而 SRIL 是关于平均冲击的定律，其随年龄以 1/√age 边际衰减，因此需要一个时间原点。

Conclusion: Bonart 的扩散性论证依赖"市场白化元订单流"这一强假设，该假设导致的可证伪预测与经验证据相悖，且其匿名交易再归属要求市场具有不现实的高效率。此外，扩散性仅约束冲击方差而非均值，无法单独解释以 1/√age 衰减的平均冲击律；SRIL 需要一个时间原点，这是扩散性路径必须额外承担的假设。

Abstract: The square-root impact law (SRIL), $I = Yσ\sqrt{Q/V}$, bundles two facts that a single mechanism must explain at once: a shape (impact proportional to square-root of traded volume $Q$) and an amplitude ($Y=O(1)$, independent of the participation rate $\varphi$). Bonart has recently proposed an elegant solution: if realized and counterfactual prices are both diffusive, information-neutral impact must have white increments and the SRIL follows without the wart. We reformulate and simplify his argument, and foreground the ingredient he himself regards as essential --- that the market whitens the stationary stream of each participant's metaorders, not any isolated one. The Lillo--Mike--Farmer model satisfies his premises, yet gives an isolated metaorder the super-square-root impact $\varphi^{(1-γ)/2}Q^{(1+γ)/2}$, $γ\in(0,1)$, because single metaorders (i.e. not part of a stream) are statistically invisible and thus priced mechanically. We then construct an explicit multi-agent propagator that realizes Bonart's whitening, and show it reproduces the clean SRIL precisely when the market can attribute trades to their issuer. But correct re-attribution of anonymous trades is a formidable task, which requires markets to behave as implausibly efficient signal processing machines. The stream hypothesis makes a sharp, falsifiable prediction --- isolated metaorders, well separated in time, should not obey the SRIL --- that does not seem agree with empirical data known to us. Independently, a volume-conservation argument singles out the square-root law and lays bare the step the diffusivity route must assume: diffusivity fixes the variance of impact --- an additive, stationary, age-blind quantity --- whereas the SRIL is a law for the mean impact, which marginally decays as $1/\sqrt{\text{age}}$ and therefore demands an origin of time.

</details>


<div id='nlin.SI'></div>

# nlin.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [165] [Nonlinear Spectral Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09081)
*Ludovica Falsi,Francesco Coppini,Claudio Conti*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 该工作提出以非线性傅里叶变换（nonlinear Fourier transform）为核心的计算框架，利用非线性薛定谔方程（nonlinear Schrödinger equation）中波传播产生的非线性谱计算，将组合优化推广至高阶问题并实现布尔门。通过四个输入"令牌"（tokens），演示了对三变量全部256个布尔函数（含3-SAT实例）的可编程性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多机器学习算法与模拟计算、光学计算设备依赖傅里叶变换，其谱模平方可自然映射为成对相互作用（如Ising模型），从而用于组合优化。然而该线性框架的计算能力受限，作者希望借助非线性扩展突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 研究非线性薛定谔方程，引入由多个"令牌"（tokens，即以多步势能编码的批量信息）构成的输入，分析波传播中产生的非线性谱计算。分别在弱非线性区（nonlinearity以微扰修正形式生成高阶相互作用）和孤子区（solitonic regime，通过孤子生成实现离散非线性谱的数字计算）两种机制下讨论。

Result: 非线性谱计算不仅将组合优化推广到高阶问题，且可实现布尔门。使用四个输入令牌，展示了三变量全部256个布尔函数的可编程性，包括3-SAT实例。

Conclusion: 可积非线性传播（integrable nonlinear propagation）为高阶计算与逻辑计算提供了前所未有的单一物理平台，为模拟计算、机器学习以及经典与量子信息处理中的新算法开辟了道路。

Abstract: Many machine learning algorithms rely on the Fourier transform, which is also the inner core of many analog computing devices and, specifically, of optical computing. The square of the modulus of the Fourier spectrum naturally maps onto pairwise interactions, as in the renowned Ising model, making it applicable to combinatorial optimization. Here we show how the nonlinear Fourier transform, related to integrable nonlinear partial differential equations, significantly widens the computational capability and also enables a wide variety of computing gates. We study specifically the case of the nonlinear Schrödinger equation; we introduce an input composed by ``tokens'', i.e., batches of information encoded in a multi-step potential, and we show how the nonlinear spectral computing arising in the wave propagation not only generalizes combinatorial optimization to higher order problems, but also realizes boolean gates. In the weakly nonlinear regime, nonlinearity generates higher-order interactions as perturbative corrections to the pairwise terms. In the solitonic regime, the discrete nonlinear spectrum performs digital computation through soliton generation. By using four input tokens, we demonstrate the programmability of all 256 Boolean functions of three variables, including 3-SAT instances. Our results show that integrable nonlinear propagation provides an unprecedented single physical platform for higher-order and logical computation, opening the way to a rich variety of new algorithms for analog computing, machine learning, and classical and quantum information processing.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.soft'></div>

# cond-mat.soft [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [Phase-Field Fracture Simulation of Highly Deformable Thin Structures via a Discrete Differential Geometry Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08997)
*Bohan Zhang,Bo Wang,Huajiang Ouyang,Zhigang Wu,Yu Zhou,Weicheng Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的离散微分几何（discrete differential geometry, DDG）相场（phase-field）框架，用于高度可变形薄结构中的断裂，并结合退化-删除与拓扑重构以模拟裂纹完全张开及断后重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薄弹性柔性结构依赖大幅几何变形实现力学功能，其断裂行为与结构构型的演化强耦合，现有方法难以统一描述这种几何依赖的断裂过程。

Method: 在三角化中面上以离散几何量表述膜与弯曲弹性，将相场定义于同一离散表面以描述裂纹萌生与扩展；采用交错（staggered）格式求解耦合问题，用活动集（active-set）方法强制相场不可逆；通过退化-删除（degradation-deletion）移除近乎完全失效单元并重构 DDG 拓扑。

Result: 二维面内断裂预测在裂纹演化与力学响应上与几何非线性有限元（finite element）结果吻合；三维撕裂中可解析面外变形与裂纹扩展的耦合，包括实验中观察到的初始平行裂纹的汇聚与合并；在代表柔性电子的岛-桥（island-bridge）结构中复现了方向依赖变形模式、失稳与跃变（snap-through）以及构型与断裂的耦合演化。

Conclusion: 该框架是研究几何依赖断裂并支持柔性薄结构损伤容限设计的有效工具。

Abstract: Thin elastic flexible structures exploit large geometric deformation to achieve mechanical functionality, making their fracture behavior strongly coupled with the evolving structural configuration. This work presents a unified discrete differential geometry (DDG) phase-field framework for fracture in highly deformable thin structures. Membrane and bending elasticity are formulated from discrete geometric measures on a triangulated midsurface, while the phase field is defined on the same discrete surface to describe onset of crack growth and subsequent propagation. The coupled problem is solved using a staggered scheme, with phase-field irreversibility enforced by an active-set method. A degradation-deletion procedure removes nearly fully failed elements and reconstructs the DDG topology, enabling complete crack opening and substantial post-fracture reconfiguration. For two-dimensional in-plane fracture problems, the DDG predictions agree closely with geometrically nonlinear finite element results in both crack evolution and mechanical response. In three-dimensional tearing, the framework resolves the interaction between out-of-plane deformation and crack propagation, including the experimentally observed convergence and coalescence of initially parallel cracks. Its engineering applicability is further demonstrated using an island--bridge structure representative of flexible electronics. The simulations capture direction-dependent deformation modes, instability and snap-through during loading, and the coupled evolution of structural configuration and fracture. These results establish the proposed framework as an effective tool for investigating geometry-dependent fracture and supporting the damage-tolerant design of flexible thin structures.

</details>


### [167] [Contamination dynamics of active rods in long microchannels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09204)
*Jason Palos,Chase Brown,Mykhailo Potomkin,Shawn D. Ryan*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究以活性杆（active rods）为模型，结合三维蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulations）与一维两态速度跳跃模型（two-state velocity-jump model），刻画了压力驱动微通道中活性杆逆流扩散与浓度分布规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活性杆是模拟细菌、精子等运动微生物的有效模型，其在微流控通道中沿壁逆流游动，对导管与微流控器件的细菌污染具实际意义，故需理解其群体沿长通道的扩散行为。

Method: 采用分层建模：先对三维模型进行蒙特卡洛模拟，考察净逆流输运与流速、旋转扩散（rotational diffusion）、通道截面形状的关系；据此推导一维两态速度跳跃模型，对指数分布持续时长（persistence times）求连续注入浓度的演化方程及长时间极限的闭式解。

Result: 模拟显示净逆流输运随流速非单调变化、随旋转扩散减弱并依赖通道截面形状；上、下游持续时长服从指数衰减分布。分析表明，当平均上游速度为正时，源上游出现浓度平台（concentration plateau），否则呈指数衰减；用三维模拟参数驱动的一维模型重现了蒙特卡洛浓度剖面。

Conclusion: 一维两态速度跳跃模型能有效复现三维模拟结果，并与近期微流控器件中细菌污染的实验观测一致，为理解活性颗粒逆流输运与污染扩散提供了简化且可解析的框架。

Abstract: Active rods are elongated, self-propelled particles that serve as effective models for motile microorganisms, such as bacteria and sperm cells. When confined to a microfluidic channel, active rods exhibit a distinctive motility pattern: they tend to swim against the fluid flow along channel walls. In pressure-driven channel flow, they alternate between long upstream runs along the walls and short downstream spurts in the bulk. This behavior has practical implications for bacterial contamination of catheters and microfluidic devices against the flow. We study the spreading of a population of active rods along long microchannels using a hierarchy of models. Monte Carlo simulations of a three-dimensional model show that net upstream transport depends non-monotonically on the flow rate, decreases with rotational diffusion, and depends on the shape of the channel cross-section. The simulations also show that upstream and downstream persistence times follow exponentially decaying distributions. This motivates the derivation of a one-dimensional, two-state velocity-jump model. For exponentially distributed persistence times, we derive the evolution equation for the concentration of continuously injected rods and determine its long-time limit in closed form. This analysis reveals a concentration plateau upstream of the source when the mean upstream velocity is positive and exponential decay otherwise. Using parameters measured from the three-dimensional simulations, the one-dimensional model reproduces the concentration profiles from the Monte Carlo simulations, and these results are consistent with recent experimental observations of bacterial contamination in microfluidic devices.

</details>


### [168] [Multiscale Decoupling in Peptide-Membrane Interactions: From Elastic Softening to Domain-Mediated Stiffening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09595)
*Veerendra K. Sharma,Shuo Qian,Gergely Nagy,Sonam Raghav,Sajal K. Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究结合中子自旋回波（NSE）、中子膜衍射（NMD）、压力-面积（P-A）等温线及已有准弹性中子散射（QENS）数据，揭示aurein对脂质膜力学响应具有组成依赖性（zwitterionic DMPC持续软化，anionic DMPC/DMPG非单调先软化后硬化），并发现分子尺度脂质横向扩散与介观集体刚度可解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 膜组成在决定抗菌肽（AMPs）如何与脂质膜相互作用并改变其物理性质中起核心作用，但组成依赖的力学与结构响应机制尚待阐明。

Method: 采用中子自旋回波（NSE）谱、中子膜衍射（NMD）、压力（P）-面积（A）等温线，并辅以已报道的准弹性中子散射（QENS）测量，研究aurein作用于zwitterionic DMPC与anionic DMPC/DMPG膜的力学与结构响应。

Result: DMPC膜中aurein引起持续软化（弯曲刚度和面积压缩模量随浓度下降），NMD显示轻微双层变薄但氘代烃区无显著肽贡献；DMPC/DMPG膜呈非单调响应，先软化后显著硬化；P-A等温线独立重现上述相反力学响应；NSE与QENS联合揭示多尺度解耦：aurein抑制分子尺度脂质横向扩散，同时降低介观集体膜刚度。

Conclusion: 膜组成决定aurein的集体力学响应，而分子脂质动力学可在更短长度与时间尺度上独立响应，表明分子脂质动力学与集体膜力学可在同一肽扰动下呈相反演化方向。

Abstract: Membrane composition plays a central role in determining how antimicrobial peptides (AMPs) interact with and alter the physical properties of lipid membranes. Here, we investigate the composition-dependent mechanical and structural response of lipid membranes to the AMP aurein using neutron spin-echo (NSE) spectroscopy, neutron membrane diffraction (NMD), and pressure (P)-area (A) isotherms, complemented by previously reported quasielastic neutron scattering (QENS) measurements. In a zwitterionic DMPC membrane, aurein induces progressive softening, manifested by a concentration-dependent decrease in both the bending rigidity and area compressibility modulus. NMD reveals a slight bilayer thinning but no prominent peptide-associated contribution within the deuterated hydrocarbon region, indicating that the progressive mechanical softening is not accompanied by increasingly deep peptide penetration into the hydrophobic core. In contrast, anionic phosphatidylglycerol (PG)-containing DMPC/DMPG membranes exhibit a non-monotonic response, with initial softening followed by pronounced stiffening at higher peptide concentrations. Complementary P-A isotherm measurements independently reproduce these contrasting mechanical responses, showing progressive softening of DMPC but stiffening of DMPC/DMPG. Most importantly, the combined NSE and QENS results reveal a striking multiscale decoupling: aurein suppresses molecular-scale lipid lateral diffusion while simultaneously reducing collective membrane stiffness at the mesoscopic scale, demonstrating that molecular lipid dynamics and collective membrane mechanics can evolve in opposite directions under the same peptide perturbation.Together, these results show that membrane composition governs the collective mechanical response to aurein, while molecular lipid dynamics can respond independently at shorter length and time scales.

</details>


### [169] [Resolving the Effects of the Mixing Process on Hierarchical Structures in Polymer Nanocomposites by Bayesian Ultra-Small-Angle X-ray Scattering Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09653)
*Yui Hayashi,Kazuki Mita,Shigeo Kuwamoto,Masato Okada*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该工作将贝叶斯推断（Bayesian inference）与统一拟合（Unified Fit）结合，用于区分常规分析难以分辨的聚合物纳米复合材料（polymer nanocomposites）超小角X射线散射（ultra-small-angle X-ray scattering, USAXS）曲线，并据此识别出与力学性能相关的团聚体结构描述符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同混合工艺制备的聚合物纳米复合材料，其USAXS曲线在常规分析下几乎无法区分，因而难以解析混合历史对结构的影响，也缺乏考察加工—结构—性能关系的不确定性量化基础。

Method: 采用贝叶斯统一拟合（Bayesian Unified Fit），对三种经不同混合工艺制备的炭黑（carbon black）填充三元乙丙共聚橡胶（ethylene propylene diene copolymer rubber）样品，比较其团聚体尺寸、内部质量分形结构（mass-fractal structure）与表面粗糙度。

Result: 常规统一拟合（Unified Fit）显示三个样品的炭黑分散几乎相同；而贝叶斯统一拟合给出的回转半径（radius of gyration）、质量分形维数（mass fractal dimension）与表面分形维数（surface fractal dimension）在三个样品间呈现一致的排序，该排序与报道的拉伸强度（tensile strength）和断裂伸长率（elongation at break）排序相吻合。

Conclusion: 结果表明推断出的团聚体层级结构与宏观力学响应具有物理意义，并识别出团聚体尺寸可作为橡胶增强（rubber reinforcement）的候选结构描述符，同时为聚合物纳米复合材料中加工—结构—性能关系的研究提供了具备不确定性意识（uncertainty-aware）的基础。

Abstract: Bayesian inference was combined with Unified Fit to resolve the effects of the mixing history on polymer nanocomposites whose ultra-small-angle X-ray scattering profiles are nearly indistinguishable by conventional analysis. Bayesian Unified Fit was applied to comparing the aggregate size, internal mass-fractal structure, and surface roughness for three carbon black--filled ethylene propylene diene copolymer rubber samples prepared by different mixing processes. Whereas conventional Unified Fit reported essentially identical carbon-black dispersions, the radius of gyration, mass fractal dimension, and surface fractal dimension for the three samples all followed the same ordering, which coincides with the reported ordering of the tensile strength and elongation at break and suggests that the inferred aggregate hierarchy is physically meaningful for the macroscopic mechanical response. These results identify the aggregate size as a candidate structural descriptor of rubber reinforcement and provide an uncertainty-aware basis for examining processing--structure--property relationships in polymer nanocomposites.

</details>


### [170] [Self-organization in Chiral Gliding Filaments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10099)
*Josue Yaedalm Son,Hyejeong Kim,Vahid Nasirimarekan*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究揭示丝状蓝细菌（filamentous cyanobacteria）通过手性滑行（chiral gliding）驱动个体丝体间的主动缠结（active tangling），引发网络快速收缩与致密聚集，并证明其机械相互作用产生显著收缩应力（contractile stresses），从而驱动宏观尺度自组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 丝状蓝细菌在水生环境中可形成千米级的菌膜（mats），但其宏观自组织背后的物理机制尚不明确，此为本文的研究动机。

Method: 将丝状蓝细菌组装体置于准二维（quasi-two-dimensional）受限空间中进行动力学观测，并定量测定单接触力（single-contact forces）。

Result: 观测到手性滑行驱动个体丝体之间的主动缠结，导致网络快速收缩并形成密集堆积的聚集体（aggregates）；单接触力定量结果显示这些机械相互作用产生显著收缩应力。

Conclusion: 手性滑行可能已进化为一种物理机制，用以最大化丝体间相互作用并驱动大规模集体组织。

Abstract: Filamentous cyanobacteria form extensive kilometer-scale mats in aquatic environments, yet the physical mechanisms driving their macroscopic self-organization remain elusive. Here, we investigate the dynamics of filamentous cyanobacterial assemblies within a quasi-two-dimensional confinement. Our observations reveal that chiral gliding drives active tangling between individual filaments, inducing rapid network contraction and the formation of densely packed aggregates. By quantifying single-contact forces, we demonstrate that these mechanical interactions generate significant contractile stresses. Together, our findings sug- gest that chiral gliding has evolved as a physical mechanism to maximize inter-filament interactions and drive large-scale collective organization.

</details>
