### [171] [Sensitivity targets for dark photon searches in charged kaon decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08911)
*M. Fabbrichesi,E. Gabrielli,K. Müürsepp*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在带电K介子衰变中通过双π道和单π辐射道探测不可见暗光子的前景，并首次将辐射过程作为互补探针、同时将分析扩展至有质量暗光子。结果显示在低暗光子质量区域三体模式尤为关键，其主导贡献来自维度五的磁偶极味变夸克相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有的双π道 K⁺→π⁺π⁰γ_D 曾被提议为 NA62 上无质量暗光子搜寻的黄金道，但缺乏互补探针且未覆盖有质量暗光子情形。由于两体衰变 K⁺→π⁺γ_D 在低暗光子质量下被强烈压低并在无质量极限下消失，需要引入三体模式以拓展探测覆盖范围。

Method: 引入辐射过程 K⁺→π⁺γ γ_D 作为互补探针，并将两种三体模式分析扩展至有质量暗光子。指出无质量情形下不存在由动能混合（kinetic mixing）诱导的可重整化相互作用，主导贡献来自维度五磁偶极算符所中介的有效非可重整化味变夸克相互作用 s→d γ_D；有质量情形下动能混合在现有混合参数限制下贡献可忽略。采用手征夸克模型（chiral quark model）估计偶极算符的相关强子矩阵元，并纳入 K⁺→π⁺X（共振缺失能量）、K⁺→π⁺νν̄ 及 K⁰–K̄⁰ 混合的约束。

Result: 基于上述计算，确定了这些衰变模式所允许的分支比（branching ratios），以及 NA62 和未来带电K介子设施所需的探测灵敏度（sensitivities）。

Conclusion: 三体衰变模式（双π道与单π辐射道）在有质量与无质量暗光子情形下均为有效探测通道，其主导机制为味变磁偶极算符；所得分支比与灵敏度要求可为 NA62 及未来带电K介子设施的暗光子搜寻提供理论依据。

Abstract: We investigate the prospects for detecting invisible dark photons in charged kaon decays through the two-pion channel $K^+\toπ^+π^0\,γ_D$ and the one-pion radiative decay $K^+\toπ^+γ\,γ_D$, where $γ$ and $γ_D$ denote the ordinary photon and dark photon, respectively. The former decay was previously proposed as a golden channel for massless dark photon searches at NA62, while here we introduce the radiative process as a complementary probe and extend both analyses to massive dark photons. The three-body modes are particularly relevant at low dark photon masses, where the two-body decay $K^+\toπ^+\, γ_D$ becomes strongly suppressed and vanishes in the massless limit. For massless dark photons, renormalizable interactions induced by kinetic mixing are absent, and the dominant contribution arises from the effective non-renormalizable flavor-changing quark interaction $s\to d\, γ_D$ mediated by a dimension-five magnetic-dipole operator. This is also true for massive dark photons. Their kinetic mixing contributes negligibly to these channels, after considering the existing bounds on the mixing parameter. Only the same flavor changing magnetic-dipole operator as in the massless case is relevant. Using the chiral quark model, we estimate the relevant hadronic matrix elements of the dipole operator and account for constraints from $K^+\toπ^+\,X$, where $X$ denotes resonant missing energy, $K^+\toπ^+ν\barν$, and $K^0\!\!-\!\bar{K}^0$ mixing. Based on these calculations, we determine the allowed branching ratios for these decay modes and the sensitivities required at NA62 and future charged-kaon facilities.

</details>


### [172] [Phenomenology of axion-meson mixing and scattering processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10084)
*Noé Duarte-González*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在手征微扰理论（chiral perturbation theory, χPT）框架下研究类轴子粒子（axion-like particle, ALP）与轻赝标量介子的混合，及其对低能散射与衰变过程的影响。工作推导了领头阶的动能与质量混合、给出物理ALP质量至 O(F²/f_a²) 的修正，并构建了含任意数目ALP的四体振幅紧凑参数化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALP与赝标量介子之间的混合会影响低能散射与衰变过程，而现有处理通常针对特定质量或耦合情形；同时同位旋破缺（isospin breaking）对于某些原本禁戒的过程至关重要，需要更一般且自洽的框架。

Method: 从最一般的ALP有效拉格朗日量（effective Lagrangian）出发，在ALP衰变常数与同位旋破缺的领头阶推导ALP-介子动能混合与质量混合，适用于任意ALP质量；并由此构造包含赝标量介子与任意数目ALP的四体振幅的紧凑参数化。

Result: 得到物理ALP质量至 O(F²/f_a²) 的表达式，重现QCD轴子（QCD axion）极限，并发现任何裸质量（bare mass）为零的ALP都共享QCD轴子质量。以 K K̄ → P⁰ a 散射为例展示了所给振幅参数化的应用。

Conclusion: 该框架适用于一般ALP质量与耦合，且自洽地纳入同位旋破缺，这对原本禁戒的过程是必需的。

Abstract: We study the mixing between a generic axion-like particle (ALP) and the light pseudoscalar mesons within chiral perturbation theory ($χ$PT), and its consequences for low-energy scattering and decay processes. Starting from the most general ALP effective Lagrangian, we derive the ALP-meson kinetic and mass mixing induced at leading order in the ALP decay constant and isospin breaking for arbitrary ALP masses. As a result, we obtain the physical ALP mass to $\mathcal{O}(F^2/f_a^2)$, recovering the QCD-axion limit and finding that any ALP with a vanishing bare mass shares the QCD-axion mass. We provide a compact parameterization of every four-body amplitude involving pseudoscalar mesons and an arbitrary number of ALPs. As an illustration, we show the $K\bar{K}\to P^0a$ scattering. The framework applies for general ALP masses and couplings and consistently incorporates isospin breaking, essential for otherwise forbidden processes.

</details>


### [173] [Search prospect for electrophilic bosons at the $μ$TRISTAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10185)
*Amit Adhikary,Anindya Datta,Dilip Kumar Ghosh*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出，与电子主导耦合的中介粒子（mediator）在B工厂（B factory）运动学上限之上仍缺乏限制，而拟议的μTRISTAN对撞机的非对称μ⁺e⁻模式（√s=346 GeV）适合覆盖该窗口。针对轴子样粒子（axion-like particle）与Z'规范玻色子（Z' gauge boson）两类最小实现，通过四轻子（four-lepton）末态中的电子-正电子不变质量共振搜索，给出耦合量级约10⁻⁵的95% CL预期灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与电子主要耦合的中介粒子在B工厂运动学范围之上约束很弱，该区域在强子对撞机（hadron collider）上难以触及，且现有e⁺e⁻数据仅部分覆盖。因此需要一种能覆盖该质量窗口的实验方案，以填补约束空白。

Method: 利用拟议μTRISTAN对撞机的非对称μ⁺e⁻模式，工作能量√s=346 GeV。将轴子样粒子与Z'玻色子作为与电子流（electron current）的有效相互作用（effective interactions）处理，二者自电子线辐射并衰变为电子-正电子对，形成共同的四轻子特征，其标准模型（Standard Model）对应过程可控。在电子-正电子不变质量谱中进行共振搜索（resonance search），中介粒子质量范围取5.5–300 GeV。

Result: 在5.5–300 GeV质量区间，预测95% CL灵敏度在质量较轻端达到约10⁻⁵的耦合量级。相较现有上限，赝标量（pseudoscalar）情形改善超过四个数量级，矢量（vector）情形改善最多达两个数量级，并对所考虑的最重质量提供唯一约束。

Conclusion: 非对称μ⁺e⁻模式的μTRISTAN对撞机在346 GeV能量下可有效探测B工厂之上、强子对撞机难以触及的轻中介粒子质量窗口，对轴子样粒子与Z'玻色子均给出显著优于现有界限的预期灵敏度。

Abstract: Mediators coupled predominantly to the electron remain weakly constrained above the kinematic reach of the $B$~factories, a region that is difficult to access at hadron colliders and only partially covered by existing $e^+e^-$ data. We point out that the asymmetric $μ^+e^-$ mode of the proposed $μ$TRISTAN collider, operating at $\sqrt{s}=346$~GeV, is well suited to this window, and quantify its reach for two minimal realisations: an axion-like particle and a $Z'$ gauge boson, treated as effective interactions with the electron current. Both are radiated off the electron line and decay to an electron-positron pair, giving a common four-lepton signature whose Standard Model counterpart is under good control. A resonance search in the electron-positron invariant mass, performed for mediator masses between $5.5$ and $300$~GeV, yields projected $95\%$~CL sensitivities to couplings of order $10^{-5}$ at the light end of this range, improving on existing bounds by more than four orders of magnitude for the pseudoscalar and by up to two orders of magnitude for the vector, and providing the only constraint on the heaviest masses considered.

</details>


### [174] [Toward Simulations of Cold Baryogenesis via Supercooled Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09257)
*Naoya Kitajima,Shota Nakagawa,Yuichiro Nakai,Yaoduo Wang,Junxuan Xu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过三维格点模拟（lattice simulation）研究两场模型中强过冷一级相变的冷重子生成（cold baryogenesis），发现即使势强非简并（non-degenerate）、潜热大，Chern-Simons 数产生仍高效，冷重子生成在此区间依然可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过冷一级相变（supercooled first-order phase transition）可产生可观测引力波（gravitational waves, GWs），但伴随的熵注入（entropy injection）会稀释原有重子不对称（baryon asymmetry），故需在相变期间或之后实现重子生成。

Method: 对两场模型进行三维格点模拟，其中负责相变的标量场与希格斯（Higgs）部分分离；采用由 bounce 解得到的现实初始条件，追踪气泡膨胀与碰撞并研究 Chern-Simons 数的产生，并用有效 CP 破坏算符（effective CP-violating operator）估算重子不对称。

Result: 即使势强非简并且潜热（latent heat）大（有利于增强 GW 信号），Chern-Simons 数仍能高效产生；基于模拟的 Chern-Simons 动力学与有效 CP 破坏算符，可估算出相应的重子不对称。

Conclusion: 结果表明冷重子生成（cold baryogenesis）在强过冷区间依然可行，为从宇宙学一级相变建立重子生成迈出重要一步。

Abstract: Supercooled first-order phase transitions provide an attractive framework for generating observable gravitational waves (GWs), but the accompanying entropy injection dilutes any pre-existing baryon asymmetry, motivating baryogenesis during or after the phase transition. Cold baryogenesis offers a promising mechanism in which bubble collisions generate Higgs winding configurations and the Chern-Simons number through out-of-equilibrium dynamics. In the present study, we perform three-dimensional lattice simulations of a two-field model where the scalar field responsible for the phase transition is separated from the Higgs sector. Using realistic initial conditions obtained from bounce solutions, we follow the bubble expansion and collisions and study the generation of Chern-Simons number. It is found that efficient Chern-Simons production persists even for strongly non-degenerate potentials with large latent heat, which are favorable for enhancing GW signals. We also estimate the resulting baryon asymmetry using the simulated Chern-Simons dynamics and an effective CP-violating operator. Our results demonstrate that cold baryogenesis remains viable in the strongly supercooled regime, providing an important step toward establishing baryogenesis from cosmological first-order phase transitions.

</details>


### [175] [Tensor-structure sum rules for spin-1 targets at all $Q^2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09862)
*Vladimir Pascalutsa,Marc Vanderhaeghen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文基于精确的 Siegert 关系（Siegert relations）推导出自旋为 1 及更高自旋靶的张量结构函数（tensor structure functions）的一族求和规则（sum rules），并给出可实验检验的受保护矩（protected moment）组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对自旋 1 及以上靶的张量结构函数，需要源于精确对称性关系的求和规则，以约束极化张量分布并连接已有的理论框架（如 HJM 基、twist-two OPE 与 ET 型条件）。

Method: 以精确 Siegert 关系为基础，利用混合的秩二（rank-two）TT–LT 退化（degeneracy）组合相反交叉宇称（opposite crossing parity）的振幅，在超收敛（superconvergence）假设下导出积分关系；并借助加权 HJM 矩确定相应的减除函数（subtraction functions）。

Result: 在 Hoodbhoy–Jaffe–Manohar（HJM）基中给出 ∫₀¹ dx [b₂(x,Q²) − 3b₄(x,Q²)] = 0；在大 Q² 下该受保护矩与 Detmold 的靶质量完备（target-mass-complete）twist-two OPE 一致；在严格 Bjorken 投影且忽略 b₄ 时，约化为 Efremov–Teryaev（ET）型条件 ∫₀¹ dx x b₁(x) = 0，其测量范围内的表现与 HERMES 数据相符。

Conclusion: 受保护组合可通过分离的张量 LT 与 TT 响应被独立提取，为 JLab 及未来 EIC 提供直接的实验检验策略。

Abstract: We derive a family of sum rules for the tensor structure functions of targets with spin one or higher, rooted in exact Siegert relations. The mixed rank-two $TT$--$LT$ degeneracy combines amplitudes of opposite crossing parity and, under the superconvergence assumption, gives $\int_0^1\! d x\,[b_2(x,Q^2)-3b_4(x,Q^2)]=0$ in the Hoodbhoy--Jaffe--Manohar (HJM) basis. At large $Q^2$, the protected moment agrees with Detmold's target-mass-complete twist-two OPE; in the strict Bjorken projection, and if $b_4$ is neglected, it reduces to the Efremov--Teryaev (ET)-type condition $\int_0^1\! d x\,x\,b_1(x)=0$, whose measured-range footprint is consistent with the HERMES data. Two further rank-two combinations connect crossing-even amplitudes and determine the corresponding subtraction functions through weighted HJM moments. The protected combination can be isolated through separated tensor $LT$ and $TT$ responses, providing a direct experimental strategy for JLab and, ultimately, a future EIC.

</details>


<div id='physics.geo-ph'></div>

# physics.geo-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [Quantification of plastic deformation in anorthite using micropillar compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10081)
*Sarah Incel,Chunhua Tian,Anja Schreiber,Vladimir Roddatis,Sandra Korte-Kerzel*

Main category: physics.geo-ph

TL;DR: 本研究采用微柱压缩（micropillar compression）技术，定量测定了钙长石（anorthite）中特定滑移系（slip system）的强度及机械孪生（mechanical twinning）所需应力，揭示其晶体塑性（crystal plasticity）各机制的相对难易程度与温度依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 斜长石（plagioclase）是地壳中最丰富的矿物群，可能控制或强烈影响地壳流变学（crustal rheology），但其晶体塑性认识有限，因为多数实验条件下脆性变形（brittle deformation）占据主导。为克服过去量化斜长石晶体塑性变形的困难，本研究聚焦钙长石开展定量测量。

Method: 利用聚焦离子束（focused-ion beam）从同一钙长石单晶的三个不同取向切面加工出直径约1微米的微柱，在扫描电子显微镜（scanning-electron microscope）内对共36个微柱进行单轴变形，温度分别为25、300或800摄氏度。多数微柱以恒定位移速率变形，对应约0.001 1/s的应变率。

Result: 机械（钠长石律/pericline）孪生的临界分切应力（critical resolved shear stress, CRSS）约为300 MPa，且如其他材料研究预期，对温度或应变率变化不敏感。取向为激活滑移的晶体微柱屈服应力随温度升高显著下降。在所施加条件下，孪生是最易激活的变形机制，其次为(011)[100]滑移（与温度无关）；而(011)[0-11]或(0-11)[011]滑移始终是最难激活的机制。

Conclusion: 微柱压缩可有效量化钙长石的晶体塑性机制强度；孪生最易、特定滑移系最难，且不同机制的屈服行为对温度呈现差异化响应，为理解斜长石及地壳流变学提供实验依据。

Abstract: Plagioclase is the most abundant mineral group of the crust and will therefore either control or strongly influence crustal rheology. Yet, we still know little about crystal plasticity in plagioclase as brittle deformation dominates under most laboratory conditions. To overcome past challenges in quantifying crystal plastic deformation in plagioclase, we used micropillar compression to quantify the strength of specific slip systems as well as the stresses necessary for mechanical twinning in anorthite. Pillars with diameters of around 1 micrometre were milled from three differently oriented cuts of the same anorthite single crystals using focused-ion beam. Uniaxial deformation of 36 pillars in total was then conducted inside a scanning-electron microscope under either 25, 300, or 800 degrees Celsius. Most pillars were deformed with a constant displacement rate that corresponds to a strain rate of around 0.001 1/s on the pillars. We find that the critical resolved shear stress (CRSS) for mechanical (pericline) twinning lies at around 300 MPa and is, as expected from previous studies on other materials, insensitive to changes in temperature or strain rate. Pillar deformation in crystals oriented to activate slip exhibit a strong decrease in yield stress with increasing temperature. Among the deformation mechanisms active during pillar deformation, twinning is the easiest under the applied conditions, followed by slip on (011)[100] regardless of temperature. Slip on (011)[0-11] or (0-11)[011] is consistently the hardest mechanism to activate in our anorthite pillars.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [Nonequivalence of expected free energy and revised system integrated information in noisy permutation networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03735)
*Katsuaki Tanabe*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该论文辨析自由能原理（free-energy principle, FEP）与整合信息论（integrated information theory, IIT）的关系：修正版 IIT 的局部量可写成惊奇度（surprisal）与变分自由能（variational free energy）形式，但标量期望自由能（expected free energy）无法决定系统整合信息，证实局部对应与全局不等价并存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于厘清 FEP/主动推断（active inference）与 IIT 两类理论能否相互归约，具体拆分为两问：修正版 IIT 量是否具有局部自由能式表示，以及标量期望自由能能否决定系统整合信息。

Method: 方法上先证明 2026 版 IIT 的内在分化（intrinsic differentiation）与规定（specification）项存在精确惊奇度表示，进而给出精确推断下的变分自由能表示；随后构造因果拓扑可变而局部转移可靠性固定的含噪二元置换网络（noisy binary permutation networks），并在同质状态下比较两类量。

Result: 结果发现：在同质状态、共同偏好与观测模型下，各置换给出一致的单步预测分布与状态风险期望自由能，但修正版 IIT 可区分多循环置换（整流整合为零）与单一含噪循环（中等可靠性下整合为正）；对 n 单元含噪单循环推导出系统整合信息的闭式解，证明最小信息划分（minimum-information partition）恰切断两条因果边，并给出 n 增大时最优可靠性趋近 1、联合规定状态概率趋近 1/2、最大整合趋近 1 ibit 的渐近行为；n=2-5 的数值计算与 n=8 的穷举划分枚举均复现解析结果。

Conclusion: 结论指出，局部惊奇度/自由能对应与全局非等价性可以共存：相等的期望自由能并不意味着相等的修正版 IIT 系统整合信息，因此任何 IIT 与 FEP/主动推断的融合都必须保留超越标量自由能值的反事实因果信息（counterfactual causal information）。

Abstract: The free-energy principle (FEP) and active inference describe adaptive systems through probabilistic generative models and free-energy functionals, whereas integrated information theory (IIT) characterizes intrinsic cause-effect organization. We separate two questions: whether revised IIT quantities admit local free-energy-like representations and whether scalar expected free energy can determine system integrated information. We show that the 2026 IIT intrinsic-differentiation and specification terms admit exact surprisal representations, and hence variational-free-energy representations under exact inference. We then construct noisy binary permutation networks whose causal topology varies while local transition reliability is fixed. In a homogeneous state, all permutations yield the same one-step predictive distribution and state-risk expected free energy under common preferences and observation models, yet revised IIT distinguishes them: multicycle permutations have zero whole-system integration, whereas a single noisy cycle has positive integration at intermediate reliability. For an n-unit noisy single cycle, we derive a closed-form expression for revised system integrated information, prove that the minimum-information partition cuts exactly two causal edges, and show that as n grows the optimal reliability approaches unity, the joint specified-state probability approaches one half, and the maximum integration approaches one ibit. Numerical IIT calculations for n = 2-5 and exhaustive partition enumeration through n = 8 reproduce the analytical results. Thus, local surprisal/free-energy correspondence coexists with global non-equivalence: equal expected free energy does not imply equal revised IIT system integrated information. Any synthesis of IIT with FEP/active inference must retain counterfactual causal information beyond a scalar free-energy value.

</details>


### [178] [Structure alone supports efficient visual computation in the Drosophila visual system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10023)
*Eudald Correig-Fraga,Roger Guimerà,Marta Sales-Pardo*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该研究将成年果蝇（Drosophila melanogaster）校对后的连接组（connectome）与解剖学上忠实的复眼模型耦合，构建仅依赖连接组的视觉计算模型，并证明实测连接与眼几何共同设定了高效视觉计算的运行点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是实测的突触接线（synaptic wiring）在多大程度上决定计算。研究旨在检验在布线经济性（wiring economy）约束下，精确连接是否具有功能后果。

Method: 视觉信息输入眼模型，再传至连接组，最终由以凯尼恩细胞（Kenyon cell）为中心的线性解码器（linear decoder）读出。该模型固定解剖图与眼几何，仅可学习标量突触增益（synaptic gains）与神经元阈值；并将生物图与逐步保留生物突触约束的随机化集合进行对比。

Result: 模型支持多任务视觉（multitask vision），包括颜色辨别、形状分类，以及符合近似数量感知（approximate number perception）比率依赖标度的数量辨别。在匹配布线成本（matched wiring cost）下，生物网络准确率持续更高；而当约束更少的重接线（rewiring）以布线膨胀为代价时可超越生物网络。

Conclusion: 实测连接与眼几何共同为视觉计算设定了高效运行点（efficient operating points）。

Abstract: Understanding the extent to which measured synaptic wiring determines computation remains a central challenge. Here, we couple the proofread adult Drosophila melanogaster connectome to an anatomically faithful model of its eye. Visual information is inputted in the eye model, then passed to the connectome, and finally read from a Kenyon-cell-centered linear decoder. This creates a connectome-only model in which the anatomical graph and eye geometry are fixed and only scalar synaptic gains and neuronal thresholds may be learned. The model supports multitask vision, including color discrimination, shape classification, and numerical discrimination that follows a ratio-dependent scaling characteristic of approximate number perception. To test whether precise connectivity is consequential under wiring economy, we compare the biological graph to randomized ensembles that increasingly preserve biological synaptic constraints. At matched wiring cost, the biological network consistently yields higher accuracy, whereas less constrained rewiring surpasses it at the cost of inflated wiring. These findings indicate that the measured connectivity and eye geometry jointly set efficient operating points for visual computation.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [Spacing statistics for a point scatterer on the cubic three-torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10031)
*Christopher Lutsko*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究三次三环面（cubic three-torus）上不动点相互作用（fixed point interaction）的新特征值，其归一化相邻间距收敛于一个与相互作用参数无关的概率分布，并通过三平方同余密度（three-squares congruence densities）构造的随机亚纯函数的零点刻画该分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究三次三环面上不动点相互作用的新特征值谱统计，旨在揭示其相邻间距是否存在与相互作用参数无关的普适极限分布，并给出该分布的显式刻画。

Method: 证明结合三种手段：周期均方逼近（periodic mean-square approximation）、加权希尔伯特变换估计（weighted Hilbert-transform estimate），以及保留素数间相互作用的傅里叶估计（Fourier estimates）。分布由三平方同余密度构造的随机亚纯函数（random meromorphic function）的连续零点刻画。

Result: 均值归一化的连续间距（mean-one consecutive spacings）收敛到一个不依赖相互作用参数的概率律；其小间隙分布（small-gap distribution）形如 s^5Ψ(log_2(s^{-2}))+o(s^5)，其中 Ψ 连续、为正且单周期。

Conclusion: 新特征值的间距统计具有参数无关的普适极限律，该律可由三平方同余密度生成的随机亚纯函数零点描述，其小间隙行为由连续正的单周期函数 Ψ 控制。

Abstract: We study the new eigenvalues of a fixed point interaction on the cubic three-torus. Their mean-one consecutive spacings converge to a probability law independent of the interaction parameter. We characterize this law by the consecutive zeros of a random meromorphic function built from three-squares congruence densities. Its small-gap distribution has the form $s^5Ψ(\log_2(s^{-2}))+o(s^5)$, where $Ψ$ is continuous, positive, and one-periodic. The proof combines periodic mean-square approximation, a weighted Hilbert-transform estimate, and Fourier estimates that retain the interactions between primes.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [180] [Predicting activation-barrier and plasticity-onset statistics in a model of glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09619)
*Makoto Suda,Edan Lerner,Eran Bouchbinder*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该研究将非线性模式（nonlinear modes）框架应用于一个非谐平均场模型（anharmonic mean-field model），解析预测了激活势垒与外力的渐近分布及其极值标度，并通过高效采样算法数值验证，推进了对玻璃态能量景观（energy landscape）与激活动力学的理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该非谐平均场模型此前已统一重现多种低温玻璃现象（包括非声子谱性质、线性微观力学与强驱动弹塑性动力学），暗示其能量景观（energy landscape）本质上是玻璃态的。因此需要进一步刻画该景观的激活势垒与塑性起始行为，并将结论推广至有限维玻璃与真实玻璃。

Method: 采用非线性模式（nonlinear modes）框架对模型进行解析推导，得到激活势垒分布 p(ΔU)∼(ΔU)^{1/4} 与塑性起始外力分布 p(f_c)∼f_c^{2/3} 的渐近形式、极值标度（extreme-value scaling）及超出渐近区间的 ⟨ΔU⟩。同时开发高效算法用于采样模型玻璃势能景观的极小值（minima）与鞍点（saddles），并识别出与激活玻璃动力学物理相关的集体自由度子集。

Result: 解析预测给出的势垒分布标度 p(ΔU)∼(ΔU)^{1/4}、外力阈值分布标度 p(f_c)∼f_c^{2/3}、极值标度以及渐近区间之外的 ⟨ΔU⟩ 均通过数值采样得到定量确认。

Conclusion: 上述解析预测预期同时适用于平均场模型与有限维玻璃，所识别的相关集体自由度同样应适用于现实玻璃，从而为激活玻璃动力学提供了可推广的理论与计算框架。

Abstract: A recently introduced anharmonic mean-field model unifiedly reproduced a broad range of low-temperature glass phenomena --- including harmonic nonphononic spectral properties, linear micromechanics and strongly driven elasto-plastic dynamics --- indicating that its underlying energy landscape is intrinsically glassy. Here, we apply a nonlinear modes framework to the model and derive analytic predictions for the asymptotic distributions of activation barriers $p(Δ{U})\!\sim\!(Δ{U})^{1/4}$ and the external force needed for the onset of plasticity $p(f_{\rm c})\sim f_{\rm c}^{2/3}$, for their extreme-value scaling and for $\langleΔ{U}\rangle$ beyond the asymptotic regime. These predictions are expected to equally apply to the mean-field model and to finite-dimensional glasses. We develop efficient algorithms for sampling minima and saddles of the model's glassy potential energy landscape, and quantitatively confirm the theoretical predictions. This progress is enabled by identifying a subset of collective degrees of freedom that are physically relevant for activated glassy dynamics, which like the theoretical predictions should apply to realistic glasses.

</details>


### [181] [Revealing more on complex energy landscapes by passing less local information: Cavity approach with trust region in non-convex optimization problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10096)
*Zhiqi Xu,Chi Ho Yeung*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该研究提出带信赖域（trust region）的腔消息传递（cavity message-passing）算法，通过限制局部消息中传递的信息量以改善消息收敛，从而刻画非凸优化问题中崎岖能量景观（energy landscape）的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸优化问题的能量景观是高维曲面，难以揭示、可视化与分析；消息传递算法（或统计物理中的腔方法）常因景观崎岖导致消息不收敛，无法识别局部极小值。研究旨在通过限制局部消息传递的信息量来改善收敛，并刻画复杂能量景观的特征。

Method: 研究一个凸性由单一参数控制的路由优化问题（routing optimization problem），引入带信赖域（trust region）的腔消息传递算法，并在同一问题实例的多次独立实现中，对崎岖能量景观的收敛局部极小值集合进行采样。

Result: 观察到三类具有不同能量景观特征的区间：（1）最大化崎岖区间（maximally rugged regime），低能极小值的多样性出现峰值；（2）中间区间，低能解呈层级式多簇组织（hierarchical multi-cluster organization）；（3）平滑区间，由单一极小值主导。附加测试表明，当信赖域规模较小或中等时，收敛能量对其规模基本不敏感。

Conclusion: 带信赖域的腔消息传递方法可有效识别崎岖能量景观的特征，且在信赖域规模较小或中等时结果对规模不敏感，体现出该方法的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Energy landscapes of non-convex optimization problems are high-dimensional surfaces that are difficult to reveal, visualize, or analyze. Message-passing algorithms, or equivalently cavity approaches in statistical physics, may fail to identify local minima as messages do not converge owing to the ruggedness of the energy landscapes. Here we aim to reveal the characteristics of these complex energy landscapes by limiting the amount of information passed in local messages, improving the convergence of messages for local minima. By studying a routing optimization problem with its convexity governed by a single parameter, we introduce a cavity message-passing algorithm with a trust region, and sample ensembles of converged local minima of rugged energy landscapes across many independent realizations of the same problem instances. We observe three regimes with different characteristics of the energy landscapes: (1) a maximally rugged regime with a peak in the diversity of low-energy minima; (2) an intermediate regime with a hierarchical multi-cluster organization of low-energy solutions; and (3) a smooth regime dominated by a single minimum. Additional tests show that the converged energies are largely insensitive to the size of the trust region if the size is small or moderate, demonstrating the robustness of the proposed approach in identifying characteristics of rugged energy landscapes.

</details>


<div id='physics.chem-ph'></div>

# physics.chem-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [Accelerating dynamic polarizability calculations of organic molecules using equivariant graph neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09389)
*Houssam Metni,Maria Kraus,Marie Louise Schubert,Marjan Krstic,Carsten Rockstuhl,Pascal Friederich*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种等变图神经网络（equivariant graph neural network），可直接从分子的3D几何与紫外-可见光谱（UV-vis spectra）预测频率依赖的复值动态极化率张量（dynamic polarizability tensor），在QM9与哈佛有机光伏数据集上成功复现其主要特征，并在器件级光学仿真中达到与TD-DFT结果高度一致（$R^2=0.94$）的载流子产生率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测有机分子的动态极化率张量对模拟光电器件中的光-物质相互作用至关重要，但时 dependent density functional theory（TD-DFT）等传统量子化学方法计算成本高昂，限制了大规模筛选。

Method: 构建等变图神经网络架构，以易获取的分子信息（如3D几何与UV-vis光谱）为输入，直接预测频率依赖的复值动态极化率张量，并学习其色散与吸收特征。

Result: 在QM9与哈佛有机光伏数据集上，模型成功复现动态极化率张量的主要特征，并在不同分子间具备泛化能力；采用地球移动距离（earth mover's distance）等指标评估。在下游器件级光学仿真中，基于预测极化率得到的电荷载流子产生率与TD-DFT结果一致（$R^2 = 0.94$）。

Conclusion: 该方法可作为TD-DFT等传统方法的补充甚至替代方案，实现更快速、更高效的光活性有机分子筛选，服务于光电子应用。

Abstract: Predicting the dynamic polarizability tensor of organic molecules is essential for simulating light-matter interactions in optoelectronic devices, yet conventional quantum-chemical methods such as time-dependent density functional theory (TD-DFT) are computationally expensive and limit large-scale screening. We present an equivariant graph neural network architecture that predicts frequency-dependent, complex-valued dynamic polarizability tensors directly from readily available molecular information, such as 3D geometry and UV-vis spectra. The model learns to approximate the shape and magnitude of the dynamic polarizability tensor, capturing both dispersive and absorptive features that define the molecular optical response. We evaluate the model on molecules from both the QM9 dataset and the Harvard organic photovoltaic dataset using various metrics, including the earth mover's distance, which is suitable for comparing spectral quantities. For both datasets, the model successfully reproduces the main features of the dynamic polarizability tensor and generalizes across diverse molecules. To assess the applicability, we further validated the model using a downstream workflow to calculate the optical properties of organic photovoltaic devices. We used the model-predicted polarizabilities to perform device-level optical simulations, yielding charge carrier generation rates in agreement with TD-DFT-based results ($R^2 = 0.94$). Our approach offers a promising complement, or even an alternative, to conventional methods such as TD-DFT, enabling faster and more efficient screening of photoactive organic molecules for optoelectronic applications.

</details>


### [183] [How Good Is DFT for Solid-Liquid Interfaces? A Comparison With the Random-Phase Approximation for Water on Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09685)
*Xavier R. Advincula,Yair Litman,Jiuyang Shi,Flaviano Della Pia,Christoph Schran,Angelos Michaelides*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该研究以随机相位近似（random-phase approximation, RPA）为高精度参考，在石墨烯-水界面评估了跨越雅各布阶梯（Jacob's Ladder）各层级的交换-相关（exchange-correlation, XC）泛函，发现各泛函在界面结构、润湿性、摩擦与振动光谱上分歧显著，并提出可迁移的多观测量评分框架，筛出六个可靠泛函。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水-固界面模拟结果（接触角、摩擦等）因所用模型不同而系统性地不一致，无论是经验力场还是密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）交换-相关近似，且此前缺乏可判定其可靠性的准确计算参考。因此需要建立一个以高精度方法为基准的评估体系。

Method: 以RPA作为参考，在石墨烯-水界面上比较跨越雅各布阶梯各层级的XC近似；通过分别基于DFT与RPA数据训练的机器学习势（machine-learned potentials, MLPs）实现大规模采样，用以刻画界面结构、润湿性、摩擦及振动和频产生（vibrational sum-frequency generation）光谱；并引入可迁移的多观测量评分框架，将每个泛函与RPA的一致程度浓缩为单一指标。

Result: 各XC近似与RPA之间以及彼此之间均存在实质性分歧。评分框架识别出六个可作为可靠起点的泛函：B3LYP-D3(0)、revPBE-D3(0)、revPBE-D3(BJ)、B97M-rV、r²SCAN与revPBE0-D3(0)。该排序不遵循泛函家族或雅各布阶梯层级的简单规律；但一致的趋势是，动力学与光谱性质比结构性质更难被DFT重现。

Conclusion: 该工作为泛函选择提供了依据，并给出一种可针对任意原子模型（包括新一代基础MLP）在固-液界面与高精度参考进行基准测试的通用策略。其意义还在于可判定哪些模型能把实测接触角、摩擦与振动光谱可靠地关联到其背后的分子结构。

Abstract: Simulations of water at solid interfaces routinely disagree on basic properties, from contact angle to friction, depending on the model used, whether an empirical force field or a density functional theory (DFT) exchange-correlation (XC) approximation. Yet no accurate computational reference has been available to determine which, if any, is reliable. Here we assess XC approximations spanning the rungs of Jacob's Ladder against the random-phase approximation (RPA) at the graphene-water interface. Using machine-learned potentials (MLPs) trained on both DFT and RPA data, we achieve the extensive sampling required to characterize interfacial structure, wettability, friction, and vibrational sum-frequency generation spectra. We find that XC approximations disagree substantially with RPA and with each other. To make sense of this, we introduce a transferable, multi-observable scoring framework that distills each functional's agreement with RPA into a single measure. Six functionals emerge as reliable starting points for studies of graphene-water and other dispersion-dominated interfaces, namely B3LYP-D3(0), revPBE-D3(0), revPBE-D3(BJ), B97M-rV, r$^{2}$SCAN, and revPBE0-D3(0). Notably, this ranking follows no simple rule based on functional family or rung of Jacob's Ladder. What does hold across functionals is that dynamical and spectroscopic properties tend to be harder for DFT to reproduce than structural ones, with direct consequences for functional selection. Our framework also offers a general strategy for benchmarking any atomistic model, including the new generation of foundational MLPs, against high-accuracy references at solid--liquid interfaces. Beyond simulation, this work identifies which models can be trusted to connect measured contact angles, friction, and vibrational spectra to the molecular structure behind them.

</details>


### [184] [Cotunneling-Assisted Redistribution of Nonpassivity in a Photosynthetic Junction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10001)
*Manjet Rajak,Trishna Kalita,Debasish Sharma,Himangshu Prabal Goswami*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该研究将光系统II反应中心建模为非平衡分子结，考察共隧穿（cotunneling）如何影响被动态（passive-state）序。结果显示，共隧穿在醌受体、供体-脱镁叶绿素自由基对与激子态之间重新分配可提取非平衡能量，且高库仑相互作用下不产生被动态重排。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解共隧穿这一微观输运过程如何影响光系统II反应中心（Photosystem II reaction center）中非平衡能量存储与被动态排序。

Method: 采用完整色素激发流形（excitation manifold），在Nakajima-Zwanzig形式（formalism）与真实谱密度下计算激子与电荷转移速率；共隧穿通过由醌侧双电子构型构造的双还原受体态（doubly reduced acceptor state）引入，并以ergotropy与能量电容（energetic capacitance）量化被动态置换。

Result: ergotropy与能量电容的热力学平滑性可掩盖微观状态重排；共隧穿将可提取非平衡能量在醌受体、供体-脱镁叶绿素自由基对（donor-pheophytin radical pair）及激子态间再分配，非活性支（inactive branch）色素贡献显著；高库仑相互作用下共隧穿不引起被动态重排；ergotropy、能量电容与平均能量间的分段线性关系揭示了共隧穿导致的布居重排。

Conclusion: 共隧穿可在热力学量看似平滑的情况下驱动微观布居重排并重新分配非平衡能量，其效应受库仑相互作用强度调控，分段线性关系可作为识别此类重排的判据。

Abstract: We investigate how cotunneling affects passive-state ordering in a Photosystem II reaction center modelled as a nonequilibrium molecular junction. We use a full pigment excitation manifold, with excitonic and charge-transfer rates computed within a Nakajima-Zwanzig formalism using realistic spectral densities. Cotunneling is introduced through a microscopically constructed doubly reduced acceptor state obtained from relevant quinone-side two-electron configurations, while photoexcitation is allowed into the full excitonic manifold. Passive-state permutations are quantified through ergotropy and energetic capacitance. We find that thermodynamic smoothness in ergotropy and energetic capacitance can conceal microscopic state rearrangements. Cotunneling redistributes the extractable nonequilibrium energy among the quinone acceptor, the donor-pheophytin radical pair, and excitonic states with strong contributions from pigments on the inactive branch. At high Coulomb interaction, cotunneling produces no passive-state reordering. Piecewise-linear relations among ergotropy, energetic capacitance, and mean energy further reveal the population reorderings generated by cotunneling.

</details>


<div id='math.PR'></div>

# math.PR [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [Down-the-middle isometries for the Airy and KPZ sheets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26902)
*Duncan Dauvergne,Fardin Syed*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文发现 Airy 面（Airy sheet）与 Airy 线系综（Airy line ensemble）之间存在一种称为“中间向下等距（down-the-middle isometry）”的新耦合，并据此给出扩展 Airy 面作为布朗最后通过渗流（Brownian last passage percolation）标度极限的新构造，同时研究了有向景观（directed landscape）中测地线的聚合（coalescence）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要指出该耦合为 Airy 面与 Airy 线系综之间的一种新型联系，其动机在于借此获得 Airy 面的新构造并推进对有向景观中测地线聚合的理解。摘要未明确说明更具体的研究缺口。

Method: 核心方法为构造“down-the-middle isometry”耦合：在该耦合中，Airy 面由起点位于 $\mathcal{A}_k(0)$（某 $k \in \mathbb{N}$）、终点位于顶线 $\mathcal{A}_1$ 的最后通过值（last passage values）编码。基于此耦合，将（扩展）Airy 面刻画为布朗最后通过渗流的标度极限（scaling limit）。

Result: 得到了（扩展）Airy 面作为布朗最后通过渗流标度极限的一个新构造，并据此研究了有向景观中测地线的聚合现象。该耦合还被指出可推广至离散聚合物（discrete polymers）、最后通过渗流模型，以及 KPZ 线系综（KPZ line ensemble）与 KPZ 面（KPZ sheet）。

Conclusion: Airy 面与 Airy 线系综之间可经由 down-the-middle isometry 建立联系，该联系既为 Airy 面提供了新的标度极限构造，也可用于研究测地线聚合，并具有向离散模型及 KPZ 相关对象的推广性。

Abstract: We find a new coupling between the Airy sheet $\mathcal{S}$ and the Airy line ensemble $\{ \mathcal{A}_n \}_{n \in \mathbb{N}}$, which we call the down-the-middle isometry. In this coupling, the Airy sheet is encoded by last passage values that start at $\mathcal{A}_k(0)$ for some $k \in \mathbb{N}$ and end on the top line $\mathcal{A}_1$. Using this coupling, we give a new construction of the (extended) Airy sheet as a scaling limit of Brownian last passage percolation and study coalescence of geodesics in the directed landscape. This coupling extends to discrete polymers and last passage percolation models, as well as to the KPZ line ensemble and KPZ sheet.

</details>


### [186] [Conditional Flat Exponential Directed Last Passage Percolation under a One-Point Upper Large Deviation Event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09018)
*Rishi Dadlani,Shaotian Sun*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究指数型有向最后通过渗流（exponential directed last passage percolation）中“线到点”模型的罕见大通过时间条件下的极限形状与涨落。作者基于精确多点公式的鞍点分析（steepest-descent analysis）给出启发式预测并证明若干区间的涨落结果，发现近对角线涨落由带有额外共享高斯分量的布朗桥（Brownian bridges）刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指数型有向最后通过渗流与串联排队（tandem queues）和相互作用粒子系统（interacting particle systems）相关。既有工作多集中于起点固定的“点到点”模型，而起点可在线边界内变动的情形（线到点）尚待扩展，尤其在罕见大通过时间条件下的条件行为。

Method: 针对起点在一条线边界内可变的线到点模型，施加“到固定终点的最后通过时间异常大”的条件；将近期点到点模型的结果推广，利用精确多点公式（exact multi-point formulas）进行鞍点分析（steepest-descent analysis）。

Result: 给出条件极限形状（conditional limit shape）的启发式预测，并在特定区间证明相应的涨落结果；计算表明近对角线处的条件涨落可由布朗桥经由一个额外的共享高斯分量耦合来描述。

Conclusion: 线到点模型在罕见大通过时间条件下呈现出与点到点模型相关的极限形状与涨落结构，其近对角线涨落为共享高斯分量耦合的布朗桥，提示起点变动会引入附加的全局相关性。

Abstract: Exponential directed last passage percolation is a random maximization model connected to tandem queues and interacting particle systems. We study the line-to-point model of the percolation, in which the starting point is allowed to vary inside a line boundary, conditioned on an atypically large last-passage time to a fixed endpoint. Extending recent results for the point-to-point model where the starting point is fixed, we develop heuristic predictions for the conditional limit shape and prove corresponding fluctuation results in certain regimes through steepest-descent analysis of exact multi-point formulas. Our calculations suggest that the conditional fluctuations near diagonal are described by Brownian bridges coupled through an additional shared Gaussian component.

</details>


### [187] [Two dimensional Villain model in the BKT phase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10083)
*Jiwoon Park,Wei Wu*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文在足够低温下建立了二维 Villain 模型（Villain model）两点函数（two-point function）的精确渐近行为，证实了 Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless 理论（BKT theory）的一个核心预言，并通过重整化群流刻画了幂律指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless 理论对二维低温相中两点函数呈幂律衰减有核心预言，但缺乏严格的渐近刻画；本文旨在为二维 Villain 模型提供该预言的严格证明。

Method: 通过对偶高温离散高斯模型（dual high-temperature Discrete Gaussian model）中一个边缘线缺陷可观测量的重整化群流（renormalisation group flow）来刻画幂律指数（power law exponent）。

Result: 在足够低温下获得了二维 Villain 模型两点函数的精确渐近形式，并借由重整化群流对幂律指数给出了刻画。

Conclusion: 结果证实了 BKT 理论的一项核心预言，表明低温二维 Villain 模型的幂律行为可由对偶离散高斯模型中边缘线缺陷的重整化群流定量描述。

Abstract: We establish sharp two-point function asymptotics for the two dimensional Villain model at sufficiently low temperatures, confirming a central prediction in the Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless theory. We characterise the power law exponent through the renormalisation group flow of a marginal line defect observable in the dual high-temperature Discrete Gaussian model.

</details>


### [188] [The Tutte embedding of tree-weighted planar maps converges to Liouville quantum gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10501)
*Nina Holden*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文证明：在临界玻尔兹曼测度下采样、带宏观边界并装饰有生成树的平面地图（planar map），其 Tutte 嵌入（Tutte embedding）在边界长度趋于无穷时收敛到参数 γ=√2 的刘维尔量子引力圆盘（Liouville quantum gravity disk）；并且在给定地图与生成树的条件下，其上的简单随机游走（simple random walk）在时间重参数化意义下收敛到平面布朗运动（planar Brownian motion）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目标是建立临界平面地图与刘维尔量子引力之间的对应关系，并刻画此类地图上随机游走的连续极限。摘要未给出更具体的研究背景陈述。

Method: 考察从临界玻尔兹曼测度（critical Boltzmann measure）采样的、装饰有生成树（spanning tree）的宏观边界平面地图，采用 Tutte 嵌入作为规范化嵌入方式，令边界长度趋于无穷，并研究其极限几何与随机游走行为。

Result: 平面地图 M 在 Tutte 嵌入下收敛到参数 γ=√2 的刘维尔量子引力圆盘（Liouville quantum gravity disk）。在给定 (M,T) 的条件下，M 上的简单随机游走收敛到平面布朗运动（planar Brownian motion），差异仅为时间的重参数化。

Conclusion: 结果表明临界玻尔兹曼平面地图（带生成树装饰）的标度极限可由 γ=√2 的刘维尔量子引力描述，且其上的随机游走在重参数化后与平面布朗运动一致。摘要未额外给出推广或未解决问题。

Abstract: Suppose $M$ is a planar map with a macroscopic boundary decorated by a spanning tree $T$, such that $(M,T)$ has been sampled from the critical Boltzman measure. We show that $M$ converges to a Liouville quantum gravity disk with parameter $γ=\sqrt{2}$ under the Tutte embedding as the length of the boundary of $M$ goes to infinity. Furthermore, conditioned on $(M,T)$, the simple random walk on $M$ converges to a planar Brownian motion modulo reparametrization of time.

</details>


### [189] [Vertex crossings in a symmetric Markov multinomial model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09258)
*Arjun Pemmasani*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究多方向高尔顿板（Galton board）中小球在各钉子处以固定概率保持方向、否则随机转向的情形，将各方向的访问次数视为单纯形（simplex）上的点。核心问题是单纯形各面的最优格子（bin）何时与角点同样可能；一阶上所有面同时追平，作者通过二阶展开打破这一简并，给出每个面的显式常数，并据此刻画长板情形下最可能格子从角点直接跃迁至中心的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小球很少转向时，最可能的格子仅由从不转向的球可达，即单纯形的角点；但随板长增加、期望转向次数增大，非角点格子可能变得同样或更为可能。作者希望确定各面（face）的最优格子追平角点的具体时刻，并理解这一竞争在何种条件下发生。

Method: 将问题建模为多方向高尔顿板上的随机游走：每步以固定概率保持原方向，否则在其他方向中随机选择，记录各方向访问次数并映射到单纯形上的点。通过一阶与二阶渐近展开分析各面的最可能格子与角点的相对可能性，并计算每个面对应的显式常数；同时考察非均匀初始分布与弱外场（weak external field）对常数的影响。

Result: 一阶展开下，所有面在同一时刻追平角点，存在简并；二阶展开打破该简并，并为每个面给出显式常数。由此得到长板上的跃迁刻画：当期望转向次数增长至板长的对数（logarithm of the length）的任意固定倍数时，最可能格子发生一次跳跃，从角点直接变为中心。

Conclusion: 非均匀初始分布（nonuniform start）或弱外场会改变上述竞争常数，可能使某个中间面（intermediate face）胜出，即改变长板上最可能格子的最终归属。

Abstract: We observe a ball bouncing down a Galton board with any number of directions. At each peg it either keeps its direction with some fixed probability or randomly turns to one of the other directions. Its bin measures how often it went each way, a point of a simplex. When the ball rarely turns, the most likely bins are the corners, which only a ball that never turns can reach. We ask when the best bin of each face of the simplex becomes as likely as a corner. To first order every face catches up at the same moment. We break this tie at second order, with an explicit constant for each face. Hence on a long board, as the expected number of turns grows to any fixed multiple of the length's logarithm, the most likely bin jumps once, from the corners straight to the center. A nonuniform start or a weak external field changes the constants, potentially allowing an intermediate face to win.

</details>
